# Leviticus

ויקרא

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus)

עבודת הקורבנות במשכן, דיני טהרה ומצוות אחרות העוסקות בעבודה חקלאית, בהתנהגות חברתית, ובמועדים.

Laws of sacrificial worship in the Mishkan (Tabernacle), ritual purity, and other topics like agriculture, ethics, and holidays.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Torah

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 859, License: CC-BY-SA)

Versions (En):
  - Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009 (Segments: 859, License: CC-BY)
  - The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 859, License: CC-BY-NC)
  - The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein (Segments: 859, License: CC-BY)


## Chapter 1

###### Leviticus 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:1)


וַיִּקְרָ֖א אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֑ה וַיְדַבֵּ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלָ֔יו מֵאֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵ֖ד לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And He called to Moshe, and Adonoy spoke to him from the Tent of Meeting, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
AND the Lord called to Moshe, and spoke to him out of the Tent of Meeting, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He called (vayikra) [("kriyah" is an expression of endearment)] to Moses [(G-d's voice reached Moses' ears only)], and the L-rd spoke (vayedaber) [(There is "calling" only for (initial) "speaking" (dibbur), and not for pauses (subsections within the sections)] to him [and not to Aaron] from the tent of meeting [(the voice "cutting off" and not traveling beyond the tent of meeting)], "saying" [i.e., Speak to them (Israel) with "suasion"]


###### Leviticus 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:2)


דַּבֵּ֞ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵהֶ֔ם אָדָ֗ם כִּֽי־יַקְרִ֥יב מִכֶּ֛ם קׇרְבָּ֖ן לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה מִן־הַבְּהֵמָ֗ה מִן־הַבָּקָר֙ וּמִן־הַצֹּ֔אן תַּקְרִ֖יבוּ אֶת־קׇרְבַּנְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them; [if] a man among you will bring an offering to Adonoy; of animals, cattle or sheep you should bring your offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, If any man of you bring an offering to the Lord, of the cattle shall you bring your offering, of the herd, and of the flock.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel and say to them: A man (adam) [(Just as Adam did not sacrifice what was stolen, so, you)], if he offer from you an offering to the L-rd, from the beasts [(but not all — to exclude participants, active or passive, in sodomy)], from the cattle [(to exclude objects of idolatry)] and from the sheep [(to exclude animals designated for idolatry) ("from the beasts" like cattle and sheep, but not undomesticated animals) ("and from the sheep" — to exclude animals which gored men to death)] shall you (plural) offer [(two may offer a burnt-offering in partnership)] your (plural) offering [(It may be a communal gift offering)].


###### Leviticus 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:3)


אִם־עֹלָ֤ה קׇרְבָּנוֹ֙ מִן־הַבָּקָ֔ר זָכָ֥ר תָּמִ֖ים יַקְרִיבֶ֑נּוּ אֶל־פֶּ֜תַח אֹ֤הֶל מוֹעֵד֙ יַקְרִ֣יב אֹת֔וֹ לִרְצֹנ֖וֹ לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If his offering is a burnt-offering of cattle he shall bring an unblemished male; to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting he shall bring it, that it be favorably accepted from him before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If his offering be a burnt sacrifice of the herd, let him offer a male without blemish: he shall offer it at the door of the Tent of Meeting, that he may be accepted before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If his offering is a burnt-offering, from the cattle [("from") — to exclude a treifah (a "torn," ritually unfit, animal)], a male, [and not a female], without blemish, shall he offer it; to the door of the tent of meeting shall he bring it [(He must attend to getting it to the azarah (the Temple court)], of his own volition, before the L-rd [(There is no semichah (4) at a bamah (a temporary altar)].


###### Leviticus 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:4)


וְסָמַ֣ךְ יָד֔וֹ עַ֖ל רֹ֣אשׁ הָעֹלָ֑ה וְנִרְצָ֥ה ל֖וֹ לְכַפֵּ֥ר עָלָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall lay his hand on the head of the burnt-offering and it will be favorably accepted from him to achieve his atonement.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall put his hand upon the head of the burnt offering; and it shall be accepted for him to make atonement for him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall place (vesamach) his hand on the head of the [(excluding (from semichah) the burnt-offering of fowl)] burnt-offering [(including an obligatory burnt-offering and the burnt-offering of sheep)], and it shall be acceptable for him to make atonement for him [for transgressions of positive commandments or for negative commandments linked to positive commandments].


###### Leviticus 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:5)


וְשָׁחַ֛ט אֶת־בֶּ֥ן הַבָּקָ֖ר לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְ֠הִקְרִ֠יבוּ בְּנֵ֨י אַהֲרֹ֤ן הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ אֶת־הַדָּ֔ם וְזָרְק֨וּ אֶת־הַדָּ֤ם עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֙חַ֙ סָבִ֔יב אֲשֶׁר־פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall slaughter the calf before Adonoy and the sons of Aharon, the *kohanim,* will bring the blood and they will sprinkle the blood all around the altar, which is at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall kill the bullock before the Lord: and the priests, Aharon’s sons, shall bring the blood, and sprinkle the blood round about upon the altar that is by the door of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall slaughter [(It is permissible for a non-priest to slaughter)] the bullock before the L-rd [in the azarah], and the sons of Aaron, the priests [(they must be fit for the priesthood)] shall present [i.e., receive and bring] the blood, and they shall dash the blood [(including blood which intermingled with that of an offering of its kind or of another kind)] on the altar roundabout [(two "dashings" (from a vessel) on two (diagonally) opposite corners of the altar)], which is at the entrance of the tent of meeting [(but not when the tent of meeting is dismantled)].


###### Leviticus 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:6)


וְהִפְשִׁ֖יט אֶת־הָעֹלָ֑ה וְנִתַּ֥ח אֹתָ֖הּ לִנְתָחֶֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall skin the burnt-offering and cut it into its pieces.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall flay the burnt offering, and cut it into its pieces.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall flay the burnt-offering [(all burnt-offerings are included)], and he shall cut it into its pieces [(but not its pieces into pieces)].


###### Leviticus 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:7)


וְ֠נָתְנ֠וּ בְּנֵ֨י אַהֲרֹ֧ן הַכֹּהֵ֛ן אֵ֖שׁ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ וְעָרְכ֥וּ עֵצִ֖ים עַל־הָאֵֽשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The sons of Aharon the *kohein* shall place [ignite] a fire on the altar and they shall arrange logs on the fire.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the sons of Aharon the priest shall put fire upon the altar, and lay the wood in order upon the fire:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Aaron the priest [(in his "priesthood," If he officiated in the garments of an ordinary priest, his service is invalid)] shall put fire upon the altar [(Even though fire descends from heaven, it is a mitzvah to bring man-made fire)], and they shall arrange wood upon the fire.


###### Leviticus 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:8)


וְעָרְכ֗וּ בְּנֵ֤י אַהֲרֹן֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים אֵ֚ת הַנְּתָחִ֔ים אֶת־הָרֹ֖אשׁ וְאֶת־הַפָּ֑דֶר עַל־הָעֵצִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־הָאֵ֔שׁ אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The sons of Aharon, the *kohanim,* shall arrange the pieces, the head and the fat on the logs which are on the fire that is on the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priests, Aharon’s sons, shall lay the parts, the head, and the fat, in order upon the wood that is on the fire which is upon the altar:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Aaron, the priests [(in their "priesthood." If they officiate in the eight garments of the high-priest, their service is invalid)] shall arrange the pieces, the head [(which was cut off and not flayed)] and the suet [(which is brought up with the head to cover the shechitah site (a deferential act)] upon the wood upon the fire, which is upon the altar [(Pieces of wood should not stick out beyond the wood pile)].


###### Leviticus 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:9)


וְקִרְבּ֥וֹ וּכְרָעָ֖יו יִרְחַ֣ץ בַּמָּ֑יִם וְהִקְטִ֨יר הַכֹּהֵ֤ן אֶת־הַכֹּל֙ הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה עֹלָ֛ה אִשֵּׁ֥ה רֵֽיחַ־נִיח֖וֹחַ לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall wash its innards and its legs in water; and the *kohein* shall burn it all on the altar [for the purpose of] a burnt-offering, a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but its inwards and its legs shall he wash in water: and the priest shall burn all on the altar, to be a burnt sacrifice, an offering made by fire, of a sweet savour to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And its innards and its legs shall he wash with water, and the Cohein shall smoke the whole upon the altar, [specifically] as a burnt-offering, a fire-offering [(He must slaughter it to that end)], a sweet savor, ["gratifying," (in that His will was done)], to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:10)


וְאִם־מִן־הַצֹּ֨אן קׇרְבָּנ֧וֹ מִן־הַכְּשָׂבִ֛ים א֥וֹ מִן־הָעִזִּ֖ים לְעֹלָ֑ה זָכָ֥ר תָּמִ֖ים יַקְרִיבֶֽנּוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If his offering is from flocks of sheep—of lambs or of goats—for a burnt-offering, he shall bring an unblemished male.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if his offering be of the flocks, namely, of the sheep, or of the goats, for a burnt sacrifice; he shall bring it a male without blemish.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, [(Whatever applies to "cattle" (3) applies to "flock" and vice versa)] if from the flock is his offering, of the sheep or of the goats, [(excluding the old, sick, or malodorous)], as a burnt-offering, a male [(and not one whose sex is in doubt or a hermaphrodite), without blemish shall he offer it.


###### Leviticus 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:11)


וְשָׁחַ֨ט אֹת֜וֹ עַ֣ל יֶ֧רֶךְ הַמִּזְבֵּ֛חַ צָפֹ֖נָה לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְזָרְק֡וּ בְּנֵי֩ אַהֲרֹ֨ן הַכֹּהֲנִ֧ים אֶת־דָּמ֛וֹ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ סָבִֽיב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall slaughter it on the north side of the altar before Adonoy; and the sons of Aharon, the *kohanim,* will sprinkle its blood all around the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall kill it on the side of the altar northward before the Lord: and the priests, Aharon’s sons, shall sprinkle its blood round about on the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall slaughter it on the side of the altar northward before the L-rd [(There is no "northward" (requirement) at a bamah (a temporary altar)], and the sons of Aaron, the Cohanim, shall dash its blood on the altar roundabout.


###### Leviticus 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:12)


וְנִתַּ֤ח אֹתוֹ֙ לִנְתָחָ֔יו וְאֶת־רֹאשׁ֖וֹ וְאֶת־פִּדְר֑וֹ וְעָרַ֤ךְ הַכֹּהֵן֙ אֹתָ֔ם עַל־הָֽעֵצִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־הָאֵ֔שׁ אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall sever it into its pieces—and its head, and [remove] its fats; and the *kohein* shall arrange them on the logs which are on the fire that is on the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall cut it into its pieces, with its head and its fat: and the priest shall lay them in order on the wood that is on the fire which is on the altar:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall cut it into its pieces, and its head, and its suet; and the Cohein shall arrange them upon the wood upon the fire, which is upon the altar.


###### Leviticus 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:13)


וְהַקֶּ֥רֶב וְהַכְּרָעַ֖יִם יִרְחַ֣ץ בַּמָּ֑יִם וְהִקְרִ֨יב הַכֹּהֵ֤ן אֶת־הַכֹּל֙ וְהִקְטִ֣יר הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה עֹלָ֣ה ה֗וּא אִשֵּׁ֛ה רֵ֥יחַ נִיחֹ֖חַ לַיהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall wash the innards and feet in water and the *kohein* shall bring it all and burn it on the altar. It is a burnt-offering; a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but he shall wash the inwards and the legs with water: and the priest shall bring it all, and burn it upon the altar: it is a burnt sacrifice, an offering made by fire, of a sweet savour to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the innards and the legs he shall wash with water, and the Cohein shall offer the whole, and he shall smoke it upon the altar. It is a burnt-offering, a fire-offering, a sweet savor to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:14)


וְאִ֧ם מִן־הָע֛וֹף עֹלָ֥ה קׇרְבָּנ֖וֹ לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה וְהִקְרִ֣יב מִן־הַתֹּרִ֗ים א֛וֹ מִן־בְּנֵ֥י הַיּוֹנָ֖ה אֶת־קׇרְבָּנֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If his burnt-offering to Adonoy is of fowl, he shall bring either turtledoves or the young pigeons [as] his offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the burnt sacrifice for his offering to the Lord be of birds, then he shall bring his offering of turtledoves, or of young pigeons.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if from the fowl [(excluding a bird that is missing a limb)], a burnt-offering, is his offering to the L-rd, then shall he offer his offering from the turtle-doves [(grown ones and not small ones)] or from the young pigeons [(small ones and not grown ones)] [("from the turtle-doves or from the young pigeons") — excluding those whose plumage has just begun brightening].


###### Leviticus 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:15)


וְהִקְרִיב֤וֹ הַכֹּהֵן֙ אֶל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חַ וּמָלַק֙ אֶת־רֹאשׁ֔וֹ וְהִקְטִ֖יר הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חָה וְנִמְצָ֣ה דָמ֔וֹ עַ֖ל קִ֥יר הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall bring it near the altar and nip off its head [with his fingernail] and burn it on the altar; and its blood shall be squeezed out onto the wall of the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall bring it to the altar, and wring off its head, and burn it on the altar; and its blood shall be wrung out at the side of the altar:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall bring it [(even a single pigeon)] near to the altar, and he shall pinch off its head [(He cuts with his nail against the nape until he reaches the shechitah signs — gullet and windpipe — which he severs)], and he shall smoke it upon the altar; and its blood shall be wrung out [before smoking] against the wall of the altar [(He presses the shechitah site against it and the blood drains down)].


###### Leviticus 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:16)


וְהֵסִ֥יר אֶת־מֻרְאָת֖וֹ בְּנֹצָתָ֑הּ וְהִשְׁלִ֨יךְ אֹתָ֜הּ אֵ֤צֶל הַמִּזְבֵּ֙חַ֙ קֵ֔דְמָה אֶל־מְק֖וֹם הַדָּֽשֶׁן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall remove its crop [together] with its feathers and throw it beside the altar, eastward, to the place of the ashes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall remove its crop with its feathers, and cast it beside the altar on the east part, by the place of the ashes:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall remove its crop with its entrails, and he shall cast it beside the altar on the east [of the ramp], in the place of the ashes [removed from the altar and from the menorah].


###### Leviticus 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/1:17)


וְשִׁסַּ֨ע אֹת֣וֹ בִכְנָפָיו֮ לֹ֣א יַבְדִּיל֒ וְהִקְטִ֨יר אֹת֤וֹ הַכֹּהֵן֙ הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה עַל־הָעֵצִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־הָאֵ֑שׁ עֹלָ֣ה ה֗וּא אִשֵּׁ֛ה רֵ֥יחַ נִיחֹ֖חַ לַיהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall rip it apart by its wings, not completely dividing it. The *kohein* shall burn it on the altar, on the logs which are on the fire; it is a burnt-offering, a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall rend it by its wings, but shall not divide it asunder: and the priest shall burn it on the altar, on the wood that is on the fire: it is a burnt sacrifice, an offering made by fire, of a sweet savour to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall rend it with its feathers [(He need not pluck them out)]; he shall not divide it [completely (but he tears it along its back)]. And the Cohein shall smoke it on the altar, on the wood which is on the fire. It is a burnt-offering, a fire-offering, a sweet savor to the L-rd.



## Chapter 2

###### Leviticus 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:1)


וְנֶ֗פֶשׁ כִּֽי־תַקְרִ֞יב קׇרְבַּ֤ן מִנְחָה֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה סֹ֖לֶת יִהְיֶ֣ה קׇרְבָּנ֑וֹ וְיָצַ֤ק עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ שֶׁ֔מֶן וְנָתַ֥ן עָלֶ֖יהָ לְבֹנָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When a person brings a meal-offering to Adonoy, his offering shall be of fine flour; he shall pour oil upon it and place frankincense upon it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when any will offer a meal offering to the Lord, his offering shall be of fine flour; and he shall pour oil upon it, and put frankincense upon it:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a soul offer [(The Holy One Blessed be He says: "I shall account it to him as if he had offered his soul")] a meal-offering to the L-rd [and he does not specify which, of the five kinds of meal-offering], fine [wheat] flour (soleth) shall be his offering [(this one alone being called "soleth," because it is the only one of the five of which the fistful is taken before baking)]. And he [(even a non-priest)] shall pour oil upon it [(upon all of it)] and he shall put frankincense upon it [(upon part of it)].


###### Leviticus 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:2)


וֶֽהֱבִיאָ֗הּ אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י אַהֲרֹן֮ הַכֹּהֲנִים֒ וְקָמַ֨ץ מִשָּׁ֜ם מְלֹ֣א קֻמְצ֗וֹ מִסׇּלְתָּהּ֙ וּמִשַּׁמְנָ֔הּ עַ֖ל כׇּל־לְבֹנָתָ֑הּ וְהִקְטִ֨יר הַכֹּהֵ֜ן אֶת־אַזְכָּרָתָהּ֙ הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה אִשֵּׁ֛ה רֵ֥יחַ נִיחֹ֖חַ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring it to the sons of Aharon, the *kohanim*; from there, [a *kohein*] takes his fistful—from its flour and oil—with all its frankincense. The *kohein* shall burn its memorial portion on the altar, a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall bring it to Aharon’s sons the priests: and he shall take from it his handful of its fine flour, and of its oil, with all its frankincense; and the priest shall burn the memorial part of it on the altar, to be an offering made by fire, a sweet savour to the Lord:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall bring it to the sons of Aaron, the Cohanim, and he (the Cohein) shall take from there [(from wherever the donor is standing in the azarah)] his full fistful, [covering three fingers over the palm of his hand. (From the fistful on is the mitzvah of the kehunah)], of its fine flour and its oil, aside from all of its frankincense [i.e., the frankincense is not included in the fistful]. And the Cohein shall smoke [(the frankincense, too, is smoked)] its "remembrance" [(the fistful, the remembrance of the meal-offering, whereby its owner is remembered for good)] upon the altar, a fire-offering, a sweet savor to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:3)


וְהַנּוֹתֶ֙רֶת֙ מִן־הַמִּנְחָ֔ה לְאַהֲרֹ֖ן וּלְבָנָ֑יו קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁ֖ים מֵאִשֵּׁ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whatever remains of the meal-offering belongs to Aharon and his sons; [it is] holy of holies of the fire-offerings of Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the remnant of the meal offering shall be Aharon’s and his sons’: it is a thing most holy of the offerings of the Lord made by fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And what is left from the meal-offering shall be for Aaron [(i.e., for the high-priest) without apportionment] and for his sons [with apportionment]. It is holy of holies [to them] of the fire-offerings of the L-rd. [They have no portion in it until the fistful has been smoked.]


###### Leviticus 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:4)


וְכִ֥י תַקְרִ֛ב קׇרְבַּ֥ן מִנְחָ֖ה מַאֲפֵ֣ה תַנּ֑וּר סֹ֣לֶת חַלּ֤וֹת מַצֹּת֙ בְּלוּלֹ֣ת בַּשֶּׁ֔מֶן וּרְקִיקֵ֥י מַצּ֖וֹת מְשֻׁחִ֥ים בַּשָּֽׁמֶן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And when you bring a meal-offering that has been baked in an oven, [it shall be unleavened] matzoh-loaves of fine flour mixed with oil, or [unleavened] matzoh wafers smeared with oil.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if thou bring a sacrifice of meal offering baked in the oven, it shall be unleavened cakes of fine flour mingled with oil, or unleavened wafers anointed with oil.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if you bring an offer of a meal-offering baked in an oven, [i.e., If one says: "I vow a meal-offering baked in an oven"], (it shall be) of fine flour: [ten] unleavened cakes mixed with oil, or [ten] unleavened wafers spread with oil. [It may be "second" or "third" oil (and does not require the first oil that comes out of the olive as the menorah does.)]


###### Leviticus 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:5)


וְאִם־מִנְחָ֥ה עַל־הַֽמַּחֲבַ֖ת קׇרְבָּנֶ֑ךָ סֹ֛לֶת בְּלוּלָ֥ה בַשֶּׁ֖מֶן מַצָּ֥ה תִהְיֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If your offering is a pan-baked meal offering, it shall be of fine flour mixed with oil; it shall be [unleavened] matzoh.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if thy sacrifice be a meal offering baked in a pan, it shall be of fine flour unleavened, mingled with oil.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a meal-offering (baked) on a griddle is your offering, of fine flour mixed with oil [(while it is yet flour)], unleavened bread shall it be.


###### Leviticus 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:6)


פָּת֤וֹת אֹתָהּ֙ פִּתִּ֔ים וְיָצַקְתָּ֥ עָלֶ֖יהָ שָׁ֑מֶן מִנְחָ֖ה הִֽוא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Break it into pieces and pour oil on it; it is a meal-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt part it in pieces, and pour oil on it: it is a meal offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall break it [(i.e., all meal-offerings that are baked before the fistful is taken)] into pieces [before kemitzah (the taking of the fistful)], and you shall pour oil upon it [(but not upon a meal-offering baked in an oven)]; it is a meal-offering.


###### Leviticus 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:7)


וְאִם־מִנְחַ֥ת מַרְחֶ֖שֶׁת קׇרְבָּנֶ֑ךָ סֹ֥לֶת בַּשֶּׁ֖מֶן תֵּעָשֶֽׂה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If your offering is a deep-pan meal offering, you shall make it with fine flour and oil.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if thy sacrifice be a meal offering baked in a frying pan, it shall be made of fine flour with oil.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a meal-offering of the stewing pan is your offering, of fine flour in oil shall it be made.


###### Leviticus 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:8)


וְהֵבֵאתָ֣ אֶת־הַמִּנְחָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֧ר יֵעָשֶׂ֛ה מֵאֵ֖לֶּה לַיהֹוָ֑ה וְהִקְרִיבָהּ֙ אֶל־הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְהִגִּישָׁ֖הּ אֶל־הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall bring the meal-offering that will be made in any of the aforementioned ways to Adonoy. He shall bring it to the *kohein* and he will present it to the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt bring the meal offering that is made of these things to the Lord: and when it is presented to the priest, he shall bring it to the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall bring the meal-offering that shall be made of [one of] these kinds to the L-rd; and he [the donor] shall present it to the Cohein, and he shall touch it to the [south-west corner of the] altar.


###### Leviticus 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:9)


וְהֵרִ֨ים הַכֹּהֵ֤ן מִן־הַמִּנְחָה֙ אֶת־אַזְכָּ֣רָתָ֔הּ וְהִקְטִ֖יר הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חָה אִשֵּׁ֛ה רֵ֥יחַ נִיחֹ֖חַ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall separate from the meal-offering its memorial portion and burn it on the altar, a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall take from the meal offering its memorial part, and shall burn it on the altar: it is an offering made by fire, of a sweet savour to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall lift from the meal-offering its remembrance [the fistful], and he shall smoke it upon the altar, a fire-offering, a sweet savor to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:10)


וְהַנּוֹתֶ֙רֶת֙ מִן־הַמִּנְחָ֔ה לְאַהֲרֹ֖ן וּלְבָנָ֑יו קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁ֖ים מֵאִשֵּׁ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whatever remains of the meal-offering belongs to Aharon and his sons; [it is] holy of holies [most holy] of the fire-offering of Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And that which is left of the meal offering shall be Aharon’s and his sons’: it is a thing most holy of the offerings of the Lord made by fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And what is left from the meal-offering shall be for Aaron and his sons, holy of holies of the fire-offerings of the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:11)


כׇּל־הַמִּנְחָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר תַּקְרִ֙יבוּ֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה לֹ֥א תֵעָשֶׂ֖ה חָמֵ֑ץ כִּ֤י כׇל־שְׂאֹר֙ וְכׇל־דְּבַ֔שׁ לֹֽא־תַקְטִ֧ירוּ מִמֶּ֛נּוּ אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All meal-offerings that you bring to Adonoy are not to be made leavened, because all sourdough and all honey you shall not burn from them a fire-offering to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
No meal offering, which you shall bring to the Lord, shall be made with leaven: for you shall burn no leaven, nor any honey, in any offering of the Lord made by fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All of the meal-offering that you offer up to the L-rd shall not be made with leaven; for all leaven and all "honey" [any sweet-fruit extract] you shall not smoke from it a fire-offering to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:12)


קׇרְבַּ֥ן רֵאשִׁ֛ית תַּקְרִ֥יבוּ אֹתָ֖ם לַיהֹוָ֑ה וְאֶל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֥חַ לֹא־יַעֲל֖וּ לְרֵ֥יחַ נִיחֹֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall bring them as a first [fruit-]offering to Adonoy but they shall not go up [be placed atop] the altar as a pleasing fragrance.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for the offerings of the firstfruits, you shall offer them to the Lord: but they shall not be burnt on the altar for a sweet savour.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As a first-offering [the two loaves of Atzereth (Shavuoth), which are of leaven, and the bikkurim, of fruit-honey (e.g., figs and dates)] you may offer them up to the L-rd; but they shall not come up to the altar for a sweet savor.


###### Leviticus 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:13)


וְכׇל־קׇרְבַּ֣ן מִנְחָתְךָ֮ בַּמֶּ֣לַח תִּמְלָח֒ וְלֹ֣א תַשְׁבִּ֗ית מֶ֚לַח בְּרִ֣ית אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ מֵעַ֖ל מִנְחָתֶ֑ךָ עַ֥ל כׇּל־קׇרְבָּנְךָ֖ תַּקְרִ֥יב מֶֽלַח׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall salt all your meal-offerings with salt and you shall not omit salt, the covenant of your God from [being placed] upon your meal-offerings. You shall bring salt on every one of your offerings.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thy every meal offering shalt thou season with salt; nor shalt thou suffer the salt of the covenant of thy God to be lacking from thy meal offering: with all thy offerings thou shalt offer salt.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every offering of your meal-offering you shall salt with salt. And you shall not cut off the salt of the covenant of your G-d from your meal-offering. [G-d made a covenant for salt from the six days of creation, when the lower waters were "promised" that salt would be offered up on the altar and that water would be offered as a libation (on Succoth).] With all of your offerings [(With the burnt-offering of beast and bird and with the devoted portions (emurim) of all sacrifices)] shall you offer salt.


###### Leviticus 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:14)


וְאִם־תַּקְרִ֛יב מִנְחַ֥ת בִּכּוּרִ֖ים לַיהֹוָ֑ה אָבִ֞יב קָל֤וּי בָּאֵשׁ֙ גֶּ֣רֶשׂ כַּרְמֶ֔ל תַּקְרִ֕יב אֵ֖ת מִנְחַ֥ת בִּכּוּרֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you bring a meal-offering of first grains to Adonoy, of newly ripened crops, roasted over fire, ground [full and moist] kernels, you shall bring your first grain meal-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if thou offer a meal offering of thy firstfruits to the Lord, thou shalt offer for the meal offering of thy firstfruits ears of corn dried by the fire, corn beaten out of fresh ears.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when you offer a meal-offering of first-fruits to the L-rd [the meal-offering of the omer], ripened grain [barley] in the ear parched with fire [to stiffen it for milling], groats of the fresh ear [i.e., ground while it is moist] shall you bring the meal-offering of your first-fruits.


###### Leviticus 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:15)


וְנָתַתָּ֤ עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ שֶׁ֔מֶן וְשַׂמְתָּ֥ עָלֶ֖יהָ לְבֹנָ֑ה מִנְחָ֖ה הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall put oil on it and place frankincense upon it; it is a meal-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt put oil upon it, and lay on frankincense: it is a meal offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall put oil upon it, and you shall put frankincense upon it; it is a meal-offering.


###### Leviticus 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/2:16)


וְהִקְטִ֨יר הַכֹּהֵ֜ן אֶת־אַזְכָּרָתָ֗הּ מִגִּרְשָׂהּ֙ וּמִשַּׁמְנָ֔הּ עַ֖ל כׇּל־לְבֹנָתָ֑הּ אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַיהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall burn its memorial portion of its ground flour and of its oil and all of its frankincense; [it is] a fire-offering to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall burn the memorial part of it, of its beaten corn, and of its oil, with all its frankincense: it is an offering made by fire to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall smoke its remembrance of its groats and of its oil, with all its frankincense, a fire-offering to the L-rd.



## Chapter 3

###### Leviticus 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:1)


וְאִם־זֶ֥בַח שְׁלָמִ֖ים קׇרְבָּנ֑וֹ אִ֤ם מִן־הַבָּקָר֙ ה֣וּא מַקְרִ֔יב אִם־זָכָר֙ אִם־נְקֵבָ֔ה תָּמִ֥ים יַקְרִיבֶ֖נּוּ לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If his offering is a peace -offering; if his offering is of cattle [whether] male or female; he shall bring it unblemished before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if his offering be a sacrifice of peace offering, if he offer it of the herd; whether it be a male or female, he shall offer it without blemish before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a sacrifice of peace-offerings (shelamim) [(so called because they make peace (shalom) in the world)] is his offering — if he offers it from the herd — whether male or female — without blemish shall he offer it before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:2)


וְסָמַ֤ךְ יָדוֹ֙ עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ קׇרְבָּנ֔וֹ וּשְׁחָט֕וֹ פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד וְזָרְק֡וּ בְּנֵי֩ אַהֲרֹ֨ן הַכֹּהֲנִ֧ים אֶת־הַדָּ֛ם עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ סָבִֽיב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall lay his hand on the head of his offering and slaughter it at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting. The sons of Aharon, the *kohanim,* will sprinkle the blood all around the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall lay his hand upon the head of his offering, and kill it at the door of the Tent of Meeting: and Aharon’s sons the priests shall sprinkle the blood on the altar round about.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall place his hand upon the head of his offering, and he shall slaughter it at the door of the tent of meeting; and the sons of Aaron, the Cohanim, shall dash the blood on the altar roundabout.


###### Leviticus 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:3)


וְהִקְרִיב֙ מִזֶּ֣בַח הַשְּׁלָמִ֔ים אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַיהֹוָ֑ה אֶת־הַחֵ֙לֶב֙ הַֽמְכַסֶּ֣ה אֶת־הַקֶּ֔רֶב וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־הַחֵ֔לֶב אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַקֶּֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring from the peace-offering, as a fire-offering to Adonoy, the fat covering the innards, and all the fat on the innards.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall offer of the sacrifice of the peace offering an offering made by fire to the Lord; the fat that covers the inwards, and all the fat that is upon the inwards,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall present from the sacrifice of the peace-offerings a fire-offering to the L-rd: the fat that covers the innards and all the fat that is on the innards [According to R. Yishmael, to include the fat on the stomach; according to R. Akiva, to include the fat on the small intestines].


###### Leviticus 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:4)


וְאֵת֙ שְׁתֵּ֣י הַכְּלָיֹ֔ת וְאֶת־הַחֵ֙לֶב֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עֲלֵהֶ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַכְּסָלִ֑ים וְאֶת־הַיֹּתֶ֙רֶת֙ עַל־הַכָּבֵ֔ד עַל־הַכְּלָי֖וֹת יְסִירֶֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver, he shall remove together with the kidneys.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the two kidneys, and the fat that is on them, which is by the flanks, and the appendage of the liver, with the kidneys, it shall he take away.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the two kidneys and the fat which is upon them, which is on the loins; and the lobe with [some of] the liver, aside from the kidneys, he shall remove it.


###### Leviticus 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:5)


וְהִקְטִ֨ירוּ אֹת֤וֹ בְנֵֽי־אַהֲרֹן֙ הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה עַל־הָ֣עֹלָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר עַל־הָעֵצִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־הָאֵ֑שׁ אִשֵּׁ֛ה רֵ֥יחַ נִיחֹ֖חַ לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The sons of Aharon shall burn it on the altar along with the burnt-offering that is on the logs which are on the fire; a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aharon’s sons shall burn it on the altar upon the burnt sacrifice, which is upon the wood that is on the fire: it is an offering made by fire of a sweet savour to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Aaron shall smoke it on the altar, aside from the burnt-offering [(the continual burnt-offering preceding every other sacrifice on the wood pile)], which is upon the wood, which is upon the fire, a fire-offering, a sweet savor to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:6)


וְאִם־מִן־הַצֹּ֧אן קׇרְבָּנ֛וֹ לְזֶ֥בַח שְׁלָמִ֖ים לַיהֹוָ֑ה זָכָר֙ א֣וֹ נְקֵבָ֔ה תָּמִ֖ים יַקְרִיבֶֽנּוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If his offering is of sheep as a peace-offering to Adonoy, he shall bring an unblemished male or female.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if his offering for a sacrifice of peace offering to the Lord be of the flock; male or female, he shall offer it without blemish.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if his offering is from the flock, for a sacrifice of peace-offerings to the L-rd, male or female, without blemish shall he offer it.


###### Leviticus 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:7)


אִם־כֶּ֥שֶׂב הֽוּא־מַקְרִ֖יב אֶת־קׇרְבָּנ֑וֹ וְהִקְרִ֥יב אֹת֖וֹ לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he is bringing a lamb as his offering, he shall bring it before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If he offer a lamb for his offering, then shall he offer it before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If a lamb he offers for his offering, he shall offer it before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:8)


וְסָמַ֤ךְ אֶת־יָדוֹ֙ עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ קׇרְבָּנ֔וֹ וְשָׁחַ֣ט אֹת֔וֹ לִפְנֵ֖י אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד וְ֠זָרְק֠וּ בְּנֵ֨י אַהֲרֹ֧ן אֶת־דָּמ֛וֹ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ סָבִֽיב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall lay his hand on the head of his offering and slaughter it at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting. The sons of Aharon, the *kohanim* will sprinkle its blood all around the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall lay his hand upon the head of his offering, and kill it before the Tent of Meeting: and Aharon’s sons shall sprinkle its blood round about upon the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall place his hand upon the head of his offering, and he shall slaughter it before the tent of meeting, and Aaron's sons shall dash its blood on the altar roundabout [see 1:5].


###### Leviticus 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:9)


וְהִקְרִ֨יב מִזֶּ֣בַח הַשְּׁלָמִים֮ אִשֶּׁ֣ה לַיהֹוָה֒ חֶלְבּוֹ֙ הָאַלְיָ֣ה תְמִימָ֔ה לְעֻמַּ֥ת הֶעָצֶ֖ה יְסִירֶ֑נָּה וְאֶת־הַחֵ֙לֶב֙ הַֽמְכַסֶּ֣ה אֶת־הַקֶּ֔רֶב וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־הַחֵ֔לֶב אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַקֶּֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring of the peace-offering as a fire-offering to Adonoy, its fat; the entire tail; he shall remove it right near the back-bone. The fat covering the innards, and all the fat on the innards.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall offer of the sacrifice of the peace offering an offering made by fire to the Lord; its fat, and the whole fat tail, it shall he take off hard by the backbone; and the fat that covers the inwards, and all the fat that is upon the inwards,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall offer of the sacrifice of the peace-offerings a fire-offering to the L-rd, its [choicest] fat — the fat-tail entire; above the kidneys, he shall remove it, and the fat that covers the innards and all the fat that is on the innards.


###### Leviticus 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:10)


וְאֵת֙ שְׁתֵּ֣י הַכְּלָיֹ֔ת וְאֶת־הַחֵ֙לֶב֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עֲלֵהֶ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַכְּסָלִ֑ים וְאֶת־הַיֹּתֶ֙רֶת֙ עַל־הַכָּבֵ֔ד עַל־הַכְּלָיֹ֖ת יְסִירֶֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver he shall remove together with the kidneys.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the two kidneys, and the fat that is upon them, which is by the flanks; and the appendage of the liver, with the kidneys, it shall he take away.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the two kidneys and the fat which is upon them, which is on the loins; and the lobe with [some of] the liver, aside from the kidneys, he shall remove it.


###### Leviticus 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:11)


וְהִקְטִיר֥וֹ הַכֹּהֵ֖ן הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חָה לֶ֥חֶם אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַיהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall burn it on the altar; it is the food of the fire-offering of Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall burn it on the altar: it is the food of the offering made by fire to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall smoke it upon the altar, the food of a fire-offering to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:12)


וְאִ֥ם עֵ֖ז קׇרְבָּנ֑וֹ וְהִקְרִיב֖וֹ לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If his offering is a goat, he shall bring it before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if his offering be a goat, then he shall offer it before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a goat is his offering, he shall offer it before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:13)


וְסָמַ֤ךְ אֶת־יָדוֹ֙ עַל־רֹאשׁ֔וֹ וְשָׁחַ֣ט אֹת֔וֹ לִפְנֵ֖י אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד וְ֠זָרְק֠וּ בְּנֵ֨י אַהֲרֹ֧ן אֶת־דָּמ֛וֹ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ סָבִֽיב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall lay his hand on its head and slaughter it in front of the Tent of Meeting. The sons of Aharon will sprinkle its blood all around the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall lay his hand upon its head, and kill it before the Tent of Meeting: and the sons of Aharon shall sprinkle its blood upon the altar round about.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall place his hand upon its head and he shall slaughter it before the tent of meeting, and Aaron's sons shall dash its blood on the altar roundabout.

וְהִקְרִ֤יב מִמֶּ֙נּוּ֙ קׇרְבָּנ֔וֹ אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה אֶת־הַחֵ֙לֶב֙ הַֽמְכַסֶּ֣ה אֶת־הַקֶּ֔רֶב וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־הַחֵ֔לֶב אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַקֶּֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring from his offering as a fire-offering to Adonoy, the fat covering the innards, and all the fat on the innards,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall offer his offering from it, an offering made by fire to the Lord; the fat that covers the inwards, and all the fat that is upon the inwards,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall present from it his offering, a fire-offering to the L-rd, the fat that covers the innards and all the fat upon the innards.


###### Leviticus 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:15)


וְאֵת֙ שְׁתֵּ֣י הַכְּלָיֹ֔ת וְאֶת־הַחֵ֙לֶב֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עֲלֵהֶ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַכְּסָלִ֑ים וְאֶת־הַיֹּתֶ֙רֶת֙ עַל־הַכָּבֵ֔ד עַל־הַכְּלָיֹ֖ת יְסִירֶֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
the two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver he shall remove together with the kidneys.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the two kidneys, and the fat that is upon them, which is by the flanks, and the appendage of the liver, with the kidneys, it shall he take away.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the two kidneys and the fat which is upon them, which is on the loins, and the lobe with [some of] the liver, aside from the kidneys, he shall remove it.


###### Leviticus 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:16)


וְהִקְטִירָ֥ם הַכֹּהֵ֖ן הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חָה לֶ֤חֶם אִשֶּׁה֙ לְרֵ֣יחַ נִיחֹ֔חַ כׇּל־חֵ֖לֶב לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall burn them on the altar, the food of the fire-offering for a pleasing fragrance; all fat [belongs] to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall burn them upon the altar: it is the food of the offering made by fire for a sweet savour: all the fat is the Lord’s.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall smoke them upon the altar, the food of a fire-offering for a sweet savor, all the fat for the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/3:17)


חֻקַּ֤ת עוֹלָם֙ לְדֹרֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם בְּכֹ֖ל מוֹשְׁבֹֽתֵיכֶ֑ם כׇּל־חֵ֥לֶב וְכׇל־דָּ֖ם לֹ֥א תֹאכֵֽלוּ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
As an everlasting statute for all your descendants in all of your dwelling places; you may not eat any fat, nor any blood.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It shall be a perpetual statute for your generations throughout all your dwellings, that you eat neither fat nor blood.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A perpetual statute for your generations in all your dwellings; all fat and all blood you shall not eat.



## Chapter 4

###### Leviticus 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:2)


דַּבֵּ֞ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵל֮ לֵאמֹר֒ נֶ֗פֶשׁ כִּֽי־תֶחֱטָ֤א בִשְׁגָגָה֙ מִכֹּל֙ מִצְוֺ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר לֹ֣א תֵעָשֶׂ֑ינָה וְעָשָׂ֕ה מֵאַחַ֖ת מֵהֵֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying; if a person unwittingly transgresses any commandment of Adonoy which should not be done and commits one of these sins.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el saying, If a soul shall sin through ignorance against any of the commandments of the Lord concerning things which ought not to be done, and shall do against any of them:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel, saying: A soul, if he sin unwittingly (by transgression) of all the mitzvoth of the L-rd [(of those negative commandments punishable by kareth ("cutting off") for willful transgression, and subject to a sin-offering for unwitting transgression)] which may not be done, and he do of [i.e., (even) part of] one of them [e.g., writing part of a name on Shabbath, like "Shem" from "Shemuel" or "Dan" from "Daniel"] —


###### Leviticus 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:3)


אִ֣ם הַכֹּהֵ֧ן הַמָּשִׁ֛יחַ יֶחֱטָ֖א לְאַשְׁמַ֣ת הָעָ֑ם וְהִקְרִ֡יב עַ֣ל חַטָּאתוֹ֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר חָטָ֜א פַּ֣ר בֶּן־בָּקָ֥ר תָּמִ֛ים לַיהֹוָ֖ה לְחַטָּֽאת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the anointed *kohein* should sin to bring guilt on the people, he shall bring for his sin which he committed a young, unblemished bullock to Adonoy, as a sin-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if the priest that is anointed do sin to bring guiltiness on the people; then let him bring for his sin, which he has sinned, a young bullock without blemish to the Lord for a sin offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If the anointed Cohein shall sin to the guilt of the people [(If the high-priest sins, it is "to the guilt of the people," for they depend upon him to atone for them and to pray for them)], then he shall offer for his sin which he has sinned a young bullock [three years old], unblemished, to the L-rd, as a sin-offering.


###### Leviticus 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:4)


וְהֵבִ֣יא אֶת־הַפָּ֗ר אֶל־פֶּ֛תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵ֖ד לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְסָמַ֤ךְ אֶת־יָדוֹ֙ עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ הַפָּ֔ר וְשָׁחַ֥ט אֶת־הַפָּ֖ר לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring the bullock to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting before Adonoy. He shall lay his hand on the head of the bullock and slaughter the bullock before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall bring the bullock to the door of the Tent of Meeting before the Lord; and shall lay his hand upon the bullock’s head, and kill the bullock before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall bring the bullock to the door of the tent of meeting before the L-rd, and he shall place his hand on the head of the bullock, and he shall slaughter the bullock before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:5)


וְלָקַ֛ח הַכֹּהֵ֥ן הַמָּשִׁ֖יחַ מִדַּ֣ם הַפָּ֑ר וְהֵבִ֥יא אֹת֖וֹ אֶל־אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The anointed *kohein* shall take some of the bullocks blood and bring it inside the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest that is anointed shall take of the bullock’s blood, and bring it to the Tent of Meeting:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the anointed Cohein shall take from the blood of the bullock and bring it to the tent of meeting [to the mishkan (and, in the Temple, to the heichal)].


###### Leviticus 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:6)


וְטָבַ֧ל הַכֹּהֵ֛ן אֶת־אֶצְבָּע֖וֹ בַּדָּ֑ם וְהִזָּ֨ה מִן־הַדָּ֜ם שֶׁ֤בַע פְּעָמִים֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶת־פְּנֵ֖י פָּרֹ֥כֶת הַקֹּֽדֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall dip his finger into the blood and he shall sprinkle from the blood seven times, before Adonoy, in front of the Holy Curtain.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall dip his finger in the blood, and sprinkle of the blood seven times before the Lord, before the veil of the sanctuary.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall dip his finger into the blood, and he shall sprinkle from the blood seven times before the L-rd, before the parocheth of the holiness [i.e., in alignment between the staves of the ark. Though the high-priest has sinned, its holiness still inheres in it (see 17)].


###### Leviticus 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:7)


וְנָתַן֩ הַכֹּהֵ֨ן מִן־הַדָּ֜ם עַל־קַ֠רְנ֠וֹת מִזְבַּ֨ח קְטֹ֤רֶת הַסַּמִּים֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּאֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד וְאֵ֣ת**׀** כׇּל־דַּ֣ם הַפָּ֗ר יִשְׁפֹּךְ֙ אֶל־יְסוֹד֙ מִזְבַּ֣ח הָעֹלָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall put from the blood atop the corners of the spice-burning altar before Adonoy, which is inside the Tent of Meeting. He shall spill all the [remaining] bullocks blood into the base of the altar of the burnt-offering, which is at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall put some of the blood upon the horns of the altar of sweet incense before the Lord, which is in the Tent of Meeting: and shall pour all the blood of the bullock at the bottom of the altar of the burnt offering, which is at the door of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall put of the blood on the horns of the altar of the smoking of the incense before the L-rd, which is in the tent of meeting; and all the [remaining] blood of the bullock he shall pour at the base of the altar of the burnt-offering, which is at the door of the tent of meeting.


###### Leviticus 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:8)


וְאֶת־כׇּל־חֵ֛לֶב פַּ֥ר הַֽחַטָּ֖את יָרִ֣ים מִמֶּ֑נּוּ אֶת־הַחֵ֙לֶב֙ הַֽמְכַסֶּ֣ה עַל־הַקֶּ֔רֶב וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־הַחֵ֔לֶב אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַקֶּֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All the fat of the sin-offering bullock he shall separate from it; the fat covering the innards, all the fat on the innards,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall take off from it all the fat of the bullock for the sin offering; the fat that covers the inwards, and all the fat that is upon the inwards,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the fat of the bullock [(The Yom Kippur bullock is hereby included)] of the sin-offering [(This includes the "idolatry goats" as subject to the burning of kidneys, fats, and lobe)] he shall take from it [(before dismembering it)]: the fat which covers the innards and all the fat which is on the innards.


###### Leviticus 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:9)


וְאֵת֙ שְׁתֵּ֣י הַכְּלָיֹ֔ת וְאֶת־הַחֵ֙לֶב֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עֲלֵיהֶ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַכְּסָלִ֑ים וְאֶת־הַיֹּתֶ֙רֶת֙ עַל־הַכָּבֵ֔ד עַל־הַכְּלָי֖וֹת יְסִירֶֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
the two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver, he shall remove together with the kidneys,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the two kidneys, and the fat that is upon them, which is by the flanks, and the appendage of the liver, with the kidneys, it shall he take away,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the two kidneys and the fat that is upon them, which is on the loins, and the lobe with [some of] the liver, aside from the kidneys — he shall remove it.


###### Leviticus 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:10)


כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר יוּרַ֔ם מִשּׁ֖וֹר זֶ֣בַח הַשְּׁלָמִ֑ים וְהִקְטִירָם֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן עַ֖ל מִזְבַּ֥ח הָעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
in the same manner as they were separated from the ox of the peace-offering. The *kohein* shall burn them on the altar of the burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
as it was taken off from the bullock of the peace offering: and the priest shall burn them upon the altar of the burnt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As it is removed from the ox of the sacrifice of the peace-offerings [(Just as peace-offerings must be intended as such (lishmah), so this requires lishmah; and just as peace-offerings bring peace to the world, so does this)], and the Cohein shall smoke them on the altar of the burnt-offering.


###### Leviticus 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:11)


וְאֶת־ע֤וֹר הַפָּר֙ וְאֶת־כׇּל־בְּשָׂר֔וֹ עַל־רֹאשׁ֖וֹ וְעַל־כְּרָעָ֑יו וְקִרְבּ֖וֹ וּפִרְשֽׁוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The bullocks skin, all its flesh with its head and feet, its innards and its waste [that is in its intestines].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the skin of the bullock, and all its flesh, with its head, and with its legs, and its inwards, and its dung,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the skin of the bullock and all its flesh, with its head and with its legs, and its innards and its dung.


###### Leviticus 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:12)


וְהוֹצִ֣יא אֶת־כׇּל־הַ֠פָּ֠ר אֶל־מִח֨וּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶ֜ה אֶל־מָק֤וֹם טָהוֹר֙ אֶל־שֶׁ֣פֶךְ הַדֶּ֔שֶׁן וְשָׂרַ֥ף אֹת֛וֹ עַל־עֵצִ֖ים בָּאֵ֑שׁ עַל־שֶׁ֥פֶךְ הַדֶּ֖שֶׁן יִשָּׂרֵֽף׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall take out the entire bullock beyond the encampment, to a pure [undefiled] place, where the ashes are thrown and burn it on logs, in a fire. It shall be burned where the ashes are thrown.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
even the whole bullock shall he carry forth outside the camp to a clean place, where the ashes are poured out, and burn it on the wood with fire: where the ashes are poured out shall it be burnt.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall take the entire bullock outside the camp [outside the three camps (and, in the Temple, outside the city)] to a clean place, where the ashes [from the altar] are poured out; and he shall burn it on wood with fire. Where the ashes are poured out [(even if there are no ashes there)] shall it be burnt.


###### Leviticus 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:13)


וְאִ֨ם כׇּל־עֲדַ֤ת יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ יִשְׁגּ֔וּ וְנֶעְלַ֣ם דָּבָ֔ר מֵעֵינֵ֖י הַקָּהָ֑ל וְ֠עָשׂ֠וּ אַחַ֨ת מִכׇּל־מִצְוֺ֧ת יְהֹוָ֛ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־תֵעָשֶׂ֖ינָה וְאָשֵֽׁמוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the entire congregation of Yisroel erred and the matter [that error] was concealed from the eyes of the community, and they did [transgress] one of the commandments of Adonoy that should not be done, they incur guilt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the whole congregation of Yisra᾽el sin through ignorance, and the thing be hid from the eyes of the assembly, and they have done something against any of the commandments of the Lord concerning things which should not be done, and have incurred guilt;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the whole congregation of Israel [the Sanhedrin] err, and a thing be hid from the eyes of the assembly [(If they erroneously rule something punishable by kareth to be permitted)], and they [the people, on the strength of their ruling] do one of all the mitzvoth of the L-rd which are not to be done, and they are guilty —


###### Leviticus 4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:14)


וְנֽוֹדְעָה֙ הַֽחַטָּ֔את אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָטְא֖וּ עָלֶ֑יהָ וְהִקְרִ֨יבוּ הַקָּהָ֜ל פַּ֤ר בֶּן־בָּקָר֙ לְחַטָּ֔את וְהֵבִ֣יאוּ אֹת֔וֹ לִפְנֵ֖י אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[When] they realize the sin which they committed, the community shall bring a young bullock as a sin-offering and they shall bring it to the front of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when the sin, which they have sinned, is known, then the congregation shall offer a young bullock for the sin, and bring it before the Tent of Meeting.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If the sin become known wherein they have sinned, then the assembly shall offer a young bullock for a sin-offering, and they shall bring it before the tent of meeting.


###### Leviticus 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:15)


וְ֠סָמְכ֠וּ זִקְנֵ֨י הָעֵדָ֧ה אֶת־יְדֵיהֶ֛ם עַל־רֹ֥אשׁ הַפָּ֖ר לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְשָׁחַ֥ט אֶת־הַפָּ֖ר לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The elders of the congregation shall lay their hands on the head of the bullock, before Adonoy and he shall slaughter the bullock before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the elders of the congregation shall lay their hands on the head of the bullock before the Lord: and the bullock shall be killed before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the elders of the congregation shall place their hands upon the head of the bullock before the L-rd, and he shall slaughter the bullock before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:16)


וְהֵבִ֛יא הַכֹּהֵ֥ן הַמָּשִׁ֖יחַ מִדַּ֣ם הַפָּ֑ר אֶל־אֹ֖הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The anointed *kohein* shall bring some of the bullocks blood inside the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest that is anointed shall bring of the bullock’s blood to the Tent of Meeting:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the anointed Cohein shall bring of the blood of the bullock to the tent of meeting.


###### Leviticus 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:17)


וְטָבַ֧ל הַכֹּהֵ֛ן אֶצְבָּע֖וֹ מִן־הַדָּ֑ם וְהִזָּ֞ה שֶׁ֤בַע פְּעָמִים֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה אֵ֖ת פְּנֵ֥י הַפָּרֹֽכֶת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall dip his finger into some of the blood and sprinkle [it] seven times before Adonoy, in front of the curtain.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall dip his finger in some of the blood, and sprinkle it seven times before the Lord, before the veil.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall dip his finger from the blood, and he shall sprinkle seven times before the L-rd before the parocheth [(but not, as in (6) "the parocheth of the holiness") Once all have sinned, the holiness departs.)]


###### Leviticus 4:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:18)


וּמִן־הַדָּ֞ם יִתֵּ֣ן**׀** עַל־קַרְנֹ֣ת הַמִּזְבֵּ֗חַ אֲשֶׁר֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּאֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד וְאֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַדָּ֗ם יִשְׁפֹּךְ֙ אֶל־יְסוֹד֙ מִזְבַּ֣ח הָעֹלָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall put some of the blood atop the corners of the altar which is before Adonoy, inside the Tent of Meeting. He shall spill all the [remaining] blood into the base of the altar of the burnt-offering, which is at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall put some of the blood upon the horns of the altar which is before the Lord, that is in the Tent of Meeting, and shall pour out all the blood at the bottom of the altar of the burnt offering, which is at the door of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of the blood shall he put upon the horns of the altar which is before the L-rd, which is in the tent of meeting; and all of the [remaining] blood shall he pour at the [western] base of the altar of the burnt-offering, which is at the door of the tent of meeting.


###### Leviticus 4:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:19)


וְאֵ֥ת כׇּל־חֶלְבּ֖וֹ יָרִ֣ים מִמֶּ֑נּוּ וְהִקְטִ֖יר הַמִּזְבֵּֽחָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall remove all its fat from it and burn it on the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall lift off all its fat, and burn it on the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of its fat shall he take off from it, and he shall smoke it upon the altar.


###### Leviticus 4:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:20)


וְעָשָׂ֣ה לַפָּ֔ר כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר עָשָׂה֙ לְפַ֣ר הַֽחַטָּ֔את כֵּ֖ן יַעֲשֶׂה־לּ֑וֹ וְכִפֶּ֧ר עֲלֵהֶ֛ם הַכֹּהֵ֖ן וְנִסְלַ֥ח לָהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall do to the bullock [the same] as he did to the sin-offering bullock, so he shall do to it. The *kohein* will atone for them and they will be pardoned.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall do with the bullock as he did with the bullock for a sin offering, so shall he do with this: and the priest shall make atonement for them, and it shall be forgiven them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall do with this bullock as he did with the bullock of the sin-offering [of the anointed Cohein]; so shall he do with this. And the Cohein shall make atonement for them and it shall be forgiven them.


###### Leviticus 4:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:21)


וְהוֹצִ֣יא אֶת־הַפָּ֗ר אֶל־מִחוּץ֙ לַֽמַּחֲנֶ֔ה וְשָׂרַ֣ף אֹת֔וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׂרַ֔ף אֵ֖ת הַפָּ֣ר הָרִאשׁ֑וֹן חַטַּ֥את הַקָּהָ֖ל הֽוּא׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall take the bullock outside, beyond the encampment, and burn it in the same manner as he burned the first bullock; it is a communal sin-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall carry the bullock outside the camp, and burn it as he burned the first bullock: it is a sin offering for the congregation.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall take the bullock outside the camp, and he shall burn it as he burned the first bullock; it is the sin-offering for the assembly.


###### Leviticus 4:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:22)


אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָשִׂ֖יא יֶֽחֱטָ֑א וְעָשָׂ֡ה אַחַ֣ת מִכׇּל־מִצְוֺת֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהָ֜יו אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹא־תֵעָשֶׂ֛ינָה בִּשְׁגָגָ֖ה וְאָשֵֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a leader sins, [who] transgresses one of the commandments of Adonoy, his God, that should not be done, [if he transgressed] unwittingly and incurs guilt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When a ruler has sinned, and done something through ignorance against any one of the commandments of the Lord his God concerning things which should not be done, and has incurred guilt;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If (asher) a leader sin [("asher," similar to "ashrei" (fortunate). Fortunate is the generation whose leader takes it to heart to make atonement for his unwitting sin. (It goes without saying that he repents of his willful sins)], and he do one of all the mitzvoth of the L-rd his G-d, which are not to be done, unwittingly, and he be guilty —


###### Leviticus 4:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:23)


אֽוֹ־הוֹדַ֤ע אֵלָיו֙ חַטָּאת֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָטָ֖א בָּ֑הּ וְהֵבִ֧יא אֶת־קׇרְבָּנ֛וֹ שְׂעִ֥יר עִזִּ֖ים זָכָ֥ר תָּמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or, [if his sin] becomes known to him—[that] which he committed. He shall bring as his offering an unblemished, male goat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or if his sin, wherein he has sinned, come to his knowledge; he shall bring his offering, a kid of the goats, a male without blemish:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If his sin becomes known to him wherein he has sinned, then he shall bring as his offering a kid of goats, a male without blemish.


###### Leviticus 4:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:24)


וְסָמַ֤ךְ יָדוֹ֙ עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ הַשָּׂעִ֔יר וְשָׁחַ֣ט אֹת֗וֹ בִּמְק֛וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִשְׁחַ֥ט אֶת־הָעֹלָ֖ה לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה חַטָּ֖את הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall lay his hand on the goats head and slaughter it in the place where the burnt-offering is slaughtered, before Adonoy; it is a sin-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall lay his hand upon the head of the goat, and kill it in the place where they kill the burnt offering before the Lord: it is a sin offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall place his hand on the head of the goat, and he shall slaughter it in the place where the burnt-offering is slaughtered before the L-rd [i.e., northwards (1:11)]. It is a sin-offering [To be kasher, it must be offered as such (lishmah)]


###### Leviticus 4:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:25)


וְלָקַ֨ח הַכֹּהֵ֜ן מִדַּ֤ם הַֽחַטָּאת֙ בְּאֶצְבָּע֔וֹ וְנָתַ֕ן עַל־קַרְנֹ֖ת מִזְבַּ֣ח הָעֹלָ֑ה וְאֶת־דָּמ֣וֹ יִשְׁפֹּ֔ךְ אֶל־יְס֖וֹד מִזְבַּ֥ח הָעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall take some blood of the sin-offering with his finger and put it atop the corners of the altar of the burnt-offering. He shall spill its [remaining] blood into the base of the altar of the burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall take of the blood of the sin offering with his finger, and put it upon the horns of the altar of burnt offering, and shall pour out its blood at the bottom of the altar of burnt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall take of the blood of the sin-offering with his finger and he shall place it on the horns of the altar of the burnt-offering, and its [remaining] blood he shall pour at the base of the altar of the burnt-offering.


###### Leviticus 4:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:26)


וְאֶת־כׇּל־חֶלְבּוֹ֙ יַקְטִ֣יר הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה כְּחֵ֖לֶב זֶ֣בַח הַשְּׁלָמִ֑ים וְכִפֶּ֨ר עָלָ֧יו הַכֹּהֵ֛ן מֵחַטָּאת֖וֹ וְנִסְלַ֥ח לֽוֹ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall burn all its fat on the altar as [he burned] the fat of the peace-offering. The *kohein* will atone for him, for his transgression and he will be pardoned.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall burn all its fat upon the altar, as the fat of the sacrifice of the peace offering: and the priest shall make atonement for him as concerning his sin, and it shall be forgiven him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of its fat shall he smoke upon the altar, as the fat of the sacrifice of the peace-offerings [of a goat]; and the Cohein shall make atonement for him for his sin, and it shall be forgiven him.


###### Leviticus 4:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:27)


וְאִם־נֶ֧פֶשׁ אַחַ֛ת תֶּחֱטָ֥א בִשְׁגָגָ֖ה מֵעַ֣ם הָאָ֑רֶץ בַּ֠עֲשֹׂתָ֠הּ אַחַ֨ת מִמִּצְוֺ֧ת יְהֹוָ֛ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־תֵעָשֶׂ֖ינָה וְאָשֵֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a person should unwittingly sin [and he is] one of the ordinary people, by transgressing one of the commandments of Adonoy that should not be done and he incurs guilt,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if anyone of the common people sin through ignorance, by doing something against any one of the commandments of the Lord concerning things which ought not to be done, and be guilty;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a single soul sin unwittingly, of the people of the land, in doing it, one of the mitzvoth of the L-rd which are not to be done, and he be guilty —


###### Leviticus 4:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:28)


א֚וֹ הוֹדַ֣ע אֵלָ֔יו חַטָּאת֖וֹ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָטָ֑א וְהֵבִ֨יא קׇרְבָּנ֜וֹ שְׂעִירַ֤ת עִזִּים֙ תְּמִימָ֣ה נְקֵבָ֔ה עַל־חַטָּאת֖וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָטָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
or, if his sin becomes known to him—which he committed. He shall bring [as] his offering an unblemished she-goat for his sin which he committed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or if his sin, which he has sinned, come to his knowledge: then he shall bring his offering, a kid of the goats, a female without blemish, for his sin which he has sinned.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If his sin becomes known to him wherein he sinned, then he shall bring as his offering a kid of goats, a female without blemish, for his sin which he has sinned.


###### Leviticus 4:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:29)


וְסָמַךְ֙ אֶת־יָד֔וֹ עַ֖ל רֹ֣אשׁ הַֽחַטָּ֑את וְשָׁחַט֙ אֶת־הַ֣חַטָּ֔את בִּמְק֖וֹם הָעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall lay his hand on the head of the sin-offering and slaughter the sin-offering in the [same] place as the burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall lay his hand upon the head of the sin offering, and slay the sin offering in the place of the burnt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall place his hand on the head of the sin-offering, and he shall slaughter the sin-offering in the place of the burnt-offering.


###### Leviticus 4:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:30)


וְלָקַ֨ח הַכֹּהֵ֤ן מִדָּמָהּ֙ בְּאֶצְבָּע֔וֹ וְנָתַ֕ן עַל־קַרְנֹ֖ת מִזְבַּ֣ח הָעֹלָ֑ה וְאֶת־כׇּל־דָּמָ֣הּ יִשְׁפֹּ֔ךְ אֶל־יְס֖וֹד הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall take from its blood with his finger and put it atop the corners of the altar of the burnt-offering. He shall spill all the [remaining] blood into the base of the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall take of its blood with his finger, and put it upon the horns of the altar of burnt offering, and shall pour out all its blood at the bottom of the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall take of its blood with his finger and he shall place it on the horns of the altar of the burnt-offering, and all of its [remaining] blood he shall pour at the base of the altar.


###### Leviticus 4:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:31)


וְאֶת־כׇּל־חֶלְבָּ֣הּ יָסִ֗יר כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר הוּסַ֣ר חֵ֘לֶב֮ מֵעַ֣ל זֶ֣בַח הַשְּׁלָמִים֒ וְהִקְטִ֤יר הַכֹּהֵן֙ הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה לְרֵ֥יחַ נִיחֹ֖חַ לַיהֹוָ֑ה וְכִפֶּ֥ר עָלָ֛יו הַכֹּהֵ֖ן וְנִסְלַ֥ח לֽוֹ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall remove all its fat in the same manner as was removed the fat from the peace-offering. The *kohein* shall burn it on the altar as a pleasing fragrance to Adonoy. And the *kohein* will atone for him and he will be pardoned.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall take away all its fat, as the fat is taken away from off the sacrifice of the peace offering; and the priest shall burn it upon the altar for a sweet savour to the Lord; and the priest shall make atonement for him, and it shall be forgiven him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all its fat shall he remove, as the fat was removed from the sacrifice of the peace-offerings [of a goat]. And the Cohein shall smoke it upon the altar as a sweet savor to the L-rd; and the Cohein shall make atonement for him, and it shall be forgiven him.


###### Leviticus 4:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:32)


וְאִם־כֶּ֛בֶשׂ יָבִ֥יא קׇרְבָּנ֖וֹ לְחַטָּ֑את נְקֵבָ֥ה תְמִימָ֖ה יְבִיאֶֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he brings a sheep as his offering as a sin-offering, he shall bring an unblemished female.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if he bring a lamb for a sin offering, he shall bring it a female without blemish.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a lamb he brings as his offering for a sin-offering, a female without blemish shall he bring it.


###### Leviticus 4:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:33)


וְסָמַךְ֙ אֶת־יָד֔וֹ עַ֖ל רֹ֣אשׁ הַֽחַטָּ֑את וְשָׁחַ֤ט אֹתָהּ֙ לְחַטָּ֔את בִּמְק֕וֹם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִשְׁחַ֖ט אֶת־הָעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall lay his hand on the head of the sin-offering and slaughter it as a sin-offering, in the [same] place where he slaughters the burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall lay his hand upon the head of the sin offering, and slay it for a sin offering in the place where they kill the burnt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall place his hand upon the head of the sin-offering, and he shall slaughter it [specifically] as a sin-offering in the place where the burnt-offering is slaughtered.

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall take some blood from the sin-offering with his finger and put it atop the corners of the altar of the burnt-offering. He shall spill all its [remaining] blood into the base of the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall take of the blood of the sin offering with his finger, and put it upon the horns of the altar of burnt offering, and shall pour out all its blood at the bottom of the altar:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall take from the blood of the sin-offering with his finger and put it on the horns of the altar of the burnt-offering; and he shall pour out all its blood at the base of the altar.


###### Leviticus 4:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/4:35)


וְאֶת־כׇּל־חֶלְבָּ֣הּ יָסִ֗יר כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר יוּסַ֥ר חֵֽלֶב־הַכֶּ֘שֶׂב֮ מִזֶּ֣בַח הַשְּׁלָמִים֒ וְהִקְטִ֨יר הַכֹּהֵ֤ן אֹתָם֙ הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה עַ֖ל אִשֵּׁ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְכִפֶּ֨ר עָלָ֧יו הַכֹּהֵ֛ן עַל־חַטָּאת֥וֹ אֲשֶׁר־חָטָ֖א וְנִסְלַ֥ח לֽוֹ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall remove all its fat in the same manner as was removed the fat of the lamb of the peace-offering. The *kohein* shall burn them on the altar on the fires of Adonoy. The *kohein* will atone for him, for his sin which he committed and he will be pardoned.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall take away all its fat, as the fat of the lamb is taken away from the sacrifice of the peace offering; and the priest shall burn them upon the altar, upon the pyres of the Lord: and the priest shall make atonement for his sin that he has committed, and it shall be forgiven him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
