And all its fat he shall remove, as the fat of the lamb is removed from the sacrifice of the peace-offerings [i.e., with the addition of the fat-tail]. And the Cohein shall smoke them upon the altar, upon the [fire made for the] fire-offerings of the L-rd. And the Cohein shall make atonement for him for the sin which he has sinned, and it shall be forgiven him.



## Chapter 5

###### Leviticus 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:1)


וְנֶ֣פֶשׁ כִּֽי־תֶחֱטָ֗א וְשָֽׁמְעָה֙ ק֣וֹל אָלָ֔ה וְה֣וּא עֵ֔ד א֥וֹ רָאָ֖ה א֣וֹ יָדָ֑ע אִם־ל֥וֹא יַגִּ֖יד וְנָשָׂ֥א עֲוֺנֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a person sins, and heard an oath [demanding his testimony] [in a case where] he is a witness; [for] he either saw or knew [something relevant], if he does not testify, he will bear [the burden of] his iniquity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a person sin, and hear the voice of adjuration, and is a witness, whether he has seen or known of it; if he do not utter it, then he shall bear his iniquity.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a soul sin and hear the voice of an oath [i.e., if he were besworn to give testimony if he knows it], and he were a witness, having seen or known — if he does not tell, then he shall bear his sin.


###### Leviticus 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:2)


א֣וֹ נֶ֗פֶשׁ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּגַּע֮ בְּכׇל־דָּבָ֣ר טָמֵא֒ אוֹ֩ בְנִבְלַ֨ת חַיָּ֜ה טְמֵאָ֗ה א֚וֹ בְּנִבְלַת֙ בְּהֵמָ֣ה טְמֵאָ֔ה א֕וֹ בְּנִבְלַ֖ת שֶׁ֣רֶץ טָמֵ֑א וְנֶעְלַ֣ם מִמֶּ֔נּוּ וְה֥וּא טָמֵ֖א וְאָשֵֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or, if a person touches anything impure either the carcass of an impure wild animal, or the carcass of an impure animal of pasture, or the carcass of an impure creeping animal; and this [impurity] was concealed from him, he is impure and incurs guilt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Or if a person touch any unclean thing, whether it be a carcass of an unclean beast, or a carcass of unclean cattle, or the carcass of unclean creeping things, and if it be hidden from him, so that he be unclean, and guilty:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or if a soul touch any unclean thing [and, after becoming unclean, eat consecrated food or enter the sanctuary, willful violation being punishable by kareth] — whether it (the unclean thing) be the carcass of an unclean animal, or the carcass of an unclean beast, or the carcass of an unclean creeping thing — and it [the uncleanliness] were hidden from him, and he were unclean and he be guilty [in eating consecrated food or in entering the sanctuary] —


###### Leviticus 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:3)


א֣וֹ כִ֤י יִגַּע֙ בְּטֻמְאַ֣ת אָדָ֔ם לְכֹל֙ טֻמְאָת֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִטְמָ֖א בָּ֑הּ וְנֶעְלַ֣ם מִמֶּ֔נּוּ וְה֥וּא יָדַ֖ע וְאָשֵֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or, if he touches the impurities of man whatever that impurity may be that causes impurity and this [impurity] was concealed from him; [when] he realizes [he is impure], he incurs guilt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or if he touch the uncleanness of man, whatever uncleanness it be with which a man shall be defiled, and it be hid from him; and he come to know of it, and be guilty:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or if he touch the uncleanliness of man [(The allusion is to dead body uncleanliness)], for all his uncleanliness [including touching zavin or zavoth (men or women affected by a genital flux)] where he becomes unclean [(including touching one who cohabited with a niddah)] through it [(including swallowing the carcass of a (ritually) clean fowl)], and it were hidden from him, [i.e., he forgot that he had become unclean], and he (then) knew, and he be guilty [by eating consecrated food or entering the sanctuary],


###### Leviticus 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:4)


א֣וֹ נֶ֡פֶשׁ כִּ֣י תִשָּׁבַע֩ לְבַטֵּ֨א בִשְׂפָתַ֜יִם לְהָרַ֣ע**׀** א֣וֹ לְהֵיטִ֗יב לְ֠כֹ֠ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְבַטֵּ֧א הָאָדָ֛ם בִּשְׁבֻעָ֖ה וְנֶעְלַ֣ם מִמֶּ֑נּוּ וְהוּא־יָדַ֥ע וְאָשֵׁ֖ם לְאַחַ֥ת מֵאֵֽלֶּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or, if a person swears, orally expressing to do harm [that he will do something which is to his detriment] or to do good [that he will do something that is to his benefit]. Regarding all a person will expresses in an oath and [that oath] was concealed from himself, when he realizes [his sin] and he incurs guilt, for any one of these [aforementioned sins].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or if a person swear, pronouncing with his lips to do evil, or to do good, whatever it be that a man shall pronounce with an oath, and it be hid from him; when he knows of it, then he shall be guilty in one of these.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or if a soul swore, pronouncing with his lips, [but not in his heart alone] to do ill or to do good [to himself], for all that a man will pronounce with an oath, [including an oath concerning the past], and it were hidden from him, [and he transgressed his oath], and he (then) knew, and he be guilty in one of these — [then he brings an oleh veyored ("sliding scale") offering (but for violation of an oath in denial of a money claim, the offering is a guilt-offering.)]


###### Leviticus 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:5)


וְהָיָ֥ה כִֽי־יֶאְשַׁ֖ם לְאַחַ֣ת מֵאֵ֑לֶּה וְהִ֨תְוַדָּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָטָ֖א עָלֶֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It shall be that when he incurs guilt for any of these [aforementioned sins] he shall confess that which he transgressed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, when he shall be guilty in one of these things, that he shall confess that he has sinned in that thing:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then it shall be, if he be guilty for one of these, then he shall confess wherein he has sinned.


###### Leviticus 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:6)


וְהֵבִ֣יא אֶת־אֲשָׁמ֣וֹ לַיהֹוָ֡ה עַ֣ל חַטָּאתוֹ֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר חָטָ֜א נְקֵבָ֨ה מִן־הַצֹּ֥אן כִּשְׂבָּ֛ה אֽוֹ־שְׂעִירַ֥ת עִזִּ֖ים לְחַטָּ֑את וְכִפֶּ֥ר עָלָ֛יו הַכֹּהֵ֖ן מֵחַטָּאתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring his guilt-offering to Adonoy for his sin that he committed a female of the sheep, a lamb or a she-goat as a sin-offering. The *kohein* will atone for him, for his sin.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall bring his guilt offering to the Lord for his sin which he has sinned, a female from the flock, a lamb or a kid of the goats, for a sin offering; and the priest shall make atonement for him concerning his sin.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall bring (the offering for) his guilt to the L-rd, for his sin which he has sinned, a female from the flock, a lamb or a goat-kid, for a sin-offering; and the Cohein shall make atonement for him for his sin.


###### Leviticus 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:7)


וְאִם־לֹ֨א תַגִּ֣יעַ יָדוֹ֮ דֵּ֣י שֶׂה֒ וְהֵבִ֨יא אֶת־אֲשָׁמ֜וֹ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָטָ֗א שְׁתֵּ֥י תֹרִ֛ים אֽוֹ־שְׁנֵ֥י בְנֵֽי־יוֹנָ֖ה לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה אֶחָ֥ד לְחַטָּ֖את וְאֶחָ֥ד לְעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If his means are not sufficient for [the acquisition of] a sheep, then he shall bring for his guilt-offering,—[he] who has sinned,—two turtledoves or two young pigeons to Adonoy one for a sin-offering and one for a burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if he be not able to bring a lamb, then he shall bring for his trespass, which he has committed, two turtledoves, or two young pigeons, to the Lord; one for a sin offering, and the other for a burnt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if his hand cannot attain enough for a lamb, then he shall bring (the offering of) his guilt wherein he has sinned, two turtle-doves or two young pigeons to the L-rd, one for a sin-offering and one for a burnt-offering.


###### Leviticus 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:8)


וְהֵבִ֤יא אֹתָם֙ אֶל־הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְהִקְרִ֛יב אֶת־אֲשֶׁ֥ר לַחַטָּ֖את רִאשׁוֹנָ֑ה וּמָלַ֧ק אֶת־רֹאשׁ֛וֹ מִמּ֥וּל עׇרְפּ֖וֹ וְלֹ֥א יַבְדִּֽיל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring them to the *kohein* and he shall bring that which is for a sin-offering first. He [the *kohein*] shall shear its head [with his fingernail] opposite of its neck, but not separate [the head] completely.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall bring them to the priest, who shall offer that which is for the sin offering first, and wring off its head from its neck, but shall not divide it asunder:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall bring them to the Cohein, and he shall sacrifice the one for the sin-offering first [and then the burnt-offering. (First, pardon is secured; then the "gift" is presented.)] And he shall pinch its head alongside its oref (the back of the head sloping to the nape) [i.e., He pinches the nape], but he shall not sunder it (the head). [He pinches only one of the (shechitah) signs (gullet or windpipe)].


###### Leviticus 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:9)


וְהִזָּ֞ה מִדַּ֤ם הַחַטָּאת֙ עַל־קִ֣יר הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חַ וְהַנִּשְׁאָ֣ר בַּדָּ֔ם יִמָּצֵ֖ה אֶל־יְס֣וֹד הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ חַטָּ֖את הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall sprinkle some blood of the sin-offering on to the wall of the altar. And the remaining blood shall be squeezed into the base of the altar; it is a sin-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall sprinkle of the blood of the sin offering upon the side of the altar; and the rest of the blood shall be wrung out at the bottom of the altar: it is a sin offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall sprinkle from the blood of the sin-offering on the wall of the altar [by grasping the oref and "squirting" the blood upon the altar], and what remains of the blood shall be drained out at the base of the altar. It is a sin-offering. [It must be specifically intended as such.]


###### Leviticus 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:10)


וְאֶת־הַשֵּׁנִ֛י יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה עֹלָ֖ה כַּמִּשְׁפָּ֑ט וְכִפֶּ֨ר עָלָ֧יו הַכֹּהֵ֛ן מֵחַטָּאת֥וֹ אֲשֶׁר־חָטָ֖א וְנִסְלַ֥ח לֽוֹ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall do (offer) the second [turtledove pigeon] as a burnt-offering, as prescribed. The *kohein* will atone from him, for his sin that he committed and he will be pardoned.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall offer the second for a burnt offering, according to the prescribed order: and the priest shall make atonement for him for his sin which he has sinned, and it shall be forgiven him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the second one he shall make a burnt-offering as prescribed (1:14), and the Cohein shall make atonement for him for his sin which he has sinned, and it shall be forgiven him.


###### Leviticus 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:11)


וְאִם־לֹא֩ תַשִּׂ֨יג יָד֜וֹ לִשְׁתֵּ֣י תֹרִ֗ים אוֹ֮ לִשְׁנֵ֣י בְנֵי־יוֹנָה֒ וְהֵבִ֨יא אֶת־קׇרְבָּנ֜וֹ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָטָ֗א עֲשִׂירִ֧ת הָאֵפָ֛ה סֹ֖לֶת לְחַטָּ֑את לֹא־יָשִׂ֨ים עָלֶ֜יהָ שֶׁ֗מֶן וְלֹא־יִתֵּ֤ן עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ לְבֹנָ֔ה כִּ֥י חַטָּ֖את הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If his means are not sufficient for the acquisition of two turtledoves or two young pigeons, he shall bring as his offering,—[he] who has sinned,—one tenth of an epha of fine flour as a sin-offering. He shall not put oil upon it, nor shall he place frankincense upon it, for it is a sin-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if he be not able to bring two turtledoves, or two young pigeons, then he that sinned shall bring for his offering the tenth part of an efa of fine flour for a sin offering; he shall put no oil upon it, neither shall he put any frankincense upon it: for it is a sin offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if his hand does not attain to two turtle-doves or to two young pigeons, then he shall bring his offering wherein he has sinned, a tenth of an ephah of fine flour for a sin-offering; he shall not place oil upon it and he shall not put frankincense upon it, for it is a sin-offering, [which should not be ornate].


###### Leviticus 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:12)


וֶהֱבִיאָהּ֮ אֶל־הַכֹּהֵן֒ וְקָמַ֣ץ הַכֹּהֵ֣ן׀מִ֠מֶּ֠נָּה מְל֨וֹא קֻמְצ֜וֹ אֶת־אַזְכָּרָתָהּ֙ וְהִקְטִ֣יר הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה עַ֖ל אִשֵּׁ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה חַטָּ֖את הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring it to the *kohein* and the *kohein* shall take a fistful from it [as] its memorial portion and burn it on the altar on the fires of Adonoy; it is a sin-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then shall he bring it to the priest, and the priest shall take his handful of it, the memorial part of it, and burn it on the altar, upon the pyres of the Lord: it is a sin offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall bring it to the Cohein, and the Cohein shall take a fistful from it, his entire fistful, as its remembrance; and he shall smoke it upon the altar, upon the [fire made for the] fire-offerings of the L-rd. It is a sin-offering [and requires kemitzah (taking the fistful) and smoking lishmah].


###### Leviticus 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:13)


וְכִפֶּר֩ עָלָ֨יו הַכֹּהֵ֜ן עַל־חַטָּאת֧וֹ אֲשֶׁר־חָטָ֛א מֵֽאַחַ֥ת מֵאֵ֖לֶּה וְנִסְלַ֣ח ל֑וֹ וְהָיְתָ֥ה לַכֹּהֵ֖ן כַּמִּנְחָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* will atone for him, for his sin that he committed in one of these [three] ways and he will be pardoned. [The remaining portion] shall belong to the *kohein* as a meal-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall make atonement for him in regard to his sin that he has sinned in one of these, and it shall be forgiven him: and it shall be the priest’s, like the meal offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall make atonement for him, for his sin (al chatatho) that he has sinned [If he sinned when rich and set aside money for a lamb or a goat, and then became poor, he may bring two turtle-doves from part of that money. If he set aside money for two turtle-doves and then became poorer, he may bring a tenth of an ephah from part of that money. If he set aside money for a tenth of an ephah and then became rich, (our instance) he adds to the money and brings the offering of a rich man. Hence, "al chatatho" — lit., "in addition to (what was set aside for) his sin," as opposed to "mechatatho" — lit., "from (what was set aside for) his sin," mentioned in the other instances (6 and 10)], with one of these [three atonements relative to the means of the donor], and it shall be forgiven him, and it [what is left over from the fistful] shall be to the Cohein, as [with] a meal-offering [in general].


###### Leviticus 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:14)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:15)


נֶ֚פֶשׁ כִּֽי־תִמְעֹ֣ל מַ֔עַל וְחָֽטְאָה֙ בִּשְׁגָגָ֔ה מִקׇּדְשֵׁ֖י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְהֵבִיא֩ אֶת־אֲשָׁמ֨וֹ לַֽיהֹוָ֜ה אַ֧יִל תָּמִ֣ים מִן־הַצֹּ֗אן בְּעֶרְכְּךָ֛ כֶּֽסֶף־שְׁקָלִ֥ים בְּשֶֽׁקֶל־הַקֹּ֖דֶשׁ לְאָשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a person wrongfully deviates and unwittingly sins [by using the] sacred property of Adonoy, he shall bring his guilt-offering to Adonoy, an unblemished ram, from the sheep, based on the valuation of holy silver shekalim, for a guilt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If a person commit a trespass, and sin through ignorance, in the holy things of the Lord; then he shall bring for his guilt to the Lord a ram without blemish out of the flocks, by value (two) silver shekels, after the shekel of the sanctuary, for a guilt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A soul, if he change [something from sacred to profane] and sin unwittingly [by deriving benefit] from the holy things of the L-rd [i.e., those which are dedicated to His name (as opposed to lower order sanctities)], then he shall bring his guilt-offering to the L-rd, a [two-year-old] ram, without blemish, from the flock, by your valuation, silver shekalim [i.e., It must be worth at least two selaim], according to the shekel of the sanctuary, for a guilt-offering.


###### Leviticus 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:16)


וְאֵ֣ת אֲשֶׁר֩ חָטָ֨א מִן־הַקֹּ֜דֶשׁ יְשַׁלֵּ֗ם וְאֶת־חֲמִֽישִׁתוֹ֙ יוֹסֵ֣ף עָלָ֔יו וְנָתַ֥ן אֹת֖וֹ לַכֹּהֵ֑ן וְהַכֹּהֵ֗ן יְכַפֵּ֥ר עָלָ֛יו בְּאֵ֥יל הָאָשָׁ֖ם וְנִסְלַ֥ח לֽוֹ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
That which he sinned [making use] of the sacred property, he shall pay for and add an additional fifth to that [amount] and give it to the *kohein.* The *kohein* will atone for him with the guilt-offering ram and he will be pardoned.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall make amends for the wrong that he has done in the holy thing, and shall add the fifth part to it, and give it to the priest: and the priest shall make atonement for him with the ram of the guilt offering, and it shall be forgiven him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for what he has sinned in the holy thing [(the principal)] he shall pay, and its fifth he shall add to it, and he shall give it to the Cohein; and the Cohein shall make atonement for him with the ram of the guilt-offering, and it shall be forgiven him.


###### Leviticus 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:17)


וְאִם־נֶ֙פֶשׁ֙ כִּ֣י תֶֽחֱטָ֔א וְעָֽשְׂתָ֗ה אַחַת֙ מִכׇּל־מִצְוֺ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר לֹ֣א תֵעָשֶׂ֑ינָה וְלֹֽא־יָדַ֥ע וְאָשֵׁ֖ם וְנָשָׂ֥א עֲוֺנֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a person sins and did [transgress] one of the commandments of Adonoy that should not be done, [however,] he is uncertain [that he sinned], and he [nevertheless] incurs guilt and will bear [the burden of] his iniquity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a person sin, and commit any of these things which are forbidden to be done by the commandments of the Lord; though he know it not, yet is he guilty, and shall bear his iniquity.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a soul sin and do one of all the mitzvoth of the L-rd which may not be done [(and which is punishable by kareth)], and he not know [that he has definitely sinned (as when one ate a piece of meat and was later informed that one of the two pieces before him was forbidden, so that he might have eaten the forbidden piece)], and he be guilty (in that respect), then he shall bear his sin.


###### Leviticus 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:18)


וְ֠הֵבִ֠יא אַ֣יִל תָּמִ֧ים מִן־הַצֹּ֛אן בְּעֶרְכְּךָ֥ לְאָשָׁ֖ם אֶל־הַכֹּהֵ֑ן וְכִפֶּר֩ עָלָ֨יו הַכֹּהֵ֜ן עַ֣ל שִׁגְגָת֧וֹ אֲשֶׁר־שָׁגָ֛ג וְה֥וּא לֹֽא־יָדַ֖ע וְנִסְלַ֥ח לֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring an unblemished ram, from the sheep, based on the valuation as a guilt-offering, to the *kohein.* The *kohein* will atone for him, for the error which he did unintentionally, and he did not know, and he will be pardoned.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall bring a ram without blemish out of the flock, according to the valuation, for a guilt offering, to the priest: and the priest shall make atonement for him concerning his ignorance wherein he erred and knew it not, and it shall be forgiven him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall bring a ram without blemish, from the flock, by your valuation [see 15] for a guilt-offering to the Cohein; and the Cohein shall make atonement for him for his unwitting sin which he committed, for he did not know, and it shall be forgiven him. [But if he discovers later that he definitely sinned, he is not forgiven until he brings a sin-offering.]


###### Leviticus 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:19)


אָשָׁ֖ם ה֑וּא אָשֹׁ֥ם אָשַׁ֖ם לַיהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It is a guilt-offering [for] he certainly incurred guilt before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is a guilt offering: he has certainly trespassed against the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It [(to exclude the guilt-offering of a Nazirite (Numbers 6:12)] is a guilt-offering [which requires a ram worth two selaim]; he has been guilty (ashom asham) to the L-rd ["ashom asham" — to include (in the above) the guilt-offering (for violation) of a betrothed handmaid (19:20)]


###### Leviticus 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:20)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:21)


נֶ֚פֶשׁ כִּ֣י תֶחֱטָ֔א וּמָעֲלָ֥ה מַ֖עַל בַּיהֹוָ֑ה וְכִחֵ֨שׁ בַּעֲמִית֜וֹ בְּפִקָּד֗וֹן אֽוֹ־בִתְשׂ֤וּמֶת יָד֙ א֣וֹ בְגָזֵ֔ל א֖וֹ עָשַׁ֥ק אֶת־עֲמִיתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a person sins and wrongfully deviates from Adonoy and denies to his friend the possession of a keepsake placed in his trust or, a deposit [regarding money that was loaned or in partnership,] or theft [claiming that something was stolen,] or, oppressed [he withheld wages from] his friend.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If a person sin, and commit a trespass against the Lord, and lie to his neighbour in that which was delivered him to keep, or in a loan, or in a thing taken away by violence, or have wronged his neighbour;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If a soul sin and commit a profanation against the L-rd [(When he "denies," he "denies" the L-rd, who witnessed what was done)], and he deny to his neighbor a pledge, or a deposit [for business capital or as a loan] or a theft; or if he oppressed his neighbor [i.e., if he held back the wages of a hired laborer] —


###### Leviticus 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:22)


אֽוֹ־מָצָ֧א אֲבֵדָ֛ה וְכִ֥חֶשׁ בָּ֖הּ וְנִשְׁבַּ֣ע עַל־שָׁ֑קֶר עַל־אַחַ֗ת מִכֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה הָאָדָ֖ם לַחֲטֹ֥א בָהֵֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or, if he found a lost item and denies it and swears falsely in any of all these [aforementioned instances], whatever someone will do to sin in these instances.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or have found that which was lost, and have lied concerning it, and have sworn falsely; in any of all these that a man does, sinning in that:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or if he find a lost object and deny it, and swear falsely in one of all [these things] wherein a man sins [and swears falsely, denying a money claim] —


###### Leviticus 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:23)


וְהָיָה֮ כִּֽי־יֶחֱטָ֣א וְאָשֵׁם֒ וְהֵשִׁ֨יב אֶת־הַגְּזֵלָ֜ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר גָּזָ֗ל א֤וֹ אֶת־הָעֹ֙שֶׁק֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׁ֔ק א֚וֹ אֶת־הַפִּקָּד֔וֹן אֲשֶׁ֥ר הׇפְקַ֖ד אִתּ֑וֹ א֥וֹ אֶת־הָאֲבֵדָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר מָצָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It shall be that when he [realizes his] sin and incurs guilt, he shall return the stolen property that he stole, or, the wages that he withheld or, whatever keepsake that was placed in his trust or, the lost item which he found.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then it shall be, because he has sinned, and is guilty, that he shall restore that which he took violently away, or the thing which he has deceitfully acquired, or that which was delivered him to keep, or the lost thing which he found,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, when [he acknowledges, penitentially, that] he sinned and [that] he is guilty, then he shall return the theft that he has stolen, or the oppression wherein he has oppressed, or the pledge which was deposited with him, or the lost object which he found.


###### Leviticus 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:24)


א֠וֹ מִכֹּ֞ל אֲשֶׁר־יִשָּׁבַ֣ע עָלָיו֮ לַשֶּׁ֒קֶר֒ וְשִׁלַּ֤ם אֹתוֹ֙ בְּרֹאשׁ֔וֹ וַחֲמִשִׁתָ֖יו יֹסֵ֣ף עָלָ֑יו לַאֲשֶׁ֨ר ה֥וּא ל֛וֹ יִתְּנֶ֖נּוּ בְּי֥וֹם אַשְׁמָתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or, anything about which he will swear falsely. He shall pay its price and add an additional fifth to that [amount] to whomever that object belongs he shall give it on the day of his guilt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or all that about which he has sworn falsely; he shall even restore it in the principal, and shall add the fifth part more to it, and give it to him to whom it belongs, in the day of his trespass offering;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or of all that he swears upon falsely — and he shall pay it at its head [i.e., he shall return the principal], and its fifths shall he add to it [i.e., If he gave the claimant the principal and swore that he had given him the fifth (and subsequently admitted that he had sworn falsely, the fifth is accounted a principal and) he pays a fifth on the fifth (and so, progressively,) until the principal is less than the value of a perutah]. To whom it belongs shall he give it on the day of his (acknowledgement of his) guilt.


###### Leviticus 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:25)


וְאֶת־אֲשָׁמ֥וֹ יָבִ֖יא לַיהֹוָ֑ה אַ֣יִל תָּמִ֧ים מִן־הַצֹּ֛אן בְּעֶרְכְּךָ֥ לְאָשָׁ֖ם אֶל־הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring his guilt-offering to Adonoy an unblemished ram, from the sheep based on the valuation, as a guilt-offering to the *kohein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall bring his guilt offering to the Lord, a ram without blemish out of the flock, according to the value of a guilt offering, to the priest:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his guilt-offering shall he bring to the L-rd, a ram without blemish from the flock, by your valuation, for a guilt-offering to the Cohein.


###### Leviticus 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/5:26)


וְכִפֶּ֨ר עָלָ֧יו הַכֹּהֵ֛ן לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה וְנִסְלַ֣ח ל֑וֹ עַל־אַחַ֛ת מִכֹּ֥ל אֲשֶֽׁר־יַעֲשֶׂ֖ה לְאַשְׁמָ֥ה בָֽהּ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* will atone for him before Adonoy and he will be pardoned for anything that he will do through which guilt incurs.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall make atonement for him before the Lord: and it shall be forgiven him for anything of all that he has done wherein to incur guilt.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall make atonement for him before the L-rd, and it shall be forgiven him for one of all (the transgressions) that he does to incur guilt thereby.



## Chapter 6

###### Leviticus 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:2)


צַ֤ו אֶֽת־אַהֲרֹן֙ וְאֶת־בָּנָ֣יו לֵאמֹ֔ר זֹ֥את תּוֹרַ֖ת הָעֹלָ֑ה הִ֣וא הָעֹלָ֡ה עַל֩ מוֹקְדָ֨הֿ¹ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֤חַ כׇּל־הַלַּ֙יְלָה֙ עַד־הַבֹּ֔קֶר וְאֵ֥שׁ הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ תּ֥וּקַד בּֽוֹ׃

footnotes:
¹ (בספרי תימן מוֹקְדָ֨הֿ במ״ם רגילה)


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Command Aharon and his sons, saying; this is the law of the burnt-offering. It is the burnt-offering [which remains] on its pyre on the altar all night, until the morning, and the fire of the altar will be kept burning on it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Command Aharon and his sons, saying, This is the Tora of the burnt offering: It is the burnt offering, which shall be burning upon the altar all night until the morning, and the fire of the altar shall be kept burning in it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Command (tzav) Aaron and his sons ["tzav" connotes a prompting to zeal, for the immediate situation and for future generations. R. Shimon said: Scripture must prompt thus all the more where no profit is to be derived (the Cohanim not benefitting from the burnt-offering)]: This [(that it is permitted to smoke the fat and limbs the entire night, and that certain offerings must be taken down from the altar and others not)] is the law of the burnt-offering. It is the burnt-offering [(to exclude animals involved, actively or passively, in sodomy, and the like, which were invalidated before they entered the azarah (and which, if brought up on the altar must be taken down)] (It shall remain) on its fire-wood on the altar the entire night until the morning; and the fire of the altar shall be burning in it.


###### Leviticus 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:3)


וְלָבַ֨שׁ הַכֹּהֵ֜ן מִדּ֣וֹ בַ֗ד וּמִֽכְנְסֵי־בַד֮ יִלְבַּ֣שׁ עַל־בְּשָׂרוֹ֒ וְהֵרִ֣ים אֶת־הַדֶּ֗שֶׁן אֲשֶׁ֨ר תֹּאכַ֥ל הָאֵ֛שׁ אֶת־הָעֹלָ֖ה עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ וְשָׂמ֕וֹ אֵ֖צֶל הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall dress in his linen garment and he shall wear linen pants on his [body] skin; and he shall separate the ashes when the fire consumes the burnt-offering on the altar and place it next to the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall put on his linen garment, and his linen breeches shall he put on his flesh, and take up the ashes which the fire has consumed with the burnt offering on the altar, and he shall put them beside the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall put on his ["fit to size"] linen garment [the kutoneth], and linen breeches shall he put [directly] on his flesh [nothing intervening], and he shall lift [with a fire-pan] the ashes of the fire's consuming the burnt-offering on the altar [(If he finds unconsumed pieces he returns them to the altar)], and he shall put them beside the altar. [He takes a full fire-pan of the consumed inner pieces and puts them on the east of the ramp.]


###### Leviticus 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:4)


וּפָשַׁט֙ אֶת־בְּגָדָ֔יו וְלָבַ֖שׁ בְּגָדִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֑ים וְהוֹצִ֤יא אֶת־הַדֶּ֙שֶׁן֙ אֶל־מִח֣וּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶ֔ה אֶל־מָק֖וֹם טָהֽוֹר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall [then] remove his garments and dress in other garments. He shall take out the ashes beyond the encampment, to a pure [undefiled] place.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall put off his garments, and put on other garments, and carry the ashes outside the camp to a clean place.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall take off his garments and don other garments [(This is not obligatory but derech eretz (proper conduct), so that he not soil the garments in which he ministers)], and he shall take out the ashes [when they accumulate on the altar to the point where there is not enough room for the wood pile (This, unlike the removal of the ashes above (3), is not a daily requirement)] outside the camp to a clean place.


###### Leviticus 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:5)


וְהָאֵ֨שׁ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֤חַ תּֽוּקַד־בּוֹ֙ לֹ֣א תִכְבֶּ֔ה וּבִעֵ֨ר עָלֶ֧יהָ הַכֹּהֵ֛ן עֵצִ֖ים בַּבֹּ֣קֶר בַּבֹּ֑קֶר וְעָרַ֤ךְ עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ הָֽעֹלָ֔ה וְהִקְטִ֥יר עָלֶ֖יהָ חֶלְבֵ֥י הַשְּׁלָמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The fire on the altar shall be kindled with it it shall not go out. The *kohein* shall burn logs upon it each and every morning and arrange the burnt-offering on it and burn upon it the fats of the peace-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the fire upon the altar shall be kept burning in it; it shall not be put out: and the priest shall burn wood on it every morning, and lay the burnt offering in order upon it; and he shall burn on it the fat of the peace offerings.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the fire of the altar shall be burning in it; it shall not go out. And the Cohein shall kindle wood on it every morning. And he shall arrange the burnt-offering upon it [The continual (daily) burnt-offering takes precedence], and he shall smoke upon it the fats of the peace-offerings.


###### Leviticus 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:6)


אֵ֗שׁ תָּמִ֛יד תּוּקַ֥ד עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ לֹ֥א תִכְבֶּֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A continual fire shall be lit on the altar; it shall not go out.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The fire shall ever be burning upon the altar; it shall never go out.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A perpetual fire [the fire called "perpetual" (Exodus 27:20), that by which the menorah is kindled] shall be burning on the [outer] altar; it shall not go out [One who extinguishes the altar fire transgresses two negative commandments.]


###### Leviticus 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:7)


וְזֹ֥את תּוֹרַ֖ת הַמִּנְחָ֑ה הַקְרֵ֨ב אֹתָ֤הּ בְּנֵֽי־אַהֲרֹן֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶל־פְּנֵ֖י הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the law of the meal-offering. The sons of Aharon bring it before Adonoy, to the front of the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this is the Tora of the meal offering: the sons of Aharon shall offer it before the Lord, before the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this is the law of the meal-offering, [that all of them require oil and frankincense]. The sons of Aaron shall bring it near [to the southwest corner of the altar], before the L-rd, [in the west, towards the tent of meeting], in front of the altar [the south, where the ramp is].


###### Leviticus 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:8)


וְהֵרִ֨ים מִמֶּ֜נּוּ בְּקֻמְצ֗וֹ מִסֹּ֤לֶת הַמִּנְחָה֙ וּמִשַּׁמְנָ֔הּ וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־הַלְּבֹנָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַמִּנְחָ֑ה וְהִקְטִ֣יר הַמִּזְבֵּ֗חַ רֵ֧יחַ נִיחֹ֛חַ אַזְכָּרָתָ֖הּ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall separate from it, when he takes his fistful, from some of the meal-offerings fine flour and some of its oil and all the frankincense which is on the meal-offering. He shall burn, on the altar a pleasing fragrance; its memorial portion, to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall lift from it his handful, of the flour of the meal offering, and of its oil, and all the frankincense which is upon the meal offering, and shall burn it upon the altar for a sweet savour, even the memorial part of it, to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall take from it [from the combined mixture (of flour, oil, and frankincense), that there be a full issaron (a tenth) at the time of kemitzah], with his fistful, [that he not make a measure for the fistful], from the fine flour of the meal-offering [(it should not be mixed with another)] and its oil [(The fistful is taken from the place where the oil is thickest)], and all the frankincense which is on the meal-offering [(He collects the frankincense after taking the fistful and smokes it)], and he shall smoke it upon the altar, a sweet savor, its remembrance to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:9)


וְהַנּוֹתֶ֣רֶת מִמֶּ֔נָּה יֹאכְל֖וּ אַהֲרֹ֣ן וּבָנָ֑יו מַצּ֤וֹת תֵּֽאָכֵל֙ בְּמָק֣וֹם קָדֹ֔שׁ בַּחֲצַ֥ר אֹֽהֶל־מוֹעֵ֖ד יֹאכְלֽוּהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whatever remains from it shall be eaten by Aharon and his sons. It must be eaten as *matzoh,* in a sacred place. In the courtyard of the Tent of Meeting they shall eat it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the remainder of it shall Aharon and his sons eat: with unleavened bread shall it be eaten in the holy place; in the court of the Tent of Meeting they shall eat it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And what is left of it shall be eaten by Aaron and his sons. It shall be eaten unleavened; in a holy place, in the court of the tent of meeting shall they eat it.


###### Leviticus 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:10)


לֹ֤א תֵאָפֶה֙ חָמֵ֔ץ חֶלְקָ֛ם נָתַ֥תִּי אֹתָ֖הּ מֵאִשָּׁ֑י קֹ֤דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁים֙ הִ֔וא כַּחַטָּ֖את וְכָאָשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It should not be baked [as] leavened. I have given it to them as their portion from my fire-offerings. It is holy of holies [most holy], as [are] the sin-offering and as the guilt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It shall not be baked with leaven. I have given it to them for their portion of my offerings made by fire; it is most holy, as is the sin offering, and the guilt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It shall not be baked with leaven, their portion [(Even what is left over may not be baked with leaven)] (Their portion) have I given it of My fire-offerings; it is holy of holies. It [the meal-offering of the sinner] is like the sin-offering [in that if he did not take the fistful lishmah it is pasul] and [a gift meal-offering is] like the guilt-offering [in that if he did not take the fistful lishmah it is kasher].


###### Leviticus 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:11)


כׇּל־זָכָ֞ר בִּבְנֵ֤י אַהֲרֹן֙ יֹֽאכְלֶ֔נָּה חׇק־עוֹלָם֙ לְדֹרֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם מֵאִשֵּׁ֖י יְהֹוָ֑ה כֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־יִגַּ֥ע בָּהֶ֖ם יִקְדָּֽשׁ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every male descendant of Aharon may eat it, an everlasting statute for all your descendants from the fire-offerings of Adonoy. whatever shall touch them will become sacred.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All the males among the children of Aharon shall eat of it. It shall be a fixed portion throughout your generations of the offerings of the Lord made by fire: everyone that touches them shall be holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every male [even one with a blemish] among the children of Aaron may eat it. It is a statute forever for your generations of the fire-offerings of the L-rd. All [mundane foods or foods of lower order sanctity] that touch them (the meal-offerings) [and absorb aught of them] shall be sanctified [like them. If the meal-offering is pasul, they become pasul; if it is kasher, they must be eaten with the stringencies of the meal-offering].


###### Leviticus 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:12)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:13)


זֶ֡ה קׇרְבַּן֩ אַהֲרֹ֨ן וּבָנָ֜יו אֲשֶׁר־יַקְרִ֣יבוּ לַֽיהֹוָ֗ה בְּיוֹם֙ הִמָּשַׁ֣ח אֹת֔וֹ עֲשִׂירִ֨ת הָאֵפָ֥ה סֹ֛לֶת מִנְחָ֖ה תָּמִ֑יד מַחֲצִיתָ֣הּ בַּבֹּ֔קֶר וּמַחֲצִיתָ֖הּ בָּעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the offering of Aharon and his sons that they shall offer to Adonoy on the day that he is anointed; one tenth of an *ephah* of fine flour as a meal-offering, always [daily]. Half of it [he shall offer] in the morning and half of it [he shall offer] in the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the offering of Aharon and of his sons, which they shall offer to the Lord on the day when he is anointed; the tenth part of an efa of fine flour for a meal offering perpetual, half of it in the morning, and half of it at night.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the offering of Aaron and his sons [(The ordinary Cohanim, too, offer the tenth of an ephah, but only on the day of their investiture, whereas the high-priest does so every day)], which they shall offer up to the L-rd on the day that he is anointed; the tenth part of an ephah of fine flour, a perpetual meal-offering — half of it in the morning and half of it in the evening.


###### Leviticus 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:14)


עַֽל־מַחֲבַ֗ת בַּשֶּׁ֛מֶן תֵּעָשֶׂ֖ה מֻרְבֶּ֣כֶת תְּבִיאֶ֑נָּה תֻּפִינֵי֙ מִנְחַ֣ת פִּתִּ֔ים תַּקְרִ֥יב רֵֽיחַ־נִיחֹ֖חַ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In a frying pan, with oil it shall be made; you shall bring it saturated, well-baked, a meal-offering of broken pieces, you shall bring a pleasing fragrance to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In a pan it shall be made with oil; and when it is well soaked, thou shalt bring it in: and the baked pieces of the meal offering shalt thou offer for a sweet savour to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On a griddle, with oil, shall it be made; soaked [scalded with hot (oil) to saturation] shall you bring it, baked many times [(After scalding it he bakes it in an oven and then he fries it on a griddle)]. A meal-offering of pieces shall you offer it, a sweet savor to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:15)


וְהַכֹּהֵ֨ן הַמָּשִׁ֧יחַ תַּחְתָּ֛יו מִבָּנָ֖יו יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה אֹתָ֑הּ חׇק־עוֹלָ֕ם לַיהֹוָ֖ה כָּלִ֥יל תׇּקְטָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* who is anointed in his stead from among his sons, he shall [also] do it; an everlasting statute to Adonoy, it shall be entirely burnt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest of his sons that is anointed in his place shall offer it: it is a statute for ever to the Lord; it shall be wholly burnt.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And The Cohein that is anointed from his sons in his place shall offer it, a statute forever for the L-rd; it shall be entirely smoked. [No fistful is taken. The same applies to all gift meal-offerings of a Cohein.]


###### Leviticus 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:16)


וְכׇל־מִנְחַ֥ת כֹּהֵ֛ן כָּלִ֥יל תִּהְיֶ֖ה לֹ֥א תֵאָכֵֽל׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every meal-offering of a *kohein* shall be completely burnt and may not be eaten.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For every meal offering of the priest shall be wholly burnt: it shall not be eaten.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every meal-offering of a Cohein shall be entire, [exclusively for the L-rd]; it shall not be eaten.


###### Leviticus 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:17)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:18)


דַּבֵּ֤ר אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹן֙ וְאֶל־בָּנָ֣יו לֵאמֹ֔ר זֹ֥את תּוֹרַ֖ת הַֽחַטָּ֑את בִּמְק֡וֹם אֲשֶׁר֩ תִּשָּׁחֵ֨ט הָעֹלָ֜ה תִּשָּׁחֵ֤ט הַֽחַטָּאת֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁ֖ים הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Aharon and his sons, saying: this is the law of the sin-offering. In the place where the burnt-offering is slaughtered, the sin-offering shall be slaughtered, before Adonoy; it is holy of holies [most holy].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to Aharon and to his sons, saying, This is the Tora of the sin offering: In the place where the burnt offering is killed shall the sin offering be killed before the Lord: it is most holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to Aaron and his sons, saying: This is the law of the sin-offering. In the place where the burnt-offering is slaughtered, there shall the sin-offering be slaughtered, before the L-rd; it is holy of holies.


###### Leviticus 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:19)


הַכֹּהֵ֛ן הַֽמְחַטֵּ֥א אֹתָ֖הּ יֹאכְלֶ֑נָּה בְּמָק֤וֹם קָדֹשׁ֙ תֵּֽאָכֵ֔ל בַּחֲצַ֖ר אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* who offers it as a sin-offering, shall eat it. It shall be eaten in a sacred place, in the Courtyard of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The priest that offers it for sin shall eat it: in the holy place shall it be eaten, in the court of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The Cohein who offers it as a sin-offering [(to exclude one who is not clean at the time of the sprinkling of the blood)] shall eat it. In a holy place shall it be eaten, in the court of the tent of meeting.


###### Leviticus 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:20)


כֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־יִגַּ֥ע בִּבְשָׂרָ֖הּ יִקְדָּ֑שׁ וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר יִזֶּ֤ה מִדָּמָהּ֙ עַל־הַבֶּ֔גֶד אֲשֶׁר֙ יִזֶּ֣ה עָלֶ֔יהָ תְּכַבֵּ֖ס בְּמָק֥וֹם קָדֹֽשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whatever shall touch its flesh shall become holy and when of its blood is sprinkled on the garment, whatever it is sprinkled upon, you shall wash in a sacred place.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whatever shall touch its flesh shall be holy: and when there is sprinkled of its blood upon any garment, thou shalt wash that on which it was sprinkled in the holy place.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All that touches its flesh shall be sanctified [see 11]. And what shall be sprinkled of its blood upon a garment, that [place] which has been sprinkled upon, you shall wash in a holy place [the azarah].


###### Leviticus 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:21)


וּכְלִי־חֶ֛רֶשׂ אֲשֶׁ֥ר תְּבֻשַּׁל־בּ֖וֹ יִשָּׁבֵ֑ר וְאִם־בִּכְלִ֤י נְחֹ֙שֶׁת֙ בֻּשָּׁ֔לָה וּמֹרַ֥ק וְשֻׁטַּ֖ף בַּמָּֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
An earthenware vessel in which it was cooked, must be broken. If it was cooked in a copper vessel, it must be scoured and rinsed with water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the earthen vessel in which it is boiled shall be broken: and if it be boiled in a brass pot, it shall be both scoured, and rinsed in water.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And an earthen vessel in which it is cooked shall be broken [(because what is absorbed becomes nothar ("leftover" holy food)]. And if it is cooked in a copper vessel, it shall be scoured and rinsed with water [to discharge what was absorbed.]


###### Leviticus 6:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:22)


כׇּל־זָכָ֥ר בַּכֹּהֲנִ֖ים יֹאכַ֣ל אֹתָ֑הּ קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁ֖ים הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every male among the *kohanim* may eat it; it is holy of holies [=most holy].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All the males among the priests shall eat of it: it is most holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every male among the Cohanim may eat it; it is holy of holies.


###### Leviticus 6:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/6:23)


וְכׇל־חַטָּ֡את אֲשֶׁר֩ יוּבָ֨א מִדָּמָ֜הּ אֶל־אֹ֧הֶל מוֹעֵ֛ד לְכַפֵּ֥ר בַּקֹּ֖דֶשׁ לֹ֣א תֵאָכֵ֑ל בָּאֵ֖שׁ תִּשָּׂרֵֽף׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every sin-offering whose blood is to be brought inside the Tent of Meeting to bring about atonement inside the Sanctuary may not be eaten; it must be burned in fire.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And no sin offering, of which any of the blood is brought into the Tent of Meeting to be sprinkled in the holy place, shall be eaten: it shall be burnt in the fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every [(all kodshim are included)] sin-offering whereof any of its blood is brought to the tent of meeting to make atonement in the holy place shall not be eaten [(If he brings the blood of an "outer" sin-offering into the sanctuary, it becomes pasul)]; in fire shall it be burned.



## Chapter 7

###### Leviticus 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:1)


וְזֹ֥את תּוֹרַ֖ת הָאָשָׁ֑ם קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁ֖ים הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the law of the guilt-offering; it is holy of holies [=most holy].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this is the Tora of the guilt offering: it is most holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this is the law of the guilt-offering; it is holy of holies. [It is offered, but its substitute is not offered.]


###### Leviticus 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:2)


בִּמְק֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִשְׁחֲטוּ֙ אֶת־הָ֣עֹלָ֔ה יִשְׁחֲט֖וּ אֶת־הָאָשָׁ֑ם וְאֶת־דָּמ֛וֹ יִזְרֹ֥ק עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ סָבִֽיב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In the place where they will slaughter the burnt-offering they shall slaughter the guilt-offering, and they shall sprinkle its blood all around the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the place where they kill the burnt offering shall they kill the guilt offering: and its blood shall be sprinkled round about upon the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the place where they slaughter the burnt-offering [(A communal burnt-offering is also slaughtered in the north)] shall they slaughter the guilt-offering, and its blood shall he dash on the altar roundabout.


###### Leviticus 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:3)


וְאֵ֥ת כׇּל־חֶלְבּ֖וֹ יַקְרִ֣יב מִמֶּ֑נּוּ אֵ֚ת הָֽאַלְיָ֔ה וְאֶת־הַחֵ֖לֶב הַֽמְכַסֶּ֥ה אֶת־הַקֶּֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring all its fat from it; the [fat] tail, the fat covering the innards,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall offer of it all the fat; the fat tail, and the fat that covers the inwards,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of its fat shall he offer of it: the fat-tail [(for only a ram or a lamb is brought as a guilt-offering)] and the fat that covers the innards.


###### Leviticus 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:4)


וְאֵת֙ שְׁתֵּ֣י הַכְּלָיֹ֔ת וְאֶת־הַחֵ֙לֶב֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עֲלֵיהֶ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַכְּסָלִ֑ים וְאֶת־הַיֹּתֶ֙רֶת֙ עַל־הַכָּבֵ֔ד עַל־הַכְּלָיֹ֖ת יְסִירֶֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
the two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver, he shall remove together with the kidneys.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the two kidneys, and the fat that is on them, which is by the flanks, and the appendage above the liver, with the kidneys, it shall he take away:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the two kidneys and the fat which is upon them, which is on the loins, and the lobe with [some of] the liver, aside from the kidneys, he shall remove it.


###### Leviticus 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:5)


וְהִקְטִ֨יר אֹתָ֤ם הַכֹּהֵן֙ הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַיהֹוָ֑ה אָשָׁ֖ם הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall burn them on the altar, a fire-offering to Adonoy; it is a guilt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall burn them upon the altar for an offering made by fire to the Lord: it is a guilt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall smoke them upon the altar, a fire-offering to the L-rd; it is a guilt-offering [(until, in certain circumstances, its name is removed from it by its being consigned to pasture until it sustains a blemish)]


###### Leviticus 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:6)


כׇּל־זָכָ֥ר בַּכֹּהֲנִ֖ים יֹאכְלֶ֑נּוּ בְּמָק֤וֹם קָדוֹשׁ֙ יֵאָכֵ֔ל קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁ֖ים הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All males among the *kohanim* may eat it. It must be eaten in a sacred place; it is most holy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every male among the priests shall eat of it: it shall be eaten in the holy place: it is most holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every male among the Cohanim may eat it. In a holy place shall it be eaten; it is holy of holies.


###### Leviticus 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:7)


כַּֽחַטָּאת֙ כָּֽאָשָׁ֔ם תּוֹרָ֥ה אַחַ֖ת לָהֶ֑ם הַכֹּהֵ֛ן אֲשֶׁ֥ר יְכַפֶּר־בּ֖וֹ ל֥וֹ יִהְיֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
As the sin-offering is, so is the guilt-offering; one law applies to them both; the *kohein* who will bring about atonement with it, it shall belong to him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As the sin offering is, so is the guilt offering: there is one Tora for them: the priest that makes atonement therewith shall have it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As is the sin-offering, so is the guilt-offering. There is one law for them: the Cohein who [may] make atonement therewith, to him shall it be [(to exclude from sharing in it one who has immersed in the daytime (and is not clean until the evening), one who lacks atonement (until he brings a sacrifice), and a mourner (before the burial of his kin)].


###### Leviticus 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:8)


וְהַ֨כֹּהֵ֔ן הַמַּקְרִ֖יב אֶת־עֹ֣לַת אִ֑ישׁ ע֤וֹר הָֽעֹלָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הִקְרִ֔יב לַכֹּהֵ֖ן ל֥וֹ יִהְיֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* who brings a burnt-offering of an individual, the skin of the burnt-offering that he brought, shall belong to the *kohein,* it shall belong to him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest that offers any man’s burnt offering, the priest shall have for himself the skin of the burnt offering which he has offered.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein who offers up the burnt-offering of a man, the hide of the burnt-offering which he offered up is the Cohein's; to him shall it be.


###### Leviticus 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:9)


וְכׇל־מִנְחָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר תֵּֽאָפֶה֙ בַּתַּנּ֔וּר וְכׇל־נַעֲשָׂ֥ה בַמַּרְחֶ֖שֶׁת וְעַֽל־מַחֲבַ֑ת לַכֹּהֵ֛ן הַמַּקְרִ֥יב אֹתָ֖הּ ל֥וֹ תִֽהְיֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every meal-offering that shall be baked in an oven and every one made in a deep pot or frying pan, [belong] to the *kohein* who offers it; it shall belong to him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the meal offering that is baked in the oven, and all that is dressed in the frying pan, and in the pan, shall be the priest’s that offers it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every meal-offering that is baked in the oven, and all that is made in the stewing-pan and on the griddle, to the Cohein who offers it up, to him [i.e., to the priestly family officiating that day] shall it be.


###### Leviticus 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:10)


וְכׇל־מִנְחָ֥ה בְלוּלָֽה־בַשֶּׁ֖מֶן וַחֲרֵבָ֑ה לְכׇל־בְּנֵ֧י אַהֲרֹ֛ן תִּהְיֶ֖ה אִ֥ישׁ כְּאָחִֽיו׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every meal-offering mixed with oil or dry, shall belong to all the sons of Aharon, each equally.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And every meal offering, mingled with oil, and dry, shall all the sons of Aharon have, one as well as another.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every meal-offering mixed with oil [(a gift meal-offering)] or dry [(the sinner's meal-offering and the meal-offering of jealousy (Numbers 5:15), which contain no oil)], to all the sons of Aaron shall it be, one man as well as another.


###### Leviticus 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:11)


וְזֹ֥את תּוֹרַ֖ת זֶ֣בַח הַשְּׁלָמִ֑ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר יַקְרִ֖יב לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the law of the peace-offering that one will bring to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this is the Tora of the peace offerings, which he shall offer to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this is the law of the sacrifice of peace-offerings which one shall offer to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:12)


אִ֣ם עַל־תּוֹדָה֮ יַקְרִיבֶ֒נּוּ֒ וְהִקְרִ֣יב**׀** עַל־זֶ֣בַח הַתּוֹדָ֗ה חַלּ֤וֹת מַצּוֹת֙ בְּלוּלֹ֣ת בַּשֶּׁ֔מֶן וּרְקִיקֵ֥י מַצּ֖וֹת מְשֻׁחִ֣ים בַּשָּׁ֑מֶן וְסֹ֣לֶת מֻרְבֶּ֔כֶת חַלֹּ֖ת בְּלוּלֹ֥ת בַּשָּֽׁמֶן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he brings it as thanksgiving offering he shall bring along with his thanksgiving offering *matzoh* [unleavened] loaves mixed with oil, *matzoh* [unleavened] wafers anointed with oil and loaves of saturated fine flour mixed with oil.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If he offer it for a thanksgiving, then he shall offer with the sacrifice of thanksgiving unleavened cakes mingled with oil, and unleavened wafers anointed with oil, and cakes mingled with oil, of fine flour, well soaked.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If for thanksgiving [for a miracle wrought for him, e.g., recovering from an illness] he shall offer it, then he shall offer with the sacrifice of thanksgiving, unleavened cakes mixed with oil, and unleavened wafers spread with oil, and [unleavened] cakes of soaked fine flour mixed with oil [(ten cakes of each of the four kinds)].


###### Leviticus 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:13)


עַל־חַלֹּת֙ לֶ֣חֶם חָמֵ֔ץ יַקְרִ֖יב קׇרְבָּנ֑וֹ עַל־זֶ֖בַח תּוֹדַ֥ת שְׁלָמָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
With loaves of leavened bread he shall bring his offering, along with his peace-offering of thanksgiving.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Together with loaves of leavened bread shall he make his offering, these to be added to his peace offering of thanksgiving.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With cakes of leavened bread shall he offer his offering, with the sacrifice of his thanksgiving peace-offerings. [(The bread does not become consecrated until the sacrifice is slaughtered)].


###### Leviticus 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:14)


וְהִקְרִ֨יב מִמֶּ֤נּוּ אֶחָד֙ מִכׇּל־קׇרְבָּ֔ן תְּרוּמָ֖ה לַיהֹוָ֑ה לַכֹּהֵ֗ן הַזֹּרֵ֛ק אֶת־דַּ֥ם הַשְּׁלָמִ֖ים ל֥וֹ יִהְיֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
From it, he shall bring one from each offering as separated portion to Adonoy. To that *kohein* who sprinkles the blood of the peace-offering, it shall belong to him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And of it he shall offer one out of each offering for a heave offering to the Lord, and it shall be the priest’s that sprinkles the blood of the peace offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall offer of it one [bread] of each offering [i.e., of each kind of the four breads], a gift-offering to the L-rd. To the Cohein who dashes (against the altar) the blood of the peace-offerings, to him shall it be.


###### Leviticus 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:15)


וּבְשַׂ֗ר זֶ֚בַח תּוֹדַ֣ת שְׁלָמָ֔יו בְּי֥וֹם קׇרְבָּנ֖וֹ יֵאָכֵ֑ל לֹֽא־יַנִּ֥יחַ מִמֶּ֖נּוּ עַד־בֹּֽקֶר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The flesh of his thanksgiving peace-offering on the day of its sacrifice it may be eaten. He shall not leave over any of it until morning.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the flesh of the sacrifice of his peace offering for thanksgiving shall be eaten the same day that it is offered; he shall not leave any of it until the morning.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the flesh of the sacrifice of his thanksgiving peace-offerings [(to include for being eaten on the same day and the succeeding night the sin-offering, guilt-offering, and ram of the Nazirite, and the festival offering of the fourteenth of Nissan)], on the day of his offering shall it [and its bread] be eaten. He shall not leave over of it until morning.


###### Leviticus 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:16)


וְאִם־נֶ֣דֶר**׀** א֣וֹ נְדָבָ֗ה זֶ֚בַח קׇרְבָּנ֔וֹ בְּי֛וֹם הַקְרִיב֥וֹ אֶת־זִבְח֖וֹ יֵאָכֵ֑ל וּמִֽמׇּחֳרָ֔ת וְהַנּוֹתָ֥ר מִמֶּ֖נּוּ יֵאָכֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If either a vow or a voluntary offering, are the sacrifices of his offerings, it shall be eaten on the day of his offering. And on the next day, the remnant of it may [also] be eaten.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if the sacrifice of his offering be a vow, or a voluntary offering, it shall be eaten on the same day that he offers his sacrifice: and on the morrow also the remainder of it shall be eaten:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a vow or a gift [(and not thanksgiving for a miracle)] is the sacrifice of his offering, [(then it does not require bread and it may be eaten for two days)], on the day that he offers his sacrifice shall it be eaten; and on the next day, that which remains of it [from the first day] shall be eaten.


###### Leviticus 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:17)


וְהַנּוֹתָ֖ר מִבְּשַׂ֣ר הַזָּ֑בַח בַּיּוֹם֙ הַשְּׁלִישִׁ֔י בָּאֵ֖שׁ יִשָּׂרֵֽף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whatever flesh is left over from the offering shall be burned in fire, on the third day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but that which remains of the flesh of the sacrifice on the third day shall be burnt with fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And what remains from the flesh of the sacrifice on the third day, with fire shall it be burned.


###### Leviticus 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:18)


וְאִ֣ם הֵאָכֹ֣ל יֵ֠אָכֵ֠ל מִבְּשַׂר־זֶ֨בַח שְׁלָמָ֜יו בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי֮ לֹ֣א יֵרָצֶה֒ הַמַּקְרִ֣יב אֹת֗וֹ לֹ֧א יֵחָשֵׁ֛ב ל֖וֹ פִּגּ֣וּל יִהְיֶ֑ה וְהַנֶּ֛פֶשׁ הָאֹכֶ֥לֶת מִמֶּ֖נּוּ עֲוֺנָ֥הּ תִּשָּֽׂא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If it will be eaten, of the flesh of the peace-offering on the third day, it will not be favorably accepted. [As to] whoever brings it, it will not be credited to him; it is an abomination and anyone who eats of it shall bear [the burden of] its iniquity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if any of the flesh of the sacrifice of his peace offering be eaten at all on the third day, it shall not be accepted, neither shall it be imputed to him who offers it: it shall be an abomination, and the person that eats of it shall bear his iniquity.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if there be [thought (by the offerer) to be] eaten of the flesh of the sacrifice of his peace-offerings on the third day [i.e., If, when it is sacrificed he thinks to eat it on the third day], it shall not be accepted. He that offers it shall not bethink himself [to eat it on the third day]. It shall be piggul ("rejected"), and the soul that eats of it [even within the permitted time] shall bear its sin.


###### Leviticus 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:19)


וְהַבָּשָׂ֞ר אֲשֶׁר־יִגַּ֤ע בְּכׇל־טָמֵא֙ לֹ֣א יֵֽאָכֵ֔ל בָּאֵ֖שׁ יִשָּׂרֵ֑ף וְהַ֨בָּשָׂ֔ר כׇּל־טָה֖וֹר יֹאכַ֥ל בָּשָֽׂר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The flesh [of offerings] that will touch any unclean [thing] may not be eaten; it shall be burned in fire. [As to] the [pure] flesh, every [ritually] pure person may eat [the] meat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the flesh that touches any unclean thing shall not be eaten; it shall be burnt with fire: and as for the flesh, all that are clean shall eat of it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the flesh [of consecrated peace-offerings] that shall touch any unclean thing shall not be eaten; it shall be burned with fire. And the flesh [(to include as permitted what remains inside (the permitted eating bounds) of a limb that has projected outside)] — everyone that is clean [and not only the owner] may eat the flesh.


###### Leviticus 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:20)


וְהַנֶּ֜פֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר־תֹּאכַ֣ל בָּשָׂ֗ר מִזֶּ֤בַח הַשְּׁלָמִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לַיהֹוָ֔ה וְטֻמְאָת֖וֹ עָלָ֑יו וְנִכְרְתָ֛ה הַנֶּ֥פֶשׁ הַהִ֖וא מֵעַמֶּֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The person who eats of the flesh of the peace-offering belonging to Adonoy while his uncleaness is yet on him, that persons soul shall be cut off from its people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the person that eats of the flesh of the sacrifice of peace offerings, that pertain to the Lord, having his uncleanness upon him, that soul shall be cut off from his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the soul that eats flesh of the sacrifice of the peace-offerings which is the L-rd's, and his uncleanliness is upon him [i.e., if he himself (as opposed to the offering) is unclean], that soul shall be cut off from its people [(but a clean person who eats an unclean offering does not incur kareth)].


###### Leviticus 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:21)


וְנֶ֜פֶשׁ כִּֽי־תִגַּ֣ע בְּכׇל־טָמֵ֗א בְּטֻמְאַ֤ת אָדָם֙ א֣וֹ**׀** בִּבְהֵמָ֣ה טְמֵאָ֗ה א֚וֹ בְּכׇל־שֶׁ֣קֶץ טָמֵ֔א וְאָכַ֛ל מִבְּשַׂר־זֶ֥בַח הַשְּׁלָמִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר לַיהֹוָ֑ה וְנִכְרְתָ֛ה הַנֶּ֥פֶשׁ הַהִ֖וא מֵעַמֶּֽיהָ׃{פ}
¹

footnotes:
¹ (אין פרשה בספרי ספרד ואשכנז)


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A person who touches anything unclean, be it the impurities of man or an unclean animal, or any unclean creeping creature and then eats some flesh of the peace-offering belonging to Adonoy, that persons soul shall be cut off from its people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover the person that shall touch any unclean thing, such as the uncleanness of man, of any unclean beast, or any abominable unclean thing, and eat of the flesh of the sacrifice of peace offerings, which pertain to the Lord, that soul shall be cut off from his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a soul touch any unclean thing, of the uncleanliness of a man or of an unclean beast, or of any unclean abomination, and he eat of the flesh of the sacrifice of the peace-offerings, which is the L-rd's, then that soul shall be cut off from its people.


###### Leviticus 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:22)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:23)


דַּבֵּ֛ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר כׇּל־חֵ֜לֶב שׁ֥וֹר וְכֶ֛שֶׂב וָעֵ֖ז לֹ֥א תֹאכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying: any fat of oxen, sheep or goats you must not eat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, saying, You shall eat no manner of fat, of ox, or of sheep, or of goat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel, saying: All fat (cheilev) of ox or sheep or goat you shall not eat.


###### Leviticus 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:24)


וְחֵ֤לֶב נְבֵלָה֙ וְחֵ֣לֶב טְרֵפָ֔ה יֵעָשֶׂ֖ה לְכׇל־מְלָאכָ֑ה וְאָכֹ֖ל לֹ֥א תֹאכְלֻֽהוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The fat of an improperly slaughtered animal and the fat of a fatally wounded animal may be used for any purpose, but you are surely not permitted to eat it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the fat of the beast that dies of itself, and the fat of that which is torn with beasts, may be used in any other use: but you shall by no means eat of it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the fat of (a beast that is) neveilah (carrion) or the fat of treifah ("torn") may be used for all service [(The fat of neveilah does not render one unclean)], and you shall not eat it. [(The interdict of eating neveilah and treifah "takes" upon that of eating cheilev, so that the eater transgresses two negative commandments)].


###### Leviticus 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:25)


כִּ֚י כׇּל־אֹכֵ֣ל חֵ֔לֶב מִ֨ן־הַבְּהֵמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר יַקְרִ֥יב מִמֶּ֛נָּה אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַיהֹוָ֑ה וְנִכְרְתָ֛ה הַנֶּ֥פֶשׁ הָאֹכֶ֖לֶת מֵֽעַמֶּֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For, anyone who eats fat of an animal from which is offered a fire-offering to Adonoy, the soul who ate it shall be cut off from its people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For whoever eats the fat of the beast, of which men offer an offering made by fire to the Lord, the soul that eats it shall be cut off from his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For all who eat fat of the beast, of which one presents a fire-offering to the L-rd, the soul that eats shall be cut off from its people.


###### Leviticus 7:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:26)


וְכׇל־דָּם֙ לֹ֣א תֹאכְל֔וּ בְּכֹ֖ל מוֹשְׁבֹתֵיכֶ֑ם לָע֖וֹף וְלַבְּהֵמָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not eat any blood, in all your dwelling places, be it of fowl or of beast.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover you shall eat no manner of blood, whether it be of bird or of beast, in any of your dwellings.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all blood you shall not eat, in all of your habitations [(the interdict being a function of the body and not of the land)], of bird and of beast [(to exclude the blood of fish and locusts)].


###### Leviticus 7:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:27)


כׇּל־נֶ֖פֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר־תֹּאכַ֣ל כׇּל־דָּ֑ם וְנִכְרְתָ֛ה הַנֶּ֥פֶשׁ הַהִ֖וא מֵֽעַמֶּֽיהָ׃{פ}
¹

footnotes:
¹ (אין פרשה בספרי תימן)


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Any person who eats any blood, that soul shall be cut off from its people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whoever it be that eats any manner of blood, that soul shall be cut off from his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Any soul that eats any blood, that soul shall be cut off from its people.


###### Leviticus 7:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:28)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 7:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:29)


דַּבֵּ֛ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר הַמַּקְרִ֞יב אֶת־זֶ֤בַח שְׁלָמָיו֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה יָבִ֧יא אֶת־קׇרְבָּנ֛וֹ לַיהֹוָ֖ה מִזֶּ֥בַח שְׁלָמָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying: Whoever shall bring the sacrifice of his peace-offering to Adonoy, he shall bring his offering to Adonoy from his peace-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, saying, He that offers his peace offering to the Lord shall bring his offering to the Lord of the sacrifice of his peace offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel, saying: He who presents the sacrifice of his peace-offerings to the L-rd shall bring his offering to the L-rd from the sacrifice of his peace-offerings.


###### Leviticus 7:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:30)


יָדָ֣יו תְּבִיאֶ֔ינָה אֵ֖ת אִשֵּׁ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה אֶת־הַחֵ֤לֶב עַל־הֶֽחָזֶה֙ יְבִיאֶ֔נּוּ אֵ֣ת הֶחָזֶ֗ה לְהָנִ֥יף אֹת֛וֹ תְּנוּפָ֖ה לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
His hands shall bring the fire-offerings of Adonoy; he shall bring the fat on the breast and the breast [itself] to wave them as a wave-offering before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His own hands shall bring the offerings of the Lord made by fire, the fat with the breast, it shall he bring, that the breast may be waved for a wave offering before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His hands shall bring the fire-offerings of the L-rd, [the hand of the owner above, with the fat and breasts in it, and the hand of the Cohein below, and he (the Cohein) lifts them]. The fat on the breast shall he (a Cohein) bring it [from the slaughtering site, so that when it is placed in the hand of the Cohein who waves it, the breast is above and the fat below; and, after the waving, it is placed — breast below and fat above — in the hand of a third Cohein who smokes it] — the breast, to wave it as a wave-offering before the L-rd [i.e., the breast is not part of the fire-offering, but part of the wave-offering].


###### Leviticus 7:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:31)


וְהִקְטִ֧יר הַכֹּהֵ֛ן אֶת־הַחֵ֖לֶב הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חָה וְהָיָה֙ הֶֽחָזֶ֔ה לְאַהֲרֹ֖ן וּלְבָנָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall burn the fat on the altar and the breast shall belong to Aharon and to his sons.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall burn the fat upon the altar: but the breast shall be Aharon’s and his sons’.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall smoke the fat upon the altar, and [i.e., after which] the breast shall be for Aaron and for his sons.


###### Leviticus 7:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:32)


וְאֵת֙ שׁ֣וֹק הַיָּמִ֔ין תִּתְּנ֥וּ תְרוּמָ֖ה לַכֹּהֵ֑ן מִזִּבְחֵ֖י שַׁלְמֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The right thigh you shall give as a separated portion to the *kohein,* from your peace-offerings.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the right shoulder shall you give to the priest for a heave offering from the sacrifices of your peace offerings.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the right shok (from knee to thigh) shall you give as an offering to the Cohein from the sacrifices of your peace-offerings.


###### Leviticus 7:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:33)


הַמַּקְרִ֞יב אֶת־דַּ֧ם הַשְּׁלָמִ֛ים וְאֶת־הַחֵ֖לֶב מִבְּנֵ֣י אַהֲרֹ֑ן ל֧וֹ תִהְיֶ֛ה שׁ֥וֹק הַיָּמִ֖ין לְמָנָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever offers the blood of the peace-offering and the fat who is of the sons of Aharon, the right thigh shall belong to him as a portion.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He among the sons of Aharon, that offers the blood of the peace offering, and the fat, shall have the right shoulder for his part.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He who offers the blood of the peace-offerings and the fat, of the sons of Aaron [i.e., he who is fit to offer them (to exclude one who is unclean at the time of the sprinkling of the blood or the smoking of the fats)], to him shall be the right shok as a portion.


###### Leviticus 7:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:34)


כִּי֩ אֶת־חֲזֵ֨ה הַתְּנוּפָ֜ה וְאֵ֣ת**׀** שׁ֣וֹק הַתְּרוּמָ֗ה לָקַ֙חְתִּי֙ מֵאֵ֣ת בְּנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מִזִּבְחֵ֖י שַׁלְמֵיהֶ֑ם וָאֶתֵּ֣ן אֹ֠תָ֠ם לְאַהֲרֹ֨ן הַכֹּהֵ֤ן וּלְבָנָיו֙ לְחׇק־עוֹלָ֔ם מֵאֵ֖ת בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
