Because the breast wave-offering and the thigh [that is] the uplifted portion I have taken from Bnei Yisroel. from their peace-offerings and I have given them to Aharon the *kohein* and to his sons, as an everlasting statute, from Bnei Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the wave breast and the heave shoulder have I taken of the children of Yisra᾽el from off the sacrifices of their peace offerings, and have given them to Aharon the priest and to his sons their fixed portion for ever from among the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the breast of the waving [(forward and back)] and the thigh of the lifting [(up and down)] have I taken from the children of Israel from the sacrifices of their peace-offerings, and I have given them to Aaron the Cohein and to his sons as an everlasting statute from the children of Israel.


###### Leviticus 7:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:35)


זֹ֣את מִשְׁחַ֤ת אַהֲרֹן֙ וּמִשְׁחַ֣ת בָּנָ֔יו מֵאִשֵּׁ֖י יְהֹוָ֑ה בְּיוֹם֙ הִקְרִ֣יב אֹתָ֔ם לְכַהֵ֖ן לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is [for] the anointing of Aharon and [for] the anointing of his sons from the fire-offering of Adonoy, on the day He brought them to serve before Adonoy,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the portion of the anointing of Aharon, and of the anointing of his sons, out of the offerings of the Lord made by fire, on the day when he presented them to minister to the Lord in the priest’s office;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the (portion of the) anointment of Aaron and the anointment of his sons from the fire-offerings of the L-rd on the day He drew them near to minister to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 7:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:36)


אֲשֶׁר֩ צִוָּ֨ה יְהֹוָ֜ה לָתֵ֣ת לָהֶ֗ם בְּיוֹם֙ מׇשְׁח֣וֹ אֹתָ֔ם מֵאֵ֖ת בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל חֻקַּ֥ת עוֹלָ֖ם לְדֹרֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
that Adonoy commanded to give to them, on the day He anointed them, from Bnei Yisroel, an everlasting statute for all their descendants.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
which the Lord commanded to be given them of the children of Yisra᾽el, on the day that he anointed them, as a fixed portion for ever throughout their generations.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Which the L-rd commanded to give to them on the day He anointed them, from the children of Israel, a statute forever, throughout their generations.


###### Leviticus 7:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:37)


זֹ֣את הַתּוֹרָ֗ה לָֽעֹלָה֙ לַמִּנְחָ֔ה וְלַֽחַטָּ֖את וְלָאָשָׁ֑ם וְלַ֨מִּלּוּאִ֔ים וּלְזֶ֖בַח הַשְּׁלָמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the law for the burnt-offering, the meal-offering, the sin-offering and the guilt-offering; for the installation-sacrifices and for the peace-offering,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the Tora of the burnt offering, of the meal offering, and of the sin offering, and of the guilt offering, and of the consecration offering, and of the sacrifice of the peace offering;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the law of the burnt-offering, of the meal-offering, and of the sin-offering, and of the guilt-offering, and of the offering of investiture [of the priesthood], and of the sacrifice of the peace-offerings.


###### Leviticus 7:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/7:38)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר צִוָּ֧ה יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶת־מֹשֶׁ֖ה בְּהַ֣ר סִינָ֑י בְּי֨וֹם צַוֺּת֜וֹ אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל לְהַקְרִ֧יב אֶת־קׇרְבְּנֵיהֶ֛ם לַיהֹוָ֖ה בְּמִדְבַּ֥ר סִינָֽי׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
that Adonoy commanded Moshe on Mount Sinai, on the day that he commanded Bnei Yisroel to bring their offerings to Adonoy in the desert of Sinai.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
which the Lord commanded Moshe in Mount Sinay, on the day that he commanded the children of Yisra᾽el to present their offerings to the Lord, in the wilderness of Sinay.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Which the L-rd commanded Moses in Mount Sinai on the day that He commanded the children of Israel to present their offerings to the L-rd in the desert of Sinai.



## Chapter 8

###### Leviticus 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, to say:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:2)


קַ֤ח אֶֽת־אַהֲרֹן֙ וְאֶת־בָּנָ֣יו אִתּ֔וֹ וְאֵת֙ הַבְּגָדִ֔ים וְאֵ֖ת שֶׁ֣מֶן הַמִּשְׁחָ֑ה וְאֵ֣ת**׀** פַּ֣ר הַֽחַטָּ֗את וְאֵת֙ שְׁנֵ֣י הָֽאֵילִ֔ים וְאֵ֖ת סַ֥ל הַמַּצּֽוֹת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Take Aharon, along with his sons, the garments, the anointing oil, the bull for the sin-offering, the two rams and a basket of *matzos*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take Aharon and his sons with him, and the garments, and the anointing oil, and a bullock for the sin offering, and two rams, and a basket of unleavened bread;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[This section was stated seven days before the erection of the mishkan (there being no "before" and "after" in the Torah)] Take Aaron [("Take" him with words, predispose him)] and his sons with him, and the garments, and the oil of anointment, and the bullock of the sin-offering, and the two rams, and the basket of unleavened bread [(see Exodus 28), the instructions being reiterated as a prompting to zeal on the first day of the investiture].


###### Leviticus 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:3)


וְאֵ֥ת כׇּל־הָעֵדָ֖ה הַקְהֵ֑ל אֶל־פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Assemble the entire congrega-tion at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and gather all the congregation together to the door of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And assemble all of the congregation at the door of the tent of meeting [(this being one of the places where the "small" contained the "great")].


###### Leviticus 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:4)


וַיַּ֣עַשׂ מֹשֶׁ֔ה כַּֽאֲשֶׁ֛ר צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֹת֑וֹ וַתִּקָּהֵל֙ הָֽעֵדָ֔ה אֶל־פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe did that which Adonoy commanded him and the congregation was assembled at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe did as the Lord commanded him; and the assembly was gathered together at the door of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses did as the L-rd commanded him, and the congregation was assembled at the door of the tent of meeting.


###### Leviticus 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:5)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר מֹשֶׁ֖ה אֶל־הָעֵדָ֑ה זֶ֣ה הַדָּבָ֔ר אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה לַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe said to the congregation, This is the edict that Adonoy commanded to do.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe said to the congregation, This is the thing which the Lord commanded to be done.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses said to the congregation: This [what you will see me doing] is [not for my honor nor for my brother's, but it is] the thing which the L-rd has commanded to do.


###### Leviticus 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:6)


וַיַּקְרֵ֣ב מֹשֶׁ֔ה אֶֽת־אַהֲרֹ֖ן וְאֶת־בָּנָ֑יו וַיִּרְחַ֥ץ אֹתָ֖ם בַּמָּֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe brought Aharon and his sons near and bathed them in water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe brought Aharon and his sons, and washed them with water.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses drew near Aaron and his sons and he washed them with water.


###### Leviticus 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:7)


וַיִּתֵּ֨ן עָלָ֜יו אֶת־הַכֻּתֹּ֗נֶת וַיַּחְגֹּ֤ר אֹתוֹ֙ בָּֽאַבְנֵ֔ט וַיַּלְבֵּ֤שׁ אֹתוֹ֙ אֶֽת־הַמְּעִ֔יל וַיִּתֵּ֥ן עָלָ֖יו אֶת־הָאֵפֹ֑ד וַיַּחְגֹּ֣ר אֹת֗וֹ בְּחֵ֙שֶׁב֙ הָֽאֵפֹ֔ד וַיֶּאְפֹּ֥ד ל֖וֹ בּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He placed the tunic on him, girded him with the sash, dressed him in the robe, placed the *ephod* on him girded him with the *ephod*-sash and tightened it for him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he put upon him the coat, and girded him with the girdle, and clothed him with the robe, and put the efod upon him, and he girded him with the artistically wrought girdle of the efod, and with it he bound it to him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he put upon him [(see Exodus 29)] the kutoneth, and he girded him with the avneit, and he clothed him with the me'il, and he put upon him the ephod, and he girded him with the cheishev of the ephod, and he bound it to him therewith.

וַיָּ֥שֶׂם עָלָ֖יו אֶת־הַחֹ֑שֶׁן וַיִּתֵּן֙ אֶל־הַחֹ֔שֶׁן אֶת־הָאוּרִ֖ים וְאֶת־הַתֻּמִּֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He put the breastplate on him and he placed in the breastplate the *urim* and the *tumim.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Leviticus 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:9)


וַיָּ֥שֶׂם אֶת־הַמִּצְנֶ֖פֶת עַל־רֹאשׁ֑וֹ וַיָּ֨שֶׂם עַֽל־הַמִּצְנֶ֜פֶת אֶל־מ֣וּל פָּנָ֗יו אֵ֣ת צִ֤יץ הַזָּהָב֙ נֵ֣זֶר הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־מֹשֶֽׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He placed the turban on his head and he placed, on the front of the turban, the golden plate, the crown of holiness, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he put the mitre upon his head; also upon the mitre, upon its forepart, did he put the golden plate, the holy crown; as the Lord commanded Moshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he put the mitznefeth upon his head; and he set upon the mitznefeth, upon its forefront, the golden tzitz, the holy crown, as the L-rd had commanded Moses [(see Exodus 28:37)]


###### Leviticus 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:10)


וַיִּקַּ֤ח מֹשֶׁה֙ אֶת־שֶׁ֣מֶן הַמִּשְׁחָ֔ה וַיִּמְשַׁ֥ח אֶת־הַמִּשְׁכָּ֖ן וְאֶת־כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר־בּ֑וֹ וַיְקַדֵּ֖שׁ אֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe took the anointing-oil and anointed the Tabernacle and everything inside it and sanctified them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe took the anointing oil, and anointed the tabernacle and all that was in it, and sanctified them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses took the oil of anointment and he anointed the mishkan and all that was in it, and he sanctified them.


###### Leviticus 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:11)


וַיַּ֥ז מִמֶּ֛נּוּ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ שֶׁ֣בַע פְּעָמִ֑ים וַיִּמְשַׁ֨ח אֶת־הַמִּזְבֵּ֜חַ וְאֶת־כׇּל־כֵּלָ֗יו וְאֶת־הַכִּיֹּ֛ר וְאֶת־כַּנּ֖וֹ לְקַדְּשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He sprinkled some of it on the altar seven times; he anointed the altar and all its utensils, the washing basin and its base, to sanctify them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he sprinkled of it upon the altar seven times, and anointed the altar and all its vessels, both the laver and its pedestal, to sanctify them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses sprinkled of it upon the altar seven times; and he anointed the altar and all of its vessels, and the laver and its base, to sanctify them.


###### Leviticus 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:12)


וַיִּצֹק֙ מִשֶּׁ֣מֶן הַמִּשְׁחָ֔ה עַ֖ל רֹ֣אשׁ אַהֲרֹ֑ן וַיִּמְשַׁ֥ח אֹת֖וֹ לְקַדְּשֽׁוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He poured some of the anointing-oil on Aharons head and anointed him, to sanctify him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he poured of the anointing oil upon Aharon’s head, and anointed him, to sanctify him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he poured from the oil of anointment upon the head of Aaron, and he anointed him to sanctify him.


###### Leviticus 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:13)


וַיַּקְרֵ֨ב מֹשֶׁ֜ה אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י אַהֲרֹ֗ן וַיַּלְבִּשֵׁ֤ם כֻּתֳּנֹת֙ וַיַּחְגֹּ֤ר אֹתָם֙ אַבְנֵ֔ט וַיַּחֲבֹ֥שׁ לָהֶ֖ם מִגְבָּע֑וֹת כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־מֹשֶֽׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe brought near the sons of Aharon and dressed them in tunics, girded them with sashes, and tied high turbans for them [to wear], just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe brought Aharon’s sons, and put coats upon them, and girded them with girdles, and put turbans upon them; as the Lord commanded Moshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses drew near the sons of Aaron, and he clothed them with kutanoth, and he girded them with the avneit, and he bound migbaoth upon them, as the L-rd had commanded Moses.


###### Leviticus 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:14)


וַיַּגֵּ֕שׁ אֵ֖ת פַּ֣ר הַֽחַטָּ֑את וַיִּסְמֹ֨ךְ אַהֲרֹ֤ן וּבָנָיו֙ אֶת־יְדֵיהֶ֔ם עַל־רֹ֖אשׁ פַּ֥ר הַֽחַטָּֽאת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He brought near the sin-offering bullock and Aharon and his sons laid their hands on the head of the sin-offering bullock.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he brought the bullock for the sin offering: and Aharon and his sons laid their hands upon the head of the bullock for the sin offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he presented the bullock of the sin-offering, and Aaron and his sons placed their hands on the head of the bullock of the sin-offering.


###### Leviticus 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:15)


וַיִּשְׁחָ֗ט וַיִּקַּ֨ח מֹשֶׁ֤ה אֶת־הַדָּם֙ וַ֠יִּתֵּ֠ן עַל־קַרְנ֨וֹת הַמִּזְבֵּ֤חַ סָבִיב֙ בְּאֶצְבָּע֔וֹ וַיְחַטֵּ֖א אֶת־הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ וְאֶת־הַדָּ֗ם יָצַק֙ אֶל־יְס֣וֹד הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חַ וַֽיְקַדְּשֵׁ֖הוּ לְכַפֵּ֥ר עָלָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He slaughtered it; and Moshe took the blood and put it atop the corners of the altar all around with his finger and he purified the altar. He poured the blood into the base of the altar and sanctified it, to atone upon it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he slaughtered it; and Moshe took the blood, and put it upon the horns of the altar round about with his finger, and purified the altar, and poured the blood at the bottom of the altar, and sanctified it, to make atonement upon it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he slaughtered (it), and Moses took the blood, and he put it on the horns of the altar roundabout with his finger. And he purified the altar [(He cleaned and purified it from its state of profaneness to enter into that of holiness)], and the (remaining) blood he poured out at the base of the altar, and he sanctified it [by this service] to make atonement upon it [i.e., to effect upon it all the atonements thenceforward].

וַיִּקַּ֨ח מֹשֶׁ֜ה מִשֶּׁ֣מֶן הַמִּשְׁחָ֗ה וּמִן־הַדָּם֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֒חַ֒ וַיַּ֤ז עַֽל־אַהֲרֹן֙ עַל־בְּגָדָ֔יו וְעַל־בָּנָ֛יו וְעַל־בִּגְדֵ֥י בָנָ֖יו אִתּ֑וֹ וַיְקַדֵּ֤שׁ אֶֽת־אַהֲרֹן֙ אֶת־בְּגָדָ֔יו וְאֶת־בָּנָ֛יו וְאֶת־בִּגְדֵ֥י בָנָ֖יו אִתּֽוֹ׃

From the entrance of the Tent of Meeting you shall not go out for seven days until the time that your days of installation are complete, because for seven days your hands will be filled.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall not go out of the door of the Tent of Meeting for seven days, until the days of your consecration be at an end: for seven days shall he consecrate you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from the door of the tent of meeting you shall not go out seven days, until the day of the fulfillment of the days of your investiture; for seven days shall He invest your hands.


###### Leviticus 8:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:34)


כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָׂ֖ה בַּיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה צִוָּ֧ה יְהֹוָ֛ה לַעֲשֹׂ֖ת לְכַפֵּ֥ר עֲלֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Just as you have done on this day, Adonoy commanded him to do, to bring about atonement.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As he has done this day, so the Lord has commanded to do, to make atonement for you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As He has done this day, so has the L-rd commanded to do [all seven days] to make atonement for you.


###### Leviticus 8:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:35)


וּפֶ֩תַח֩ אֹ֨הֶל מוֹעֵ֜ד תֵּשְׁב֨וּ יוֹמָ֤ם וָלַ֙יְלָה֙ שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֔ים וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֛ם אֶת־מִשְׁמֶ֥רֶת יְהֹוָ֖ה וְלֹ֣א תָמ֑וּתוּ כִּי־כֵ֖ן צֻוֵּֽיתִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
At the entrance of the Tent of Meeting you shall sit day and night for seven days and keep the charge of Adonoy and you will not die, because I have been thus commanded.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall abide at the door of the Tent of Meeting day and night for seven days, and keep the charge of the Lord, that you die not: for so I am commanded.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And at the door of the tent of meeting shall you sit day and night seven days. And you shall keep the charge of the L-rd that you not die [(Failing to do so, you incur the penalty of death)]; for thus have I been commanded.


###### Leviticus 8:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/8:36)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ אַהֲרֹ֖ן וּבָנָ֑יו אֵ֚ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּיַד־מֹשֶֽׁה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon and his sons fulfilled all the edicts that Adonoy commanded through Moshe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So Aharon and his sons did all the things which the Lord had commanded by the hand of Moshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Aaron and his sons did all the things that the L-rd commanded by the hand of Moses, [not deviating to the right or to the left.]



## Chapter 9

###### Leviticus 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:1)


וַֽיְהִי֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֔י קָרָ֣א מֹשֶׁ֔ה לְאַהֲרֹ֖ן וּלְבָנָ֑יו וּלְזִקְנֵ֖י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And it was on the eight day [that] Moshe called to Aharon and to his sons and to the elders of Israel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass on the eighth day, that Moshe called Aharon and his sons, and the elders of Yisra᾽el:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was on the eighth day [of the investiture, the first of Nissan, the day of the erection of the mishkan] that Moses called to Aaron and to his sons and to the elders of Israel, [to impress upon them that Aaron and his sons entered into the high-priesthood by divine command and not of their own initiative].


###### Leviticus 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:2)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֗ן קַח־לְ֠ךָ֠ עֵ֣גֶל בֶּן־בָּקָ֧ר לְחַטָּ֛את וְאַ֥יִל לְעֹלָ֖ה תְּמִימִ֑ם וְהַקְרֵ֖ב לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He said to Aharon, Take, for yourself, a young calf, for a sin-offering and a ram—for a burnt-offering—unblemished ones, and bring them before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he said to Aharon, Take thee a young calf for a sin offering, and a ram for a burnt offering, without blemish, and offer them before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to Aaron: Take for yourself a bull-calf for a sin-offering, [Aaron being apprised that this calf was his atonement for the golden calf which he had made], and a ram for a burnt-offering, without blemish, and offer them before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:3)


וְאֶל־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל תְּדַבֵּ֣ר לֵאמֹ֑ר קְח֤וּ שְׂעִיר־עִזִּים֙ לְחַטָּ֔את וְעֵ֨גֶל וָכֶ֧בֶשׂ בְּנֵי־שָׁנָ֛ה תְּמִימִ֖ם לְעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
To Bnei Yisroel you shall speak, saying, take a he-goat, for a sin-offering and a calf and a lamb, both one year old—unblemished—for a burnt-offering,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And to the children of Yisra᾽el thou shalt speak, saying, Take a kid of the goats for a sin offering; and a calf and a lamb, both of the first year, without blemish, for a burnt offering;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to the children of Israel shall you speak, saying: Take a he-goat for a sin-offering and a calf and a lamb of the first year, without blemish, for a burnt-offering.


###### Leviticus 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:4)


וְשׁ֨וֹר וָאַ֜יִל לִשְׁלָמִ֗ים לִזְבֹּ֙חַ֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וּמִנְחָ֖ה בְּלוּלָ֣ה בַשָּׁ֑מֶן כִּ֣י הַיּ֔וֹם יְהֹוָ֖ה נִרְאָ֥ה אֲלֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
an ox and a ram for a peace-offering, to sacrifice before Adonoy and a meal-offering mixed with oil, for today, Adonoy will appear to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
also a bullock and a ram for peace offerings, to sacrifice before the Lord; and a meal offering mingled with oil: for today the Lord will appear to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And an ox and a ram for peace-offerings to sacrifice before the L-rd, and a meal-offering mixed with oil; for today the L-rd will appear to you [to repose His shechinah in the work of your hands (for which reason these offerings are obligatory on this day)].


###### Leviticus 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:5)


וַיִּקְח֗וּ אֵ֚ת אֲשֶׁ֣ר צִוָּ֣ה מֹשֶׁ֔ה אֶל־פְּנֵ֖י אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד וַֽיִּקְרְבוּ֙ כׇּל־הָ֣עֵדָ֔ה וַיַּֽעַמְד֖וּ לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They took that which Moshe commanded, before the Tent of Meeting; and the entire congregation approached and they stood before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they brought that which Moshe commanded before the Tent of Meeting: and all the congregation drew near and stood before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they brought what Moses had commanded in front of the tent of meeting, and all the congregation drew near and stood before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:6)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר מֹשֶׁ֔ה זֶ֧ה הַדָּבָ֛ר אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה תַּעֲשׂ֑וּ וְיֵרָ֥א אֲלֵיכֶ֖ם כְּב֥וֹד יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe said: This is what Adonoy commanded you to do: and the glory of Adonoy will appear to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe said, This is the thing which the Lord commanded you to do: and the glory of the Lord shall appear to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses said: This is the thing which the L-rd commanded that you should do so that there appear to you the glory of the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:7)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר מֹשֶׁ֜ה אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֗ן קְרַ֤ב אֶל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֙חַ֙ וַעֲשֵׂ֞ה אֶת־חַטָּֽאתְךָ֙ וְאֶת־עֹ֣לָתֶ֔ךָ וְכַפֵּ֥ר בַּֽעַדְךָ֖ וּבְעַ֣ד הָעָ֑ם וַעֲשֵׂ֞ה אֶת־קׇרְבַּ֤ן הָעָם֙ וְכַפֵּ֣ר בַּֽעֲדָ֔ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe said to Aharon: Approach the altar and perform [the services of] your sin-offering and your burnt-offering and atone on your [own] behalf and on behalf of the people; and perform [the service of] the peoples offering and atone on their behalf, just as Adonoy commanded.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe said to Aharon, Go to the altar, and offer thy sin offering, and thy burnt offering, and make atonement for thyself, and for the people: and offer the offering of the people, and make atonement for them; as the Lord commanded.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses said to Aaron: Draw near to the altar [(Aaron was ashamed and afraid to draw near, whereupon Moses said to him: Why are you ashamed? For this were you chosen!)], and offer your sin-offering [the bull-calf] and your burnt-offering [the ram], and make atonement for yourself and for the people; and offer the offering of the people [the he-goat, the calf, and the lamb], and make atonement for them, as the L-rd has commanded.


###### Leviticus 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:8)


וַיִּקְרַ֥ב אַהֲרֹ֖ן אֶל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ וַיִּשְׁחַ֛ט אֶת־עֵ֥גֶל הַחַטָּ֖את אֲשֶׁר־לֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon approached the altar and slaughtered the calf that he had for a sin-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Aharon therefore went to the altar, and slaughtered the calf of the sin offering, which was for himself.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Aaron drew near to the altar and slaughtered the calf of the sin-offering which was his.


###### Leviticus 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:9)


וַ֠יַּקְרִ֠בוּ בְּנֵ֨י אַהֲרֹ֣ן אֶת־הַדָּם֮ אֵלָיו֒ וַיִּטְבֹּ֤ל אֶצְבָּעוֹ֙ בַּדָּ֔ם וַיִּתֵּ֖ן עַל־קַרְנ֣וֹת הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ וְאֶת־הַדָּ֣ם יָצַ֔ק אֶל־יְס֖וֹד הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharons sons brought the blood to him and he dipped his finger into the blood and put it atop the corners of the altar and he poured the [remaining] blood into the base of the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the sons of Aharon brought the blood to him: and he dipped his finger in the blood, and put it upon the horns of the altar, and poured out the blood at the bottom of the altar:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Aaron presented the blood to him, and he dipped his finger in the blood, and he put it on the horns of the altar; and he poured out the blood at the foundation of the altar.


###### Leviticus 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:10)


וְאֶת־הַחֵ֨לֶב וְאֶת־הַכְּלָיֹ֜ת וְאֶת־הַיֹּתֶ֤רֶת מִן־הַכָּבֵד֙ מִן־הַ֣חַטָּ֔את הִקְטִ֖יר הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חָה כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־מֹשֶֽׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The fat, kidneys and the lobe of the liver from the sin-offering, he burned on the altar, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but the fat, and the kidneys, and the appendage above the liver of the sin offering, he burnt upon the altar; as the Lord commanded Moshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the fat and the kidneys and the lobe with [some of] of the liver of the sin-offering he caused to smoke upon the altar, as the L-rd had commanded Moses.


###### Leviticus 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:11)


וְאֶת־הַבָּשָׂ֖ר וְאֶת־הָע֑וֹר שָׂרַ֣ף בָּאֵ֔שׁ מִח֖וּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The flesh and the skin, he burned in a fire beyond the encampment.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the flesh and the hide he burnt with fire outside the camp.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the flesh and the skin he burned with fire outside the camp.


###### Leviticus 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:12)


וַיִּשְׁחַ֖ט אֶת־הָעֹלָ֑ה וַ֠יַּמְצִ֠אוּ בְּנֵ֨י אַהֲרֹ֤ן אֵלָיו֙ אֶת־הַדָּ֔ם וַיִּזְרְקֵ֥הוּ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ סָבִֽיב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He slaughtered the burnt-offering; and Aharons sons presented the blood to him, and he sprinkled it all around the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he slaughtered the burnt offering; and Aharon’s sons presented to him the blood, which he sprinkled round about upon the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he slaughtered the burnt-offering, and Aaron's sons presented the blood to him, and he dashed it against the altar roundabout.


###### Leviticus 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:13)


וְאֶת־הָעֹלָ֗ה הִמְצִ֧יאוּ אֵלָ֛יו לִנְתָחֶ֖יהָ וְאֶת־הָרֹ֑אשׁ וַיַּקְטֵ֖ר עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They presented the burnt-offering to him with its limbs severed and [with] the head, and he burned them on the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they presented the burnt offering to him, with its pieces, and the head: and he burnt them upon the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the burnt-offering they presented to him with its pieces and the head, and he smoked them upon the altar.


###### Leviticus 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:14)


וַיִּרְחַ֥ץ אֶת־הַקֶּ֖רֶב וְאֶת־הַכְּרָעָ֑יִם וַיַּקְטֵ֥ר עַל־הָעֹלָ֖ה הַמִּזְבֵּֽחָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He washed the innards and the feet and burned them on the burnt-offering, on the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he did wash the inwards and the legs, and burnt them upon the burnt offering on the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he washed the innards and the legs, and he smoked them upon the burnt-offering on the altar.


###### Leviticus 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:15)


וַיַּקְרֵ֕ב אֵ֖ת קׇרְבַּ֣ן הָעָ֑ם וַיִּקַּ֞ח אֶת־שְׂעִ֤יר הַֽחַטָּאת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לָעָ֔ם וַיִּשְׁחָטֵ֥הוּ וַֽיְחַטְּאֵ֖הוּ כָּרִאשֽׁוֹן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He brought the peoples offering; he took the peoples sin-offering-goat, and he slaughtered it and offered it as a sin-offering, in the same manner as the first one.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he brought the people’s offering, and took the goat which was the sin offering for the people, and slaughtered it, and offered it for sin, as the first.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he presented the offering of the people, and he took the goat of the sin-offering which was the people's, and he slaughtered it, and he offered it as a sin-offering, like the first [i.e., like his calf].


###### Leviticus 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:16)


וַיַּקְרֵ֖ב אֶת־הָעֹלָ֑ה וַֽיַּעֲשֶׂ֖הָ כַּמִּשְׁפָּֽט׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He brought the burnt-offering and he did it according to the rule.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he brought the burnt offering, and offered it according to the prescribed manner.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he presented the burnt-offering, and he offered it according to the ordinance [see Chapter 1].


###### Leviticus 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:17)


וַיַּקְרֵב֮ אֶת־הַמִּנְחָה֒ וַיְמַלֵּ֤א כַפּוֹ֙ מִמֶּ֔נָּה וַיַּקְטֵ֖ר עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ מִלְּבַ֖ד עֹלַ֥ת הַבֹּֽקֶר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He brought the meal-offering; he filled his palm from it and burned it on the altar: aside from the burnt-offering [that was brought] in the morning.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he brought the meal offering, and took a handful of it, and burnt it upon the altar, beside the burnt sacrifice of the morning.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he presented the meal-offering and he filled his hand from it [viz., the kemitzah], and he smoked it upon the altar, aside from the burnt-offering of the morning. [He offered all of these after the continual burnt-offering.]


###### Leviticus 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:18)


וַיִּשְׁחַ֤ט אֶת־הַשּׁוֹר֙ וְאֶת־הָאַ֔יִל זֶ֥בַח הַשְּׁלָמִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר לָעָ֑ם וַ֠יַּמְצִ֠אוּ בְּנֵ֨י אַהֲרֹ֤ן אֶת־הַדָּם֙ אֵלָ֔יו וַיִּזְרְקֵ֥הוּ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ סָבִֽיב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He slaughtered the ox and the ram, the peace-offerings of the people; and Aharons sons presented the blood to him and he sprinkled it all around the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He also slaughtered the bullock and the ram for a sacrifice of peace offering, which was for the people: and Aharon’s sons presented to him the blood, which he sprinkled on the altar round about.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he slaughtered the ox and the ram, the sacrifice of the peace-offerings which was the people's, and Aaron's sons presented the blood to him, and he dashed it against the altar roundabout.


###### Leviticus 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:19)


וְאֶת־הַחֲלָבִ֖ים מִן־הַשּׁ֑וֹר וּמִ֨ן־הָאַ֔יִל הָֽאַלְיָ֤ה וְהַֽמְכַסֶּה֙ וְהַכְּלָיֹ֔ת וְיֹתֶ֖רֶת הַכָּבֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The fats of the ox and of the ram; the fat-tail, the [fat] covering [the innards], the kidneys and the lobe of the liver.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the fat of the bullock and of the ram, the fat tail, and that which covers the inwards, and the kidneys, and the appendage of the liver:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the fats of the ox and of the ram, the fat-tail, and [the fat] that covered [the innards], and the kidneys, and the lobe with [some of] the liver.


###### Leviticus 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:20)


וַיָּשִׂ֥ימוּ אֶת־הַחֲלָבִ֖ים עַל־הֶחָז֑וֹת וַיַּקְטֵ֥ר הַחֲלָבִ֖ים הַמִּזְבֵּֽחָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They put the fats on top of the breasts and he burned the fats on the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and they put the fat upon the breasts, and he burnt the fat upon the altar:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they put the fats on the breasts [(After the waving, the Cohein who had waved them (with the breast above and the fat below) put them into the hand of the Cohein who was to smoke them, breast below and fat above)], and he smoked the fats on the altar.


###### Leviticus 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:21)


וְאֵ֣ת הֶחָז֗וֹת וְאֵת֙ שׁ֣וֹק הַיָּמִ֔ין הֵנִ֧יף אַהֲרֹ֛ן תְּנוּפָ֖ה לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר צִוָּ֥ה מֹשֶֽׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The breasts and the right thigh, Aharon waved [as] a wave-offering before Adonoy, just as Moshe commanded.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the breasts and the right shoulder Aharon waved for a wave offering before the Lord; as Moshe commanded.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the breasts and the right thigh Aaron waved as a wave-offering before the L-rd, as the L-rd had commanded Moses.


###### Leviticus 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:22)


וַיִּשָּׂ֨א אַהֲרֹ֧ן אֶת־יָדָ֛ו אֶל־הָעָ֖ם וַֽיְבָרְכֵ֑ם וַיֵּ֗רֶד מֵעֲשֹׂ֧ת הַֽחַטָּ֛את וְהָעֹלָ֖ה וְהַשְּׁלָמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon raised his hands toward the people and he blessed them. He descended [from the altar where he] had offered the sin-offering, the burnt-offering and the peace-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aharon lifted up his hand towards the people, and blessed them, and came down from offering the sin offering, and the burnt offering, and the peace offerings.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Aaron lifted his hands to the people and he blessed them [the priestly blessing], and he came down from offering the sin-offering and the burnt-offering and the peace-offerings.


###### Leviticus 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:23)


וַיָּבֹ֨א מֹשֶׁ֤ה וְאַהֲרֹן֙ אֶל־אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֔ד וַיֵּ֣צְא֔וּ וַֽיְבָרְכ֖וּ אֶת־הָעָ֑ם וַיֵּרָ֥א כְבוֹד־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־כׇּל־הָעָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Moshe and Aharon went [in]to the Tent of Meeting and [then] they went outside and blessed the people, and the glory of Adonoy appeared to the entire people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe and Aharon went into the Tent of Meeting, and came out, and blessed the people: and the glory of the Lord appeared to all the people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses and Aaron went into the tent of meeting [for Moses to teach Aaron the service of the incense (which is offered there)], and they went out and blessed the people. [They said (Psalms 90:17): "And let the pleasantness of the L-rd be upon us." Up to that point the Shechinah had not rested in the mishkan. Seeing their distress over this, Moses had said to them (9:6): "This (the sacrificial service that follows) is the thing that you should do so that there appear to you the glory of the L-rd"], and, [Aaron having performed that service], the glory of the L-rd appeared to all the people.


###### Leviticus 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/9:24)


וַתֵּ֤צֵא אֵשׁ֙ מִלִּפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וַתֹּ֙אכַל֙ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חַ אֶת־הָעֹלָ֖ה וְאֶת־הַחֲלָבִ֑ים וַיַּ֤רְא כׇּל־הָעָם֙ וַיָּרֹ֔נּוּ וַֽיִּפְּל֖וּ עַל־פְּנֵיהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A fire came forth from before Adonoy and consumed what was on the altar; the burnt-offering and the fats. All the people saw and they raised their voices in praise, and they fell on their faces.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there came a fire out from before the Lord, and consumed upon the altar the burnt offering and the fat: which, when all the people saw, they shouted, and fell on their faces.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a fire came forth from before the L-rd, and it consumed upon the altar the burnt-offering and the fats. And all the people saw and they exalted (the L-rd), and they fell upon their faces.



## Chapter 10

###### Leviticus 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:1)


וַיִּקְח֣וּ בְנֵֽי־אַ֠הֲרֹ֠ן נָדָ֨ב וַאֲבִיה֜וּא אִ֣ישׁ מַחְתָּת֗וֹ וַיִּתְּנ֤וּ בָהֵן֙ אֵ֔שׁ וַיָּשִׂ֥ימוּ עָלֶ֖יהָ קְטֹ֑רֶת וַיַּקְרִ֜יבוּ לִפְנֵ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ אֵ֣שׁ זָרָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹ֦א צִוָּ֖ה אֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Nodov and Avihu, Aharons sons, took, each of them his fire-pan, placed fire on it and then placed incense upon it and they brought before Adonoy a strange fire, which He had not commanded them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Nadav and Avihu, the sons of Aharon, took each of them his censer, and put fire in it, and put incense on it, and offered strange fire before the Lord, which He commanded them not.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Aaron, Nadav and Avihu, took, each of them his coal-pan, and they put fire therein, and they laid incense thereon; and they offered before the L-rd a strange fire, which He had not commanded them.


###### Leviticus 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:2)


וַתֵּ֥צֵא אֵ֛שׁ מִלִּפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה וַתֹּ֣אכַל אוֹתָ֑ם וַיָּמֻ֖תוּ לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A fire came forth from before Adonoy and consumed them, and they died in the presence of Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And a fire went out from the Lord, and devoured them, and they died before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a fire came out from before the L-rd and it consumed them, and they died before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:3)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר מֹשֶׁ֜ה אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֗ן הוּא֩ אֲשֶׁר־דִּבֶּ֨ר יְהֹוָ֤ה׀לֵאמֹר֙ בִּקְרֹבַ֣י אֶקָּדֵ֔שׁ וְעַל־פְּנֵ֥י כׇל־הָעָ֖ם אֶכָּבֵ֑ד וַיִּדֹּ֖ם אַהֲרֹֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe said to Aharon It is as Adonoy spoke, saying: through those that are near me I shall be sanctified, and in the presence of the entire people I will be glorified, and Aharon was silent.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Moshe said to Aharon, This is it that which the Lord spoke, saying, I will be sanctified in them that come near me, and before all the people I will be glorified. And Aharon held his peace.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses said to Aaron: This is what the L-rd spoke [(Exodus 29:43): "And I shall be appointed there (in the tent of meeting) for the children of Israel, venikdash bichvodi" — Read it "bimechubadai" (i.e., "and it will be sanctified by My honored ones")], saying: "With My near ones will I be sanctified, and before all the people will I be glorified." And Aaron kept his peace.


###### Leviticus 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:4)


וַיִּקְרָ֣א מֹשֶׁ֗ה אֶל־מִֽישָׁאֵל֙ וְאֶ֣ל אֶלְצָפָ֔ן בְּנֵ֥י עֻזִּיאֵ֖ל דֹּ֣ד אַהֲרֹ֑ן וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם קִ֞֠רְב֞֠וּ שְׂא֤וּ אֶת־אֲחֵיכֶם֙ מֵאֵ֣ת פְּנֵי־הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ אֶל־מִח֖וּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe called to Mishael and to Elzafan the sons of Uziel, Aharon’s uncle, and said to them; Come closer and carry your brothers from the presence of the Sanctuary, to beyond the encampment.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe called Misha᾽el and Elżafan, the sons of ῾Uzzi᾽el the uncle of Aharon, and said to them, Come near, carry your brethren from before the sanctuary out of the camp.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses called to Mishael and to Eltzafan, the sons of Uziel, the uncle of Aaron [Uziel was Amram's brother (6:18)]. And he said to them: Draw near; carry your brothers from before the sanctuary outside of the camp [(as one would say: Remove the body from before the bride so as not to spoil the occasion.)]


###### Leviticus 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:5)


וַֽיִּקְרְב֗וּ וַיִּשָּׂאֻם֙ בְּכֻתֳּנֹתָ֔ם אֶל־מִח֖וּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶ֑ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּ֥ר מֹשֶֽׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They came closer and they carried them in their tunics to beyond the encampment, just as Moshe spoke.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So they went near, and carried them in their coats out of the camp; as Moshe had said.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they drew near, and they carried them in their [the dead ones'] tunics [(Their garments were not burned. Two "threads" of fire entered their nostrils and killed them)] outside the camp, as Moses had spoken.


###### Leviticus 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:6)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר מֹשֶׁ֣ה אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֡ן וּלְאֶלְעָזָר֩ וּלְאִֽיתָמָ֨ר׀בָּנָ֜יו רָֽאשֵׁיכֶ֥ם אַל־תִּפְרָ֣עוּ**׀** וּבִגְדֵיכֶ֤ם לֹֽא־תִפְרֹ֙מוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א תָמֻ֔תוּ וְעַ֥ל כׇּל־הָעֵדָ֖ה יִקְצֹ֑ף וַאֲחֵיכֶם֙ כׇּל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל יִבְכּוּ֙ אֶת־הַשְּׂרֵפָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר שָׂרַ֥ף יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe said to Aharon and to Elozor and Isomor, his sons: Do not let your hair grow long and do not tear your garments so that you will not die and bring [Divine] anger on the entire congregation; your brothers, the entire House of Yisroel shall weep over the conflagration that Adonoy set ablaze.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe said to Aharon, and to El῾azar and to Itamar, his sons, Let the hair of your heads not grow long, neither rend your clothes; lest you die, and lest anger come upon all the people: but let your brethren, the whole house of Yisra᾽el bewail the burning which the Lord has kindled.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses said to Aaron and to Elazar and to Ithamar, his sons: Your hair do not grow long [(whence it is derived that a mourner may not cut his hair.) But you, do not "dampen the joy" of the Holy One Blessed be He], and your garments do not rend, that you not die and He be wroth with the entire congregation. And let your brethren, the entire house of Israel, mourn the burning that the L-rd has burned [(whence it is derived that it is incumbent upon all to mourn the suffering of sages.)]


###### Leviticus 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:7)


וּמִפֶּ֩תַח֩ אֹ֨הֶל מוֹעֵ֜ד לֹ֤א תֵֽצְאוּ֙ פֶּן־תָּמֻ֔תוּ כִּי־שֶׁ֛מֶן מִשְׁחַ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה עֲלֵיכֶ֑ם וַֽיַּעֲשׂ֖וּ כִּדְבַ֥ר מֹשֶֽׁה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
From the entrance of the Tent of Meeting you shall not go forth lest you die; for the anointing oil of Adonoy is on you and they did as Moshe had said.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall not go out from the door of the Tent of Meeting lest you die: for the anointing oil of the Lord is upon you. And they did according to the word of Moshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from the door of the tent of meeting do not go out, lest you die; for the anointing oil of the L-rd is upon you. And they did according to the word of Moses.


###### Leviticus 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:8)


וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֖ן לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Aharon, saying,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Aharon, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Aaron, saying:


###### Leviticus 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:9)


יַ֣יִן וְשֵׁכָ֞ר אַל־תֵּ֣שְׁתְּ**׀** אַתָּ֣ה**׀** וּבָנֶ֣יךָ אִתָּ֗ךְ בְּבֹאֲכֶ֛ם אֶל־אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵ֖ד וְלֹ֣א תָמֻ֑תוּ חֻקַּ֥ת עוֹלָ֖ם לְדֹרֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not drink wine or any other intoxicant, you and your sons with you, when you enter the Tent of Meeting and you will not die; this is an everlasting statute throughout your generations.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not drink wine or strong drink, thou, nor thy sons with thee, when you enter the Tent of Meeting, lest you die: it shall be a statute for ever throughout your generations:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Wine or strong drink do not drink, you and your sons with you, when you come to the tent of meeting, so that you not die, an everlasting statute throughout your generations.


###### Leviticus 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:10)


וּֽלְהַבְדִּ֔יל בֵּ֥ין הַקֹּ֖דֶשׁ וּבֵ֣ין הַחֹ֑ל וּבֵ֥ין הַטָּמֵ֖א וּבֵ֥ין הַטָּהֽוֹר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[So that you may] distinguish between what is sacred and what is mundane, between what is impure and what is pure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that you may differentiate between holy and unholy, and between unclean and clean;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that you distinguish between the holy and the profane [(If a Cohein ministers while inebriated, his service is invalid)], and between the unclean and the clean.


###### Leviticus 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:11)


וּלְהוֹרֹ֖ת אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אֵ֚ת כׇּל־הַ֣חֻקִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבֶּ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם בְּיַד־מֹשֶֽׁה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[And that you may] teach Bnei Yisroel all the statutes that Adonoy spoke to them, through Moshe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that you may teach the children of Yisra᾽el all the statutes which the Lord has spoken to them by the hand of Moshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to teach the children of Israel all the statutes that the L-rd spoke to them by the hand of Moses.


###### Leviticus 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:12)


וַיְדַבֵּ֨ר מֹשֶׁ֜ה אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֗ן וְאֶ֣ל אֶ֠לְעָזָ֠ר וְאֶל־אִ֨יתָמָ֥ר׀בָּנָיו֮ הַנּֽוֹתָרִים֒ קְח֣וּ אֶת־הַמִּנְחָ֗ה הַנּוֹתֶ֙רֶת֙ מֵאִשֵּׁ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וְאִכְל֥וּהָ מַצּ֖וֹת אֵ֣צֶל הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ כִּ֛י קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁ֖ים הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe spoke to Aharon and to Elozor and Isomor, his surviving sons; Take the meal-offering which remains of the fire-offering of Adonoy and eat it [as] *matzos* [unleavened] beside the altar, for it is holy of holies [=most holy].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe spoke to Aharon, and to El῾azar and to Itamar, his sons that were left, Take the meal offering that remains of the offerings of the Lord made by fire, and eat it without leaven beside the altar: for it is most holy:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses spoke to Aaron and to Elazar and to Ithamar, his remaining sons [("remaining" from death. They, too, were to have died because of the sin of the golden calf, but Moses' prayer (Deuteronomy 9:20) annulled half the decree)]: Take the meal-offering remaining from the fire-offerings of the L-rd [the meal-offering of the eighth day and the meal-offering of Nachshon] and eat it unleavened beside the altar, for it is holy of holies.


###### Leviticus 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:13)


וַאֲכַלְתֶּ֤ם אֹתָהּ֙ בְּמָק֣וֹם קָד֔וֹשׁ כִּ֣י חׇקְךָ֤ וְחׇק־בָּנֶ֙יךָ֙ הִ֔וא מֵאִשֵּׁ֖י יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּי־כֵ֖ן צֻוֵּֽיתִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall eat it in a sacred place, for it is your portion and the portion of your sons of the fire-offerings of Adonoy, for so have I been commanded.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and you shall eat it in the holy place, because it is thy due, and thy sons’ due, of the sacrifices of the Lord made by fire: for so I am commanded.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall eat it in a holy place, for it is your portion and the portion of your sons from the fire-offerings of the L-rd [(Daughters have no portion in kodshim (consecrated foods)]; for thus have I been commanded [that they shall eat it in a state of aninuth (before the burial of their kin, even though this is normally forbidden)].


###### Leviticus 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:14)


וְאֵת֩ חֲזֵ֨ה הַתְּנוּפָ֜ה וְאֵ֣ת**׀** שׁ֣וֹק הַתְּרוּמָ֗ה תֹּֽאכְלוּ֙ בְּמָק֣וֹם טָה֔וֹר אַתָּ֕ה וּבָנֶ֥יךָ וּבְנֹתֶ֖יךָ אִתָּ֑ךְ כִּֽי־חׇקְךָ֤ וְחׇק־בָּנֶ֙יךָ֙ נִתְּנ֔וּ מִזִּבְחֵ֥י שַׁלְמֵ֖י בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The breast of the wave-offering and the thigh that is the separated portion you shall eat in a pure (undefiled) place, you and your sons and daughters with you, for as your portion and as the portion of your sons they are given from the sacrifices of the peace-offerings of Bnei Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the wave breast and heave shoulder shall you eat in a clean place; thou, and thy sons, and thy daughters with thee: for they are thy due, and thy sons’ due, which are given out of the sacrifices of peace offerings of the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the breast of the waving and the shok of the lifting shall you eat in a clean place [(These lower order offerings, as opposed to the preceding holy of holies, need not be eaten in a holy place (within the court), but it suffices that they be eaten in a place that is clean (of lepers, i.e., within the camp of Israel)], you and your sons [(having a portion)] and your daughters with you [(the daughters not having a portion, but permitted to eat it as a gift)]. For as your portion and the portion of your sons are they given from the sacrifices of the peace-offerings of the children of Israel.


###### Leviticus 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:15)


שׁ֣וֹק הַתְּרוּמָ֞ה וַחֲזֵ֣ה הַתְּנוּפָ֗ה עַ֣ל אִשֵּׁ֤י הַחֲלָבִים֙ יָבִ֔יאוּ לְהָנִ֥יף תְּנוּפָ֖ה לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְהָיָ֨ה לְךָ֜ וּלְבָנֶ֤יךָ אִתְּךָ֙ לְחׇק־עוֹלָ֔ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The thigh that is the separated portion and the breast that is the wave-offering, on top of the fats of the fire offering, they shall bring, to wave as a wave-offering before Adonoy. It will be for you and for your sons with you an everlasting statute, just as Adonoy commanded.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The heave shoulder and the wave breast shall they bring with the offerings made by fire of the fat, to wave it for a wave offering before the Lord; and it shall be thine, and thy sons’ with thee, as a portion for ever; as the Lord commanded.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The shok of the lifting and the breast of the waving, upon the fire-offerings of the fats shall they bring [(the fats being below at the time of waving)], to wave a wave-offering before the L-rd. And it shall be for you and for your sons with you as a statute forever, as the L-rd has commanded.


###### Leviticus 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:16)


וְאֵ֣ת**׀** שְׂעִ֣יר הַֽחַטָּ֗את דָּרֹ֥שׁ דָּרַ֛שׁ מֹשֶׁ֖ה וְהִנֵּ֣ה שֹׂרָ֑ף וַ֠יִּקְצֹ֠ף עַל־אֶלְעָזָ֤ר וְעַל־אִֽיתָמָר֙ בְּנֵ֣י אַהֲרֹ֔ן הַנּוֹתָרִ֖ם לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Regarding the goat of the sin-offering; Moshe made a detailed inquiry and behold! It was burned. He was angry with Elozor and Isomor, the remaining sons of Aharon, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe diligently sought the goat of the sin offering, and, behold, it was burnt: and he was angry with El῾azar and Itamar, the sons of Aharon that were left alive, saying.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for the goat of the sin-offering [the goat of the additional offerings of the New Moon] Moses inquired, inquired, and, behold, it was burned [(two inquiries: Why has this goat been burned, and why have the other goats (the he-goat [9:3] and that of Nachshon [Numbers 7:16] been eaten?)] And he was wroth with Elazar and Ithamar the remaining sons of Aaron [(Out of deference to Aaron he turned his face to the sons in anger)] to say [(He said to them: Answer my words)]:


###### Leviticus 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:17)


מַדּ֗וּעַ לֹֽא־אֲכַלְתֶּ֤ם אֶת־הַחַטָּאת֙ בִּמְק֣וֹם הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ כִּ֛י קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁ֖ים הִ֑וא וְאֹתָ֣הּ**׀** נָתַ֣ן לָכֶ֗ם לָשֵׂאת֙ אֶת־עֲוֺ֣ן הָעֵדָ֔ה לְכַפֵּ֥ר עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Why did you not eat the sin-offering in a sacred place, for it is holy of holies [=most holy], and He gave it to you, to bear the iniquity of the congregation, to atone for them before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why have you not eaten the sin offering in the holy place, seeing it is most holy, and God has given it you to bear the iniquity of the congregation, to make atonement for them before the Lord?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why did you not eat the sin-offering in the holy place? [Is it possible that it was taken outside of the (court) curtains and (thereby) became pasul] because it is holy of holies? [They answered: No. — whereupon he said: Why, then, did you not eat it] when He has given it to you to forgive the sin of the congregation, to make atonement for them before the L-rd? ["the Cohanim eating and the offerers receiving atonement", (the Rosh Chodesh goat [as opposed to the two others, whose purpose was not atonement,) atoning for uncleanliness of the sanctuary and its holy things)]


###### Leviticus 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:18)


הֵ֚ן לֹא־הוּבָ֣א אֶת־דָּמָ֔הּ אֶל־הַקֹּ֖דֶשׁ פְּנִ֑ימָה אָכ֨וֹל תֹּאכְל֥וּ אֹתָ֛הּ בַּקֹּ֖דֶשׁ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוֵּֽיתִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Behold its blood was not brought inside the Sanctuary, you should certainly have eaten it in a holy place as I have commanded.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, the blood of it was not brought in within the holy place: you should indeed have eaten it in the holy place, as I commanded.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behold, its blood was not brought within the sanctuary [in which instance you would have been obliged to burn it (see 6:23)]. You should then have eaten it in the holy place as I commanded you [in respect to the meal-offering (12), even though you are onenim!]


###### Leviticus 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:19)


וַיְדַבֵּ֨ר אַהֲרֹ֜ן אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֗ה הֵ֣ן הַ֠יּ֠וֹם הִקְרִ֨יבוּ אֶת־חַטָּאתָ֤ם וְאֶת־עֹֽלָתָם֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וַתִּקְרֶ֥אנָה אֹתִ֖י כָּאֵ֑לֶּה וְאָכַ֤לְתִּי חַטָּאת֙ הַיּ֔וֹם הַיִּיטַ֖ב בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon spoke [in reply] to Moshe, Behold, today they brought their sin-offerings and their burnt-offerings before Adonoy and then such [tragedy] befell me. If I had eaten the sin-offering today, would that have been good in the eyes of Adonoy?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aharon said to Moshe, Behold, this day have they offered their sin offering and their burnt offering before the Lord; and such things have befallen me that if I had eaten the sin offering today, should it have been accepted in the sight of the Lord?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Aaron spoke ["with heart"] to Moses [(knowing that Moses' words were really meant for him and that his sons were maintaining a deferential silence)]: Now did they this day offer their sin-offering and their burnt-offering before the L-rd! [Moses had said to them: Is it possible that you sprinkled its blood being onenim! (the service of an onein being pasul) — to which Aaron replied: Is it they, ordinary priests, who offered the sacrifice? Is it not I who did so? (the high-priest sacrificing even when he is an onein)] Now if there had befallen me [even the death of other kin for whom I must become an onein (see Chapter 21)], such as these [my sons], and I had eaten the sin-offering this day [(an onein is permitted to eat at night)], would it be good in the eyes of the L-rd! [(If you have heard (from the L-rd) that this (such eating) is permitted with kodshim for a particular occasion (the investiture), this is no license for permitting it in "ongoing" kodshim (such as the Rosh Chodesh goat)].


###### Leviticus 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/10:20)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֣ע מֹשֶׁ֔ה וַיִּיטַ֖ב בְּעֵינָֽיו׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe heard, and it was good in his eyes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when Moshe heard that, he was content.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses heard, and it was good in his eyes. [He admitted (that he had heard this from the L-rd but had forgotten) instead of "covering up" (by saying that he had not heard)].



## Chapter 11

###### Leviticus 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה וְאֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֖ן לֵאמֹ֥ר אֲלֵהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying to them:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying to them,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses and to Aaron [(Moses to tell Aaron)] to say to them [(Aaron to tell Elazar and Ithamar)]:


###### Leviticus 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:2)


דַּבְּר֛וּ אֶל־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר זֹ֤את הַֽחַיָּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תֹּאכְל֔וּ מִכׇּל־הַבְּהֵמָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר עַל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying: these are the living things that you may eat from among all the animals that are on the earth.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, saying, These are the beasts which you shall eat among all the beasts that are on the earth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel, saying [(All of the above were made equal in this embassy for having remained silent and accepted G-d's decree (the death of Nadav and Avihu) with love)]: This [(Moses took hold of the animal and showed it to Israel)] is the animal (chayyah) [(connoting "chayyim" (life) — Because Israel cleaves to the L-rd and merits (eternal) life, it is (to that end) separated from what is unclean and given mitzvoth)] that you may eat, of every beast that is on the earth [("beast" is in the class of "animal")]:


###### Leviticus 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:3)


כֹּ֣ל**׀** מַפְרֶ֣סֶת פַּרְסָ֗ה וְשֹׁסַ֤עַת שֶׁ֙סַע֙ פְּרָסֹ֔ת מַעֲלַ֥ת גֵּרָ֖ה בַּבְּהֵמָ֑ה אֹתָ֖הּ תֹּאכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All that have a cloven hoof, and they [the hooves] are [completely] split and that [also] chews its cud among animals, you may eat it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whatever parts the hoof, and is clovenfooted, and chews the cud, among the beasts, that shall you eat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Whatever has parted hooves, and whose hooves are wholly cloven, and which chews its cud [lit., "which brings up its cud" (to chew again)] in the beast [(including an embryo found in the mother)] it shall you eat [(a positive commandment added to the negative (below) to make for "double" transgression in the instance of non-observance)]


###### Leviticus 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:4)


אַ֤ךְ אֶת־זֶה֙ לֹ֣א תֹֽאכְל֔וּ מִֽמַּעֲלֵי֙ הַגֵּרָ֔ה וּמִמַּפְרִסֵ֖י הַפַּרְסָ֑ה אֶֽת־הַ֠גָּמָ֠ל כִּֽי־מַעֲלֵ֨ה גֵרָ֜ה ה֗וּא וּפַרְסָה֙ אֵינֶ֣נּוּ מַפְרִ֔יס טָמֵ֥א ה֖וּא לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, these in particular you may not eat of those that chew their cud and that have cloven hooves: the camel, for he chews his cud but does not have a split hoof it is unclean to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevertheless these shall you not eat of them that chew the cud, or of them that divide the hoof: the camel, because he chews the cud, but does not part the hoof: he is unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only this shall you not eat of those that chew the cud and of those whose hooves are parted: the camel, because it chews the cud and its hoof is not parted, it is unclean to you.


###### Leviticus 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:5)


וְאֶת־הַשָּׁפָ֗ן כִּֽי־מַעֲלֵ֤ה גֵרָה֙ ה֔וּא וּפַרְסָ֖ה לֹ֣א יַפְרִ֑יס טָמֵ֥א ה֖וּא לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The rabbit, for it chews its cud but does not have a cloven hoof it is unclean to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the coney, because he chews the cud, but does not part the hoof; he is unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the coney, because it chews the cud but has not parted hooves, it is unclean to you.


###### Leviticus 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:6)


וְאֶת־הָאַרְנֶ֗בֶת כִּֽי־מַעֲלַ֤ת גֵּרָה֙ הִ֔וא וּפַרְסָ֖ה לֹ֣א הִפְרִ֑יסָה טְמֵאָ֥ה הִ֖וא לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The hare, for it chews its cud but it does not have a cloven hoof it is unclean to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the hare, because he chews the cud, but does not part the hoof; he is unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the hare, because it chews the cud but has not parted hooves, it is unclean to you.


###### Leviticus 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:7)


וְאֶת־הַ֠חֲזִ֠יר כִּֽי־מַפְרִ֨יס פַּרְסָ֜ה ה֗וּא וְשֹׁסַ֥ע שֶׁ֙סַע֙ פַּרְסָ֔ה וְה֖וּא גֵּרָ֣ה לֹֽא־יִגָּ֑ר טָמֵ֥א ה֖וּא לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The pig, for though it has cloven hooves, and its hooves are [completely] split—it does not chew its cud—it is unclean to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the swine, though he divide the hoof, and be clovenfooted, yet he chews not the cud; he is unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the swine, because its hooves are parted and it is cloven-footed, but it does not chew the cud, it is unclean to you.


###### Leviticus 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:8)


מִבְּשָׂרָם֙ לֹ֣א תֹאכֵ֔לוּ וּבְנִבְלָתָ֖ם לֹ֣א תִגָּ֑עוּ טְמֵאִ֥ים הֵ֖ם לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not eat of their flesh and you may not touch their carcasses; they are unclean to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Of their flesh shall you not eat, and their carcass shall you not touch; they are unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Of their flesh you shall not eat [(The exhortation applies to flesh, but not to bones, sinews, horns, or hooves)], and their carcass you shall not touch [on a festival]; they are unclean to you [(Do not make yourself unclean through them and (thus) disqualify yourself from appearing in the Temple on the festival)].


###### Leviticus 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:9)


אֶת־זֶה֙ תֹּֽאכְל֔וּ מִכֹּ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּמָּ֑יִם כֹּ֣ל אֲשֶׁר־לוֹ֩ סְנַפִּ֨יר וְקַשְׂקֶ֜שֶׂת בַּמַּ֗יִם בַּיַּמִּ֛ים וּבַנְּחָלִ֖ים אֹתָ֥ם תֹּאכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These you may eat, of all things that are in water; all that have fins and scales in the waters, in oceans and in rivers; you may eat them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These shall you eat of all that are in the waters: whatever has fins and scales in the waters, in the seas, and in the rivers, them shall you eat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This may you eat of all that is in the waters: All that have fins and scales in the water, in the seas and in the rivers — those may you eat.


###### Leviticus 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:10)


וְכֹל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֵֽין־ל֜וֹ סְנַפִּ֣יר וְקַשְׂקֶ֗שֶׂת בַּיַּמִּים֙ וּבַנְּחָלִ֔ים מִכֹּל֙ שֶׁ֣רֶץ הַמַּ֔יִם וּמִכֹּ֛ל נֶ֥פֶשׁ הַחַיָּ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּמָּ֑יִם שֶׁ֥קֶץ הֵ֖ם לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All that do not have fins and scales [that are] in the oceans and rivers; of any creature that creeps in the water and of any living creature that is in the water; they are repulsive to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all that have not fins and scales in the seas, and in the rivers, of all that move in the waters, and of any living thing which is in the waters, they are abominable to you:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all that do not have fins and scales in the seas and in the rivers, of every sheretz of the waters [(sheretz) a low creature that creeps and moves along the ground], and of every living creature that is in the waters — they are detestable to you.


###### Leviticus 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:11)


וְשֶׁ֖קֶץ יִהְי֣וּ לָכֶ֑ם מִבְּשָׂרָם֙ לֹ֣א תֹאכֵ֔לוּ וְאֶת־נִבְלָתָ֖ם תְּשַׁקֵּֽצוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They shall be repulsive to you, you shall not eat of their flesh and their carcasses shall be repulsive by you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and they shall be abominable to you; you shall not eat of their flesh, but their carcasses you shall abominate.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And detestable shall they be to you [(This is to include mixtures where their taste is discernible)]. Of their flesh you shall not eat [(The exhortation does not apply to fins and bones)], and their carcass shall you hold in detestation [(to include midges filtered from their native element)].


###### Leviticus 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:12)


כֹּ֣ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֵֽין־ל֛וֹ סְנַפִּ֥יר וְקַשְׂקֶ֖שֶׂת בַּמָּ֑יִם שֶׁ֥קֶץ ה֖וּא לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anything which does not have fins and scales in the water, is repulsive to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whatever has no fins or scales in the waters, that shall be an abomination to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Whatever has no fins and scales in the water [(but not something that "sheds" them in being removed from the water)] is detestable to you.


###### Leviticus 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:13)


וְאֶת־אֵ֙לֶּה֙ תְּשַׁקְּצ֣וּ מִן־הָע֔וֹף לֹ֥א יֵאָכְל֖וּ שֶׁ֣קֶץ הֵ֑ם אֶת־הַנֶּ֙שֶׁר֙ וְאֶת־הַפֶּ֔רֶס וְאֵ֖ת הָעׇזְנִיָּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These you shall consider repulsive from among the fowl; they may not be eaten; they are repulsive: the eagle, the white-tailed eagle and the bearded vulture.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And these are they which you shall have in abomination among the birds; they shall not be eaten, they are abominable: the eagle, and the bearded vulture and the black vulture.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And these shall you hold in detestation of the fowl. They shall not be eaten [(One may not feed them to minors)]; they are detestable: the great vulture, and the bearded vulture, and the osprey.


###### Leviticus 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:14)


וְאֶ֨ת־הַדָּאָ֔ה וְאֶת־הָאַיָּ֖ה לְמִינָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The falcon and the vulture, to its kind.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the kite, and the buzzard after its kind;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the kite and the falcon after its kind. ["after its kind" denotes a class which, though containing some members which may differ in name or appearance, is, notwithstanding, one species.)]


###### Leviticus 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:15)


אֵ֥ת כׇּל־עֹרֵ֖ב לְמִינֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every raven, to its kind.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
every raven after its kind;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every raven after its kind.


###### Leviticus 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:16)


וְאֵת֙ בַּ֣ת הַֽיַּעֲנָ֔ה וְאֶת־הַתַּחְמָ֖ס וְאֶת־הַשָּׁ֑חַף וְאֶת־הַנֵּ֖ץ לְמִינֵֽהוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The ostrich, the owl, the seagull and the sparrow hawk, to its kind.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the owl, and the kestrel, and the gull, and the sparrow hawk after its kind.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the ostrich, and the night-hawk, and the sea-mew, and the hawk after its kind.


###### Leviticus 11:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:17)


וְאֶת־הַכּ֥וֹס וְאֶת־הַשָּׁלָ֖ךְ וְאֶת־הַיַּנְשֽׁוּף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The little owl, the cormorant and the eagle owl.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the little owl, and the fish fowl, and the great owl,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the little owl, and the cormorant, and the great owl.


###### Leviticus 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:18)


וְאֶת־הַתִּנְשֶׁ֥מֶת וְאֶת־הַקָּאָ֖ת וְאֶת־הָרָחָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The horned owl, the pelican and the carrion vulture.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the barn owl, and the jackdaw, and the gier eagle.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the horned owl, and the pelican, and the carrion-vulture.


###### Leviticus 11:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:19)


וְאֵת֙ הַחֲסִידָ֔ה הָאֲנָפָ֖ה לְמִינָ֑הּ וְאֶת־הַדּוּכִיפַ֖ת וְאֶת־הָעֲטַלֵּֽף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The stork and the heron, to its kind. The hoopoe and the bat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the stork, the heron after her kind, and the hoopoe, and the bat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the [white] stork, and the heron after its kind, and the lapwing and the bat.


###### Leviticus 11:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:20)


כֹּ֚ל שֶׁ֣רֶץ הָע֔וֹף הַהֹלֵ֖ךְ עַל־אַרְבַּ֑ע שֶׁ֥קֶץ ה֖וּא לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All winged insects that walk on four [legs] are repulsive to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All swarming things that fly, going upon four, shall be an abomination to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All creeping things that fly, which walk on four [such as flies, hornets, gnats, and locusts,] are detestable to you.


###### Leviticus 11:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:21)


אַ֤ךְ אֶת־זֶה֙ תֹּֽאכְל֔וּ מִכֹּל֙ שֶׁ֣רֶץ הָע֔וֹף הַהֹלֵ֖ךְ עַל־אַרְבַּ֑ע אֲשֶׁר־[ל֤וֹ] (לא) כְרָעַ֙יִם֙ מִמַּ֣עַל לְרַגְלָ֔יו לְנַתֵּ֥ר בָּהֵ֖ן עַל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, these you may eat of all the flying insects that walk on four [legs], those which have knees extending above their legs so that it hops on the ground with them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet these may you eat of every flying creeping thing that goes upon four, which have legs above their feet, to leap with upon the earth;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only this may you eat of all creeping things that fly, which walk on four — that which has kera'aim [foot-like projections] above its feet [near its neck] with which to spring from the earth.


###### Leviticus 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:22)


אֶת־אֵ֤לֶּה מֵהֶם֙ תֹּאכֵ֔לוּ אֶת־הָֽאַרְבֶּ֣ה לְמִינ֔וֹ וְאֶת־הַסׇּלְעָ֖ם לְמִינֵ֑הוּ וְאֶת־הַחַרְגֹּ֣ל לְמִינֵ֔הוּ וְאֶת־הֶחָגָ֖ב לְמִינֵֽהוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Of them, these you may eat; the locust, to its kind the *solom*-locust, to its kind, the grasshopper, to its kind and the *chogov*-hopper, to its kind.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
these of them you may eat; the locust after its kind, and the grasshopper after its kind, and the ĥargol after its kind, and the ĥagav after its kind.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These of them you may eat: the arbeh after its kind, and the salam after its kind, and the chargol after its kind, and the chagav after its kind.


###### Leviticus 11:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:23)


וְכֹל֙ שֶׁ֣רֶץ הָע֔וֹף אֲשֶׁר־ל֖וֹ אַרְבַּ֣ע רַגְלָ֑יִם שֶׁ֥קֶץ ה֖וּא לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All flying insects that have four feet are repulsive to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But all other flying creeping things, which have four feet, shall be an abomination to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every creeping thing that flies, which has four legs, it is detestable to you [(but if it has five, it is clean)].


###### Leviticus 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:24)


וּלְאֵ֖לֶּה תִּטַּמָּ֑אוּ כׇּל־הַנֹּגֵ֥עַ בְּנִבְלָתָ֖ם יִטְמָ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
As for these, [animals], you can be made unclean; all those who touch their carcasses shall be unclean until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And for these you shall be unclean: whoever touches the carcass of them shall be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And by [touching] these [that follow] you shall become unclean; all who touch their carcass will be unclean until evening.


###### Leviticus 11:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:25)


וְכׇל־הַנֹּשֵׂ֖א מִנִּבְלָתָ֑ם יְכַבֵּ֥ס בְּגָדָ֖יו וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anyone who carries of their carcasses; shall wash his garments and is unclean until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whoever bears aught of the carcass of them shall wash his clothes, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
