And everyone that carries of their carcass shall wash his clothing, and he shall be unclean until the evening. [(Carrying-uncleanliness is more stringent than touching-uncleanliness in that it requires washing of clothing.)]


###### Leviticus 11:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:26)


לְֽכׇל־הַבְּהֵמָ֡ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר הִוא֩ מַפְרֶ֨סֶת פַּרְסָ֜ה וְשֶׁ֣סַע**׀** אֵינֶ֣נָּה שֹׁסַ֗עַת וְגֵרָה֙ אֵינֶ֣נָּה מַעֲלָ֔ה טְמֵאִ֥ים הֵ֖ם לָכֶ֑ם כׇּל־הַנֹּגֵ֥עַ בָּהֶ֖ם יִטְמָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Thus every animal that has cloven hooves but its hooves are not [completely] split and it does not bring up its cud, they are unclean to you; all who touch them shall become unclean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The carcasses of every beast which parts the hoof, and is not clovenfooted, nor chews the cud, are unclean to you: everyone that touches them shall be unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
By [touching (24)] every beast whose hoof is parted but is not cloven-footed, [like the camel, whose hoof is parted above but joined below] or does not chew the cud. They are unclean to you; everyone who touches them (i.e. their carcass) shall be unclean.


###### Leviticus 11:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:27)


וְכֹ֣ל**׀** הוֹלֵ֣ךְ עַל־כַּפָּ֗יו בְּכׇל־הַֽחַיָּה֙ הַהֹלֶ֣כֶת עַל־אַרְבַּ֔ע טְמֵאִ֥ים הֵ֖ם לָכֶ֑ם כׇּל־הַנֹּגֵ֥עַ בְּנִבְלָתָ֖ם יִטְמָ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All [animals] that walk on their paws among all the living creatures that walk on four legs, they are unclean to you. All who touch their carcasses shall be unclean until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whatever goes upon its paws, among all manner of beasts that go on all four, those are unclean to you: whoever touches their carcass shall be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all that walk upon their soles [e.g., dog, bear, cat], among all animals which walk on four, they are unclean to you. All who touch their carcass will be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 11:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:28)


וְהַנֹּשֵׂא֙ אֶת־נִבְלָתָ֔ם יְכַבֵּ֥ס בְּגָדָ֖יו וְטָמֵ֣א עַד־הָעָ֑רֶב טְמֵאִ֥ים הֵ֖מָּה לָכֶֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever carries their carcasses shall wash his garments and is unclean until the evening; they are unclean to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that bears the carcass of them shall wash his clothes, and be unclean until evening: they are unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he that carries their carcass shall wash his clothes, and he shall be unclean until the evening; they are unclean to you.


###### Leviticus 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:29)


וְזֶ֤ה לָכֶם֙ הַטָּמֵ֔א בַּשֶּׁ֖רֶץ הַשֹּׁרֵ֣ץ עַל־הָאָ֑רֶץ הַחֹ֥לֶד וְהָעַכְבָּ֖ר וְהַצָּ֥ב לְמִינֵֽהוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These [also] are unclean to you among all creeping creatures that crawl on the ground; the weasel, the mouse and the toad, to its kind.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These also shall be unclean to you among the creeping things that creep upon the earth; the rat, and the mouse, and the tortoise after its kind.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this to you is what is unclean [i.e., which renders you unclean by touching them (vis-à-vis the eating of terumah and kodshim and entering the sanctuary)] among the creeping things that creep upon the earth: the weasel, and the mouse, and the great lizard after its kind.


###### Leviticus 11:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:30)


וְהָאֲנָקָ֥ה וְהַכֹּ֖חַ וְהַלְּטָאָ֑ה וְהַחֹ֖מֶט וְהַתִּנְשָֽׁמֶת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The hedgehog, the chameleon, the lizard, the slug and the mole.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the gecko, and the monitor, and the lizard, and the skink, and the chameleon.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the gecko, and the land-crocodile, and the lizard, and the sand-lizard, and the chameleon.


###### Leviticus 11:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:31)


אֵ֛לֶּה הַטְּמֵאִ֥ים לָכֶ֖ם בְּכׇל־הַשָּׁ֑רֶץ כׇּל־הַנֹּגֵ֧עַ בָּהֶ֛ם בְּמֹתָ֖ם יִטְמָ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are unclean to you among all the creeping creatures. Whoever touches them when they are dead shall be unclean until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These are unclean to you among all that creep: whoever touches them, when they are dead, shall be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are what are unclean to you among all that creep. Everyone who touches them in their death will be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 11:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:32)


וְכֹ֣ל אֲשֶׁר־יִפֹּל־עָלָיו֩ מֵהֶ֨ם׀בְּמֹתָ֜ם יִטְמָ֗א מִכׇּל־כְּלִי־עֵץ֙ א֣וֹ בֶ֤גֶד אוֹ־עוֹר֙ א֣וֹ שָׂ֔ק כׇּל־כְּלִ֕י אֲשֶׁר־יֵעָשֶׂ֥ה מְלָאכָ֖ה בָּהֶ֑ם בַּמַּ֧יִם יוּבָ֛א וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעֶ֖רֶב וְטָהֵֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Everything, wherever a part of them may fall when they are dead, shall become unclean, such as a wooden vessel, or a garment, or a hide or a sack. Every vessel with which work is done, shall be brought [put] into water, and remains unclean until the evening, [then] it becomes clean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whatever any of them falls upon, when they are dead, shall be unclean; whether it be any vessel of wood, or clothing, or skin, or sack, whatever vessel it be, in which any work is done, it must be put into water, and it shall be unclean until evening, then it shall be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all (vessels) upon which there falls of them in their death shall be unclean: of all vessels of wood, or garment, or skin, or sack. Any vessel with which work is done shall be put into water, and it shall be unclean [even after immersion] until the evening [for purposes of terumah], and then, [with the setting of the sun] it shall be clean.


###### Leviticus 11:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:33)


וְכׇ֨ל־כְּלִי־חֶ֔רֶשׂ אֲשֶׁר־יִפֹּ֥ל מֵהֶ֖ם אֶל־תּוֹכ֑וֹ כֹּ֣ל אֲשֶׁ֧ר בְּתוֹכ֛וֹ יִטְמָ֖א וְאֹת֥וֹ תִשְׁבֹּֽרוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every earthen vessel into which a part of them may fall, everything that is within it becomes unclean, and you shall break it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And every earthen vessel, into which any of them fall, whatever is in it shall be unclean; and you shall break it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every earthen vessel where there shall fall of them into its midst [(an earthen vessel becomes unclean only through its interior)], whatever is in its midst shall [in turn] become unclean, and it shall you break [(It cannot be rendered clean in a mikveh.)]


###### Leviticus 11:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:34)


מִכׇּל־הָאֹ֜כֶל אֲשֶׁ֣ר יֵאָכֵ֗ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָב֥וֹא עָלָ֛יו מַ֖יִם יִטְמָ֑א וְכׇל־מַשְׁקֶה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִשָּׁתֶ֔ה בְּכׇל־כְּלִ֖י יִטְמָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Of all foods that are eaten upon which water comes, shall be unclean. Every beverage that is drunk, in any vessel, shall be impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Of all food which may be eaten, that on which water comes shall be unclean: and all drink that may be drunk in every vessel shall be unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
["whatever is in its midst shall be unclean"] of all the food [(but not vessels) that is in it] which may be eaten [in one swallow (the size of an egg)], upon which water [and all drink] comes [(after the fruit has been picked)]; and all drink that may be drunk in every (such) vessel shall be unclean.


###### Leviticus 11:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:35)


וְ֠כֹ֠ל אֲשֶׁר־יִפֹּ֨ל מִנִּבְלָתָ֥ם׀עָלָיו֮ יִטְמָא֒ תַּנּ֧וּר וְכִירַ֛יִם יֻתָּ֖ץ טְמֵאִ֣ים הֵ֑ם וּטְמֵאִ֖ים יִהְי֥וּ לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Everything upon which any part of their carcasses fall, shall be unclean [even] an oven and a hearth shall be shattered, they are unclean and they shall be unclean to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And everything upon which any part of their carcass falls shall be unclean; whether it be oven, or ranges, they shall be broken down: for they are unclean, and shall be unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all that there shall fall of their carcass upon it shall be unclean; whether oven or stove [(hollow, movable earthenware vessels with the opening on top)], it shall be torn down [(ritual immersion not availing for earthenware)]; they are unclean. And they shall be unclean to you [("to you":) If one wishes to preserve them in their state of uncleanliness he may do so.)]


###### Leviticus 11:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:36)


אַ֣ךְ מַעְיָ֥ן וּב֛וֹר מִקְוֵה־מַ֖יִם יִהְיֶ֣ה טָה֑וֹר וְנֹגֵ֥עַ בְּנִבְלָתָ֖ם יִטְמָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, a well-spring and a pit, a gathering of water, shall be pure. [But] that which touches their carcasses shall be unclean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevertheless a fountain or pit, wherein there is a collection of water, shall be clean: but that which touches their carcass shall be unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only a spring or a pit where waters are gathered shall be clean [i.e., they are not susceptible to uncleanliness (or: he who immerses in them "shall be clean")]. But he who touches their carcass (the carcass of the animals mentioned above) shall be unclean, [even if he does so while immersed in the spring or the mikveh].


###### Leviticus 11:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:37)


וְכִ֤י יִפֹּל֙ מִנִּבְלָתָ֔ם עַל־כׇּל־זֶ֥רַע זֵר֖וּעַ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִזָּרֵ֑עַ טָה֖וֹר הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If any part of their carcasses falls on any sowing seed that will be planted, it remains [ritually] clean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if any part of their carcass fall upon any sowing seed which is to be sown, it shall be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if there fall of their carcass on any variety of seed which is to be sown, it is clean [(Scripture apprises us (see 38) that it is not considered food susceptible of uncleanliness until water comes upon it)].


###### Leviticus 11:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:38)


וְכִ֤י יֻתַּן־מַ֙יִם֙ עַל־זֶ֔רַע וְנָפַ֥ל מִנִּבְלָתָ֖ם עָלָ֑יו טָמֵ֥א ה֖וּא לָכֶֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If water was once placed on seeds and part of their carcasses fall upon them, they are unclean to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if any water be put upon the seed, and any part of their carcass fall on it, it shall be unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if water [or other liquids] be placed upon a(n) [uprooted] seed [or it fall into water], and aught of their carcass fall on it [(even after the water has dried)], it is unclean to you.


###### Leviticus 11:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:39)


וְכִ֤י יָמוּת֙ מִן־הַבְּהֵמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־הִ֥יא לָכֶ֖ם לְאׇכְלָ֑ה הַנֹּגֵ֥עַ בְּנִבְלָתָ֖הּ יִטְמָ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If any animal dies [of those] that are permitted to eat whoever touches its carcass shall be unclean until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if any beast, of which you may eat, die; he that touches its carcass shall be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if there die of the beast which is yours to eat, he that touches its carcass [(but not its bones, sinews, horns, hooves, or skin)] shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 11:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:40)


וְהָֽאֹכֵל֙ מִנִּבְלָתָ֔הּ יְכַבֵּ֥ס בְּגָדָ֖יו וְטָמֵ֣א עַד־הָעָ֑רֶב וְהַנֹּשֵׂא֙ אֶת־נִבְלָתָ֔הּ יְכַבֵּ֥ס בְּגָדָ֖יו וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever eats of its carcass shall wash his garments and is unclean until the evening. Whoever carries its carcass shall wash his garments and is unclean until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that eats of the carcass of it shall wash his clothes, and be unclean until evening: he also that bears the carcass of it shall wash his clothes, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he who eats of its carcass [i.e., one who carries or touches the amount of carcass to which the term "eating" can be applied (i.e., an olive-size)] shall wash his clothes and he shall be unclean until the evening. And he who carries its carcass shall wash his clothes and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 11:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:41)


וְכׇל־הַשֶּׁ֖רֶץ הַשֹּׁרֵ֣ץ עַל־הָאָ֑רֶץ שֶׁ֥קֶץ ה֖וּא לֹ֥א יֵאָכֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every creeping creature which crawls on the ground is repulsive, it may not be eaten.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And every creeping thing that creeps upon the earth shall be held abominable; it shall not be eaten.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every creeping thing that creeps upon the earth is detestable [(to exclude the insects in peas and beans, which do not creep upon the earth but within the food (Once they come out, however, and creep upon the earth, they are forbidden)]; it shall not be eaten [The feeder incurs punishment as the eater does.]


###### Leviticus 11:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:42)


כֹּל֩ הוֹלֵ֨ךְ עַל־גָּח֜וֹן וְכֹ֣ל**׀** הוֹלֵ֣ךְ עַל־אַרְבַּ֗ע עַ֚ד כׇּל־מַרְבֵּ֣ה רַגְלַ֔יִם לְכׇל־הַשֶּׁ֖רֶץ הַשֹּׁרֵ֣ץ עַל־הָאָ֑רֶץ לֹ֥א תֹאכְל֖וּם כִּי־שֶׁ֥קֶץ הֵֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whatever crawls on its belly and whatever goes on four [legs] or whatever has many feet; regarding all creeping creatures that crawl on the ground; you may not eat them, for they are repulsive.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whatever goes on its belly, and whatever goes upon four, or whatever has many feet among all creeping things that creep on the earth, them you shall not eat; for they are an abomination.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All [(including worms and the like)] that go upon the belly [i.e., snakes] and all that go upon four [i.e., scorpions], until all [including beetles and the like] that are many-footed [i.e., centipedes], of all creeping things that creep upon the earth, you shall not eat them, for they are detestable.


###### Leviticus 11:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:43)


אַל־תְּשַׁקְּצוּ֙ אֶת־נַפְשֹׁ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם בְּכׇל־הַשֶּׁ֖רֶץ הַשֹּׁרֵ֑ץ וְלֹ֤א תִֽטַּמְּאוּ֙ בָּהֶ֔ם וְנִטְמֵתֶ֖ם בָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not make yourselves repulsive by [eating] any creeping creature that crawls, and do not make yourselves unclean with them, for you will become unclean because of them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not make yourselves abominable with any creeping thing that creeps, neither shall you make yourselves unclean with them, that you should be defiled by them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not make your souls detestable by [eating] every creeping thing that creeps, and do not make yourselves unclean by [eating] them, that you be rendered unclean by them. [(If you make yourselves unclean by them on earth, I shall render you unclean for the world to come and for the heavenly tribunal.)]


###### Leviticus 11:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:44)


כִּ֣י אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָה֮ אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶם֒ וְהִתְקַדִּשְׁתֶּם֙ וִהְיִיתֶ֣ם קְדֹשִׁ֔ים כִּ֥י קָד֖וֹשׁ אָ֑נִי וְלֹ֤א תְטַמְּאוּ֙ אֶת־נַפְשֹׁ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם בְּכׇל־הַשֶּׁ֖רֶץ הָרֹמֵ֥שׂ עַל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For I am Adonoy, your God; you shall sanctify yourselves and you shall become holy, for I am Holy and you shall not make yourselves unclean with any creeping creature that crawls on the ground.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I am the Lord your God: you shall therefore sanctify yourselves, and you shall be holy; for I am holy: neither shall you defile yourselves with any manner of creeping thing that creeps on the earth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Just as I am holy,] for I am the L-rd your G-d, make yourselves holy, and you will be holy, [for I will sanctify you above and in the world to come.] For I am holy, and do not make your souls unclean, [transgressing many negative commandments, each punishable by stripes (e.g., "If one ate a putitha (a kind of reptile) he receives stripes four times, etc.")] with every creeping thing that creeps upon the earth.


###### Leviticus 11:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:45)


כִּ֣י**׀** אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה הַֽמַּעֲלֶ֤ה אֶתְכֶם֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם לִהְיֹ֥ת לָכֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִ֑ים וִהְיִיתֶ֣ם קְדֹשִׁ֔ים כִּ֥י קָד֖וֹשׁ אָֽנִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For I am Adonoy, who brings you up from the land of Egypt to be your God. You shall be holy, for I am Holy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I am the Lord that brings you up out of the land of Miżrayim, to be your God: you shall therefore be holy, for I am holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I am the L-rd, who brought you up out of the land of Egypt [so that you accept My mitzvoth], to be a G-d to you. And you shall be holy, for I am holy.


###### Leviticus 11:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:46)


זֹ֣את תּוֹרַ֤ת הַבְּהֵמָה֙ וְהָע֔וֹף וְכֹל֙ נֶ֣פֶשׁ הַֽחַיָּ֔ה הָרֹמֶ֖שֶׂת בַּמָּ֑יִם וּלְכׇל־נֶ֖פֶשׁ הַשֹּׁרֶ֥צֶת עַל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the law concerning the animals and the fowl and of all living creatures that swarm in the waters and for all creatures that crawl on the earth.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the Tora of the beasts, and of the birds, and of every living creature that moves in the waters, and of every creature that creeps on the earth:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the law of the beast and the bird and of every living creature that swarms in the water and of every creature that creeps on the earth.


###### Leviticus 11:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/11:47)


לְהַבְדִּ֕יל בֵּ֥ין הַטָּמֵ֖א וּבֵ֣ין הַטָּהֹ֑ר וּבֵ֤ין הַֽחַיָּה֙ הַֽנֶּאֱכֶ֔לֶת וּבֵין֙ הַֽחַיָּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר לֹ֥א תֵאָכֵֽל׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
To distinguish between the unclean and between the clean, and between the living creatures which may be eaten and between the living creature which may not be eaten.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to make a distinction between the unclean and the clean, and between the beast that may be eaten and the beast that may not be eaten.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To distinguish [(It is not enough to "learn" about them; you must have perfect "recognition" of them.)] between the unclean [i.e., what is "unclean" to you (e.g., cutting half the windpipe)] and the clean [i.e., what is "clean" to you (e.g., cutting most of the windpipe)], and between the animal [(with certain treifah signs)] which may be eaten, and the animal [(with other treifah signs)] which may not be eaten.



## Chapter 12

###### Leviticus 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:2)


דַּבֵּ֞ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר אִשָּׁה֙ כִּ֣י תַזְרִ֔יעַ וְיָלְדָ֖ה זָכָ֑ר וְטָֽמְאָה֙ שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֔ים כִּימֵ֛י נִדַּ֥ת דְּוֺתָ֖הּ תִּטְמָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying, when a woman conceives and gives birth to a male child she will be ritually unclean for seven days; as the days of her menstrual flow, she will be unclean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, saying, If a woman have conceived seed, and born a man child: then she shall be unclean seven days; as in the days of her menstrual sickness shall she be unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel, saying: If a woman give forth seed [(Just as man's creation followed that of beast and fowl, so his law follows that of beast and fowl)] ("If a woman give forth seed") [(Even if she bore something pulpy and semen-like, she becomes unclean with childbirth-uncleanliness.)] and she bear a male, she shall be unclean seven days. As the days of her menstrual flow [(even if her womb opened without blood)] shall she be unclean.


###### Leviticus 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:3)


וּבַיּ֖וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֑י יִמּ֖וֹל בְּשַׂ֥ר עׇרְלָתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the eighth day, the flesh of his foreskin shall be circumcised.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And on the eighth day the flesh of his foreskin shall be circumcised.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And on the eighth day he shall circumcise the flesh of his foreskin.


###### Leviticus 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:4)


וּשְׁלֹשִׁ֥ים יוֹם֙ וּשְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת יָמִ֔ים תֵּשֵׁ֖ב בִּדְמֵ֣י טׇהֳרָ֑הֿ בְּכׇל־קֹ֣דֶשׁ לֹֽא־תִגָּ֗ע וְאֶל־הַמִּקְדָּשׁ֙ לֹ֣א תָבֹ֔א עַד־מְלֹ֖את יְמֵ֥י טׇהֳרָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For thirty-three days she will remain [in the status] that the blood [she sees] is ritually pure. She shall not touch anything holy and she shall not come into the Sanctuary, until the days of her purity are completed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And she shall then continue in the blood of her purifying for thirty three days: she shall touch no hallowed thing, nor come in to the sanctuary, until the days of her purifying are fulfilled.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And thirty days and three days she shall abide in the blood of cleanliness. [(Even though she sees blood, she is clean.)] All that is holy [including terumah] she shall not touch [(an exhortation against eating terumah)] [for her immersion (vis-à-vis kodshim) does not "take" until sunset of the fortieth day, on the morrow of which she brings her atonement offering], and into the sanctuary she shall not come until the fulfillment of the days of her purification.


###### Leviticus 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:5)


וְאִם־נְקֵבָ֣ה תֵלֵ֔ד וְטָמְאָ֥ה שְׁבֻעַ֖יִם כְּנִדָּתָ֑הּ וְשִׁשִּׁ֥ים יוֹם֙ וְשֵׁ֣שֶׁת יָמִ֔ים תֵּשֵׁ֖ב עַל־דְּמֵ֥י טׇהֳרָֽהֿ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If she gives birth to a female she will be unclean for two weeks as during her menstruation. For sixty-six days she will remain [in the status] that the blood [she sees] is ritually pure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if she bear a female child, then she shall be unclean two weeks, as in her menstruation: and she shall continue in the blood of her purifying for sixty six days.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a female she bear, then she shall be unclean two weeks, as in her menstrual state, and sixty days and six days shall she abide in the blood of cleanliness.


###### Leviticus 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:6)


וּבִמְלֹ֣את**׀** יְמֵ֣י טׇהֳרָ֗הּ לְבֵן֮ א֣וֹ לְבַת֒ תָּבִ֞יא כֶּ֤בֶשׂ בֶּן־שְׁנָתוֹ֙ לְעֹלָ֔ה וּבֶן־יוֹנָ֥ה אוֹ־תֹ֖ר לְחַטָּ֑את אֶל־פֶּ֥תַח אֹֽהֶל־מוֹעֵ֖ד אֶל־הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When the days of her purification are completed, be it for a son or for a daughter, she shall bring a lamb, in its first year, as a burnt-offering and a young pigeon or a turtledove as a sin-offering, to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, to the *kohein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when the days of her purifying are fulfilled, for a son or for a daughter, she shall bring a lamb of the first year for a burnt offering, and a young pigeon, or a turtledove, for a sin offering, to the door of the Tent of Meeting, to the priest:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when the days of her purification are fulfilled, for a son or for a daughter, she shall bring a lamb in its first year for a burnt-offering, and a young pigeon or a turtle-dove for a sin-offering, to the door of the tent of meeting, to the Cohein.


###### Leviticus 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:7)


וְהִקְרִיב֞וֹ לִפְנֵ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ וְכִפֶּ֣ר עָלֶ֔יהָ וְטָהֲרָ֖הֿ מִמְּקֹ֣ר דָּמֶ֑יהָ זֹ֤את תּוֹרַת֙ הַיֹּלֶ֔דֶת לַזָּכָ֖ר א֥וֹ לַנְּקֵבָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He will bring it before Adonoy and atone for her and she will be cleansed from the source of her blood; this is the law regarding the woman who gives birth to a male or to a female.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who shall offer it before the Lord, and make atonement for her; and she shall be cleansed from the issue of her blood. This is the Tora for her that has born a male or a female.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall offer it [the sin-offering (Only the sin-offering is indispensable for her eating kodshim)] before the L-rd and make atonement for her, and she shall be cleansed [(whence it is seen that before this she is regarded as unclean)] from the source of her blood. This is the law for the woman who bears, whether a male or a female.


###### Leviticus 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/12:8)


וְאִם־לֹ֨א תִמְצָ֣א יָדָהּ֮ דֵּ֣י שֶׂה֒ וְלָקְחָ֣ה שְׁתֵּֽי־תֹרִ֗ים א֤וֹ שְׁנֵי֙ בְּנֵ֣י יוֹנָ֔ה אֶחָ֥ד לְעֹלָ֖ה וְאֶחָ֣ד לְחַטָּ֑את וְכִפֶּ֥ר עָלֶ֛יהָ הַכֹּהֵ֖ן וְטָהֵֽרָה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If she cannot afford [to acquire] a lamb she shall take two turtledoves or two young pigeons; one as a burnt-offering and one as a sin-offering. The *kohein* will atone for her and she will be cleansed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if she be not able to bring a lamb, then she shall bring two turtledoves, or two young pigeons; the one for the burnt offering, and the other for a sin offering: and the priest shall make atonement for her, and she shall be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if she has not means enough for a lamb, then she shall take two turtle-doves or two young pigeons, one for a burnt-offering and one for a sin-offering [(Note: The sin-offering is offered first; "burnt-offering" is just read first.)], and the Cohein shall make atonement for her and she shall be clean.



## Chapter 13

###### Leviticus 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה וְאֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֖ן לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe and Aharon, saying


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses and to Aaron, saying:


###### Leviticus 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:2)


אָדָ֗ם כִּֽי־יִהְיֶ֤ה בְעוֹר־בְּשָׂרוֹ֙ שְׂאֵ֤ת אֽוֹ־סַפַּ֙חַת֙ א֣וֹ בַהֶ֔רֶת וְהָיָ֥ה בְעוֹר־בְּשָׂר֖וֹ לְנֶ֣גַע צָרָ֑עַת וְהוּבָא֙ אֶל־אַהֲרֹ֣ן הַכֹּהֵ֔ן א֛וֹ אֶל־אַחַ֥ד מִבָּנָ֖יו הַכֹּהֲנִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When a person will have, in the skin of his flesh a spot of intense whiteness or an off-white spot, or a snow-white spot, and it forms on the skin of his body [like] the plague of *tzoraas,* he shall be brought to Aharon, the *kohein,* or to one of his sons, the *kohanim.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When a man shall have in the skin of his flesh a swelling, a scab, or bright spot, and it be in the skin of his flesh the plague of żara῾at; then he shall be brought to Aharon the priest, or to one of his sons the priests:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man, if there be in the skin of his flesh se'eth (a rising) or sapachath or bahereth (a bright spot) [types of plague-spots, one brighter than the other], and it become in the skin of his flesh a plague-spot of leprosy, then he shall be brought to Aaron the Cohein or to one of his sons, the Cohanim [(It is a Scriptural decree that only a Cohein rules on the cleanliness or uncleanliness of plague-spots)].


###### Leviticus 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:3)


וְרָאָ֣ה הַכֹּהֵ֣ן אֶת־הַנֶּ֣גַע בְּעֽוֹר־הַ֠בָּשָׂ֠ר וְשֵׂעָ֨ר בַּנֶּ֜גַע הָפַ֣ךְ**׀** לָבָ֗ן וּמַרְאֵ֤ה הַנֶּ֙גַע֙ עָמֹק֙ מֵע֣וֹר בְּשָׂר֔וֹ נֶ֥גַע צָרַ֖עַת ה֑וּא וְרָאָ֥הוּ הַכֹּהֵ֖ן וְטִמֵּ֥א אֹתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall see the plague in the skin of the flesh and [if] the hair in the skin-eruption has turned white and the appearance of the plague is deeper than the skin of his body, it is the skin-eruption of *tzoraas.* The *kohein* shall see it and declare him [to be] unclean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall look on the plague in the skin of the flesh: and when the hair in the plague is turned white, and the plague be deeper in appearance than the skin of his flesh, it is a plague of żara῾at: and the priest shall look on him, and pronounce him unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall see the plague-spot in the skin of the flesh. If hair [at least two hairs] in the plague-spot had turned [from black to] white, and the appearance of the plague-spot is deeper than the skin of his flesh [(as things in the sun seem deeper than those in the shadow)], it is a plague-spot of leprosy. And the Cohein shall see it and declare him unclean [(He shall say to him: "You are unclean.")]


###### Leviticus 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:4)


וְאִם־בַּהֶ֩רֶת֩ לְבָנָ֨ה הִ֜וא בְּע֣וֹר בְּשָׂר֗וֹ וְעָמֹק֙ אֵין־מַרְאֶ֣הָ מִן־הָע֔וֹר וּשְׂעָרָ֖הֿ לֹא־הָפַ֣ךְ לָבָ֑ן וְהִסְגִּ֧יר הַכֹּהֵ֛ן אֶת־הַנֶּ֖גַע שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If it is a snow-white spot in the skin of the flesh and it does not appear deeper than the skin and the hair did not turn white, the *kohein* shall confine the plague for seven days.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If the bright spot be white in the skin of his flesh, and be not deeper in appearance than the skin, and its hair be not turned white; then the priest shall shut up him that has the plague seven days:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if it is a white bahereth in the skin of his flesh, and its appearance is not deeper than the skin, and its hair had not turned white, then the Cohein shall quarantine the plague-spot for seven days, [not to be looked at until the end of the week].


###### Leviticus 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:5)


וְרָאָ֣הוּ הַכֹּהֵן֮ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי֒ וְהִנֵּ֤ה הַנֶּ֙גַע֙ עָמַ֣ד בְּעֵינָ֔יו לֹֽא־פָשָׂ֥ה הַנֶּ֖גַע בָּע֑וֹר וְהִסְגִּיר֧וֹ הַכֹּהֵ֛ן שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֖ים שֵׁנִֽית׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall see him on the seventh day and behold! [if] the skin-eruption has maintained its hue; the skin-eruption did not spread in the skin. The *kohein* shall confine him for seven days, a second time,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall look on him on the seventh day: and, behold, if the plague in his sight be at a stay, and the plague spread not in the skin; then the priest shall shut him up seven days more:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall see it on the seventh day; and if the plague-spot appears as it did before [in appearance and size], if the plague-spot did not spread in the skin, then the Cohein shall quarantine it for a second seven days, [but if it spread in the first week, he is a confirmed leper].


###### Leviticus 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:6)


וְרָאָה֩ הַכֹּהֵ֨ן אֹת֜וֹ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי֮ שֵׁנִית֒ וְהִנֵּה֙ כֵּהָ֣ה הַנֶּ֔גַע וְלֹא־פָשָׂ֥ה הַנֶּ֖גַע בָּע֑וֹר וְטִהֲר֤וֹ הַכֹּהֵן֙ מִסְפַּ֣חַת הִ֔וא וְכִבֶּ֥ס בְּגָדָ֖יו וְטָהֵֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall see him on the seventh day for the second time and behold! [if] the skin-eruption became dimmer and the skin-eruption did not spread in the skin, the *kohein* shall declare him [to be] clean; it is [merely] a white discoloration. He shall wash his garments and he will be cleansed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall look on him again on the seventh day: and, behold, if the plague be somewhat dimmer, and the plague spread not in the skin, the priest shall pronounce him clean: it is but a scab: and he shall wash his clothes, and be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall see it on the seventh day again; and if the plague-spot is dim, and the plague-spot has not spread in the skin, then the Cohein shall declare him clean [(but if it remained the same or spread, he is unclean)]; it is a mispachath [a type of clean plague-spot], and he shall wash his clothes and be clean [(Since he required quarantine he is called "unclean" and requires immersion.)]


###### Leviticus 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:7)


וְאִם־פָּשֹׂ֨ה תִפְשֶׂ֤ה הַמִּסְפַּ֙חַת֙ בָּע֔וֹר אַחֲרֵ֧י הֵרָאֹת֛וֹ אֶל־הַכֹּהֵ֖ן לְטׇהֳרָת֑וֹ וְנִרְאָ֥ה שֵׁנִ֖ית אֶל־הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the white discoloration has spread in the skin after it was shown to the *kohein,* who pronounced that it was clean, it must be shown a second time to the *kohein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if the scab spread much abroad in the skin, after he has been seen by the priest for his cleansing, he shall be seen by the priest again:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the mispachath spreads in the skin after he has shown himself to the Cohein for his cleansing, then he shall show himself a second time to the Cohein.


###### Leviticus 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:8)


וְרָאָה֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְהִנֵּ֛ה פָּשְׂתָ֥ה הַמִּסְפַּ֖חַת בָּע֑וֹר וְטִמְּא֥וֹ הַכֹּהֵ֖ן צָרַ֥עַת הִֽוא׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall see and behold! [if] the white discoloration has spread in the skin, the *kohein* shall declare him unclean, it is *tzoraas.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and if the priest see that, behold, the scab spreads in the skin, then the priest shall pronounce him unclean: it is żara῾at.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall see: If the mispachath has spread in the skin, then the Cohein shall declare him unclean, [whereupon he is a confirmed leper, requiring birds, shaving, and the offering (see Chapter 14)]. It [this mispachath] is leprosy.


###### Leviticus 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:9)


נֶ֣גַע צָרַ֔עַת כִּ֥י תִהְיֶ֖ה בְּאָדָ֑ם וְהוּבָ֖א אֶל־הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When there is a skin-eruption of *tzoraas* on a person he shall be brought to the *kohein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the plague of żara῾at is in a man, then he shall be brought to the priest;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A plague-spot of leprosy, if it be in a man, then he shall be brought to the Cohein.


###### Leviticus 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:10)


וְרָאָ֣ה הַכֹּהֵ֗ן וְהִנֵּ֤ה שְׂאֵת־לְבָנָה֙ בָּע֔וֹר וְהִ֕יא הָפְכָ֖ה שֵׂעָ֣ר לָבָ֑ן וּמִֽחְיַ֛ת בָּשָׂ֥ר חַ֖י בַּשְׂאֵֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall see and behold! there is an intensely white spot on the skin and it has turned the hair white or there is healthy flesh in the spot of intense whiteness,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall see him: and, behold, if a white swelling be in the skin, and it have turned the hair white, and there be quick raw flesh in the rising;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall see, and behold, a white se'eth in the skin, and it has turned the hair white, and the healthiness of living flesh within the rising, [part of the whiteness within the se'eth having assumed the appearance of flesh, this, too, is a sign of uncleanliness — white hair (even) without healthy flesh, or healthy flesh (even) without white hair. (Note: Though "healthy flesh" is stated only in reference to se'eth, it is a sign of uncleanliness in all plague-spots.)]


###### Leviticus 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:11)


צָרַ֨עַת נוֹשֶׁ֤נֶת הִוא֙ בְּע֣וֹר בְּשָׂר֔וֹ וְטִמְּא֖וֹ הַכֹּהֵ֑ן לֹ֣א יַסְגִּרֶ֔נּוּ כִּ֥י טָמֵ֖א הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
it is an old *tzoraas* in the skin of his body and the *kohein* shall declare him unclean he shall not confine him, since he is [obviously] unclean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
it is an old żara῾at in the skin of his flesh, and the priest shall pronounce him unclean, and shall not shut him up: for he is unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is old leprosy in the skin of his flesh [(There is an old wound, full of pus, beneath the healthy flesh, so that the healthy flesh is no reason to declare him clean]. And the Cohein shall declare him unclean; he shall not quarantine him, for he is unclean.


###### Leviticus 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:12)


וְאִם־פָּר֨וֹחַ תִּפְרַ֤ח הַצָּרַ֙עַת֙ בָּע֔וֹר וְכִסְּתָ֣ה הַצָּרַ֗עַת אֵ֚ת כׇּל־ע֣וֹר הַנֶּ֔גַע מֵרֹאשׁ֖וֹ וְעַד־רַגְלָ֑יו לְכׇל־מַרְאֵ֖ה עֵינֵ֥י הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And if the *tzoraas* sprouts on the skin and if the *tzoraas* covered the entire skin of the [one affected by] the eruption from his head until his feet to the extent that the *kohein* can see.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the żara῾at breaks out abroad in the skin, and the żara῾at covers all the skin of him that has the plague from his head to his foot, as far as the priest can see;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

And if the priest look on the plague of the patch, and, behold, it be not in appearance deeper than the skin, and there is no black hair in it; then the priest shall shut up him that has the plague of the patch seven days:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the Cohein see the nethek plague-spot, and, behold, its appearance is not deeper than the skin, and there is no black hair in it, then the Cohein shall quarantine the nethek plague-spot for seven days [(But if there were black hair, he is clean and quarantine is not required, black hair being a sign of cleanliness in a nethek)].


###### Leviticus 13:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:32)


וְרָאָ֨ה הַכֹּהֵ֣ן אֶת־הַנֶּ֘גַע֮ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי֒ וְהִנֵּה֙ לֹא־פָשָׂ֣ה הַנֶּ֔תֶק וְלֹא־הָ֥יָה ב֖וֹ שֵׂעָ֣ר צָהֹ֑ב וּמַרְאֵ֣ה הַנֶּ֔תֶק אֵ֥ין עָמֹ֖ק מִן־הָעֽוֹר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall see the skin-eruption on the seventh day, and behold! the *nesek* did not spread and there was no golden hair in it and the appearance of the *nesek* is not deeper than the skin.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and on the seventh day the priest shall look on the plague: and, behold, if the patch spread not, and there be in it no yellow hair, and the patch be not in sight deeper than the skin;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall see the plague-spot on the seventh day, and, behold, if the nethek has not spread, and there is no yellow hair in it [(but if it did spread or there were yellow hair, he is unclean)], and the appearance of the nethek is not deeper than the skin,


###### Leviticus 13:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:33)

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall be shaved but he shall not shave the *nesek,* and the *kohein* shall confine the *nesek* for a second seven day period.

he shall be shaved, but the patch shall not be shaved; and the priest shall shut up him that has the patch seven days more:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[(He leaves a border two hairs (thick) roundabout it, so that if it spreads, it will pass that border into the shaven area and be discernible (as having spread)]. And the Cohein shall quarantine the nethek a second seven days.


###### Leviticus 13:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:34)


וְרָאָה֩ הַכֹּהֵ֨ן אֶת־הַנֶּ֜תֶק בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִ֗י וְ֠הִנֵּ֠ה לֹא־פָשָׂ֤ה הַנֶּ֙תֶק֙ בָּע֔וֹר וּמַרְאֵ֕הוּ אֵינֶ֥נּוּ עָמֹ֖ק מִן־הָע֑וֹר וְטִהַ֤ר אֹתוֹ֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְכִבֶּ֥ס בְּגָדָ֖יו וְטָהֵֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall see the *nesek* on the seventh day and behold! if the *nesek* did not spread in the skin and its appearance is not deeper than the skin. The *kohein* shall declare him clean. He shall wash his garments and he will be clean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and on the seventh day the priest shall look on the patch: and, behold, if the patch be not spread in the skin, nor be in sight deeper than the skin; then the priest shall pronounce him clean: and he shall wash his clothes, and be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall see the nethek on the seventh day. And, behold, if the nethek has not spread in the skin, and its appearance is not deeper than the skin, then the Cohein shall declare him clean, and he shall wash his clothes and he shall be clean.

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the *nesek* spreads [extensively] in the skin after he is pronounced clean,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
the *kohein* shall see him and behold! the *nesek* has spread in the skin. The *kohein* shall not [even] look for golden hair, he is unclean.

then the priest shall look on him: and, behold, if the patch be spread in the skin, the priest shall not seek for yellow hair; he is unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

When a man or a woman have in the skin of their flesh—white spots.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

If a mans hair falls out, he is [merely] bald; he is clean.

If, on the front of his head his hair falls out, he is [merely] frontally bald; he is clean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if from the front of his face [from the slope of his head towards the face, including the temples (gabachath)], his hair fall out, he is gibeach (bald); he is clean.

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If there will be, in the baldness in back of the head or in the baldness in front of the head a white skin-eruption, tinged with red, it is *tzoraas* sprouting in his frontal baldness or in his posterior baldness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if there be in the bald head, or bald forehead, a white reddish sore; it is żara῾at sprung up in his bald head, or his bald forehead.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if there be on the karachath or on the gabachath a reddish-white plague-spot, it is blossoming leprosy on his karachath or on his gabachath.

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall see him, and, behold, a rising of the plague-spot, reddish-white, is in his karachath or in his gabachath, as the appearance of the leprosy of the skin of the flesh, [all the laws of this type of leprosy applying here (as opposed to those of boil leprosy and burn leprosy)],

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**

he is diseased, he is unclean: the priest shall pronounce him utterly unclean; his plague is in his head.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He is a leprous man; he is unclean. The Cohein shall declare him unclean, unclean [i.e., all lepers (and not only one with head-related leprosy) is included in what follows (45, etc.)]; in his head is his plague [viz., nethek leprosy].

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the diseased man in whom the plague is, his clothes shall be rent, and the hair of his head shall grow long, and he shall put a covering upon his upper lip, and shall cry, Unclean, unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the leper in whom the plague-spot is found, his clothes shall be rent and his hair shall grow long, and the hair of his lips shall he cover, [like a mourner], and "Unclean! Unclean!" shall he cry, [so that people keep away from him].

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the days that the plague-spot is in him he shall be unclean. He is unclean. Solitary shall he sit [Other unclean ones shall not sit with him. Because he, through his slander (for which leprosy is the punishment,) "separated" a husband from his wife and a man from his neighbor, he, too, shall be separated (from his fellow men)]; outside the camp [the three camps] is his dwelling.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or if it is the warp [lengthwise] threads, or in the woof [widthwise] threads, in a linen garment, or a woolen garment, or on leather or in any object made of leather.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
whether it be in the warp, or woof; of linen, or of woollen; whether in a skin, or in anything made of skin;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Leviticus 13:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:49)


וְהָיָ֨ה הַנֶּ֜גַע יְרַקְרַ֣ק**׀** א֣וֹ אֲדַמְדָּ֗ם בַּבֶּ֩גֶד֩ א֨וֹ בָע֜וֹר אֽוֹ־בַשְּׁתִ֤י אוֹ־בָעֵ֙רֶב֙ א֣וֹ בְכׇל־כְּלִי־ע֔וֹר נֶ֥גַע צָרַ֖עַת ה֑וּא וְהׇרְאָ֖ה אֶת־הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the plague is deep green or deep red, in the garment, or in the leather hide or in the warp threads, or in the woof threads, or if it is in any object made of leather; it is the eruption of *tzoraas* and it shall be shown to the *kohein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and if the plague be greenish or reddish in the garment or in the skin, either in the warp, or in the woof, or in anything of skin; it is a plague of żara῾at, and shall be shown to the priest:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, if the plague-spot is deep green or deep red in the garment or in the skin, or in the warp or in the woof, or in any article of skin, it is a plague-spot of leprosy, and it shall be shown to the Cohein.


###### Leviticus 13:50

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall see the plague and confine the eruption, for seven days.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall look at the plague, and shut up that which has the plague seven days:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
