###### Leviticus 13:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:51)


וְרָאָ֨ה אֶת־הַנֶּ֜גַע בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִ֗י כִּֽי־פָשָׂ֤ה הַנֶּ֙גַע֙ בַּ֠בֶּ֠גֶד אֽוֹ־בַשְּׁתִ֤י אֽוֹ־בָעֵ֙רֶב֙ א֣וֹ בָע֔וֹר לְכֹ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־יֵעָשֶׂ֥ה הָע֖וֹר לִמְלָאכָ֑ה צָרַ֧עַת מַמְאֶ֛רֶת הַנֶּ֖גַע טָמֵ֥א הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall see the eruption on the seventh day. If the eruption has spread in the garment or in the warp thread, or in the woof thread, or in the leather hide, or in the work made of leather, it is a prickly *tzoraas,* it is unclean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall look at the plague on the seventh day: if the plague be spread in the garment, either in the warp, or in the woof, or in a skin, or in any work that is made of skin; the plague is a malignant żara῾at; it is unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall see the plague-spot on the seventh day. If the plague-spot has spread in the garment, or in the warp, or in the woof, or in the skin, (whatever work the skin is made for), the plague-spot is "pricking" leprosy; it is unclean.


###### Leviticus 13:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:52)


וְשָׂרַ֨ף אֶת־הַבֶּ֜גֶד א֥וֹ אֶֽת־הַשְּׁתִ֣י**׀** א֣וֹ אֶת־הָעֵ֗רֶב בַּצֶּ֙מֶר֙ א֣וֹ בַפִּשְׁתִּ֔ים א֚וֹ אֶת־כׇּל־כְּלִ֣י הָע֔וֹר אֲשֶׁר־יִהְיֶ֥ה ב֖וֹ הַנָּ֑גַע כִּֽי־צָרַ֤עַת מַמְאֶ֙רֶת֙ הִ֔וא בָּאֵ֖שׁ תִּשָּׂרֵֽף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall burn the garment, or the warp [lengthwise] threads, or the woof [widthwise] threads, be it in wool or in linen, or in all leather made objects, in which there is the eruption. For it is a prickly *tzoraas.* It shall be burned in fire.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall therefore burn that garment, whether warp or woof, in woollen or in linen, or anything of skin, wherein the plague is: for it is a malignant żara῾at; it shall be burnt in the fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall burn the garment, or the warp, or the woof, of wool or of linen, or any article of skin in which the plague-spot will be; for it is "pricking" leprosy — it shall be burned in fire.


###### Leviticus 13:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:53)


וְאִם֮ יִרְאֶ֣ה הַכֹּהֵן֒ וְהִנֵּה֙ לֹא־פָשָׂ֣ה הַנֶּ֔גַע בַּבֶּ֕גֶד א֥וֹ בַשְּׁתִ֖י א֣וֹ בָעֵ֑רֶב א֖וֹ בְּכׇל־כְּלִי־עֽוֹר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the *kohein* will see, and behold! the eruption did not spread in the garment, or in the warp threads, or in the woof threads, or in any leather made object.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the priest shall look, and, behold, the plague be not spread in the garment, either in the warp, or in the woof, or in anything of skin;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the Cohein shall see, and, behold, the plague-spot has not spread in the garment, or in the warp, or in the woof, or in any article of skin,


###### Leviticus 13:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:54)


וְצִוָּה֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְכִ֨בְּס֔וּ אֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־בּ֖וֹ הַנָּ֑גַע וְהִסְגִּיר֥וֹ שִׁבְעַת־יָמִ֖ים שֵׁנִֽית׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall command that they shall wash the article having the eruption, and confine it for seven days, a second time.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then the priest shall command that they wash the thing in which the plague is, and he shall shut it up seven days more:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the Cohein shall command, and they shall wash what contains the plague-spot [i.e., the plague spot along with some of the surrounding material], and he shall quarantine it a second seven days.


###### Leviticus 13:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:55)


וְרָאָ֨ה הַכֹּהֵ֜ן אַחֲרֵ֣י**׀** הֻכַּבֵּ֣ס אֶת־הַנֶּ֗גַע וְ֠הִנֵּ֠ה לֹֽא־הָפַ֨ךְ הַנֶּ֤גַע אֶת־עֵינוֹ֙ וְהַנֶּ֣גַע לֹֽא־פָשָׂ֔ה טָמֵ֣א ה֔וּא בָּאֵ֖שׁ תִּשְׂרְפֶ֑נּוּ פְּחֶ֣תֶת הִ֔וא בְּקָרַחְתּ֖וֹ א֥וֹ בְגַבַּחְתּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall see it after the eruption was washed and behold! [even if] the eruption did not change its hue and the eruption did not spread; it is unclean, you shall burn it in fire. It is a deep mark be it in a worn [garment] or in a new [garment].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall look after the plague is washed: and, behold, if the plague have not changed its colour, and the plague be not spread; it is unclean; thou shalt burn it in the fire; it is a decay on the inner or outer surface.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall see, after the plague-spot has been washed, and, behold, the plague-spot has not changed its appearance [i.e., it has not dimmed] and the plague-spot has not spread, it is unclean. [(It goes without saying that it is unclean if it did not change its appearance and did spread)]. In fire shall you burn it; it is p'cheteth [a "low-lying" plague-spot] in its oldness or in its newness.


###### Leviticus 13:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:56)


וְאִם֮ רָאָ֣ה הַכֹּהֵן֒ וְהִנֵּה֙ כֵּהָ֣ה הַנֶּ֔גַע אַחֲרֵ֖י הֻכַּבֵּ֣ס אֹת֑וֹ וְקָרַ֣ע אֹת֗וֹ מִן־הַבֶּ֙גֶד֙ א֣וֹ מִן־הָע֔וֹר א֥וֹ מִן־הַשְּׁתִ֖י א֥וֹ מִן־הָעֵֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the *kohein* saw and behold! the eruption grew faint after it was washed; he shall tear it from the garment, or from the leather, or from the warp threads or from the woof threads.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the priest look, and, behold, the plague be somewhat dimmer after the washing of it; then he shall tear it out of the garment, or out of the skin, or out of the warp, or out of the woof:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the Cohein saw, and, behold, the plague-spot became dim after it was washed, then he shall tear it [the plague-spot site] from the garment, or from the skin, or from the warp, or from the woof, [and he shall burn it.]


###### Leviticus 13:57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:57)


וְאִם־תֵּרָאֶ֨ה ע֜וֹד בַּ֠בֶּ֠גֶד אֽוֹ־בַשְּׁתִ֤י אֽוֹ־בָעֵ֙רֶב֙ א֣וֹ בְכׇל־כְּלִי־ע֔וֹר פֹּרַ֖חַת הִ֑וא בָּאֵ֣שׁ תִּשְׂרְפֶ֔נּוּ אֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־בּ֖וֹ הַנָּֽגַע׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If it will appear again, in the garment or in the warp [lengthwise] threads, or in the woof [widthwise] threads, or in any leather-made object, it is a recurrent outbreak of *tzoraas.* You shall burn the article having the eruption in fire.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and if it appear still in the garment, either in the warp, or in the woof, or in anything of skin; it is a spreading plague: thou shalt burn that in which the plague is with fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if it be seen again in the garment, or in the warp, or in the woof, or in any article of skin, it is porachath [something whose "blossoming" recurs]. In fire shall you burn it [(the entire garment)], what contains the plague-spot.


###### Leviticus 13:58

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:58](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:58)


וְהַבֶּ֡גֶד אֽוֹ־הַשְּׁתִ֨י אוֹ־הָעֵ֜רֶב אֽוֹ־כׇל־כְּלִ֤י הָעוֹר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תְּכַבֵּ֔ס וְסָ֥ר מֵהֶ֖ם הַנָּ֑גַע וְכֻבַּ֥ס שֵׁנִ֖ית וְטָהֵֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The garment, or the warp threads, or the woof threads, or any leather objects that were washed and the eruption disappears from them, it shall be washed a second time and it shall be purified.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the garment, either warp, or woof, or whatever thing of skin it be, which thou shalt wash, if the plague be departed from them, then it shall be washed the second time, and shall be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the garment, or the warp, or the woof, or any article of skin which you shall wash [by directive of the Cohein], and the plague-spot depart [entirely] from them, then it shall be cleansed [i.e., immersed] a second time, and it shall be clean.


###### Leviticus 13:59

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:59](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/13:59)


זֹ֠את תּוֹרַ֨ת נֶֽגַע־צָרַ֜עַת בֶּ֥גֶד הַצֶּ֣מֶר**׀** א֣וֹ הַפִּשְׁתִּ֗ים א֤וֹ הַשְּׁתִי֙ א֣וֹ הָעֵ֔רֶב א֖וֹ כׇּל־כְּלִי־ע֑וֹר לְטַהֲר֖וֹ א֥וֹ לְטַמְּאֽוֹ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the law of the eruption of *tzoraas* be it in a woolen garment, or a linen garment, or in the warp [lengthwise] thread, or in the woof [widthwise] thread, or in any leather-made object, that it be pronounced pure, or that it be pronounced impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the Tora of the plague of żara῾at in a garment of woollen or linen, either in the warp, or woof, or anything of skins, to pronounce it clean, or to pronounce it unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the law of the plague-spot of leprosy, in a garment of wool or linen, or in the warp, or in the woof, or in any article of skin, to declare it clean or to declare it unclean.



## Chapter 14

###### Leviticus 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:2)


זֹ֤את תִּֽהְיֶה֙ תּוֹרַ֣ת הַמְּצֹרָ֔ע בְּי֖וֹם טׇהֳרָת֑וֹ וְהוּבָ֖א אֶל־הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This shall be the law regarding the person with *tzoraas* on the day of his purification, he shall be brought to the *kohein*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This shall be the Tora of the one stricken with żara῾at in the day of his cleansing: He shall be brought to the priest:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This shall be the law of the leper on the day of his cleansing [(whence it is derived that he is not cleansed at night)]; he shall be brought to the Cohein.


###### Leviticus 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:3)


וְיָצָא֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן אֶל־מִח֖וּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶ֑ה וְרָאָה֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְהִנֵּ֛ה נִרְפָּ֥א נֶֽגַע־הַצָּרַ֖עַת מִן־הַצָּרֽוּעַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall go out beyond the encampment. The *kohein* shall see; and behold! the skin-eruption of *tzoraas* has healed from the person with *tzoraas*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall go out of the camp; and the priest shall look, and, behold, if the plague of żara῾at be healed in the one afflicted;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall go outside the camp [(outside the three camps, where the leper was sent in the days of his confirmed leprosy)], and the Cohein shall see, and, behold, if the plague-spot of leprosy is healed from the leper,


###### Leviticus 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:4)


וְצִוָּה֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְלָקַ֧ח לַמִּטַּהֵ֛ר שְׁתֵּֽי־צִפֳּרִ֥ים חַיּ֖וֹת טְהֹר֑וֹת וְעֵ֣ץ אֶ֔רֶז וּשְׁנִ֥י תוֹלַ֖עַת וְאֵזֹֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall command to take for the person undergoing purification two live, pure [kosher] birds, cedar wood, crimson thread and hyssop.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then shall the priest command to take for him that is to be cleansed two birds alive and clean, and cedar wood, and scarlet, and hyssop:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the Cohein shall command that there be taken for the one to be cleansed two clean, living [(excluding treifah)] birds. ([Leprosy, coming upon one because of slander, birds ("chirpers") are brought)], and cedar-wood [(leprosy coming upon one (also) because of pride)], and [a tongue of] wool (dyed) scarlet (sh'ni tola'ath), and hyssop. [What is his "remedy"? Let him "lower" himself, like a worm (tola'ath) and like hyssop.]


###### Leviticus 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:5)


וְצִוָּה֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְשָׁחַ֖ט אֶת־הַצִּפּ֣וֹר הָאֶחָ֑ת אֶל־כְּלִי־חֶ֖רֶשׂ עַל־מַ֥יִם חַיִּֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall command to slaughter the one bird in an earthenware vessel, over running water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall command that one of the birds be killed in an earthen vessel over running water:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall command and he shall slaughter one bird into an earthen vessel over living water [He places the water (a quarter of a log) in the vessel first so that the blood of the bird be discernible in it.]


###### Leviticus 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:6)


אֶת־הַצִּפֹּ֤ר הַֽחַיָּה֙ יִקַּ֣ח אֹתָ֔הּ וְאֶת־עֵ֥ץ הָאֶ֛רֶז וְאֶת־שְׁנִ֥י הַתּוֹלַ֖עַת וְאֶת־הָאֵזֹ֑ב וְטָבַ֨ל אוֹתָ֜ם וְאֵ֣ת**׀** הַצִּפֹּ֣ר הַֽחַיָּ֗ה בְּדַם֙ הַצִּפֹּ֣ר הַשְּׁחֻטָ֔ה עַ֖ל הַמַּ֥יִם הַֽחַיִּֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[As for] the living bird, he shall take it; and the cedar wood, the crimson thread and the hyssop and immerse them and the live bird in the blood of the slaughtered bird, over the running water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
as for the living bird, he shall take it, and the cedar wood, and the scarlet, and the hyssop, and shall dip them and the living bird in the blood of the bird that was killed over the running water:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The living bird, he shall take it [by itself] and the cedar-wood, and the scarlet wool, and the hyssop [(all three bound together)], and he shall dip them and the living bird [together] in the blood of the slaughtered bird over the living water.


###### Leviticus 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:7)


וְהִזָּ֗ה עַ֧ל הַמִּטַּהֵ֛ר מִן־הַצָּרַ֖עַת שֶׁ֣בַע פְּעָמִ֑ים וְטִ֣הֲר֔וֹ וְשִׁלַּ֛ח אֶת־הַצִּפֹּ֥ר הַֽחַיָּ֖ה עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הַשָּׂדֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall sprinkle on the person undergoing purification from the *tzoraas*, seven times and make him pure; and send the live bird into the open field.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall sprinkle upon him that is to be cleansed from the żara῾at seven times, and shall pronounce him clean, and shall let the living bird loose into the open field.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall sprinkle upon the one to be cleansed from the leprosy seven times, and he shall cleanse him, and he shall send the living bird over the face of the field.


###### Leviticus 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:8)


וְכִבֶּס֩ הַמִּטַּהֵ֨ר אֶת־בְּגָדָ֜יו וְגִלַּ֣ח אֶת־כׇּל־שְׂעָר֗וֹ וְרָחַ֤ץ בַּמַּ֙יִם֙ וְטָהֵ֔ר וְאַחַ֖ר יָב֣וֹא אֶל־הַֽמַּחֲנֶ֑ה וְיָשַׁ֛ב מִח֥וּץ לְאׇהֳל֖וֹ שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The person undergoing purification shall wash his garments, and shave off all of his hair, and bathe in water, and he will become purified. After [this], he may come into the encampment. He must remain, outside of his tent, for seven days.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that is to be cleansed shall wash his clothes, and shave off all his hair, and bathe himself in water, and be clean: and after that he shall come into the camp, but he shall remain outside his tent seven days.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the one to be cleansed shall wash his clothes, and he shall shave all of his hair, and he shall bathe in water, and he shall be clean; and he shall come into the camp. And he shall sit outside his tent [(i.e., he shall abstain from marital relations)] for seven days.


###### Leviticus 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:9)


וְהָיָה֩ בַיּ֨וֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִ֜י יְגַלַּ֣ח אֶת־כׇּל־שְׂעָר֗וֹ אֶת־רֹאשׁ֤וֹ וְאֶת־זְקָנוֹ֙ וְאֵת֙ גַּבֹּ֣ת עֵינָ֔יו וְאֶת־כׇּל־שְׂעָר֖וֹ יְגַלֵּ֑חַ וְכִבֶּ֣ס אֶת־בְּגָדָ֗יו וְרָחַ֧ץ אֶת־בְּשָׂר֛וֹ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָהֵֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It shall be, that on the seventh day, he shall shave off all of his hair, [that of] his head, his beard and his eyebrows; he shall shave off all of his hair. He shall wash his garments, and bathe his body in water and become purified.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be on the seventh day, that he shall shave all his hair off his head and his beard and his eyebrows, even all his hair he shall shave off: and he shall bathe his clothes, also he shall wash his flesh in water, and he shall be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be on the seventh day that he shall shave all of his hair. His head, his beard, and the brows of his eyes — all of his [visible, "gathered"] hair shall he shave. And he shall wash his clothes, and he shall bathe his flesh in water, and he shall be clean.


###### Leviticus 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:10)


וּבַיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֗י יִקַּ֤ח שְׁנֵֽי־כְבָשִׂים֙ תְּמִימִ֔ם וְכַבְשָׂ֥ה אַחַ֛ת בַּת־שְׁנָתָ֖הּ תְּמִימָ֑ה וּשְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה עֶשְׂרֹנִ֗ים סֹ֤לֶת מִנְחָה֙ בְּלוּלָ֣ה בַשֶּׁ֔מֶן וְלֹ֥ג אֶחָ֖ד שָֽׁמֶן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the eighth day, he shall take two unblemished lambs and one ewe in her first year, unblemished, and three tenths fine flour, for a meal-offering, mixed with oil and one *log* of oil.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And on the eighth day he shall take two he lambs without blemish, and one ewe lamb of the first year without blemish, and three tenth measures of fine flour for a meal offering, mingled with oil, and one log of oil.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And on the eighth day he shall take two he-lambs without blemish, and one ewe-lamb of the first year, without blemish [for a sin-offering], and three tenth parts of (an ephah of) fine flour for a meal-offering, mixed with oil, [for the libations of these three lambs], and one log of oil [for the seven sprinklings and the applications on ear and thumbs].


###### Leviticus 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:11)


וְהֶעֱמִ֞יד הַכֹּהֵ֣ן הַֽמְטַהֵ֗ר אֵ֛ת הָאִ֥ישׁ הַמִּטַּהֵ֖ר וְאֹתָ֑ם לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* doing the purification, shall place the man undergoing the purification and the [aforementioned] items, before Adonoy, at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest that makes him clean shall present the man that is to be made clean, and those things, before the Lord, at the door of the Tent of Meeting:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein that cleanses shall stand the man who is to be cleansed and those (things) before the L-rd [(at the gate of Nikanor, not in the azarah itself, for he lacks atonement)], at the door of the tent of meeting.


###### Leviticus 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:12)


וְלָקַ֨ח הַכֹּהֵ֜ן אֶת־הַכֶּ֣בֶשׂ הָאֶחָ֗ד וְהִקְרִ֥יב אֹת֛וֹ לְאָשָׁ֖ם וְאֶת־לֹ֣ג הַשָּׁ֑מֶן וְהֵנִ֥יף אֹתָ֛ם תְּנוּפָ֖ה לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall take one lamb and bring it as a guilt-offering, and (with) the *log* of oil and wave them as a wave-offering before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall take one he lamb, and offer it for a guilt offering, and the log of oil, and wave them for a wave offering before the Lord:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall take one of the he-lambs, and he shall bring it near [into the azarah] as a guilt-offering [to be waved (live)], and the log of oil, and he shall wave them [the guilt-offering and the log] before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:13)


וְשָׁחַ֣ט אֶת־הַכֶּ֗בֶשׂ בִּ֠מְק֠וֹם אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִשְׁחַ֧ט אֶת־הַֽחַטָּ֛את וְאֶת־הָעֹלָ֖ה בִּמְק֣וֹם הַקֹּ֑דֶשׁ כִּ֡י כַּ֠חַטָּ֠את הָאָשָׁ֥ם הוּא֙ לַכֹּהֵ֔ן קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִׁ֖ים הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall slaughter the lamb in the place where he slaughters the sin-offering and the burnt-offering, in the sacred place; for, just as the sin-offering—is the guilt-offering; it belongs to the *kohein*, it is most holy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall slaughter the lamb in the place where he shall kill the sin offering and the burnt offering, in the holy place: for as the sin offering is the priest’s, so is the guilt offering: it is most holy:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall slaughter the lamb in the place where he slaughters the sin-offering and the burnt-offering [(on the side of the altar in the north)], in the holy place. For as the sin-offering [i.e., as all sin-offerings], the guilt-offering [i.e., this guilt-offering] is the Cohein's [i.e., it is subject to all of the Cohein's services for a sin-offering. But (unlike a sin-offering, its blood is not applied on the altar above (but it is dashed roundabout the altar below, for)] it is holy of holies. [see 7:2].


###### Leviticus 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:14)


וְלָקַ֣ח הַכֹּהֵן֮ מִדַּ֣ם הָאָשָׁם֒ וְנָתַן֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן עַל־תְּנ֛וּךְ אֹ֥זֶן הַמִּטַּהֵ֖ר הַיְמָנִ֑ית וְעַל־בֹּ֤הֶן יָדוֹ֙ הַיְמָנִ֔ית וְעַל־בֹּ֥הֶן רַגְל֖וֹ הַיְמָנִֽית׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall take from the blood of the guilt-offering and the *kohein* shall put it on the middle ridge of the right ear of the person being purified, and on the thumb of his right hand and on the big toe of his right foot.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall take some of the blood of the guilt offering, and the priest shall put it upon the tip of the right ear of him that is to be cleansed, and upon the thumb of his right hand, and upon the great toe of his right foot:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall take from the blood of the guilt-offering, and the Cohein shall place it on the tnuch [the middle wall] of the right ear of the one to be cleansed, and on the thumb of his right hand, and on the big toe of his right foot.


###### Leviticus 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:15)


וְלָקַ֥ח הַכֹּהֵ֖ן מִלֹּ֣ג הַשָּׁ֑מֶן וְיָצַ֛ק עַל־כַּ֥ף הַכֹּהֵ֖ן הַשְּׂמָאלִֽית׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall take from the *log* of the oil and he shall pour it onto the koheins left palm.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall take some of the log of oil, and pour it into the palm of his own left hand:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall take from the log of oil, and he shall pour onto the Cohein's left palm.


###### Leviticus 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:16)


וְטָבַ֤ל הַכֹּהֵן֙ אֶת־אֶצְבָּע֣וֹ הַיְמָנִ֔ית מִן־הַשֶּׁ֕מֶן אֲשֶׁ֥ר עַל־כַּפּ֖וֹ הַשְּׂמָאלִ֑ית וְהִזָּ֨ה מִן־הַשֶּׁ֧מֶן בְּאֶצְבָּע֛וֹ שֶׁ֥בַע פְּעָמִ֖ים לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall immerse his right finger in some of the oil that is on his left palm and he shall sprinkle some of the oil with his finger seven times before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall dip his right finger in the oil that is in his left hand, and shall sprinkle of the oil with his finger seven times before the Lord:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall dip his right finger from the oil on his left palm, and he shall sprinkle from the oil with his finger seven times before the L-rd [i.e., opposite the holy of holies].


###### Leviticus 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:17)


וּמִיֶּ֨תֶר הַשֶּׁ֜מֶן אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־כַּפּ֗וֹ יִתֵּ֤ן הַכֹּהֵן֙ עַל־תְּנ֞וּךְ אֹ֤זֶן הַמִּטַּהֵר֙ הַיְמָנִ֔ית וְעַל־בֹּ֤הֶן יָדוֹ֙ הַיְמָנִ֔ית וְעַל־בֹּ֥הֶן רַגְל֖וֹ הַיְמָנִ֑ית עַ֖ל דַּ֥ם הָאָשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
From the remaining oil that is on his palm, the *kohein* shall put on the middle ridge of the right ear of the person being purified, and on the thumb of his right hand, and on the big toe of his right foot; on [over] the blood of the guilt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and of the rest of the oil that is in his hand shall the priest put upon the tip of the right ear of him that is to be cleansed, and upon the thumb of his right hand, and upon the great toe of his right foot, upon the blood of the guilt offering:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from the rest of the oil which is on his palm, the Cohein shall place on the tnuch of the right ear of the one to be cleansed, and on the thumb of his right hand, and on the big toe of his right foot, upon the blood of the guilt-offering.


###### Leviticus 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:18)


וְהַנּוֹתָ֗ר בַּשֶּׁ֙מֶן֙ אֲשֶׁר֙ עַל־כַּ֣ף הַכֹּהֵ֔ן יִתֵּ֖ן עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ הַמִּטַּהֵ֑ר וְכִפֶּ֥ר עָלָ֛יו הַכֹּהֵ֖ן לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whatever oil remains that is on the palm of the *kohein*, he shall put on the head of the person undergoing purification. The *kohein* will atone for him before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the remnant of the oil that is in the priest’s hand he shall pour upon the head of him that is to be cleansed: and the priest shall make atonement for him before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And what is left over of the oil which is on the palm of the Cohein, he shall place on the head of the one who is to be cleansed, and the Cohein shall atone for him before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:19)


וְעָשָׂ֤ה הַכֹּהֵן֙ אֶת־הַ֣חַטָּ֔את וְכִפֶּ֕ר עַל־הַמִּטַּהֵ֖ר מִטֻּמְאָת֑וֹ וְאַחַ֖ר יִשְׁחַ֥ט אֶת־הָעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall do (offer) the sin-offering and atone for the person undergoing purification from his impurity and afterwards he shall slaughter the burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall offer the sin offering, and make atonement for him that is to be cleansed from his uncleanness; and afterwards he shall kill the burnt offering:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall offer up the sin-offering, and he shall atone for the one to be cleansed from his uncleanliness, and then he shall slaughter the burnt-offering.


###### Leviticus 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:20)


וְהֶעֱלָ֧ה הַכֹּהֵ֛ן אֶת־הָעֹלָ֥ה וְאֶת־הַמִּנְחָ֖ה הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חָה וְכִפֶּ֥ר עָלָ֛יו הַכֹּהֵ֖ן וְטָהֵֽר׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall raise up the burnt-offering and the meal-offering onto the altar and the *kohein* will atone for him and he will be purified.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall offer the burnt offering and the meal offering upon the altar: and the priest shall make atonement for him, and he shall be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall offer up on the altar the burnt-offering and the [libation] meal-offering [which accompanies an animal sacrifice], and the Cohein shall make atonement for him, and he shall be clean.


###### Leviticus 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:21)


וְאִם־דַּ֣ל ה֗וּא וְאֵ֣ין יָדוֹ֮ מַשֶּׂ֒גֶת֒ וְ֠לָקַ֠ח כֶּ֣בֶשׂ אֶחָ֥ד אָשָׁ֛ם לִתְנוּפָ֖ה לְכַפֵּ֣ר עָלָ֑יו וְעִשָּׂר֨וֹן סֹ֜לֶת אֶחָ֨ד בָּל֥וּל בַּשֶּׁ֛מֶן לְמִנְחָ֖ה וְלֹ֥ג שָֽׁמֶן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he is poor and his means are not sufficient, he shall take one lamb as a guilt-offering, for a wave-offering to atone for him and one tenth fine flour mixed with oil, as a meal-offering and a *log* of oil.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if he be poor, and his means do not suffice; then he shall take one lamb for a guilt offering to be waved, to make atonement for him, and one tenth measure of fine flour mingled with oil for a meal offering, and a log of oil;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if he is poor and his means do not suffice, then he shall take one he-lamb as a guilt-offering to be waved to make atonement for him, and one tenth-part of fine flour mixed with oil as a meal-offering, and a log of oil [for application on the thumbs].


###### Leviticus 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:22)


וּשְׁתֵּ֣י תֹרִ֗ים א֤וֹ שְׁנֵי֙ בְּנֵ֣י יוֹנָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר תַּשִּׂ֖יג יָד֑וֹ וְהָיָ֤ה אֶחָד֙ חַטָּ֔את וְהָאֶחָ֖ד עֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And two turtledoves or two young pigeons such as his means suffice. One shall be for a sin-offering and one shall be for a burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and two turtledoves, or two young pigeons, such as his means permit; and the one shall be a sin offering, and the other a burnt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And two turtle-doves or two young pigeons, such as his means suffice for; and one shall be a sin-offering, and the other, a burnt-offering.


###### Leviticus 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:23)


וְהֵבִ֨יא אֹתָ֜ם בַּיּ֧וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֛י לְטׇהֳרָת֖וֹ אֶל־הַכֹּהֵ֑ן אֶל־פֶּ֥תַח אֹֽהֶל־מוֹעֵ֖ד לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring them on the eighth day of his purification, to the *kohein*, to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall bring them on the eighth day for his cleansing to the priest, to the door of the Tent of Meeting, before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall bring them to the Cohein on the eighth day of his cleansing [i.e., the eighth day after the bringing of the birds and the sprinkling from the (dipping of the) cedar-wood, the hyssop, and the scarlet] to the door of the tent of meeting before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:24)


וְלָקַ֧ח הַכֹּהֵ֛ן אֶת־כֶּ֥בֶשׂ הָאָשָׁ֖ם וְאֶת־לֹ֣ג הַשָּׁ֑מֶן וְהֵנִ֨יף אֹתָ֧ם הַכֹּהֵ֛ן תְּנוּפָ֖ה לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall take the guilt-offering sheep and the *log* of oil and the *kohein* shall wave them as a wave-offering before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall take the lamb of the guilt offering, and the log of oil, and the priest shall wave them for a wave offering before the Lord:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall take the lamb of the guilt-offering, and the log of oil, and the Cohein shall wave them, a wave-offering before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:25)


וְשָׁחַט֮ אֶת־כֶּ֣בֶשׂ הָֽאָשָׁם֒ וְלָקַ֤ח הַכֹּהֵן֙ מִדַּ֣ם הָֽאָשָׁ֔ם וְנָתַ֛ן עַל־תְּנ֥וּךְ אֹֽזֶן־הַמִּטַּהֵ֖ר הַיְמָנִ֑ית וְעַל־בֹּ֤הֶן יָדוֹ֙ הַיְמָנִ֔ית וְעַל־בֹּ֥הֶן רַגְל֖וֹ הַיְמָנִֽית׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall slaughter the guilt-offering lamb. The *kohein* shall take some blood of the guilt-offering and put it on the middle ridge of the right ear of the person undergoing purification, and on the thumb of his right hand and the big toe of his right foot.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall kill the lamb of the guilt offering, and the priest shall take some of the blood of the guilt offering, and put it upon the tip of the right ear of him that is to be cleansed, and upon the thumb of his right hand, and upon the great toe of his right foot:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall slaughter the lamb of the guilt-offering, and the Cohein shall take from the blood of the guilt-offering and place it on the tnuch of the right ear of the one to be cleansed, and on the thumb of his right hand, and on the big toe of his right foot.


###### Leviticus 14:26

[This verse is to be understood as following (verse 47)] And if the Cohein shall come and see [at the end of the second week (if it remained the same at the end of the first week)] and, behold, the plague-spot has spread in the house, it is "pricking" leprosy in the house; it is unclean [and he "removes, scrapes, plasters" and shuts it up another week. And he shall break down the house, the stones of it, and its timber, and all the mortar of the house; and he shall carry them out of the city into an unclean place.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[See 43] Then he shall break down the house — its stones, its wood, and all the mortar of the house; and he shall take them outside the city to an unclean place.


###### Leviticus 14:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:46)


וְהַבָּא֙ אֶל־הַבַּ֔יִת כׇּל־יְמֵ֖י הִסְגִּ֣יר אֹת֑וֹ יִטְמָ֖א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever will come inside the house [during] all the days of its confinement, shall be impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover he that goes into the house all the while that it is shut up shall be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he who comes into the house all the days [(in a "confirmed" house, even after scraping)] that he has shut it up [(but not while it is undergoing scraping)] shall be unclean until the evening. [The clothes of one who merely "enters," however, are not rendered unclean.]


###### Leviticus 14:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:47)


וְהַשֹּׁכֵ֣ב בַּבַּ֔יִת יְכַבֵּ֖ס אֶת־בְּגָדָ֑יו וְהָאֹכֵ֣ל בַּבַּ֔יִת יְכַבֵּ֖ס אֶת־בְּגָדָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever will lie down inside the house shall wash his garments. Whoever will eat inside the house shall wash his garments.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that lies in the house shall wash his clothes; and he that eats in the house shall wash his clothes.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he who lies in the house shall wash his clothes, and he who eats in the house shall wash his clothes [i.e., one who remains in the house the amount of time it takes to eat a p'ras (a half-loaf) shall wash his clothes].


###### Leviticus 14:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:48)


וְאִם־בֹּ֨א יָבֹ֜א הַכֹּהֵ֗ן וְרָאָה֙ וְ֠הִנֵּ֠ה לֹא־פָשָׂ֤ה הַנֶּ֙גַע֙ בַּבַּ֔יִת אַחֲרֵ֖י הִטֹּ֣חַ אֶת־הַבָּ֑יִת וְטִהַ֤ר הַכֹּהֵן֙ אֶת־הַבַּ֔יִת כִּ֥י נִרְפָּ֖א הַנָּֽגַע׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the *kohein* shall come and see; and behold! the eruption did not spread in the house after the house was plastered, the *kohein* shall pronounce the house [to be] pure, for the eruption has been healed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the priest shall come in, and look upon it, and, behold, the plague has not spread in the house, after the house was plastered: then the priest shall pronounce the house clean, because the plague is healed.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[See 44] And if the Cohein comes [at the end of the second week], and he sees, and, behold, the plague-spot has not spread in the house, [he removes, scrapes, and plasters], and after plastering the house, the Cohein shall declare the house clean [if the plague-spot has not reappeared at the end of the week], for the plague spot has been healed.


###### Leviticus 14:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:49)


וְלָקַ֛ח לְחַטֵּ֥א אֶת־הַבַּ֖יִת שְׁתֵּ֣י צִפֳּרִ֑ים וְעֵ֣ץ אֶ֔רֶז וּשְׁנִ֥י תוֹלַ֖עַת וְאֵזֹֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall take, to purify the house, two birds, cedar wood, crimson thread and hyssop.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall take to cleanse the house two birds, and cedar wood, and scarlet, and hyssop:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall take to cleanse the house two birds, and cedar-wood, and scarlet, and hyssop.


###### Leviticus 14:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:50)


וְשָׁחַ֖ט אֶת־הַצִּפֹּ֣ר הָאֶחָ֑ת אֶל־כְּלִי־חֶ֖רֶשׂ עַל־מַ֥יִם חַיִּֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall slaughter one bird in an earthenware vessel, over running water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall kill one of the birds in an earthen vessel over running water:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall slaughter one bird into a vessel over living water.


###### Leviticus 14:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:51)


וְלָקַ֣ח אֶת־עֵֽץ־הָ֠אֶ֠רֶז וְאֶת־הָ֨אֵזֹ֜ב וְאֵ֣ת**׀** שְׁנִ֣י הַתּוֹלַ֗עַת וְאֵת֮ הַצִּפֹּ֣ר הַֽחַיָּה֒ וְטָבַ֣ל אֹתָ֗ם בְּדַם֙ הַצִּפֹּ֣ר הַשְּׁחוּטָ֔ה וּבַמַּ֖יִם הַֽחַיִּ֑ים וְהִזָּ֥ה אֶל־הַבַּ֖יִת שֶׁ֥בַע פְּעָמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall take the cedar wood, the hyssop, the crimson thread and the live bird and immerse them in the blood of the slaughtered bird and in the running water, and sprinkle the house seven times.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall take the cedar wood, and the hyssop, and the scarlet, and the living bird, and dip them in the blood of the slaughtered bird, and in the running water, and sprinkle the house seven times:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall take the cedar-wood, and the hyssop, and the scarlet, and the living bird, and he shall dip them in the blood of the slaughtered bird and in the living water, and he shall sprinkle upon the house seven times.


###### Leviticus 14:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:52)


וְחִטֵּ֣א אֶת־הַבַּ֔יִת בְּדַם֙ הַצִּפּ֔וֹר וּבַמַּ֖יִם הַֽחַיִּ֑ים וּבַצִּפֹּ֣ר הַחַיָּ֗ה וּבְעֵ֥ץ הָאֶ֛רֶז וּבָאֵזֹ֖ב וּבִשְׁנִ֥י הַתּוֹלָֽעַת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall purify the house with the blood of the bird, and with the running water, and with the live bird, the cedar wood, the hyssop and the crimson thread.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall cleanse the house with the blood of the bird, and with the running water, and with the living bird, and with the cedar wood, and with the hyssop, and with the scarlet:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall cleanse the house with the blood of the bird and with the living water, and with the living bird, and with the cedar-wood, and with the hyssop, and with the scarlet.


###### Leviticus 14:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:53)


וְשִׁלַּ֞ח אֶת־הַצִּפֹּ֧ר הַֽחַיָּ֛ה אֶל־מִח֥וּץ לָעִ֖יר אֶל־פְּנֵ֣י הַשָּׂדֶ֑ה וְכִפֶּ֥ר עַל־הַבַּ֖יִת וְטָהֵֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall send the living bird, beyond the city into the open field, and atone for the house and it shall be purified.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but he shall let go the living bird out of the city into the open fields, and make atonement for the house: and it shall be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall send the living bird outside the city over the face of the field, and he shall make atonement for the house, and it shall be clean.


###### Leviticus 14:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:54)


זֹ֖את הַתּוֹרָ֑ה לְכׇל־נֶ֥גַע הַצָּרַ֖עַת וְלַנָּֽתֶק׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the law for all the eruptions of *tzoraas* and for the *nesek*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the Tora for all manner of plague of żara῾at, and the patch,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the law for all plague-spots of leprosy and for a nethek.


###### Leviticus 14:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:55)


וּלְצָרַ֥עַת הַבֶּ֖גֶד וְלַבָּֽיִת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For *tzoraas* of the garment and of the house.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and for the żara῾at of a garment, and of a house,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for the leprosy of a garment and of a house.


###### Leviticus 14:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:56)


וְלַשְׂאֵ֥ת וְלַסַּפַּ֖חַת וְלַבֶּהָֽרֶת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For the spot white as wool, for the spot that is nearly as white and for the spot white as snow.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and for a swelling, and for a scab, and for a bright spot:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for a se'eth and for a sapachath and for a bahereth.


###### Leviticus 14:57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/14:57)


לְהוֹרֹ֕ת בְּי֥וֹם הַטָּמֵ֖א וּבְי֣וֹם הַטָּהֹ֑ר זֹ֥את תּוֹרַ֖ת הַצָּרָֽעַת׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
To teach [the law] of the day of becoming impure and of the day of becoming pure; this is the law of *tzoraas*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to teach when it is unclean, and when it is clean: this is the Tora of żara῾at.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To teach concerning the day it is declared unclean and the day it is declared clean. This is the law of leprosy.



## Chapter 15

###### Leviticus 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה וְאֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֖ן לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses and to Aaron, saying:


###### Leviticus 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:2)


דַּבְּרוּ֙ אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וַאֲמַרְתֶּ֖ם אֲלֵהֶ֑ם אִ֣ישׁ אִ֗ישׁ כִּ֤י יִהְיֶה֙ זָ֣ב מִבְּשָׂר֔וֹ זוֹב֖וֹ טָמֵ֥א הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them; should [discharge] flow from the body of any man, [because of] his discharge, he is impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, When any man has a running issue out of his flesh, because of his issue he is unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel and say to them: A man, if there be a flux (zov) from his flesh, [a genital discharge differing from semen in that semen is viscid, like the white of an egg, whereas zov is like barley-dough water, resembling the white of an addled egg], his flux is unclean.


###### Leviticus 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:3)


וְזֹ֛את תִּהְיֶ֥ה טֻמְאָת֖וֹ בְּזוֹב֑וֹ רָ֣ר בְּשָׂר֞וֹ אֶת־זוֹב֗וֹ אֽוֹ־הֶחְתִּ֤ים בְּשָׂרוֹ֙ מִזּוֹב֔וֹ טֻמְאָת֖וֹ הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the law of his impurity, when his discharge flows; His flesh drips [with] his discharge or his flesh is stopped up by his discharge, it causes him to become impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this shall be his uncleanness in his issue: whether his flesh run with his issue, or his flesh be stopped from his issue, it is his uncleanness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this shall be his uncleanliness in his flux. Whether his flesh [i.e., his orifice] drip his zov, [the flux issuing clear], or his flesh be stopped up with his zov, [the flux issuing thick], it is his uncleanliness.


###### Leviticus 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:4)


כׇּל־הַמִּשְׁכָּ֗ב אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִשְׁכַּ֥ב עָלָ֛יו הַזָּ֖ב יִטְמָ֑א וְכׇֽל־הַכְּלִ֛י אֲשֶׁר־יֵשֵׁ֥ב עָלָ֖יו יִטְמָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every bed [Anything that is used specifically for sleeping], upon which will lie a *zov* [a person from whom a discharge flows,] shall become impure. Every vessel upon which he sits, shall become impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every bed, whereon he that has the issue lies down, is unclean: and everything, whereon he sits, shall be unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every bed on which the zav lies down [i.e., a bed which is made for "lying" and not for any other function] shall be unclean, and every object which he sits upon [i.e., an object which is made for "sitting"] shall be unclean.


###### Leviticus 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:5)


וְאִ֕ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִגַּ֖ע בְּמִשְׁכָּב֑וֹ יְכַבֵּ֧ס בְּגָדָ֛יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A person who touches his bed shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whoever touches his bed shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man who touches what he (a zav) lay upon shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, [(the uncleanliness of what he lies upon (mishkav) being more stringent than the uncleanliness of what he touches, the first rendering a man unclean, who, in turn, renders his garments unclean; the second, rendering only food and drink unclean], and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:6)


וְהַיֹּשֵׁב֙ עַֽל־הַכְּלִ֔י אֲשֶׁר־יֵשֵׁ֥ב עָלָ֖יו הַזָּ֑ב יְכַבֵּ֧ס בְּגָדָ֛יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever sits on the vessel that was sat upon by a *zov* shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that sits on anything on which he that has the issue sat, shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And one who sits upon an object which the zav sat upon, [even if ten objects intervened (The same applies to mishkav)] shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:7)


וְהַנֹּגֵ֖עַ בִּבְשַׂ֣ר הַזָּ֑ב יְכַבֵּ֧ס בְּגָדָ֛יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever touches the flesh of a *zov* shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that touches the flesh of him that has the issue shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And one who touches the flesh of the zav shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:8)


וְכִֽי־יָרֹ֥ק הַזָּ֖ב בַּטָּה֑וֹר וְכִבֶּ֧ס בְּגָדָ֛יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the *zov* spits on a ritually clean person, he shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if he that has the issue spit upon him that is clean; then he shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the zav spit upon one who is clean, [and the spittle touched him or he carried it], he shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:9)


וְכׇל־הַמֶּרְכָּ֗ב אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִרְכַּ֥ב עָלָ֛יו הַזָּ֖ב יִטְמָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anything that is used for riding upon which a *zov* will ride shall become impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whatever saddle he that has the issue rides upon, shall be unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every saddle upon which a zav rides, [even a part of the saddle on which he does not sit] shall be unclean.


###### Leviticus 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:10)


וְכׇל־הַנֹּגֵ֗עַ בְּכֹל֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִהְיֶ֣ה תַחְתָּ֔יו יִטְמָ֖א עַד־הָעָ֑רֶב וְהַנּוֹשֵׂ֣א אוֹתָ֔ם יְכַבֵּ֧ס בְּגָדָ֛יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anyone who touches anything which is beneath him shall be impure until the evening. and whoever carries them shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whoever touches anything that was under him shall be unclean until evening: and he that bears any of those things shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whoever touches anything that was beneath him [the zav] shall be unclean until the evening [Scripture here apprises us that one who touches the saddle of a zav does not render his clothing unclean (as opposed to one who touches his mishkav)]; and one who carries them [(his zov, his spittle, his semen, his urine, his mishkav, or his saddle)] shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:11)


וְכֹ֨ל אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִגַּע־בּוֹ֙ הַזָּ֔ב וְיָדָ֖יו לֹא־שָׁטַ֣ף בַּמָּ֑יִם וְכִבֶּ֧ס בְּגָדָ֛יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a *zov* touches anyone and his hands were not rinsed in water, he shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whomever he touches that has the issue, and has not rinsed his hands in water, he shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whoever the zav touches, if he (the zav) did not wash his hands [i.e., all of his visible body areas] with water [i.e., so long as he did not immerse, even after the flux ceased and he counted seven days] ("whoever the zav touches") shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:12)


וּכְלִי־חֶ֛רֶשׂ אֲשֶׁר־יִגַּע־בּ֥וֹ הַזָּ֖ב יִשָּׁבֵ֑ר וְכׇ֨ל־כְּלִי־עֵ֔ץ יִשָּׁטֵ֖ף בַּמָּֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
An earthenware vessel which the *zov* will touch shall be broken. Any wooden vessel shall be rinsed in water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the vessel of earth, that he who has the issue touches, shall be broken: and every vessel of wood shall be rinsed in water.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And an earthen vessel which a zav touches [or moves without touching (heset)] shall be broken, and every wooden vessel shall be rinsed in water.


###### Leviticus 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:13)


וְכִֽי־יִטְהַ֤ר הַזָּב֙ מִזּוֹב֔וֹ וְסָ֨פַר ל֜וֹ שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֛ים לְטׇהֳרָת֖וֹ וְכִבֶּ֣ס בְּגָדָ֑יו וְרָחַ֧ץ בְּשָׂר֛וֹ בְּמַ֥יִם חַיִּ֖ים וְטָהֵֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When a *zov* will become ritually clean, from his discharge-impurity, he shall count for himself seven days for his purification and wash his garments, and bathe in running water, and he will be purified.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when he that has an issue is cleansed of his issue; then he shall number to himself seven days for his cleansing, and wash his clothes, and bathe his flesh in running water, and he shall be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when the zav is cleansed of his flux [i.e., when his flux ceases], then he shall count for himself seven [consecutive] days for his cleansing [i.e., not experiencing a flux], and he shall wash his clothes and bathe his flesh in running water, and he shall be clean.


###### Leviticus 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:14)


וּבַיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֗י יִֽקַּֽח־לוֹ֙ שְׁתֵּ֣י תֹרִ֔ים א֥וֹ שְׁנֵ֖י בְּנֵ֣י יוֹנָ֑ה וּבָ֣א**׀** לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה אֶל־פֶּ֙תַח֙ אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֔ד וּנְתָנָ֖ם אֶל־הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the eighth day he shall take for himself two turtledoves or two young pigeons and come before Adonoy, to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting and give them to the *kohein*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And on the eighth day he shall take for himself two turtledoves, or two young pigeons, and come before the Lord to the door of the Tent of Meeting, and give them to the priest:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And on the eighth day, he shall take for himself two turtle-doves or two young pigeons, and he shall come before the L-rd to the door of the tent of meeting, and he shall give them to the Cohein.


###### Leviticus 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:15)


וְעָשָׂ֤ה אֹתָם֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן אֶחָ֣ד חַטָּ֔את וְהָאֶחָ֖ד עֹלָ֑ה וְכִפֶּ֨ר עָלָ֧יו הַכֹּהֵ֛ן לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה מִזּוֹבֽוֹ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall do (offer) them, one as a sin-offering and the [other] one as a burnt-offering. The *kohein* will atone for him before Adonoy, from his discharge-impurity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall offer them, the one for a sin offering, and the other for a burnt offering; and the priest shall make atonement for him before the Lord because of his issue.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall offer them up, one as a sin-offering and the other as a burnt-offering; and the Cohein shall make atonement for him before the L-rd from his flux.


###### Leviticus 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:16)


וְאִ֕ישׁ כִּֽי־תֵצֵ֥א מִמֶּ֖נּוּ שִׁכְבַת־זָ֑רַע וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֛יִם אֶת־כׇּל־בְּשָׂר֖וֹ וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man, if semen issues from him, he shall bathe his entire body in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the semen goes out from a man, then he shall bathe all his flesh in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man, if there issue from him semen, then he shall bathe all of his flesh in water and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:17)


וְכׇל־בֶּ֣גֶד וְכׇל־ע֔וֹר אֲשֶׁר־יִהְיֶ֥ה עָלָ֖יו שִׁכְבַת־זָ֑רַע וְכֻבַּ֥ס בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Any garment and any leather object upon which there is semen shall be washed in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And every garment, and every skin, on which the semen is, shall be washed with water and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every garment and all skin upon which there shall be semen shall be washed in water and it shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:18)


וְאִשָּׁ֕ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִשְׁכַּ֥ב אִ֛ישׁ אֹתָ֖הּ שִׁכְבַת־זָ֑רַע וְרָחֲצ֣וּ בַמַּ֔יִם וְטָמְא֖וּ עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A woman, with whom a man will lie conjugally; they shall bathe in water, and are impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The woman also with whom a man shall lie with emission of semen, they shall both bathe themselves in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a woman, if a man lie with her a lying of semen, then they shall bathe in water and they shall be unclean until the evening [(the woman being unclean by "the King's decree" (her semen contact being internal)].


###### Leviticus 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:19)


וְאִשָּׁה֙ כִּֽי־תִהְיֶ֣ה זָבָ֔ה דָּ֛ם יִהְיֶ֥ה זֹבָ֖הּ בִּבְשָׂרָ֑הּ שִׁבְעַ֤ת יָמִים֙ תִּהְיֶ֣ה בְנִדָּתָ֔הּ וְכׇל־הַנֹּגֵ֥עַ בָּ֖הּ יִטְמָ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When a woman has a discharge [and] the discharge will be blood in her body, for seven days she will be in [the state of] her menstrual impurity. Anyone who touches her will be impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a woman have an issue, and her issue in her flesh be blood, she shall be seven days in her menstrual separation: and whoever touches her shall be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a woman, if she has a flow, if blood shall be her flow in her flesh, seven days shall she be in her niddah [contact-forbidden] state [(even if she had only one "sighting")], and whoever touches her shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:20)


וְכֹל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר תִּשְׁכַּ֥ב עָלָ֛יו בְּנִדָּתָ֖הּ יִטְמָ֑א וְכֹ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־תֵּשֵׁ֥ב עָלָ֖יו יִטְמָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Everything that she sleeps on, during her menstrual impurity, will become impure and everything that she sits on will be impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And everything that she lies upon in her separation shall be unclean: everything also that she sits upon shall be unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all that she lies upon in her niddah state shall be unclean, and all that she sits upon shall be unclean.


###### Leviticus 15:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:21)


וְכׇל־הַנֹּגֵ֖עַ בְּמִשְׁכָּבָ֑הּ יְכַבֵּ֧ס בְּגָדָ֛יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anyone who touches what she slept on, shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whoever touches her bed shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whoever touches what she lies upon shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:22)


וְכׇ֨ל־הַנֹּגֵ֔עַ בְּכׇל־כְּלִ֖י אֲשֶׁר־תֵּשֵׁ֣ב עָלָ֑יו יְכַבֵּ֧ס בְּגָדָ֛יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anyone who touches any vessel that she sits on, shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whoever touches anything that she sat upon shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whoever touches any object that she sits upon shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:23)


וְאִ֨ם עַֽל־הַמִּשְׁכָּ֜ב ה֗וּא א֧וֹ עַֽל־הַכְּלִ֛י אֲשֶׁר־הִ֥וא יֹשֶֽׁבֶת־עָלָ֖יו בְּנׇגְעוֹ־ב֑וֹ יִטְמָ֖א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he is on the bed or on the vessel that she is sitting on, when he touches it, he shall be impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be on her bed, or on anything whereon she sits, when he touches it, he shall be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if [he lies or sits] on the (i.e., her) bed [or on her seat, even without directly touching it,] he, [too, requires washing of clothes]. Or, [if he sits] upon an object [including a saddle] that she sits upon — When he touches it [the saddle], he shall be unclean until the evening [but he need not wash his clothes].


###### Leviticus 15:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:24)


וְאִ֡ם שָׁכֹב֩ יִשְׁכַּ֨ב אִ֜ישׁ אֹתָ֗הּ וּתְהִ֤י נִדָּתָהּ֙ עָלָ֔יו וְטָמֵ֖א שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֑ים וְכׇל־הַמִּשְׁכָּ֛ב אֲשֶׁר־יִשְׁכַּ֥ב עָלָ֖יו יִטְמָֽא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a man will lie conjugally with her, her menstrual impurity will be on him and he is impure for seven days. Anything used for sleeping—if he should sleep on it—it will be impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if any man lie with her at all, and her menstrual flow be upon him, he shall be unclean seven days: and all the bed on which he lies shall be unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man lie with her, then her niddah state shall be upon him [(Just as she renders men and earthenware vessels unclean, so does he)], and he shall be unclean seven days [(even though he may have lived with her only in the middle of her (seven-day) niddah state)], and every bed on which he lies shall be unclean.


###### Leviticus 15:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:25)


וְאִשָּׁ֡ה כִּֽי־יָזוּב֩ ז֨וֹב דָּמָ֜הּ יָמִ֣ים רַבִּ֗ים בְּלֹא֙ עֶת־נִדָּתָ֔הּ א֥וֹ כִֽי־תָז֖וּב עַל־נִדָּתָ֑הּ כׇּל־יְמֵ֞י ז֣וֹב טֻמְאָתָ֗הּ כִּימֵ֧י נִדָּתָ֛הּ תִּהְיֶ֖ה טְמֵאָ֥ה הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When a woman discharges her blood-flow, many days outside her menstrual cycle, or if it flows after her menstrual cycle, all the days of her discharge-impurity shall be as her days of menstrual impurity; she is impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a woman have an issue of her blood many days not in the time of her menstruation, or if it run beyond the time of her menstruation; all the days of the issue of her uncleanness shall be as the days of her menstruation: she shall be unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a woman, if the flow of her blood flows many [i.e., three] days, not in the time of her [seven-day] niddah state, [but right after those days], or if she flows [these three days] above her niddah state [i.e., with one day intervening between the new flow and her (preceding) niddah state], then [she is a zavah, and] all the days of the flow of her uncleanliness, as the days of her niddah state shall she be; she is unclean.


###### Leviticus 15:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:26)


כׇּל־הַמִּשְׁכָּ֞ב אֲשֶׁר־תִּשְׁכַּ֤ב עָלָיו֙ כׇּל־יְמֵ֣י זוֹבָ֔הּ כְּמִשְׁכַּ֥ב נִדָּתָ֖הּ יִֽהְיֶה־לָּ֑הּ וְכׇֽל־הַכְּלִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תֵּשֵׁ֣ב עָלָ֔יו טָמֵ֣א יִהְיֶ֔ה כְּטֻמְאַ֖ת נִדָּתָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anything used for sleeping upon which she will lie [on] any of her discharge-impurity-days, as the bed of her menstrual impurity it shall be to her. Every vessel upon which she sits will be impure, just as [is] her menstrual-impurity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every bed on which she lies all the days of her issue shall be to her as the bed of her menstruation: and whatever she sits upon shall be unclean, as the uncleanness of her menstruation.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every bed that she lies upon all the days of her [zavah] flow, as the bed of her niddah state shall be to her; and every object that she sits upon shall be unclean, as the uncleanliness of her niddah state.


###### Leviticus 15:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:27)


וְכׇל־הַנּוֹגֵ֥עַ בָּ֖ם יִטְמָ֑א וְכִבֶּ֧ס בְּגָדָ֛יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֖יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anyone that touches them will be impure and he shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whoever touches those things shall be unclean, and shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whoever touches them shall be unclean. And he shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, and he shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Leviticus 15:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:28)


וְאִֽם־טָהֲרָ֖ה מִזּוֹבָ֑הּ וְסָ֥פְרָה לָּ֛הּ שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֖ים וְאַחַ֥ר תִּטְהָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When she has become pure from her discharge-impurity, she shall count seven days for herself and afterwards she becomes purified.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if she be cleansed of her issue, then she shall number to herself seven days, and after that she shall be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Leviticus 15:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:29)


וּבַיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֗י תִּֽקַּֽח־לָהּ֙ שְׁתֵּ֣י תֹרִ֔ים א֥וֹ שְׁנֵ֖י בְּנֵ֣י יוֹנָ֑ה וְהֵבִיאָ֤ה אוֹתָם֙ אֶל־הַכֹּהֵ֔ן אֶל־פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the eighth day she shall take for herself, two turtledoves or two young pigeons and she shall bring them to the *kohein*, to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And on the eighth day she shall take for herself two turtles, or two young pigeons, and bring them to the priest, to the door of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And on the eighth day, she shall take for herself two turtle-doves or two young pigeons, and she shall bring them to the Cohein, to the door of the tent of meeting.


###### Leviticus 15:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:30)


וְעָשָׂ֤ה הַכֹּהֵן֙ אֶת־הָאֶחָ֣ד חַטָּ֔את וְאֶת־הָאֶחָ֖ד עֹלָ֑ה וְכִפֶּ֨ר עָלֶ֤יהָ הַכֹּהֵן֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה מִזּ֖וֹב טֻמְאָתָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall do (offer) one as a sin-offering and the [other] one as a burnt-offering. The *kohein* will atone for her before Adonoy from her discharge-impurity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall offer the one for a sin offering, and the other for a burnt offering; and the priest shall make atonement for her before the Lord for the issue of her uncleanness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall offer up one as a sin-offering and the other as a burnt-offering, and the Cohein shall make atonement for her before the L-rd from the flow of her uncleanliness.


###### Leviticus 15:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:31)


וְהִזַּרְתֶּ֥ם אֶת־בְּנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל מִטֻּמְאָתָ֑ם וְלֹ֤א יָמֻ֙תוּ֙ בְּטֻמְאָתָ֔ם בְּטַמְּאָ֥ם אֶת־מִשְׁכָּנִ֖י אֲשֶׁ֥ר בְּתוֹכָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall separate Bnei Yisroel from their impurities and they will not die because of their impurities through their defilement of My dwelling in their midst.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus shall you separate the children of Yisra᾽el from their uncleanness; that they die not in their uncleanness, when they defile my tabernacle that is among them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall separate the children of Israel from their uncleanliness, that they not die in their uncleanliness by defiling My sanctuary which is in their midst [(whence it is derived that the kareth ("cutting off") for defiling the sanctuary is called "death")].


###### Leviticus 15:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:32)


זֹ֥את תּוֹרַ֖ת הַזָּ֑ב וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר תֵּצֵ֥א מִמֶּ֛נּוּ שִׁכְבַת־זֶ֖רַע לְטׇמְאָה־בָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the law of the *zov* and of him from whom semen issues to become impure through it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the Tora of him that has an issue, and of him whose semen goes from him, and he is defiled with it;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the law of the zav [of only one "sighting"] and of one [i.e., he is like one] from whom semen issues forth to be made unclean thereby [i.e., he is unclean until the evening].


###### Leviticus 15:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/15:33)


וְהַדָּוָה֙ בְּנִדָּתָ֔הּ וְהַזָּב֙ אֶת־זוֹב֔וֹ לַזָּכָ֖ר וְלַנְּקֵבָ֑ה וּלְאִ֕ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִשְׁכַּ֖ב עִם־טְמֵאָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For the menstruant during her period of separation and for one who has a discharge for a male and for a female; and for the man that will lie conjugally with an impure woman.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and of her that is sick in her menstrual flow, and of him that has an issue, of the man, and of the woman, and of him that lies with her that is unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And ("this is the law") of her who flows in her niddah state, and of him who flows his flux [(two or three "sightings")], for male or female, and for a man who lies with a woman who is unclean.



## Chapter 16

###### Leviticus 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֔ה אַחֲרֵ֣י מ֔וֹת שְׁנֵ֖י בְּנֵ֣י אַהֲרֹ֑ן בְּקׇרְבָתָ֥ם לִפְנֵי־יְהֹוָ֖ה וַיָּמֻֽתוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy spoke to Moshe after the death of two sons of Aharon, who brought an [unauthorized] offering before Adonoy and they died.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe after the death of the two sons of Aharon, when they came near before the Lord, and died;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses after the death of the two sons of Aaron when they drew near before the L-rd and they died [(their death serving as "reinforcement" for the injunction that follows)]:


###### Leviticus 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:2)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֗ה דַּבֵּר֮ אֶל־אַהֲרֹ֣ן אָחִ֒יךָ֒ וְאַל־יָבֹ֤א בְכׇל־עֵת֙ אֶל־הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ מִבֵּ֖ית לַפָּרֹ֑כֶת אֶל־פְּנֵ֨י הַכַּפֹּ֜רֶת אֲשֶׁ֤ר עַל־הָאָרֹן֙ וְלֹ֣א יָמ֔וּת כִּ֚י בֶּֽעָנָ֔ן אֵרָאֶ֖ה עַל־הַכַּפֹּֽרֶת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe: Speak to your brother Aharon that he not come at all times into the Holy [Sanctuary] that is inside of the Curtain, before the Ark-cover that is on the Ark so that he not die, for in a cloud I shall appear on the Ark-cover.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Lord said to Moshe, Speak to Aharon thy brother, that he come not at all times into the holy place within the veil before the covering, which is upon the ark; that he die not: for I appear in the cloud upon the ark cover.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to Moses: Speak to Aaron your brother, and let him not come at all times to the holy place within the parocheth before the kaporeth which is on the ark that he not die [(If he comes he will die)]; for with the cloud I [always] appear on the kaporeth [(And since My shechinah manifests itself there, let him take care not to come there indiscriminately)].


###### Leviticus 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:3)


בְּזֹ֛את יָבֹ֥א אַהֲרֹ֖ן אֶל־הַקֹּ֑דֶשׁ בְּפַ֧ר בֶּן־בָּקָ֛ר לְחַטָּ֖את וְאַ֥יִל לְעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
With this shall Aharon come into the Holy [Sanctuary]; with a young bullock, for a sin-offering and a ram, for a burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus shall Aharon come into the holy place: with a young bullock for a sin offering, and a ram for a burnt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With this, [(and only on Yom Kippur (29)] shall Aaron come to the holy place: with a young bullock for a sin-offering and a ram for a burnt-offering.


###### Leviticus 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:4)


כְּתֹֽנֶת־בַּ֨ד קֹ֜דֶשׁ יִלְבָּ֗שׁ וּמִֽכְנְסֵי־בַד֮ יִהְי֣וּ עַל־בְּשָׂרוֹ֒ וּבְאַבְנֵ֥ט בַּד֙ יַחְגֹּ֔ר וּבְמִצְנֶ֥פֶת בַּ֖ד יִצְנֹ֑ף בִּגְדֵי־קֹ֣דֶשׁ הֵ֔ם וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֛יִם אֶת־בְּשָׂר֖וֹ וּלְבֵשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall don a sanctified, linen tunic and linen pants shall be on his body. He shall gird himself with a linen sash and place a linen turban on his head. These are sacred garments; he shall bathe his body in water and clothe himself in them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall put on the holy linen coat, and he shall have the linen breeches upon his flesh, and shall be girded with a linen girdle, and with the linen mitre shall he be attired: these are holy garments; therefore shall he bathe his flesh in water, and so put them on.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[The high-priest does not minister within, wearing the eight garments in which he normally officiates (for the latter have gold — and "the accuser" (the golden calf) cannot be the defender (on Yom Kippur), but he wears the four linen garments of the ordinary Cohein.] A linen kethoneth of kodesh [i.e., purchased with Temple funds] shall he wear, and linen michnasayim shall there be on his flesh, and with a linen avnet shall he gird himself, and a linen mitznefeth shall he place on his head; they are garments of kodesh. And he shall bathe his flesh in water and put them on. [He required five immersions and ablutions of hands and feet on that day for five changings (between inner and outer services)].


###### Leviticus 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:5)


וּמֵאֵ֗ת עֲדַת֙ בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל יִקַּ֛ח שְׁנֵֽי־שְׂעִירֵ֥י עִזִּ֖ים לְחַטָּ֑את וְאַ֥יִל אֶחָ֖ד לְעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall take, from the congregation of Israel two he-goats for sin-offerings and one ram for a burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall take from the congregation of the children of Yisra᾽el two kids of the goats for a sin offering, and one ram for a burnt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from the congregation of the children of Israel, he shall take two he-goats for a sin-offering and one ram for a burnt-offering.


###### Leviticus 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:6)


וְהִקְרִ֧יב אַהֲרֹ֛ן אֶת־פַּ֥ר הַחַטָּ֖את אֲשֶׁר־ל֑וֹ וְכִפֶּ֥ר בַּעֲד֖וֹ וּבְעַ֥ד בֵּיתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon shall bring the sin-offering bullock which is his and atone on his own behalf and on behalf of his household.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aharon shall offer the bullock of the sin offering, which is for himself, and make atonement for himself, and for his house.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Aaron shall present the bullock of the sin-offering, which is his [(It must come from his own funds and not from community funds)], and he shall make atonement for himself and for his household [(He confesses over it his sins and those of his household)].


###### Leviticus 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:7)


וְלָקַ֖ח אֶת־שְׁנֵ֣י הַשְּׂעִירִ֑ם וְהֶעֱמִ֤יד אֹתָם֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall take the two he-goats and stand them before Adonoy, at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall take the two goats, and present them before the Lord at the door of the Tent of Meeting.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
