And he shall take the two he-goats and he shall stand them before the L-rd at the door of the tent of meeting.


###### Leviticus 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:8)


וְנָתַ֧ן אַהֲרֹ֛ן עַל־שְׁנֵ֥י הַשְּׂעִירִ֖ם גֹּרָל֑וֹת גּוֹרָ֤ל אֶחָד֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה וְגוֹרָ֥ל אֶחָ֖ד לַעֲזָאזֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon shall put lots on the two he-goats; one lot [marked] for Adonoy and one lot [marked] for Azazel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aharon shall cast lots upon the two goats; one lot for the Lord, and the other lot for ῾Azazel.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Aaron shall place lots upon the two he-goats, one lot for the L-rd, and the other lot for Azazel [a flinty crag (from which the goat was precipitated). He would place the goats on either side of him and from an urn draw lots, on one of which was written "for the L-rd"; on the other, "for Azazel")]


###### Leviticus 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:9)


וְהִקְרִ֤יב אַהֲרֹן֙ אֶת־הַשָּׂעִ֔יר אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָלָ֥ה עָלָ֛יו הַגּוֹרָ֖ל לַיהֹוָ֑ה וְעָשָׂ֖הוּ חַטָּֽאת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon shall bring the goat upon which the lot [marked] for Adonoy came up and do (offer) it as a sin-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aharon shall bring the goat upon which the Lord’s lot fell, and offer it for a sin offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Aaron shall present the he-goat on which the lot came up for the L-rd, and he shall make it a sin-offering [(When he places the lot upon it he says: "For the L-rd, a sin-offering.")]


###### Leviticus 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:10)


וְהַשָּׂעִ֗יר אֲשֶׁר֩ עָלָ֨ה עָלָ֤יו הַגּוֹרָל֙ לַעֲזָאזֵ֔ל יׇֽעֳמַד־חַ֛י לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה לְכַפֵּ֣ר עָלָ֑יו לְשַׁלַּ֥ח אֹת֛וֹ לַעֲזָאזֵ֖ל הַמִּדְבָּֽרָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The goat upon which came up the lot [marked] for Azazel shall be placed, alive, before Adonoy, to achieve atonement with it to send it to Azazel, in the desert.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the goat, on which the lot fell for ῾Azazel, shall be presented alive before the Lord, to make atonement over him, and to let him go to ῾Azazel into the wilderness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the he-goat on which the lot came up for Azazel shall be stood living before the L-rd [(whence it is derived that it is sent away for death)] to make atonement over it [i.e., to confess, viz. 21] to send it to Azazel to the desert.


###### Leviticus 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:11)


וְהִקְרִ֨יב אַהֲרֹ֜ן אֶת־פַּ֤ר הַֽחַטָּאת֙ אֲשֶׁר־ל֔וֹ וְכִפֶּ֥ר בַּֽעֲד֖וֹ וּבְעַ֣ד בֵּית֑וֹ וְשָׁחַ֛ט אֶת־פַּ֥ר הַֽחַטָּ֖את אֲשֶׁר־לֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon shall bring the sin-offering-bullock which is his and atone on his behalf and on behalf of his household, and he shall slaughter his sin-offering-bullock.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aharon shall bring the bullock of the sin offering, which is for himself, and shall make atonement for himself, and for his house, and shall kill the bullock of the sin offering which is for himself:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Aaron shall present the bullock of the sin-offering which is his, and he shall make atonement over it for himself and for his household [(a second confession for himself and for his fellow Cohanim to atone for defiling of the sanctuary and of its holy objects, viz. 16)], and he shall slaughter the bullock of the sin-offering which is his.


###### Leviticus 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:12)


וְלָקַ֣ח מְלֹֽא־הַ֠מַּחְתָּ֠ה גַּֽחֲלֵי־אֵ֞שׁ מֵעַ֤ל הַמִּזְבֵּ֙חַ֙ מִלִּפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וּמְלֹ֣א חׇפְנָ֔יו קְטֹ֥רֶת סַמִּ֖ים דַּקָּ֑ה וְהֵבִ֖יא מִבֵּ֥ית לַפָּרֹֽכֶת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall take a full hand-shovel of fiery coals from atop the altar, which is before Adonoy and full handfuls of finely-ground incense and he shall bring [them] inside of the curtain.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall take a censer full of burning coals of fire from off the altar before the Lord, and his hands full of sweet incense beaten small, and bring it inside the veil:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall take a full coal-pan of coals of fire from off the [outer] altar before the L-rd [i.e., from the side which faces the door (of the mishkan), the west], and his full handfuls of incense of spices ground [especially] small, and he shall bring them within the parocheth.


###### Leviticus 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:13)


וְנָתַ֧ן אֶֽת־הַקְּטֹ֛רֶת עַל־הָאֵ֖שׁ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְכִסָּ֣ה**׀** עֲנַ֣ן הַקְּטֹ֗רֶת אֶת־הַכַּפֹּ֛רֶת אֲשֶׁ֥ר עַל־הָעֵד֖וּת וְלֹ֥א יָמֽוּת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall put the incense on the fire before Adonoy and the cloud of incense will cover the Ark-cover that is on the [tablets of] Testimony, that he not die.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall put the incense upon the fire before the Lord, that the cloud of the incense may cover the covering that is upon the Testimony, that he die not:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall put the incense upon the fire [in the coal-pan] before the L-rd and the cloud of incense shall cover the kaporeth which is on the testimony, and he shall not die [(but, being remiss in this service, he is liable to death)].


###### Leviticus 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:14)


וְלָקַח֙ מִדַּ֣ם הַפָּ֔ר וְהִזָּ֧ה בְאֶצְבָּע֛וֹ עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הַכַּפֹּ֖רֶת קֵ֑דְמָה וְלִפְנֵ֣י הַכַּפֹּ֗רֶת יַזֶּ֧ה שֶֽׁבַע־פְּעָמִ֛ים מִן־הַדָּ֖ם בְּאֶצְבָּעֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall take some of the bullocks blood and he shall sprinkle with his finger, above the surface of the Ark-cover on its eastern part; and before the Ark-cover, he shall sprinkle, some blood, with his finger, seven times.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall take of the blood of the bullock, and sprinkle it with his finger upon the covering eastward; and before the covering shall he sprinkle of the blood with his finger seven times.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall take the blood of the bullock, and he shall sprinkle with his finger [one sprinkling] on the kaporeth, on the east, and before the kaporeth shall he sprinkle seven times of the blood with his finger. [Hence, "one above and seven below"].


###### Leviticus 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:15)


וְשָׁחַ֞ט אֶת־שְׂעִ֤יר הַֽחַטָּאת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לָעָ֔ם וְהֵבִיא֙ אֶת־דָּמ֔וֹ אֶל־מִבֵּ֖ית לַפָּרֹ֑כֶת וְעָשָׂ֣ה אֶת־דָּמ֗וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר עָשָׂה֙ לְדַ֣ם הַפָּ֔ר וְהִזָּ֥ה אֹת֛וֹ עַל־הַכַּפֹּ֖רֶת וְלִפְנֵ֥י הַכַּפֹּֽרֶת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall slaughter the he-goat sin-offering that belongs to the people, and he shall bring its blood inside of the Curtain. He shall do [with] its blood just as he did with the bullocks blood and he shall sprinkle it on the Ark-cover and before of the Ark-cover.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then shall he kill the goat of the sin offering, that is for the people, and bring its blood within the veil, and do with that blood as he did with the blood of the bullock, and sprinkle it upon the covering, and before the covering:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall slaughter the he-goat of the sin-offering which is the people's [(What the bullock atones for for the Cohanim, the he-goat "for the L-rd" atones for, for Israel)], and he shall bring its blood inside the parocheth. And he shall do with its blood as he did with the blood of the bullock [i.e., "one above and seven below"]; he shall sprinkle it on the kaporeth and before the kaporeth.


###### Leviticus 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:16)


וְכִפֶּ֣ר עַל־הַקֹּ֗דֶשׁ מִטֻּמְאֹת֙ בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וּמִפִּשְׁעֵיהֶ֖ם לְכׇל־חַטֹּאתָ֑ם וְכֵ֤ן יַעֲשֶׂה֙ לְאֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֔ד הַשֹּׁכֵ֣ן אִתָּ֔ם בְּת֖וֹךְ טֻמְאֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He will atone for the Sanctuary, from the impurities of Bnei Yisroel and from their rebellious sins for all their transgressions. He shall do likewise for the Tent of Meeting, that dwells with them in the midst of their impurity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall make atonement for the holy place, because of the uncleanness of the children of Yisra᾽el, and because of their transgressions in all their sins: and so shall he do for the Tent of Meeting, that remains among them in the midst of their uncleanness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall make atonement for the holy place from the uncleanlinesses of the children of Israel [for entering the sanctuary in a state of uncleanliness] and for their offenses [(willful entry in a state of uncleanliness)] for all of their transgressions [(unwitting entry in a state of uncleanliness without subsequent knowledge thereof)]. And so shall he do for the tent of meeting [(Just as he sprinkles of both within, one above and seven below, so he sprinkles of both on the parocheth outside, one above and seven below)], who dwells with them in the midst of their uncleanlinesses [(Though they be unclean, the Shechinah is with them)]


###### Leviticus 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:17)


וְכׇל־אָדָ֞ם לֹא־יִהְיֶ֣ה**׀** בְּאֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֗ד בְּבֹא֛וֹ לְכַפֵּ֥ר בַּקֹּ֖דֶשׁ עַד־צֵאת֑וֹ וְכִפֶּ֤ר בַּעֲדוֹ֙ וּבְעַ֣ד בֵּית֔וֹ וּבְעַ֖ד כׇּל־קְהַ֥ל יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
No man shall be present in the Tent of Meeting when he comes in to atone for the Holy [Sanctuary] until he leaves. He shall atone on his behalf and on behalf of his household and on behalf of the entire assembly of Israel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there shall be no man in the Tent of Meeting when he goes in to make atonement in the holy place, until he comes out, and have made atonement for himself, and for his household, and for all the congregation of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And no man shall be in the tent of meeting when he comes to make atonement in the holy place until he goes out. And he shall make atonement for himself, and for his household, and for the entire congregation of Israel.


###### Leviticus 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:18)


וְיָצָ֗א אֶל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֛חַ אֲשֶׁ֥ר לִפְנֵֽי־יְהֹוָ֖ה וְכִפֶּ֣ר עָלָ֑יו וְלָקַ֞ח מִדַּ֤ם הַפָּר֙ וּמִדַּ֣ם הַשָּׂעִ֔יר וְנָתַ֛ן עַל־קַרְנ֥וֹת הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ סָבִֽיב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall go out to the altar that is before Adonoy and atone on it. He shall take some of the bullocks blood and some of the he-goats blood and put it atop the corners of the altar, all around.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall go out to the altar that is before the Lord, and make atonement for it; and shall take of the blood of the bullock, and of the blood of the goat, and put it upon the horns of the altar round about.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall go out to the [golden] altar which is before the L-rd [after having stood inwards of the altar to sprinkle on the parocheth], and he shall make atonement on it — he shall take from the blood of the bullock and from the blood of the he-goat [intermixed] and he shall place it on the horns of the altar roundabout [beginning with the northeast corner].


###### Leviticus 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:19)


וְהִזָּ֨ה עָלָ֧יו מִן־הַדָּ֛ם בְּאֶצְבָּע֖וֹ שֶׁ֣בַע פְּעָמִ֑ים וְטִהֲר֣וֹ וְקִדְּשׁ֔וֹ מִטֻּמְאֹ֖ת בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall sprinkle on it from the blood that is on his finger seven times and purify it and sanctify it from the impurities of Bnei Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall sprinkle of the blood upon it with his finger seven times, and cleanse it, and hallow it from the uncleanness of the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall sprinkle upon it [on its top] with his finger seven times, and he shall cleanse it [from past uncleanlinesses], and he shall hallow it [for the future] from the uncleanlinesses of the children of Israel.


###### Leviticus 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:20)


וְכִלָּה֙ מִכַּפֵּ֣ר אֶת־הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ וְאֶת־אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵ֖ד וְאֶת־הַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ וְהִקְרִ֖יב אֶת־הַשָּׂעִ֥יר הֶחָֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When he finishes atoning for the Holy [Sanctuary] and [for] the Tent of Meeting and [for] the altar he shall bring the live he-goat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when he has made an end of atoning for the holy place, and the Tent of Meeting, and the altar, he shall present the live goat:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when he has finished making atonement for the holy place, and the tent of meeting, and the altar, then he shall draw near the living he-goat.


###### Leviticus 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:21)


וְסָמַ֨ךְ אַהֲרֹ֜ן אֶת־שְׁתֵּ֣י יָדָ֗ו עַ֣ל רֹ֣אשׁ הַשָּׂעִיר֮ הַחַי֒ וְהִתְוַדָּ֣ה עָלָ֗יו אֶת־כׇּל־עֲוֺנֹת֙ בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְאֶת־כׇּל־פִּשְׁעֵיהֶ֖ם לְכׇל־חַטֹּאתָ֑ם וְנָתַ֤ן אֹתָם֙ עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ הַשָּׂעִ֔יר וְשִׁלַּ֛ח בְּיַד־אִ֥ישׁ עִתִּ֖י הַמִּדְבָּֽרָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon shall lay his two hands on the head of the live he-goat and confess on it all the iniquities of Bnei Yisroel and all their rebellious transgressions for all their sins, and put them on the head of the goat and send it away with the man [so] designated, to the desert.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Aharon shall lay both his hands upon the head of the live goat, and confess over him all the iniquities of the children of Yisra᾽el, and all their transgressions in all their sins, putting them upon the head of the goat, and shall send him away by the hand of an appointed man into the wilderness:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Aaron shall place his two hands on the head of the living he-goat, and he shall confess over it all the transgressions of the children of Israel, and all their offenses of all their sins, and he shall place them on the head of the he-goat, and he shall send it by the hand of a readied man [readied from the day before] to the desert.


###### Leviticus 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:22)


וְנָשָׂ֨א הַשָּׂעִ֥יר עָלָ֛יו אֶת־כׇּל־עֲוֺנֹתָ֖ם אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ גְּזֵרָ֑ה וְשִׁלַּ֥ח אֶת־הַשָּׂעִ֖יר בַּמִּדְבָּֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The goat shall carry upon itself all of their iniquities to a desolate land, and [the man shall] send the goat into the desert.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the goat shall bear upon it all their iniquities to a barren land: and he shall let go the goat in the wilderness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the he-goat shall bear upon itself all of their transgressions to an uninhabited land, and he shall send out the he-goat in the desert. [Verse 24 is understood as following at this point.]


###### Leviticus 16:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:23)


וּבָ֤א אַהֲרֹן֙ אֶל־אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֔ד וּפָשַׁט֙ אֶת־בִּגְדֵ֣י הַבָּ֔ד אֲשֶׁ֥ר לָבַ֖שׁ בְּבֹא֣וֹ אֶל־הַקֹּ֑דֶשׁ וְהִנִּיחָ֖ם שָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Aharon will [then] come into the Tent of Meeting and remove the linen garments that he wore when he came into the Holy [Sanctuary] and place them there.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aharon shall come into the Tent of Meeting, and shall put off the linen garments, which he put on when he went into the holy place, and shall leave them there:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[This verse is understood as following after verse 28.] And Aaron shall come into the tent of meeting [to take out from the holy of holies the spoon and the coal pan in which he offered the incense], and he shall take off the linen garments which he put on when he went into the holy place, and he shall leave them there [(They require genizah (secreting) and are not used on another Yom Kippur.) And then he dons the golden garments for the daily afternoon burnt-offering. (Now, verse 29)]


###### Leviticus 16:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:24)


וְרָחַ֨ץ אֶת־בְּשָׂר֤וֹ בַמַּ֙יִם֙ בְּמָק֣וֹם קָד֔וֹשׁ וְלָבַ֖שׁ אֶת־בְּגָדָ֑יו וְיָצָ֗א וְעָשָׂ֤ה אֶת־עֹֽלָתוֹ֙ וְאֶת־עֹלַ֣ת הָעָ֔ם וְכִפֶּ֥ר בַּעֲד֖וֹ וּבְעַ֥ד הָעָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall wash his flesh in water in a sacred place and don his garments. He shall go out and do (offer) his burnt-offering and the peoples burnt-offering and atone on his behalf and on behalf of the people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall wash his flesh with water in the holy place, and put on his garments, and come forth, and offer his burnt offering, and the burnt offering of the people, and make atonement for himself, and for the people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[This verse is understood as following verse 22.] And he shall bathe his flesh in water in a holy place [(sanctified with the sanctity of the azarah. It, and the other four Yom Kippur immersions were on the roof of the beth haparvah. The first (daily immersion) was in an unsanctified area.)] And he shall put on his [eight regular] garments, and he shall go out [from the sanctuary to the court, where the burnt-offering altar was situated], and he shall offer up his burnt-offering [(3)] and the burnt-offering of the people [(5)], and he shall make atonement for himself and for the people.


###### Leviticus 16:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:25)


וְאֵ֛ת חֵ֥לֶב הַֽחַטָּ֖את יַקְטִ֥יר הַמִּזְבֵּֽחָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The fat of the sin-offering, he shall burn on the altar.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the fat of the sin offering shall he burn upon the altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the fat of the sin-offering [the eimurim of the bullock and the goat] he shall smoke on the [outer] altar.


###### Leviticus 16:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:26)


וְהַֽמְשַׁלֵּ֤חַ אֶת־הַשָּׂעִיר֙ לַֽעֲזָאזֵ֔ל יְכַבֵּ֣ס בְּגָדָ֔יו וְרָחַ֥ץ אֶת־בְּשָׂר֖וֹ בַּמָּ֑יִם וְאַחֲרֵי־כֵ֖ן יָב֥וֹא אֶל־הַֽמַּחֲנֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He who sends the goat to Azazel shall wash his garments, and bathe his body in water, and afterwards he shall come into the encampment.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that let go the goat for ῾Azazel shall wash his clothes, and bathe his flesh in water, and afterwards come into the camp.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he who sends the he-goat to Azazel shall wash his clothes and bathe his flesh in water, and then he may come to the camp.


###### Leviticus 16:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:27)


וְאֵת֩ פַּ֨ר הַֽחַטָּ֜את וְאֵ֣ת**׀** שְׂעִ֣יר הַֽחַטָּ֗את אֲשֶׁ֨ר הוּבָ֤א אֶת־דָּמָם֙ לְכַפֵּ֣ר בַּקֹּ֔דֶשׁ יוֹצִ֖יא אֶל־מִח֣וּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶ֑ה וְשָׂרְפ֣וּ בָאֵ֔שׁ אֶת־עֹרֹתָ֥ם וְאֶת־בְּשָׂרָ֖ם וְאֶת־פִּרְשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The sin-offering-bullock and the sin-offering-goat whose blood was brought to achieve atonement in the Holy [Sanctuary], he shall have removed beyond the encampment. They shall burn in fire their skins, their flesh and their waste [that are in their intestines].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the bullock for the sin offering, and the goat for the sin offering, whose blood was brought in to make atonement in the holy place, shall be taken outside the camp; and they shall burn in the fire their skins, and their flesh, and their dung.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the bullock of the sin-offering and the goat of the sin-offering, whose blood was brought [into the sanctuary and into the holy of holies] to make atonement in the holy place, he shall take outside the camp, and they shall burn in fire their skin, and their flesh, and their dung.


###### Leviticus 16:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:28)


וְהַשֹּׂרֵ֣ף אֹתָ֔ם יְכַבֵּ֣ס בְּגָדָ֔יו וְרָחַ֥ץ אֶת־בְּשָׂר֖וֹ בַּמָּ֑יִם וְאַחֲרֵי־כֵ֖ן יָב֥וֹא אֶל־הַֽמַּחֲנֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He who burns them shall wash his garments and bathe his body in water and afterwards he shall come into the encampment.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that burns them shall wash his clothes, and bathe his flesh in water, and afterwards he shall come into the camp.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he who burns them shall wash his clothes and he shall bathe his flesh in water, and then he shall come to the camp. [Verse 23 is understood as following at this point.]


###### Leviticus 16:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:29)


וְהָיְתָ֥ה לָכֶ֖ם לְחֻקַּ֣ת עוֹלָ֑ם בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הַ֠שְּׁבִיעִ֠י בֶּֽעָשׂ֨וֹר לַחֹ֜דֶשׁ תְּעַנּ֣וּ אֶת־נַפְשֹֽׁתֵיכֶ֗ם וְכׇל־מְלָאכָה֙ לֹ֣א תַעֲשׂ֔וּ הָֽאֶזְרָ֔ח וְהַגֵּ֖ר הַגָּ֥ר בְּתוֹכְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It shall be for an everlasting statute for you; in the seventh month, on the tenth of the month, you shall afflict yourselves and not do any work, the native-born and the proselyte residing among you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this shall be a statute for ever to you: that in the seventh month, on the tenth day of the month, you shall afflict your souls, and do no work at all, the home born or the stranger that sojourns among you:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[After verse 23] And it shall be to you an everlasting statute: In the seventh month, on the tenth day of the month, you shall afflict your souls. And all work you shall not do, the citizen and the stranger that sojourns in your midst.


###### Leviticus 16:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:30)


כִּֽי־בַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּ֛ה יְכַפֵּ֥ר עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם לְטַהֵ֣ר אֶתְכֶ֑ם מִכֹּל֙ חַטֹּ֣אתֵיכֶ֔ם לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה תִּטְהָֽרוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For on this day He will make atonement for you, to purify you from all your sins, before Adonoy, you will be purified.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for on that day will he forgive you, to cleanse you, that you may be clean from all your sins before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For on this day He shall atone for you to cleanse you of all of your sins; before the L-rd shall you be clean.


###### Leviticus 16:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:31)


שַׁבַּ֨ת שַׁבָּת֥וֹן הִיא֙ לָכֶ֔ם וְעִנִּיתֶ֖ם אֶת־נַפְשֹׁתֵיכֶ֑ם חֻקַּ֖ת עוֹלָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It is a *Shabbos* of Shabbosos to you and you must afflict yourselves; it is an everlasting statute.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It shall be a sabbath of solemn rest to you, and you shall afflict your souls, by a statute forever.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is a Sabbath of Sabbaths to you, and you shall afflict your soul; it is an everlasting statute.


###### Leviticus 16:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:32)


וְכִפֶּ֨ר הַכֹּהֵ֜ן אֲשֶׁר־יִמְשַׁ֣ח אֹת֗וֹ וַאֲשֶׁ֤ר יְמַלֵּא֙ אֶת־יָד֔וֹ לְכַהֵ֖ן תַּ֣חַת אָבִ֑יו וְלָבַ֛שׁ אֶת־בִּגְדֵ֥י הַבָּ֖ד בִּגְדֵ֥י הַקֹּֽדֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* will atone—he who himself will be anointed and who will be initiated— to serve in his fathers stead and dress in the linen garments, the sacred garments.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest, who shall be anointed, and who shall be consecrated to minister in the priest’s office in his father’s stead, shall make the atonement, and shall put on the linen clothes, the holy garments:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein [(i.e., not only Aaron)] shall make atonement, who shall be anointed, and [(even if he is not anointed, as with the high-priests from Yoshiyahu on, after the anointing oil had been secreted)], who shall be invested [with the vestments of the high priest] to minister [if he is qualified] in place of his father; and he shall put on the linen garments, the holy garments.


###### Leviticus 16:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:33)


וְכִפֶּר֙ אֶת־מִקְדַּ֣שׁ הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ וְאֶת־אֹ֧הֶל מוֹעֵ֛ד וְאֶת־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ יְכַפֵּ֑ר וְעַ֧ל הַכֹּהֲנִ֛ים וְעַל־כׇּל־עַ֥ם הַקָּהָ֖ל יְכַפֵּֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He will atone for the Holy [Sanctuary]; and for the Tent of Meeting and for the altar, he will atone; for the *kohanim* and for the entire people of the congregation, he will atone.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall make atonement for the holy sanctuary, and he shall make atonement for the Tent of Meeting, and for the altar, and he shall make atonement for the priests, and for all the people of the congregation.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall make atonement for the sanctity of the holy place. And for the tent of meeting and for the altar shall he make atonement. And for the Cohanim and for all the people of the congregation shall he make atonement.


###### Leviticus 16:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/16:34)


וְהָֽיְתָה־זֹּ֨את לָכֶ֜ם לְחֻקַּ֣ת עוֹלָ֗ם לְכַפֵּ֞ר עַל־בְּנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ מִכׇּל־חַטֹּאתָ֔ם אַחַ֖ת בַּשָּׁנָ֑ה וַיַּ֕עַשׂ כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־מֹשֶֽׁה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This shall be for you as an everlasting statute, to achieve atonement for Bnei Yisroel from all their sins, once a year. He did as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this shall be an everlasting statute to you, to make atonement for the children of Yisra᾽el for all their sins once a year. And he did as the Lord commanded Moshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this shall be to you for an everlasting statute to atone for the children of Israel from all of their sins once in the year. And he [Aaron] did [when Yom Kippur arrived] as the L-rd commanded Moses, [and not for personal aggrandizement].



## Chapter 17

###### Leviticus 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:2)


דַּבֵּ֨ר אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֜ן וְאֶל־בָּנָ֗יו וְאֶל֙ כׇּל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְאָמַרְתָּ֖ אֲלֵיהֶ֑ם זֶ֣ה הַדָּבָ֔ר אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Aharon and to his sons and to all Bnei Yisroel and say to them; this is the edict that Adonoy commanded, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to Aharon, and to his sons, and to all the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them; This is the thing which the Lord has commanded, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to Aaron and to his sons and to all the children of Israel and say to them: This is the thing that the L-rd commanded, saying:


###### Leviticus 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:3)


אִ֥ישׁ אִישׁ֙ מִבֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִשְׁחַ֜ט שׁ֥וֹר אוֹ־כֶ֛שֶׂב אוֹ־עֵ֖ז בַּֽמַּחֲנֶ֑ה א֚וֹ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִשְׁחַ֔ט מִח֖וּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Each and every person of the House of Israel who will slaughter an ox, lamb, or goat within the encampment or who will slaughter [it] beyond the encampment.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What man soever there be of the house of Yisra᾽el, that kills an ox, or lamb, or goat, in the camp, or that kills it outside the camp,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man from the house of Israel who slaughters [(consecrated animals)] an ox or a lamb, or a goat, in the camp [outside of the azarah] or who slaughters outside the camp,


###### Leviticus 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:4)


וְאֶל־פֶּ֜תַח אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵד֮ לֹ֣א הֱבִיאוֹ֒ לְהַקְרִ֤יב קׇרְבָּן֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה לִפְנֵ֖י מִשְׁכַּ֣ן יְהֹוָ֑ה דָּ֣ם יֵחָשֵׁ֞ב לָאִ֤ישׁ הַהוּא֙ דָּ֣ם שָׁפָ֔ךְ וְנִכְרַ֛ת הָאִ֥ישׁ הַה֖וּא מִקֶּ֥רֶב עַמּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting he did not bring it so that it may be brought as an offering to Adonoy, before the Mishkan of Adonoy; it shall be considered for that person as blood, he has spilled blood, and that person shall be cut off from among his people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and brings it not to the door of the Tent of Meeting, to offer an offering to the Lord before the tabernacle of the Lord; blood shall be imputed to that man; he has shed blood; and that man shall be cut off from among his people:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to the door of the tent of meeting he did not bring it to present it as an offering to the L-rd before the mishkan of the L-rd — blood shall it be reckoned to that man, [as if he had shed a man's blood (in which instance he is liable to death)]; he has spilled blood [(to include as thus liable one who sprinkles the blood outside)], and that man will be cut off from his people.


###### Leviticus 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:5)


לְמַ֩עַן֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָבִ֜יאוּ בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל אֶֽת־זִבְחֵיהֶם֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֵ֣ם זֹבְחִים֮ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הַשָּׂדֶה֒ וֶֽהֱבִיאֻ֣ם לַֽיהֹוָ֗ה אֶל־פֶּ֛תַח אֹ֥הֶל מוֹעֵ֖ד אֶל־הַכֹּהֵ֑ן וְזָ֨בְח֜וּ זִבְחֵ֧י שְׁלָמִ֛ים לַֽיהֹוָ֖ה אוֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
So that Bnei Yisroel shall bring their sacrifices that they [would] slaughter on the field, and [instead] they shall bring them to Adonoy to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, to the *kohein*, and slaughter them as peace-offerings to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to the end that the children of Yisra᾽el may bring their sacrifices, which they offer in the open field, that they may bring them to the Lord, to the door of the Tent of Meeting, to the priest, and offer them for peace offerings to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that the children of Israel bring their sacrifices, which they are wont to slaughter in the open field, and bring them to the L-rd, to the door of the tent of meeting, to the Cohein, and slaughter them as peace-offerings to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:6)


וְזָרַ֨ק הַכֹּהֵ֤ן אֶת־הַדָּם֙ עַל־מִזְבַּ֣ח יְהֹוָ֔ה פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד וְהִקְטִ֣יר הַחֵ֔לֶב לְרֵ֥יחַ נִיחֹ֖חַ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall sprinkle the blood on the altar of Adonoy at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting and burn the fat as a pleasing fragrance to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall sprinkle the blood upon the altar of the Lord at the door of the Tent of Meeting, and burn the fat for a sweet savour to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall dash the blood on the altar of the L-rd at the door of the tent of meeting, and he shall smoke the fat, a sweet savor to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:7)


וְלֹא־יִזְבְּח֥וּ עוֹד֙ אֶת־זִבְחֵיהֶ֔ם לַשְּׂעִירִ֕ם אֲשֶׁ֛ר הֵ֥ם זֹנִ֖ים אַחֲרֵיהֶ֑ם חֻקַּ֥ת עוֹלָ֛ם תִּֽהְיֶה־זֹּ֥את לָהֶ֖ם לְדֹרֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They shall no longer slaughter their sacrifices to the demons after which they go astray. This shall be an everlasting statute for them, for their descendants.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they shall no more offer their sacrifices to the demons, after whom they have gone astray. This shall be a statute for ever to them throughout their generations.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they shall sacrifice no more their sacrifices to the satyrs [demons], after which they go astray. An everlasting statute shall this be to them throughout their generations.


###### Leviticus 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:8)


וַאֲלֵהֶ֣ם תֹּאמַ֔ר אִ֥ישׁ אִישׁ֙ מִבֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וּמִן־הַגֵּ֖ר אֲשֶׁר־יָג֣וּר בְּתוֹכָ֑ם אֲשֶׁר־יַעֲלֶ֥ה עֹלָ֖ה אוֹ־זָֽבַח׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
To them you shall say: each and every person of the House of Israel and from the proselyte who will reside among them, who will bring a burnt-offering or sacrifice,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt say to them, Whatever man there be of the house of Yisra᾽el, or of the strangers who sojourn among you, that offers a burnt offering or sacrifice,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to them shall you say: A man from the house of Israel, and from the stranger that dwells in their midst, who shall offer up a burnt-offering or a sacrifice [(to include for liability one who smokes the limbs outside just as one who slaughters outside)]


###### Leviticus 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:9)


וְאֶל־פֶּ֜תַח אֹ֤הֶל מוֹעֵד֙ לֹ֣א יְבִיאֶ֔נּוּ לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת אֹת֖וֹ לַיהֹוָ֑ה וְנִכְרַ֛ת הָאִ֥ישׁ הַה֖וּא מֵעַמָּֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
and to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting he does not bring it, that it be offered to Adonoy; that person shall be cut off from his people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and brings it not to the door of the Tent of Meeting, to offer it to the Lord; that man shall be cut off from among his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and not bring it to the door of the tent of meeting to offer it to the L-rd, that man shall be cut off from his people [(His seed shall be cut off and his days shall be cut off)].


###### Leviticus 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:10)


וְאִ֨ישׁ אִ֜ישׁ מִבֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל וּמִן־הַגֵּר֙ הַגָּ֣ר בְּתוֹכָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יֹאכַ֖ל כׇּל־דָּ֑ם וְנָתַתִּ֣י פָנַ֗י בַּנֶּ֙פֶשׁ֙ הָאֹכֶ֣לֶת אֶת־הַדָּ֔ם וְהִכְרַתִּ֥י אֹתָ֖הּ מִקֶּ֥רֶב עַמָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Each and every person of the House of Israel and from the proselyte who resides among them who will eat any blood; I shall direct my anger on the soul of him who eats the blood and I will cut it off from among its people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whatever man there be of the house of Yisra᾽el, or of the strangers that sojourn among you, that eats any manner of blood: then I will set my face against that person that eats blood, and will cut him off from among his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man from the house of Israel and from the stranger that dwells in their midst that shall eat any blood [(not only that of kodshim)] — I shall direct My regard to that soul which eats the blood, and I shall cut it off from the midst of its people.


###### Leviticus 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:11)


כִּ֣י נֶ֣פֶשׁ הַבָּשָׂר֮ בַּדָּ֣ם הִוא֒ וַאֲנִ֞י נְתַתִּ֤יו לָכֶם֙ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חַ לְכַפֵּ֖ר עַל־נַפְשֹׁתֵיכֶ֑ם כִּֽי־הַדָּ֥ם ה֖וּא בַּנֶּ֥פֶשׁ יְכַפֵּֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For [the] soul of the body is in the blood and I have given it to you [to be placed] on the altar to achieve atonement for your souls, for it is the blood that will achieve atonement for the soul.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the life of the flesh is in the blood: and I have given it to you upon the altar to make atonement for your souls: for it is the blood that makes an atonement for the soul.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the life of [all] flesh is in the blood, wherefore I have given it to you upon the altar to atone for your lives; for it is the blood which atones for the life ["A life (the animal's) for a life"].


###### Leviticus 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:12)


עַל־כֵּ֤ן אָמַ֙רְתִּי֙ לִבְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כׇּל־נֶ֥פֶשׁ מִכֶּ֖ם לֹא־תֹ֣אכַל דָּ֑ם וְהַגֵּ֛ר הַגָּ֥ר בְּתוֹכְכֶ֖ם לֹא־יֹ֥אכַל דָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Therefore, I have said to Bnei Yisroel: Every soul among you shall not eat blood. The proselyte residing among you shall not eat blood.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore I said to the children of Yisra᾽el, None of you shall eat blood, neither shall any stranger that sojourns among you eat blood.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, I have said to the children of Israel: All souls of you [(the elders being exhorted over the children)] shall not eat blood; and the stranger that sojourns in your midst shall not eat blood.


###### Leviticus 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:13)


וְאִ֨ישׁ אִ֜ישׁ מִבְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל וּמִן־הַגֵּר֙ הַגָּ֣ר בְּתוֹכָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָצ֜וּד צֵ֥יד חַיָּ֛ה אוֹ־ע֖וֹף אֲשֶׁ֣ר יֵאָכֵ֑ל וְשָׁפַךְ֙ אֶת־דָּמ֔וֹ וְכִסָּ֖הוּ בֶּעָפָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Each and every person of the House of Israel and from the proselyte residing among them who will ensnare [the catch] any wild animal or fowl that is permitted to be eaten, he shall spill its blood and cover it with earth.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whatever man there be of the children of Yisra᾽el, or of the strangers that sojourn among you, who hunts venison of any beast or bird that may be eaten; he shall even pour out its blood, and cover it with dust.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man of the children of Israel and of the stranger that dwells in their midst, that shall hunt a hunting [(even if not actually "hunted")] of animal or bird that may be eaten [(unclean animals are excluded from what follows)], he shall pour out its blood and cover it with earth.


###### Leviticus 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:14)


כִּֽי־נֶ֣פֶשׁ כׇּל־בָּשָׂ֗ר דָּמ֣וֹ בְנַפְשׁוֹ֮ הוּא֒ וָֽאֹמַר֙ לִבְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל דַּ֥ם כׇּל־בָּשָׂ֖ר לֹ֣א תֹאכֵ֑לוּ כִּ֣י נֶ֤פֶשׁ כׇּל־בָּשָׂר֙ דָּמ֣וֹ הִ֔וא כׇּל־אֹכְלָ֖יו יִכָּרֵֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For [as to] the life of all flesh, its blood is its life, and I have said to Bnei Yisroel: The blood of any flesh, you should not eat, for, the life of all flesh is its blood. Whoever eats of it, shall be cut off.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the life of all flesh is its blood, on which its life depends: therefore I said to the children of Yisra᾽el, you shall eat the blood of no manner of flesh: for the life of all flesh is its blood: whoever eats it shall be cut off.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the life of all flesh — its blood is in [place of] its life [i.e., its life is dependent upon its blood]. And I have said to the children of Israel: The blood of all flesh you shall not eat; for the life of all flesh is its blood; whoever eats it shall be cut off.


###### Leviticus 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:15)


וְכׇל־נֶ֗פֶשׁ אֲשֶׁ֨ר תֹּאכַ֤ל נְבֵלָה֙ וּטְרֵפָ֔ה בָּאֶזְרָ֖ח וּבַגֵּ֑ר וְכִבֶּ֨ס בְּגָדָ֜יו וְרָחַ֥ץ בַּמַּ֛יִם וְטָמֵ֥א עַד־הָעֶ֖רֶב וְטָהֵֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anybody that will eat an improperly slaughtered animal or an animal with a fatal defect among the native-born and the proselyte, shall wash his clothing and bathe in water and is impure until the evening, and [then] becomes purified.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And every person that eats that which died of itself, or that which was torn by beasts, whether it be one of your own country, or a stranger, he shall both wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and be unclean until evening: then shall he be clean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every soul that eats neveilah (carcass) or treifah (what is "torn") [(Scripture speaks here of the neveilah of a clean fowl)], whether citizen or stranger, he shall wash his clothes and bathe in water, and he shall be unclean until the evening, and then he shall be clean.


###### Leviticus 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/17:16)


וְאִם֙ לֹ֣א יְכַבֵּ֔ס וּבְשָׂר֖וֹ לֹ֣א יִרְחָ֑ץ וְנָשָׂ֖א עֲוֺנֽוֹ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he does not wash [his clothes] and does not bathe his flesh, he bears [the burden of] his iniquity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if he wash them not, nor bathe his flesh; then he shall bear his iniquity.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if he does not wash (his clothes) and he does not bathe his flesh [and he eats kodshim or enters the sanctuary], he shall bear his sin [(kareth for not bathing his body; stripes for not washing his clothes)].



## Chapter 18

###### Leviticus 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:2)


דַּבֵּר֙ אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְאָמַרְתָּ֖ אֲלֵהֶ֑ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel and say to them: I am the L-rd your G-d [I am He who said in Sinai: "I am the L-rd your G-d," and you accepted My kingdom upon you; now accept My decrees.]


###### Leviticus 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:3)


כְּמַעֲשֵׂ֧ה אֶֽרֶץ־מִצְרַ֛יִם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יְשַׁבְתֶּם־בָּ֖הּ לֹ֣א תַעֲשׂ֑וּ וּכְמַעֲשֵׂ֣ה אֶֽרֶץ־כְּנַ֡עַן אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֲנִי֩ מֵבִ֨יא אֶתְכֶ֥ם שָׁ֙מָּה֙ לֹ֣א תַעֲשׂ֔וּ וּבְחֻקֹּתֵיהֶ֖ם לֹ֥א תֵלֵֽכוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
After the practice of the Land of Egypt in which you have lived, you shall not do, and the practice of the Land of Canaan, to which I am bringing you, you shall not do and you shall not follow their statutes [customs].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After the doings of the land of Miżrayim, in which you dwelt, shall you not do: and after the doings of the land of Kena῾an, into which I bring you, shall you not do: neither shall you walk in their practices.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As the deeds of the land of Egypt in which you dwelt you shall not do; and as the deeds of the land of Canaan, [(the Egyptians and Canaanites being the most corrupt of the peoples, and the place where Israel dwelt being the most corrupt of all)], to which I bring you [(those peoples conquered by Israel being the most corrupt of all)], you shall not do, and in their statutes [(i.e., in their customs, things which have become like "statutes" for them, like frequenting theaters and stadia)], you shall not walk.


###### Leviticus 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:4)


אֶת־מִשְׁפָּטַ֧י תַּעֲשׂ֛וּ וְאֶת־חֻקֹּתַ֥י תִּשְׁמְר֖וּ לָלֶ֣כֶת בָּהֶ֑ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall fulfill My laws and you shall keep My statutes to follow them, I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall do my judgments, and keep my ordinances, to walk in them, I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My judgments shall you do [("judgments"): "just" things, which, even if they had not been stated, would have warranted observance)], and My statutes shall you keep to walk in them [("statutes"): decrees of the King, "questioned" by the evil inclination and by the nations of the world, such as (the interdictions against) the eating of pig and the wearing of sha'atnez (a mixture of wool and linen), and the purification effected by the waters of the red heifer. ("to walk in them"): Do not exempt yourself from them by saying: "I have learned the wisdom of Israel; I shall now go and learn the wisdom of the nations"]; I am the L-rd your G-d [My decree is upon you and you are not at liberty to exempt yourself from it.]


###### Leviticus 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:5)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֤ם אֶת־חֻקֹּתַי֙ וְאֶת־מִשְׁפָּטַ֔י אֲשֶׁ֨ר יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה אֹתָ֛ם הָאָדָ֖ם וָחַ֣י בָּהֶ֑ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall keep My statutes and My laws which if a man obeys he shall live through them; I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall therefore keep my statutes, and my judgments: which if a man do, he shall live in them: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall keep My statutes and My judgments [(including what may be deduced, though not explicitly stated)], which a man shall do, and he shall live by them [in the world to come]; I am the L-rd [trusted to reward].


###### Leviticus 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:6)


אִ֥ישׁ אִישׁ֙ אֶל־כׇּל־שְׁאֵ֣ר בְּשָׂר֔וֹ לֹ֥א תִקְרְב֖וּ לְגַלּ֣וֹת עֶרְוָ֑ה אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Each and every person—to any of his close kin—You shall not approach to uncover [their] nakedness, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
None of you shall approach to any that is near of kin to him, to uncover her nakedness: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man, to all the kin of his flesh shall not draw near [(lo tikrevu, plural, women being exhorted as well as men)] to uncover nakedness (i.e., to cohabit); I am the L-rd [trusted to reward].


###### Leviticus 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:7)


עֶרְוַ֥ת אָבִ֛יךָ וְעֶרְוַ֥ת אִמְּךָ֖ לֹ֣א תְגַלֵּ֑ה אִמְּךָ֣ הִ֔וא לֹ֥א תְגַלֶּ֖ה עֶרְוָתָֽהּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your father and the nakedness of your mother you shall not uncover; she is your mother, you shall not uncover her nakedness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The nakedness of thy father, or the nakedness of thy mother, shalt thou not uncover: she is thy mother; thou shalt not uncover her nakedness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of your father [i.e., your father's wife] and the nakedness of your mother, [though she not be your father's wife], you shall not reveal. She is your mother; you shall not reveal her nakedness.


###### Leviticus 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:8)


עֶרְוַ֥ת אֵֽשֶׁת־אָבִ֖יךָ לֹ֣א תְגַלֵּ֑ה עֶרְוַ֥ת אָבִ֖יךָ הִֽוא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your fathers wife you shall not uncover; it is your fathers nakedness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The nakedness of thy father’s wife shalt thou not uncover: it is thy father’s nakedness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of the wife of your father, [even after his death], you shall not reveal; it is your father's nakedness.


###### Leviticus 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:9)


עֶרְוַ֨ת אֲחֽוֹתְךָ֤ בַת־אָבִ֙יךָ֙ א֣וֹ בַת־אִמֶּ֔ךָ מוֹלֶ֣דֶת בַּ֔יִת א֖וֹ מוֹלֶ֣דֶת ח֑וּץ לֹ֥א תְגַלֶּ֖ה עֶרְוָתָֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your sister [whether she is] your fathers daughter or your mothers daughter; whether she is born in the house, or she is born outside, you shall not uncover their nakedness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The nakedness of thy sister, the daughter of thy father, or daughter of thy mother, whether she be born at home, or born abroad, their nakedness thou shalt not uncover.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of your sister, the daughter of your father, [even by a woman that he ravished], or the daughter of your mother, whether she be born [of a woman that he is permitted to keep] at home [as a wife], or born [of a woman that he is commanded to send] outside [such as a mamzereth or a nethinah] — you shall not uncover their nakedness.


###### Leviticus 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:10)


עֶרְוַ֤ת בַּת־בִּנְךָ֙ א֣וֹ בַֽת־בִּתְּךָ֔ לֹ֥א תְגַלֶּ֖ה עֶרְוָתָ֑ן כִּ֥י עֶרְוָתְךָ֖ הֵֽנָּה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your sons daughter or your daughters daughter; you shall not uncover their nakedness, for their nakedness is your own.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The nakedness of thy son’s daughter, or of thy daughter’s daughter, their nakedness thou shalt not uncover: for theirs is thy own nakedness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of the daughter of your son [(even) by a woman that you ravished] or the daughter of your daughter [(even) by a woman that you ravished], you shall not reveal their nakedness; for they are your nakedness.


###### Leviticus 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:11)


עֶרְוַ֨ת בַּת־אֵ֤שֶׁת אָבִ֙יךָ֙ מוֹלֶ֣דֶת אָבִ֔יךָ אֲחוֹתְךָ֖ הִ֑וא לֹ֥א תְגַלֶּ֖ה עֶרְוָתָֽהּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of the daughter of your fathers wife who is born to your father; she is your sister, you shall not uncover her nakedness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The nakedness of thy father’s wife’s daughter, begotten of thy father, she is thy sister, thou shalt not uncover her nakedness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of the daughter of your father's wife, [one fit to be a "wife," not a bondswoman or a gentile], begotten of your father — she is your sister; you shall not reveal her nakedness.


###### Leviticus 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:12)


עֶרְוַ֥ת אֲחוֹת־אָבִ֖יךָ לֹ֣א תְגַלֵּ֑ה שְׁאֵ֥ר אָבִ֖יךָ הִֽוא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your fathers sister you shall not uncover; she is your fathers kin.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not uncover the nakedness of thy father’s sister: she is thy father’s near kinswoman.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of the sister of your father you shall not reveal; she is the kin of your father.


###### Leviticus 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:13)


עֶרְוַ֥ת אֲחֽוֹת־אִמְּךָ֖ לֹ֣א תְגַלֵּ֑ה כִּֽי־שְׁאֵ֥ר אִמְּךָ֖ הִֽוא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your mothers sister you shall not uncover, for she is your mothers kin.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not uncover the nakedness of thy mother’s sister: for she is thy mother’s near kinswoman.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of the sister of your mother you shall not reveal; for she is the kin of your mother.


###### Leviticus 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:14)


עֶרְוַ֥ת אֲחִֽי־אָבִ֖יךָ לֹ֣א תְגַלֵּ֑ה אֶל־אִשְׁתּוֹ֙ לֹ֣א תִקְרָ֔ב דֹּדָֽתְךָ֖ הִֽוא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your fathers brother you shall not uncover. You shall not approach his wife, she is your aunt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not uncover the nakedness of thy father’s brother, thou shalt not approach to his wife: she is thy aunt.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of the brother of your father you shall not reveal; to his wife do not come near; she is your aunt.


###### Leviticus 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:15)


עֶרְוַ֥ת כַּלָּֽתְךָ֖ לֹ֣א תְגַלֵּ֑ה אֵ֤שֶׁת בִּנְךָ֙ הִ֔וא לֹ֥א תְגַלֶּ֖ה עֶרְוָתָֽהּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your daughter-in-law you shall not uncover; she is your sons wife, you shall not uncover her nakedness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not uncover the nakedness of thy daughter in law: she is thy son’s wife; thou shalt not uncover her nakedness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of your daughter-in-law you shall not reveal. She is the wife of your son [("wife"): to exclude one he has ravished, a bondswoman, or a gentile]; you shall not reveal her nakedness.


###### Leviticus 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:16)


עֶרְוַ֥ת אֵֽשֶׁת־אָחִ֖יךָ לֹ֣א תְגַלֵּ֑ה עֶרְוַ֥ת אָחִ֖יךָ הִֽוא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your brothers wife you shall not uncover; it is your brothers nakedness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not uncover the nakedness of thy brother’s wife: it is thy brother’s nakedness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of the wife of your brother you shall not reveal; it is the nakedness of your brother.


###### Leviticus 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:17)


עֶרְוַ֥ת אִשָּׁ֛ה וּבִתָּ֖הּ לֹ֣א תְגַלֵּ֑ה אֶֽת־בַּת־בְּנָ֞הּ וְאֶת־בַּת־בִּתָּ֗הּ לֹ֤א תִקַּח֙ לְגַלּ֣וֹת עֶרְוָתָ֔הּ שַׁאֲרָ֥ה הֵ֖נָּה זִמָּ֥ה הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of a woman and her daughter you shall not uncover. The daughter of her son and the daughter of her daughter you shall not take to uncover her nakedness, they are kin; it is lewdness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not uncover the nakedness of a woman and her daughter, neither shalt thou take her son’s daughter, or her daughter’s daughter, to uncover her nakedness; for they are her near kinswomen: it is foulness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The nakedness of a [(halachically) married] woman and her daughter you shall not uncover [(But one is permitted to marry the daughter of a woman he ravished)]. The daughter of her son and the daughter of her daughter you shall not take to reveal her nakedness. They are kin; it is scheming.


###### Leviticus 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:18)


וְאִשָּׁ֥ה אֶל־אֲחֹתָ֖הּ לֹ֣א תִקָּ֑ח לִצְרֹ֗ר לְגַלּ֧וֹת עֶרְוָתָ֛הּ עָלֶ֖יהָ בְּחַיֶּֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not take a woman and her sister to vex [her] to uncover her nakedness with the other, in her lifetime.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither shalt thou take a wife to her sister, as her rival, and uncover her nakedness, beside the other in her life time.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a woman together with her sister you shall not take, to be rivals [i.e., to make one a rival to the other], to reveal her nakedness upon her in her lifetime [(If he divorced her, he may not marry her sister as long as she is alive.)]


###### Leviticus 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:19)


וְאֶל־אִשָּׁ֖ה בְּנִדַּ֣ת טֻמְאָתָ֑הּ לֹ֣א תִקְרַ֔ב לְגַלּ֖וֹת עֶרְוָתָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
To a woman while in her menstrual impurity, you shall not approach to uncover her nakedness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also thou shalt not approach to a woman in the impurity of her menstrual flow, to uncover her nakedness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to a woman in the niddah state of her uncleanliness, you shall not come near to reveal her nakedness.


###### Leviticus 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:20)


וְאֶל־אֵ֙שֶׁת֙ עֲמִֽיתְךָ֔ לֹא־תִתֵּ֥ן שְׁכׇבְתְּךָ֖ לְזָ֑רַע לְטׇמְאָה־בָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
With your friends wife you shall not lie carnally, to defile yourself through her.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover thou shalt not lie carnally with thy neighbour’s wife, to defile thyself with her.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to the wife of your neighbor do not give your lying for seed to become unclean to her.


###### Leviticus 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:21)


וּמִֽזַּרְעֲךָ֥ לֹא־תִתֵּ֖ן לְהַעֲבִ֣יר לַמֹּ֑לֶךְ וְלֹ֧א תְחַלֵּ֛ל אֶת־שֵׁ֥ם אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not hand over any of your children to be passed through [the worship of] *Molech* and you shall not defile the Name of your God, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt not let any of thy seed pass through (the fire) to Molekh, neither shalt thou profane the name of thy God: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from your seed you shall not give [to the priests of Molech (a kind of idolatry)] to pass [between two large pyres] to Molech. And you shall not desecrate the name of your G-d; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:22)


וְאֶ֨ת־זָכָ֔ר לֹ֥א תִשְׁכַּ֖ב מִשְׁכְּבֵ֣י אִשָּׁ֑ה תּוֹעֵבָ֖ה הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not lie with a male [conjugally] as one lies with a woman; it is an abomination.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not lie with a man after the manner of a woman: it is abomination.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And with a male you shall not lie the lyings of a woman; it is an abomination.


###### Leviticus 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:23)


וּבְכׇל־בְּהֵמָ֛ה לֹא־תִתֵּ֥ן שְׁכׇבְתְּךָ֖ לְטׇמְאָה־בָ֑הּ וְאִשָּׁ֗ה לֹֽא־תַעֲמֹ֞ד לִפְנֵ֧י בְהֵמָ֛ה לְרִבְעָ֖הּ תֶּ֥בֶל הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Together with any animal you shall not lie [conjugally] to defile yourself with it. A woman shall not stand before an animal to copulate with it; it is defilement.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither shalt thou lie with any beast to defile thyself with it: neither shall any woman stand before a beast to lie down before it: it is perversion.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And with every beast do not give your lying to become unclean with it. And a woman shall not stand before a beast to mount her; it is dissolute.


###### Leviticus 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:24)


אַל־תִּֽטַּמְּא֖וּ בְּכׇל־אֵ֑לֶּה כִּ֤י בְכׇל־אֵ֙לֶּה֙ נִטְמְא֣וּ הַגּוֹיִ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִ֥י מְשַׁלֵּ֖חַ מִפְּנֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not defile yourselves in any one of these [ways], for in all of these [ways] the nations have become defiled [those] that I am driving away from before you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Defile not yourselves in any of these things: for in all these the nations were defiled which I cast out before you:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not become unclean with all of these. For with all of these the nations became unclean, which I am sending away before you.


###### Leviticus 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:25)


וַתִּטְמָ֣א הָאָ֔רֶץ וָאֶפְקֹ֥ד עֲוֺנָ֖הּ עָלֶ֑יהָ וַתָּקִ֥א הָאָ֖רֶץ אֶת־יֹשְׁבֶֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The land became defiled and I judged its iniquities upon it and the land expelled its inhabitants.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the land was defiled: therefore I do punish its iniquity upon it, and the land vomits out her inhabitants.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the land became unclean, and I visited its sin upon it, and the land vomited out its inhabitants.


###### Leviticus 18:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:26)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֣ם אַתֶּ֗ם אֶת־חֻקֹּתַי֙ וְאֶת־מִשְׁפָּטַ֔י וְלֹ֣א תַעֲשׂ֔וּ מִכֹּ֥ל הַתּוֹעֵבֹ֖ת הָאֵ֑לֶּה הָֽאֶזְרָ֔ח וְהַגֵּ֖ר הַגָּ֥ר בְּתוֹכְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall adhere to My statutes and to My laws and not do any of these abominations [both] the native born and the proselyte who resides among you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall therefore keep my statutes and my judgments, and shall not commit any of these abominations; neither any of your own nation, nor any stranger that sojourns among you:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall keep My statutes and My judgments, and you shall not do any of these abominations, the citizen and the stranger that sojourns among you.


###### Leviticus 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:27)


כִּ֚י אֶת־כׇּל־הַתּוֹעֵבֹ֣ת הָאֵ֔ל עָשׂ֥וּ אַנְשֵֽׁי־הָאָ֖רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לִפְנֵיכֶ֑ם וַתִּטְמָ֖א הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For all of these abominations were done by the people of the land who came before you and the land became defiled.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for all these abominations have the men of the land done, who were before you, and the land is defiled;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For all of these abominations were done by the people of the land which were before you, and the land became unclean.


###### Leviticus 18:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:28)


וְלֹֽא־תָקִ֤יא הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ אֶתְכֶ֔ם בְּטַֽמַּאֲכֶ֖ם אֹתָ֑הּ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר קָאָ֛ה אֶת־הַגּ֖וֹי אֲשֶׁ֥ר לִפְנֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[So that] the land not expel you when you defile it as it expelled the nation before you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that the land vomit not you out also, when you defile it, as it has vomited out the nations that were before you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that the land not vomit you out by your making it unclean, as it vomited out the nation that was before you.


###### Leviticus 18:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:29)


כִּ֚י כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר יַעֲשֶׂ֔ה מִכֹּ֥ל הַתּוֹעֵבֹ֖ת הָאֵ֑לֶּה וְנִכְרְת֛וּ הַנְּפָשׁ֥וֹת הָעֹשֹׂ֖ת מִקֶּ֥רֶב עַמָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For anyone who will do any of these abominations, their souls shall be cut off, those who do these things, from among their people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For whoever shall commit any of these abominations, even the persons that commit them shall be cut off from among their people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For all who do any of these abominations, the souls [(male and female are implied)] that do so shall be cut off from the midst of their people.


###### Leviticus 18:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/18:30)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֣ם אֶת־מִשְׁמַרְתִּ֗י לְבִלְתִּ֨י עֲשׂ֜וֹת מֵחֻקּ֤וֹת הַתּֽוֹעֵבֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נַעֲשׂ֣וּ לִפְנֵיכֶ֔ם וְלֹ֥א תִֽטַּמְּא֖וּ בָּהֶ֑ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall keep my watch that you shall not do any of the abominable customs that were done before you and you will not defile yourselves through them. I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore shall you keep my ordinance, that you commit not any one of these abominable customs, which were practised before you, and that you defile not yourselves in them: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall keep My keeping [(beth-din being exhorted thereto)], not to do in the manner of the abominations that were done before you, and you shall not become unclean in them; I am the L-rd your G-d [(But if you defile yourselves, then I am not your G-d, and how do you then avail Me?)]



## Chapter 19

###### Leviticus 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:2)


דַּבֵּ֞ר אֶל־כׇּל־עֲדַ֧ת בְּנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל וְאָמַרְתָּ֥ אֲלֵהֶ֖ם קְדֹשִׁ֣ים תִּהְי֑וּ כִּ֣י קָד֔וֹשׁ אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to the entire congregation [of] Bnei Yisroel and say to them: You shall be holy, for I, Adonoy, your God, am holy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to all the congregation of the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, You shall be holy: for I the Lord your God am holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the entire congregation of the children of Israel, [(This section was stated in the presence of all, for most of the major tenets of Torah are inherent in it.)] and say to them: Holy shall you be. [Separate yourselves from illicit relations], for holy am I, the L-rd your G-d.


###### Leviticus 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:3)


אִ֣ישׁ אִמּ֤וֹ וְאָבִיו֙ תִּירָ֔אוּ וְאֶת־שַׁבְּתֹתַ֖י תִּשְׁמֹ֑רוּ אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man—you shall fear his mother and father and keep My Sabbaths, I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall fear every man his mother, and his father, and keep my sabbaths: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man, his mother and his father shall you fear, [("mother" is stated before "father" because a man normally fears his father more than he does his mother)], and My Sabbaths shall you keep [(If parents bid their children to transgress mitzvoth, they are not to be heeded)]: I am the L-rd your G-d [(Both you and your parents are obliged to honor Me.)] [What is "fear"? Not sitting in his place, not speaking in his stead, not contradicting his words.]


###### Leviticus 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:4)


אַל־תִּפְנוּ֙ אֶל־הָ֣אֱלִילִ֔ם וֵֽאלֹהֵי֙ מַסֵּכָ֔ה לֹ֥א תַעֲשׂ֖וּ לָכֶ֑ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not turn to the idols and do not make molten gods for yourselves, I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Turn not to idols, nor make to yourselves molten gods: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not turn to the elilim [(lit., "non-entities") to serve them], and molten gods [(If you turn to them, you will make them gods)] do not make for yourselves [(neither you for others nor others for you)]; I am the L-rd your G-d.


###### Leviticus 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:5)


וְכִ֧י תִזְבְּח֛וּ זֶ֥בַח שְׁלָמִ֖ים לַיהֹוָ֑ה לִֽרְצֹנְכֶ֖ם תִּזְבָּחֻֽהוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you slaughter a peace-offering to Adonoy, you shall slaughter it to be acceptable for you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if you offer a sacrifice of peace offering to the Lord, you shall offer it so that it may be favourably accepted.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when you slaughter a sacrifice of peace-offerings to the L-rd — so that you find favor [with it in the eyes of the L-rd] shall you slaughter it.


###### Leviticus 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:6)


בְּי֧וֹם זִבְחֲכֶ֛ם יֵאָכֵ֖ל וּמִֽמׇּחֳרָ֑ת וְהַנּוֹתָר֙ עַד־י֣וֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁ֔י בָּאֵ֖שׁ יִשָּׂרֵֽף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the day that it is slaughtered it may be eaten and on the next day. Whatever is left over by the third day shall be burned in fire.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It shall be eaten the same day you offer it, and on the morrow: and if aught remain until the third day, it shall be burnt in fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[i.e., Slaughter it with the intent that] it will be eaten on the day that you sacrifice it and on the morrow, and (that) what remains of it [nothar] on the third day will be burned with fire [as indicated in 7:16-17].


###### Leviticus 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:7)


וְאִ֛ם הֵאָכֹ֥ל יֵאָכֵ֖ל בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁ֑י פִּגּ֥וּל ה֖וּא לֹ֥א יֵרָצֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And if it should be eaten at all on the third day it is an abomination, it will not be favorably accepted.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be eaten at all on the third day, it is abominable; it shall not be accepted.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if it is eaten on the third day [(exegetically, if it is eaten outside of its prescribed place)], it is piggul ["abhorrent"]; it shall not be accepted.


###### Leviticus 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:8)


וְאֹֽכְלָיו֙ עֲוֺנ֣וֹ יִשָּׂ֔א כִּֽי־אֶת־קֹ֥דֶשׁ יְהֹוָ֖ה חִלֵּ֑ל וְנִכְרְתָ֛ה הַנֶּ֥פֶשׁ הַהִ֖וא מֵעַמֶּֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever eats from it shall bear his iniquity, for the sacred [things] of Adonoy he has defiled. That [persons] soul shall be cut off from its people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore everyone that eats it shall bear his iniquity, because he has profaned the hallowed thing of the Lord: and that soul shalt be cut off from among his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the eater of it [absolute nothar] shall bear his sin, for he has profaned the holy thing of the L-rd; and that soul shall be cut off from its people.


###### Leviticus 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:9)


וּֽבְקֻצְרְכֶם֙ אֶת־קְצִ֣יר אַרְצְכֶ֔ם לֹ֧א תְכַלֶּ֛ה פְּאַ֥ת שָׂדְךָ֖ לִקְצֹ֑ר וְלֶ֥קֶט קְצִֽירְךָ֖ לֹ֥א תְלַקֵּֽט׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you will reap the harvest of your land, you shall not completely reap a corner of your field, and the gleanings of your harvest you shall not gather.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when you reap the harvest of your land, thou shalt not wholly reap the corners of thy field, neither shalt thou gather the gleaning of thy harvest.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when you cut the harvest of your land, do not end off the corner (peah) of your field to cut it; and the gleaning (leket) of your harvest, [one or two ears (but not more) which fall to the ground during reaping] you shall not gather.


###### Leviticus 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:10)


וְכַרְמְךָ֙ לֹ֣א תְעוֹלֵ֔ל וּפֶ֥רֶט כַּרְמְךָ֖ לֹ֣א תְלַקֵּ֑ט לֶֽעָנִ֤י וְלַגֵּר֙ תַּעֲזֹ֣ב אֹתָ֔ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not glean your vineyard, and the fallen grapes of your vineyard, you shall not gather. For the poor and the proselyte you shall leave them, I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt not glean thy vineyard, neither shalt thou gather the single grapes of thy vineyard; thou shalt leave them for the poor and stranger: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your vineyard you shall not glean [You shall not take the oleiloth (tendrils which have no grapes bunched together or hanging down from the end)]; and the fallen fruit (peret) of your vineyard [single grapes which fall to the ground at the time of reaping] you shall not gather. To the poor man and to the stranger you shall leave them; I am the L-rd your G-d [a Judge, exacting punishment (of those who steal from the poor)].


###### Leviticus 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:11)


לֹ֖א תִּגְנֹ֑בוּ וְלֹא־תְכַחֲשׁ֥וּ וְלֹֽא־תְשַׁקְּר֖וּ אִ֥ישׁ בַּעֲמִיתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not steal. You shall not deny [a rightful claim] and each one of you shall not lie to each other.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not steal, neither deal falsely, neither lie one to another.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not steal [(an exhortation against stealing money)], and you shall not deny [(an exhortation towards the payment of the principal and the fifth in an instance of denial (5:22)], and you shall not lie one to another [(an exhortation towards the payment of the principal and the fifth in an instance of swearing falsely). If you steal, you will come to deny, to lie, and to swear falsely.]


###### Leviticus 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:12)


וְלֹֽא־תִשָּׁבְע֥וּ בִשְׁמִ֖י לַשָּׁ֑קֶר וְחִלַּלְתָּ֛ אֶת־שֵׁ֥ם אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not swear falsely by My Name profaning the Name of your God, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall not swear by my name falsely, neither shalt thou profane the name of thy God: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall not swear in My name [(even by an epithet)] falsely and (thereby) profane the name of your G-d; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:13)


לֹֽא־תַעֲשֹׁ֥ק אֶת־רֵֽעֲךָ֖ וְלֹ֣א תִגְזֹ֑ל לֹֽא־תָלִ֞ין פְּעֻלַּ֥ת שָׂכִ֛יר אִתְּךָ֖ עַד־בֹּֽקֶר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not withhold the wages of your fellow [Jew] and you shall not rob. It shall not remain overnight, the wages of a day-laborer, keeping it in your possession until morning.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not defraud thy neighbour, neither rob him: the wages of him that is hired shall not abide with thee all night until the morning.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not oppress your neighbor [(This alludes to holding back the wages of a hired worker)], and you shall not rob. There shall not abide with you the wages of a [day] laborer until [the next] morning.


###### Leviticus 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:14)


לֹא־תְקַלֵּ֣ל חֵרֵ֔שׁ וְלִפְנֵ֣י עִוֵּ֔ר לֹ֥א תִתֵּ֖ן מִכְשֹׁ֑ל וְיָרֵ֥אתָ מֵּאֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not curse a deaf person and before a blind person you shall not place a stumbling-block; you shall fear your God, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not curse the deaf, nor put a stumbling block before the blind, but shalt fear thy God: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not curse the deaf man [(i.e., any man who is alive)], and before the blind man [i.e., one who is "blind" in a certain matter], do not place a stumbling-block. And you shall fear your G-d, [who knows your intentions]; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:15)


לֹא־תַעֲשׂ֥וּ עָ֙וֶל֙ בַּמִּשְׁפָּ֔ט לֹא־תִשָּׂ֣א פְנֵי־דָ֔ל וְלֹ֥א תֶהְדַּ֖ר פְּנֵ֣י גָד֑וֹל בְּצֶ֖דֶק תִּשְׁפֹּ֥ט עֲמִיתֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
