You shall not commit injustice in judgement, you shall not favor a poor man and you shall not show honor to a powerful man. With righteousness shall you judge your fellow [Jew].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall do no unrighteousness in judgment: thou shalt not respect the person of the poor, nor honour the person of the mighty: but in righteousness shalt thou judge thy neighbour.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not do wrong in judgment [(A judge who perverts justice is called "wrong," "hated," "revolting," "rejected," "abominable")]. You shall not lift up the face of the poor man [Do not say: He is poor and the rich man is obliged to sustain him; I shall vindicate him in judgment so that he can support himself honorably.], and you shall not favor the face of the great one [Do not say: He is a wealthy man, the son of great ones; how can I shame him and look on at his shame!] In righteousness shall you judge your neighbor.


###### Leviticus 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:16)


לֹא־תֵלֵ֤ךְ רָכִיל֙ בְּעַמֶּ֔יךָ לֹ֥א תַעֲמֹ֖ד עַל־דַּ֣ם רֵעֶ֑ךָ¹ אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃

footnotes:
¹ (בכתר ארם צובה היה כתוב רֵעֶ֑יךָ)


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not be a tale-bearer among your people. You shall not stand idly by [when] the blood (life) of your fellow [is in danger]. I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not go up and down as a talebearer among thy people: neither shalt thou stand aside when mischief befalls thy neighbour: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not go spying out among your people [with an eye to slandering]. You shall not stand by the blood of your neighbor, [watching him die, when you can help him]; I am the L-rd, [trusted to reward and trusted to exact punishment].


###### Leviticus 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:17)


לֹֽא־תִשְׂנָ֥א אֶת־אָחִ֖יךָ בִּלְבָבֶ֑ךָ הוֹכֵ֤חַ תּוֹכִ֙יחַ֙ אֶת־עֲמִיתֶ֔ךָ וְלֹא־תִשָּׂ֥א עָלָ֖יו חֵֽטְא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not hate your brother in your heart; You shall certainly rebuke your friend; [but] you shall not bear a sin on his account.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not hate thy brother in thy heart: thou shalt certainly rebuke thy neighbour, and not suffer sin on his account.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not hate your brother in your heart. Reprove shall you reprove your neighbor, but do not bear sin because of him [(Do not shame him in public)].


###### Leviticus 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:18)


לֹֽא־תִקֹּ֤ם וְלֹֽא־תִטֹּר֙ אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י עַמֶּ֔ךָ וְאָֽהַבְתָּ֥ לְרֵעֲךָ֖ כָּמ֑וֹךָ אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not take revenge or bear a grudge against the children of your people. You shall love your fellow [Jew] as yourself, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not avenge, nor bear any grudge against the children of thy people, but thou shalt love thy neighbour as thyself: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not take revenge [e.g., "You did not lend me; I will not lend you!"], and you shall not bear a grudge against the children of your people [e.g.. "I am not like you who did not lend me; I shall lend you!"]. And you shall love your neighbor as yourself. [(R. Akiva said: This is an all-embracing principle in Torah)]; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:19)


אֶֽת־חֻקֹּתַי֮ תִּשְׁמֹ֒רוּ֒ בְּהֶמְתְּךָ֙ לֹא־תַרְבִּ֣יעַ כִּלְאַ֔יִם שָׂדְךָ֖ לֹא־תִזְרַ֣ע כִּלְאָ֑יִם וּבֶ֤גֶד כִּלְאַ֙יִם֙ שַֽׁעַטְנֵ֔ז לֹ֥א יַעֲלֶ֖ה עָלֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Keep My statutes; Do not cross-breed your animals with different species. Do not sow your field with a mixture of different seeds. A garment that contains *shatnez* [a forbidden mixture of linen and wool] you shall not wear.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall keep my statutes. Thou shalt not let thy cattle gender with a diverse kind: thou shalt not sow thy field with mingled seed: neither shall a garment mingled of linen and wool come upon thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My statutes (chukim), [which follow,] shall you keep [chukim are "decrees of the King," for which no reasons are given]: You shall not mate your beast with a diverse kind (kilayim); your field you shall not sow with diverse seeds (kilayim), and a mingled (kilayim, interwoven) garment (sha'atnez) shall not come upon you ["sha'atnez" — felt-stuff (of wool and linen) mingled. Acronymically, "shua" (hackled), "tavui" (spun), "nuz" (twined)].


###### Leviticus 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:20)


וְ֠אִ֠ישׁ כִּֽי־יִשְׁכַּ֨ב אֶת־אִשָּׁ֜ה שִׁכְבַת־זֶ֗רַע וְהִ֤וא שִׁפְחָה֙ נֶחֱרֶ֣פֶת לְאִ֔ישׁ וְהׇפְדֵּה֙ לֹ֣א נִפְדָּ֔תָה א֥וֹ חֻפְשָׁ֖הֿ לֹ֣א נִתַּן־לָ֑הּ בִּקֹּ֧רֶת תִּהְיֶ֛ה לֹ֥א יוּמְת֖וּ כִּי־לֹ֥א חֻפָּֽשָׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a man lies with a woman in a conjugal manner, and she is a maid-servant designated for a [specific] man and she has certainly not been redeemed, or her freedom was not given to her; there shall be an investigation they shall not be put to death, for her freedom was not given to her.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whoever lies carnally with a woman, that is a bondmaid, designated to a man, and not wholly redeemed, nor freedom given her; inquiry shall be made; they shall not be put to death, because she was not free.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man, if he lie with a woman, a lying of seed, and she be a [Canaanite] maidservant [half bondswoman-half freewoman]; bound [i.e., betrothed] to a man [a Hebrew bondsman], and she has not been redeemed [with money], or freedom has not been given to her [by writ], she shall be inquired after [towards her (but not his) receiving stripes]. They shall not be put to death, for she was not freed [(But if she had been freed, the betrothal is bona fide, and the death penalty obtains)].


###### Leviticus 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:21)


וְהֵבִ֤יא אֶת־אֲשָׁמוֹ֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה אֶל־פֶּ֖תַח אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד אֵ֖יל אָשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall bring his guilt-offering to Adonoy, to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, a guilt-offering-ram.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall bring his guilt offering to the Lord, to the door of the Tent of Meeting, a ram for a guilt offering.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he (the man who lay with her) shall bring his guilt-offering to the L-rd, to the door of the tent of meeting, a ram for a guilt-offering.


###### Leviticus 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:22)


וְכִפֶּר֩ עָלָ֨יו הַכֹּהֵ֜ן בְּאֵ֤יל הָֽאָשָׁם֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה עַל־חַטָּאת֖וֹ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָטָ֑א וְנִסְלַ֣ח ל֔וֹ מֵחַטָּאת֖וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָטָֽא׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall atone for him with the guilt-offering-ram before Adonoy for the sin which he committed and he will be forgiven for the sin which he committed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall make atonement for him with the ram of the guilt offering before the Lord for his sin which he has done: and the sin which he has done shall be forgiven him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall make atonement for him with the ram of the guilt-offering before the L-rd for his sin which he has sinned, and it shall be forgiven him for his sin which he has sinned [(deliberate as well as unwitting)].


###### Leviticus 19:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:23)


וְכִי־תָבֹ֣אוּ אֶל־הָאָ֗רֶץ וּנְטַעְתֶּם֙ כׇּל־עֵ֣ץ מַאֲכָ֔ל וַעֲרַלְתֶּ֥ם עׇרְלָת֖וֹ אֶת־פִּרְי֑וֹ שָׁלֹ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֗ים יִהְיֶ֥ה לָכֶ֛ם עֲרֵלִ֖ים לֹ֥א יֵאָכֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you will enter the land and you will plant any food-bearing tree, you shall regard its fruit as closed off. [For] three years it shall be closed off to you, it shall not be eaten.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when you shall come into the land, and shall have planted all manner of trees for food, then you shall reckon their fruit as uncircumcised: three years shall it be as uncircumcised unto you: it shall not be eaten.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when you come to the land and you plant every fruit tree, then you shall count its fruit as forbidden. Three years [beginning with the time of its planting] shall it be to you as forbidden [(Its fruit remains forbidden even if secreted until after three years)]; it shall not be eaten.


###### Leviticus 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:24)


וּבַשָּׁנָה֙ הָרְבִיעִ֔ת יִהְיֶ֖ה כׇּל־פִּרְי֑וֹ קֹ֥דֶשׁ הִלּוּלִ֖ים לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the fourth year, all of its fruit shall be holy [upon which to say] praise[s to] Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But in the fourth year all its fruit shall be holy for praisegiving to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in the fourth year, all of its fruit shall be holy for praise to the L-rd [(like ma'aser sheini. Just as ma'aser sheini is not eaten outside the walls of Jerusalem unless redeemed, this too. And this is "praise to the L-rd." He carries it there to praise the L-rd.)]


###### Leviticus 19:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:25)


וּבַשָּׁנָ֣ה הַחֲמִישִׁ֗ת תֹּֽאכְלוּ֙ אֶת־פִּרְי֔וֹ לְהוֹסִ֥יף לָכֶ֖ם תְּבוּאָת֑וֹ אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the fifth year, you may eat its fruit, so that it will increase its produce for you, I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in the fifth year shall you eat of its fruit, that it may yield to you its increase: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in the fifth year you may eat its fruit, to increase for you its produce [(Your observance of the mitzvah for four years will lead to an increase of productivity)]. I am the L-rd your G-d, [who assures you thus, and I am trusted to fulfill My assurance].


###### Leviticus 19:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:26)


לֹ֥א תֹאכְל֖וּ עַל־הַדָּ֑ם לֹ֥א תְנַחֲשׁ֖וּ וְלֹ֥א תְעוֹנֵֽנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not eat [sacred offerings] [before] the blood [is sprinkled on the altar]. You shall not act on the basis of omens nor act on the basis of auspicious times.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not eat anything with the blood: neither shall you use enchantment, nor observe times.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not eat over the blood [(an exhortation against eating the flesh of kodshim before the sprinkling of the blood, an exhortation against eating (the flesh of) a non-consecrated beast before the life has gone out of it, etc.)]. You shall not practice divination, [in the manner of those who (attempt to) divine propitious times) from certain occurrences, viz.: "His bread fell from his mouth," "A deer crossed his path," etc.], and you shall not practice soothsaying (lo teoneinu) from onoth ("times"), e.g., "This day is auspicious for beginning work," etc.]


###### Leviticus 19:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:27)


לֹ֣א תַקִּ֔פוּ פְּאַ֖ת רֹאשְׁכֶ֑ם וְלֹ֣א תַשְׁחִ֔ית אֵ֖ת פְּאַ֥ת זְקָנֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not round the corners of [the hair on] your heads and you shall not destroy [by shaving] the edges of your beard.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not round the corners of your heads, neither shalt thou mar the corners of thy beard.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not round off the corners of your head [("rounding off" is aligning the hair of one's temples with that behind his ears and that of his forehead)], and you shall not destroy the corners of your beard [the end of the beard and its borders].


###### Leviticus 19:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:28)


וְשֶׂ֣רֶט לָנֶ֗פֶשׁ לֹ֤א תִתְּנוּ֙ בִּבְשַׂרְכֶ֔ם וּכְתֹ֣בֶת קַֽעֲקַ֔ע לֹ֥א תִתְּנ֖וּ בָּכֶ֑ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Gashes for the dead do not make in your skin. Do not put tattoo marks on yourselves, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not make any cuttings in your flesh for the dead, nor print any marks upon you: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a cutting for a [dead] person you shall not make in your flesh, [as was the custom of the Emorites]; and writing that is engraved [in the skin so that it can never be removed] you shall not make upon yourselves — I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 19:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:29)


אַל־תְּחַלֵּ֥ל אֶֽת־בִּתְּךָ֖ לְהַזְנוֹתָ֑הּ וְלֹא־תִזְנֶ֣ה הָאָ֔רֶץ וּמָלְאָ֥ה הָאָ֖רֶץ זִמָּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not defile your daughter by causing her to engage in harlotry lest the land be turned astray, and the land be filled with lewdness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not prostitute thy daughter, to cause her to be a harlot; lest the land fall to harlotry, and the land become full of foulness.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not profane your daughter to make her a harlot [(the allusion is to handing over one's unwedded daughter for intercourse not for the purpose of marriage)], lest the land fall into harlotry, [producing its fruits elsewhere, and not in your land], and the land be filled with lewdness.


###### Leviticus 19:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:30)


אֶת־שַׁבְּתֹתַ֣י תִּשְׁמֹ֔רוּ וּמִקְדָּשִׁ֖י תִּירָ֑אוּ אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall keep My Shabbosos and you shall fear My Sanctuary, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall keep my sabbaths, and reverence my sanctuary: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My Sabbaths shall you keep, and My sanctuary shall you fear [(One may not enter the sanctuary with his staff, or his shoes, or with his money belt, or with the dust on his feet) — but the construction of the Temple does not override the Sabbath]; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 19:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:31)


אַל־תִּפְנ֤וּ אֶל־הָאֹבֹת֙ וְאֶל־הַיִּדְּעֹנִ֔ים אַל־תְּבַקְשׁ֖וּ לְטׇמְאָ֣ה בָהֶ֑ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not turn to mediums nor seek out oracles; do not seek [them] out, so as to defile yourselves through them. I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not apply to mediums or wizards, nor seek to be defiled by them: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not turn to the ovoth [a ba'al ov is a wizard who speaks from his armpits], and to the yidonim [a yidoni is one who places the bone of an animal called "yidoa" in his mouth, and the bone speaks]. Do not seek [to occupy yourselves with them] to be defiled by them [i.e., If you occupy yourselves with them you defile yourselves before Me, and I will abominate you.] I am the L-rd your G-d [Know whom you are exchanging for whom.]


###### Leviticus 19:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:32)


מִפְּנֵ֤י שֵׂיבָה֙ תָּק֔וּם וְהָדַרְתָּ֖ פְּנֵ֣י זָקֵ֑ן וְיָרֵ֥אתָ מֵּאֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In the presence of the elderly you shall rise and you shall respect an elder; you shall have fear of your God, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt rise up before the hoary head, and honour the face of the old man, and fear thy God: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Before the hoary head you shall rise, and you shall honor the face of the elder, [(the "elder":) one who has acquired wisdom. (What is "honor"? One should not sit in his place or contradict his words)]. And you shall fear your G-d [Do not close your eyes (to the hoary head and the elder) as if you did not see him — your G-d knows your heart]; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 19:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:33)


וְכִֽי־יָג֧וּר אִתְּךָ֛ גֵּ֖ר בְּאַרְצְכֶ֑ם לֹ֥א תוֹנ֖וּ אֹתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a proselyte comes to live with you, in your land, do not cause him anguish.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a stranger sojourn with thee in your land, you shall not wrong him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if there dwell among you a stranger in your land, you shall not oppress him [(verbally viz.: "Yesterday you served idols, and today you come to study Torah, which issued from the mouth of the Almighty!")]


###### Leviticus 19:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:34)


כְּאֶזְרָ֣ח מִכֶּם֩ יִהְיֶ֨ה לָכֶ֜ם הַגֵּ֣ר**׀** הַגָּ֣ר אִתְּכֶ֗ם וְאָהַבְתָּ֥ לוֹ֙ כָּמ֔וֹךָ כִּֽי־גֵרִ֥ים הֱיִיתֶ֖ם בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
As one of your native born he shall be considered by you, the proselyte who lives with you, and you shall love him as yourself, for you were strangers in the land of Egypt, I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the stranger that dwells with you shall be to you as one born among you, and thou shalt love him as thyself; for you were strangers in the land of Miżrayim: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As the home-born among you shall he be to you, the stranger that lives among you. And you shall love him as yourself, for you were strangers in the land of Egypt [("Do not impute your own blemish to your fellow-man")]; I am the L-rd your G-d ["your" (plural) — your G-d and his].


###### Leviticus 19:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:35)


לֹא־תַעֲשׂ֥וּ עָ֖וֶל בַּמִּשְׁפָּ֑ט בַּמִּדָּ֕ה בַּמִּשְׁקָ֖ל וּבַמְּשׂוּרָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not commit injustice in judgement; in [land] measure, in weight or in volume measure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall do no unrighteousness in judgment, in meteyard, in weight, or in measure.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not do wrong in judgment [measurement (the measurer being a "judge" and being accountable as a judge)] — in land-measure, in weight, and in liquid measure.


###### Leviticus 19:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:36)


מֹ֧אזְנֵי צֶ֣דֶק אַבְנֵי־צֶ֗דֶק אֵ֥יפַת צֶ֛דֶק וְהִ֥ין צֶ֖דֶק יִהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֑ם אֲנִי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־הוֹצֵ֥אתִי אֶתְכֶ֖ם מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Just scales and just weights a just dry measure (*ephah*) and a just liquid measure (*hin*) you shall have for yourselves, I am Adonoy, your God who has taken you out of the land of Egypt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Just balances, just weights, a just efa, and a just hin, shall you have: I am the Lord your God, who brought you out of the land of Miżrayim.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Just balances, just stones [(against which things are weighed)], a just ephah [dry measure] and a just hin [liquid measure] shall you have. I am the L-rd your G-d who took you out of the land of Egypt [to this end].


###### Leviticus 19:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/19:37)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֤ם אֶת־כׇּל־חֻקֹּתַי֙ וְאֶת־כׇּל־מִשְׁפָּטַ֔י וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֖ם אֹתָ֑ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You must keep all my statutes and all of my laws, and do [observe] them. I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore shall you observe all my statutes, and all my judgments, and do them: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall heed all of My statutes and all of My judgments, and you shall do them; I am the L-rd.



## Chapter 20

###### Leviticus 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:2)


וְאֶל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵל֮ תֹּאמַר֒ אִ֣ישׁ אִישׁ֩ מִבְּנֵ֨י יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל וּמִן־הַגֵּ֣ר**׀** הַגָּ֣ר בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִתֵּ֧ן מִזַּרְע֛וֹ לַמֹּ֖לֶךְ מ֣וֹת יוּמָ֑ת עַ֥ם הָאָ֖רֶץ יִרְגְּמֻ֥הוּ בָאָֽבֶן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And to Bnei Yisrael you shall say: that each and every man of Bnei Yisroel and from the proselytes that reside in Israel, who will give any of his children to *Molech*, [that] he shall surely be put to death; the people of the land shall stone him to death.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Again, thou shalt say to the children of Yisra᾽el, Whoever he be of the children of Yisra᾽el, or of the strangers that sojourn in Yisra᾽el, that gives any of his seed to Molekh; he shall surely be put to death: the people of the land shall stone him with stones.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to the children of Israel shall you say [(the punishments attaching to the exhortations)]: A man of the children of Israel and of the stranger who sojourns in Israel, who gives of his seed to the Molech shall be put to death [by beth-din, and if beth-din lack the power], the people of the land shall [assist them and] stone him with stones. [("the people of the land":) the people for whom the land was created, the people who are destined to inherit the land through these mitzvoth]


###### Leviticus 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:3)


וַאֲנִ֞י אֶתֵּ֤ן אֶת־פָּנַי֙ בָּאִ֣ישׁ הַה֔וּא וְהִכְרַתִּ֥י אֹת֖וֹ מִקֶּ֣רֶב עַמּ֑וֹ כִּ֤י מִזַּרְעוֹ֙ נָתַ֣ן לַמֹּ֔לֶךְ לְמַ֗עַן טַמֵּא֙ אֶת־מִקְדָּשִׁ֔י וּלְחַלֵּ֖ל אֶת־שֵׁ֥ם קׇדְשִֽׁי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I shall direct My wrath on that man and I shall cut him off from among his people, for he gave of his children to *Molech*, in order to defile My Sanctuary and profane My Holy Name.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will set my face against that man, and will cut him off from among his people; because he has given of his seed to Molekh, to defile my sanctuary, and to profane my holy name.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I shall direct My regard to that man, and I will cut him off, [(but not an entire congregation who serve the Molech)] from the midst of his people, for of his seed [("of") including his grandson, etc.] he has given to the Molech to defile My holy ones [the congregation of Israel] and to profane My holy name.


###### Leviticus 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:4)


וְאִ֡ם הַעְלֵ֣ם יַעְלִ֩ימֽוּ֩ עַ֨ם הָאָ֜רֶץ אֶת־עֵֽינֵיהֶם֙ מִן־הָאִ֣ישׁ הַה֔וּא בְּתִתּ֥וֹ מִזַּרְע֖וֹ לַמֹּ֑לֶךְ לְבִלְתִּ֖י הָמִ֥ית אֹתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the people of the land will hide their eyes from that person, when he gives of his children to *Molech*, and they do not put him to death.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the people of the land do at all hide their eyes from that man, when he gives of his seed to Molekh, and kill him not:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the people of the land avert, avert their eyes from that man [(If they avert their eyes in one thing, they are destined to avert them in many things; if the lesser sanhedrin averts its eyes, the Great Sanhedrin is destined to avert its eyes)] when he gives of his seed to the Molech, not to put him to death,


###### Leviticus 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:5)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֨י אֲנִ֧י אֶת־פָּנַ֛י בָּאִ֥ישׁ הַה֖וּא וּבְמִשְׁפַּחְתּ֑וֹ וְהִכְרַתִּ֨י אֹת֜וֹ וְאֵ֣ת**׀** כׇּל־הַזֹּנִ֣ים אַחֲרָ֗יו לִזְנ֛וֹת אַחֲרֵ֥י הַמֹּ֖לֶךְ מִקֶּ֥רֶב עַמָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I shall set My wrath against that man and his family; I shall cut him off, along with all those who go astray after him to go astray after *Molech*, [they will be cut off] from among their people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then I will set my face against that man, and against his family, and will cut him off, and all that go astray after him, going astray after Molekh, from among their people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then I shall direct My regard to that man and to his family, [who "cover up" for him], and I will cut him off, [him with kareth; his family, with afflictions], and all who go astray after him to stray after the Molech [(Subsumed in this are other idols served in the manner of Molech though that not be their normal mode of worship)] (I will cut off) from the midst of their people.


###### Leviticus 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:6)


וְהַנֶּ֗פֶשׁ אֲשֶׁ֨ר תִּפְנֶ֤ה אֶל־הָֽאֹבֹת֙ וְאֶל־הַיִּדְּעֹנִ֔ים לִזְנֹ֖ת אַחֲרֵיהֶ֑ם וְנָתַתִּ֤י אֶת־פָּנַי֙ בַּנֶּ֣פֶשׁ הַהִ֔וא וְהִכְרַתִּ֥י אֹת֖וֹ מִקֶּ֥רֶב עַמּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The person who turns to mediums and oracles to stray after them; I shall direct My wrath against that person and cut him off from among his people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the person that applies to mediums and to wizards, to go astray after them, I will even set my face against that person, and will cut him off from among his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the soul that turns to the ovoth and to the yidonim to stray after them — I shall set My face against that soul, and I shall cut him off from the midst of his people.


###### Leviticus 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:7)


וְהִ֨תְקַדִּשְׁתֶּ֔ם וִהְיִיתֶ֖ם קְדֹשִׁ֑ים כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall sanctify yourselves and be holy for I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Sanctify yourselves therefore, and be holy: for I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall sanctify yourselves [by separating from idolatry] and you shall be holy, for I am the L-rd your G-d.


###### Leviticus 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:8)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם֙ אֶת־חֻקֹּתַ֔י וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֖ם אֹתָ֑ם אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה מְקַדִּשְׁכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You must keep My statutes and do [observe] them. I am Adonoy who sanctifies you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall keep my statutes, and do them: I am the Lord who sanctifies you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall heed My statutes and do them; I am the L-rd who makes you holy.


###### Leviticus 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:9)


כִּֽי־אִ֣ישׁ אִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְקַלֵּ֧ל אֶת־אָבִ֛יו וְאֶת־אִמּ֖וֹ מ֣וֹת יוּמָ֑ת אָבִ֧יו וְאִמּ֛וֹ קִלֵּ֖ל דָּמָ֥יו בּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Any man that curses his father or his mother shall surely be put to death. [Since] he has cursed his father or mother, his blood is on him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For everyone that curses his father or his mother shall be surely put to death: he has cursed his father or his mother; his blood shall be upon him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For a man who curses his father and his mother shall be put to death. His father and his mother has he cursed [(He is liable even if he curses them after their death)]; his blood is in him [i.e., on his own head].


###### Leviticus 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:10)


וְאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִנְאַף֙ אֶת־אֵ֣שֶׁת אִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִנְאַ֖ף אֶת־אֵ֣שֶׁת רֵעֵ֑הוּ מֽוֹת־יוּמַ֥ת הַנֹּאֵ֖ף וְהַנֹּאָֽפֶת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who commits adultery with another mans wife who commits adultery with the wife of his fellow man—they shall surely be put to death—the adulterer and the adulteress.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the man that commits adultery with another man’s wife, that commits adultery with his neighbour’s wife, the adulterer and the adulteress shall surely be put to death.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man, [and not a minor], who lives with the wife of a man, [and not with the wife of a minor], who lives with the wife of his neighbor, [and not with the wife of a gentile] — the adulterer and the adulteress shall be put to death [(by strangulation)].


###### Leviticus 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:11)


וְאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִשְׁכַּב֙ אֶת־אֵ֣שֶׁת אָבִ֔יו עֶרְוַ֥ת אָבִ֖יו גִּלָּ֑ה מֽוֹת־יוּמְת֥וּ שְׁנֵיהֶ֖ם דְּמֵיהֶ֥ם בָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who will lie [conjugally] with his fathers wife has uncovered his fathers nakedness; they both shall surely be put to death, their blood is on them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the man that lies with his father’s wife has uncovered his father’s nakedness: both of them shall surely be put to death; their blood is upon them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man who lies with his father's wife, he has revealed the nakedness of his father. Both of them shall be put to death; their blood is in them.


###### Leviticus 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:12)


וְאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִשְׁכַּב֙ אֶת־כַּלָּת֔וֹ מ֥וֹת יוּמְת֖וּ שְׁנֵיהֶ֑ם תֶּ֥בֶל עָשׂ֖וּ דְּמֵיהֶ֥ם בָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who will lie [conjugally] with his daughter-in-law; they both shall surely be put to death they have committed an act of perversion, their blood is on them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man lie with his daughter in law, both of them shall surely be put to death: they have wrought unnatural sin; their blood shall be upon them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man lies with his daughter-in-law, both of them shall be put to death. They have wrought shamefully; their blood is in them.


###### Leviticus 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:13)


וְאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִשְׁכַּ֤ב אֶת־זָכָר֙ מִשְׁכְּבֵ֣י אִשָּׁ֔ה תּוֹעֵבָ֥ה עָשׂ֖וּ שְׁנֵיהֶ֑ם מ֥וֹת יוּמָ֖תוּ דְּמֵיהֶ֥ם בָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who will lie [conjugally] with [another] man, in the same manner as with a woman; they both have committed an abominable act, they shall surely be put to death, their blood is on them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If a man also lie with a man, as one lies with a woman, both of them have committed an abomination: they shall surely be put to death; their blood shall be upon them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man lies with a male the lyings of a woman ["as the reed is inserted into the tube"], an abomination has been wrought by both of them. They shall be put to death; their blood is in them.


###### Leviticus 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:14)


וְאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִקַּ֧ח אֶת־אִשָּׁ֛ה וְאֶת־אִמָּ֖הּ זִמָּ֣ה הִ֑וא בָּאֵ֞שׁ יִשְׂרְפ֤וּ אֹתוֹ֙ וְאֶתְהֶ֔ן וְלֹא־תִהְיֶ֥ה זִמָּ֖ה בְּתוֹכְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who marries a woman and her mother; it is lewdness. They shall be burned in fire, he and [one of] them, and there will no longer be such lewdness among you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man take a wife and her mother, it is wickedness: they shall be burnt with fire, both he and they; that there be no wickedness among you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man takes a woman [his mother-in-law] and her mother, it is lewdness. In fire shall they be burned, he and they, and there shall not be lewdness in your midst.


###### Leviticus 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:15)


וְאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִתֵּ֧ן שְׁכׇבְתּ֛וֹ בִּבְהֵמָ֖ה מ֣וֹת יוּמָ֑ת וְאֶת־הַבְּהֵמָ֖ה תַּהֲרֹֽגוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who lies [conjugally] with an animal shall surely be put to death, and you shall also kill the animal.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man lie with a beast, he shall surely be put to death: and you shall slay the beast.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man gives his lying to a beast, he shall be put to death, and the beast shall you kill [(because the man went astray through it)].


###### Leviticus 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:16)


וְאִשָּׁ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר תִּקְרַ֤ב אֶל־כׇּל־בְּהֵמָה֙ לְרִבְעָ֣הֿ אֹתָ֔הּ וְהָרַגְתָּ֥ אֶת־הָאִשָּׁ֖ה וְאֶת־הַבְּהֵמָ֑ה מ֥וֹת יוּמָ֖תוּ דְּמֵיהֶ֥ם בָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A woman who presents herself to an animal causing it to copulate with her; you shall kill the woman and the animal. They shall surely be killed, their blood is on them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a woman approach any beast, and lie down before it, thou shalt kill the woman, and the beast: they shall surely be put to death; their blood shall be upon them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a woman draws close to any beast so that it mount her, you shall kill the woman and the beast. They shall be put to death; their blood is in them.


###### Leviticus 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:17)


וְאִ֣ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־יִקַּ֣ח אֶת־אֲחֹת֡וֹ בַּת־אָבִ֣יו א֣וֹ בַת־אִ֠מּ֠וֹ וְרָאָ֨ה אֶת־עֶרְוָתָ֜הּ וְהִֽיא־תִרְאֶ֤ה אֶת־עֶרְוָתוֹ֙ חֶ֣סֶד ה֔וּא וְנִ֨כְרְת֔וּ לְעֵינֵ֖י בְּנֵ֣י עַמָּ֑ם עֶרְוַ֧ת אֲחֹת֛וֹ גִּלָּ֖ה עֲוֺנ֥וֹ יִשָּֽׂא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who marries his sister, either his fathers daughter or his mothers daughter, and he sees her nakedness and she sees his nakedness; it is a shameful perversion. They shall be cut off before their people he has uncovered the nakedness of his sister, he shall bear [the burden of] his iniquity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man shall take his sister, his father’s daughter, or his mother’s daughter, and see her nakedness, and she see his nakedness; it is a disgraceful deed; and they shall be cut off in the sight of their people: he has uncovered his sister’s nakedness; he shall bear his iniquity.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man takes his sister, the daughter of his father or the daughter of his mother, and sees her nakedness, and she sees his nakedness, it is shameful. And they shall be cut off before the eyes of the children of their people. The nakedness of his sister he has revealed; he shall bear his sin.


###### Leviticus 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:18)


וְ֠אִ֠ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־יִשְׁכַּ֨ב אֶת־אִשָּׁ֜ה דָּוָ֗ה וְגִלָּ֤ה אֶת־עֶרְוָתָהּ֙ אֶת־מְקֹרָ֣הּ הֶֽעֱרָ֔ה וְהִ֕וא גִּלְּתָ֖ה אֶת־מְק֣וֹר דָּמֶ֑יהָ וְנִכְרְת֥וּ שְׁנֵיהֶ֖ם מִקֶּ֥רֶב עַמָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who will lie [conjugally] with a menstruating woman and uncover her nakedness and uncover her source [womb] and she has uncovered the source of her blood; they shall both be cut off from among their people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man shall lie with a woman during her menstrual sickness, and shall uncover her nakedness; he has made naked her fountain, and she has uncovered the fountain of her blood: and both of them shall be cut off from among their people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man lies with a woman in her flow and reveals her nakedness — her fount has he bared [(peripheral intercourse is hereby connoted)], and she has revealed the fount of her blood, and both of them shall be cut off from the midst of their people.


###### Leviticus 20:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:19)


וְעֶרְוַ֨ת אֲח֧וֹת אִמְּךָ֛ וַאֲח֥וֹת אָבִ֖יךָ לֹ֣א תְגַלֵּ֑ה כִּ֧י אֶת־שְׁאֵר֛וֹ הֶעֱרָ֖ה עֲוֺנָ֥ם יִשָּֽׂאוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The nakedness of your mothers sister and of your fathers sister, you shall not uncover, for the nakedness of his close kin he will have bared they shall bear [the burden of] their iniquity.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt not uncover the nakedness of thy mother’s sister, nor of thy father’s sister: for he uncovers his near kin: they shall bear their iniquity.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the nakedness of your mother's sister [whether from her father or her mother] or your father's sister [whether from his father or his mother], you shall not reveal. Because he has bared his (close) flesh, they shall bear their sin.


###### Leviticus 20:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:20)


וְאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִשְׁכַּב֙ אֶת־דֹּ֣דָת֔וֹ עֶרְוַ֥ת דֹּד֖וֹ גִּלָּ֑ה חֶטְאָ֥ם יִשָּׂ֖אוּ עֲרִירִ֥ים יָמֻֽתוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who will lie [conjugally] with his aunt has uncovered the nakedness of his uncle they will bear [the burden of] their sin and they will die without children.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man shall lie with his uncle’s wife, he has uncovered his uncle’s nakedness: they shall bear their sin; they shall die childless.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man who lies with the wife of his uncle [his father's brother from his father], the nakedness of his uncle has he revealed. They shall bear their sin; childless shall they die. [If they have children, they will die in their lifetimes. ("Cutting off," then, (kareth), in 18:29, refers to going childless)].


###### Leviticus 20:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:21)


וְאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִקַּ֛ח אֶת־אֵ֥שֶׁת אָחִ֖יו נִדָּ֣ה הִ֑וא עֶרְוַ֥ת אָחִ֛יו גִּלָּ֖ה עֲרִירִ֥ים יִהְיֽוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who will marry his brothers wife who must be [conjugally] avoided [by him]; he has uncovered his brothers nakedness, they shall die without children.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man shall take his brother’s wife, it is an unclean thing: he has uncovered his brother’s nakedness; they shall be childless.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man takes the wife of his brother, it [this cohabitation] is niddah ["rejected," "repulsive"], the nakedness of his brother has he revealed; childless shall they be [(i.e., remain) if they have no children.]


###### Leviticus 20:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:22)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֤ם אֶת־כׇּל־חֻקֹּתַי֙ וְאֶת־כׇּל־מִשְׁפָּטַ֔י וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֖ם אֹתָ֑ם וְלֹא־תָקִ֤יא אֶתְכֶם֙ הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֲנִ֜י מֵבִ֥יא אֶתְכֶ֛ם שָׁ֖מָּה לָשֶׁ֥בֶת בָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall keep all of My statutes and all of My laws and do (observe) them [so that] you will not be expelled by the land to which I am bringing you there to reside therein.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall therefore keep all my statutes, and all my judgments, and do them: that the land, into which I bring you to dwell, vomit you not out.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall heed all of My statutes and all of My judgments, and you shall do them, so that the land not vomit you out, whither I bring you to dwell in it.


###### Leviticus 20:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:23)


וְלֹ֤א תֵֽלְכוּ֙ בְּחֻקֹּ֣ת הַגּ֔וֹי אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִ֥י מְשַׁלֵּ֖חַ מִפְּנֵיכֶ֑ם כִּ֤י אֶת־כׇּל־אֵ֙לֶּה֙ עָשׂ֔וּ וָאָקֻ֖ץ בָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not follow the customs of the nation which I am expelling before you. For they have done all these above mentioned sins and I became disgusted with them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall not walk in the practices of the nation, which I cast out before you: for they committed all these things, and therefore I abhorred them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall not walk in the statutes of the nation which I am sending away before you. For all of these (abominations) they did, and I despised them.


###### Leviticus 20:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:24)


וָאֹמַ֣ר לָכֶ֗ם אַתֶּם֮ תִּֽירְשׁ֣וּ אֶת־אַדְמָתָם֒ וַאֲנִ֞י אֶתְּנֶ֤נָּה לָכֶם֙ לָרֶ֣שֶׁת אֹתָ֔הּ אֶ֛רֶץ זָבַ֥ת חָלָ֖ב וּדְבָ֑שׁ אֲנִי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־הִבְדַּ֥לְתִּי אֶתְכֶ֖ם מִן־הָֽעַמִּֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And I have said to you [that] you shall inherit their land, and I shall give it to you that you may inherit it, a land flowing with milk and honey, I am Adonoy, your God who separated you from the [other] peoples.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But I have said to you, You shall inherit their land, and I will give it to you to possess it, a land that flows with milk and honey: I am the Lord your God, who have separated you from the peoples.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to you: You shall inherit their land, and I will give it to you to inherit, a land flowing with milk and honey. I am the L-rd your G-d who separated you from the peoples.


###### Leviticus 20:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:25)


וְהִבְדַּלְתֶּ֞ם בֵּֽין־הַבְּהֵמָ֤ה הַטְּהֹרָה֙ לַטְּמֵאָ֔ה וּבֵין־הָע֥וֹף הַטָּמֵ֖א לַטָּהֹ֑ר וְלֹֽא־תְשַׁקְּצ֨וּ אֶת־נַפְשֹֽׁתֵיכֶ֜ם בַּבְּהֵמָ֣ה וּבָע֗וֹף וּבְכֹל֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּרְמֹ֣שׂ הָֽאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־הִבְדַּ֥לְתִּי לָכֶ֖ם לְטַמֵּֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall distinguish between a [ritually] clean animal and a unclean one, between the unclean fowl and the clean one. Do not make yourselves disgusting through the animals, fowl or through any creature that creeps on the ground which I have distinguished for you as being unclean.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall therefore distinguish between clean beasts and unclean, and between unclean birds and clean: and you shall not make your souls abominable by beast, or by bird, or by any manner of living thing that creeps on the ground, which I have separated from you as unclean.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall separate between the clean beast and the unclean [i.e., between that which is clean for you and that which is unclean, between the severing of the majority of the shechitah sign (in which instance the animal is kasher) and the severing of half (in which instance it is treifah)], and between the unclean fowl and the clean; and you shall not make yourselves despicable through the beast or the fowl, or through anything with which the ground swarms, which I have separated for you as unclean [i.e., as forbidden].


###### Leviticus 20:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:26)


וִהְיִ֤יתֶם לִי֙ קְדֹשִׁ֔ים כִּ֥י קָד֖וֹשׁ אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וָאַבְדִּ֥ל אֶתְכֶ֛ם מִן־הָֽעַמִּ֖ים לִהְי֥וֹת לִֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall be holy to Me, for I, Adonoy, am holy and I have distinguished you from the [other] peoples to be Mine [and to serve Me].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall be holy to me: for I the Lord am holy, and have separated you from the peoples, that you should be mine.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall be holy unto Me, for I, the L-rd, am holy; and I have set you apart from the peoples to be Mine [If you are separate from them, you are Mine; if not, you are "Nevuchadnezzar's."]


###### Leviticus 20:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/20:27)


וְאִ֣ישׁ אֽוֹ־אִשָּׁ֗ה כִּי־יִהְיֶ֨ה בָהֶ֥ם א֛וֹב א֥וֹ יִדְּעֹנִ֖י מ֣וֹת יוּמָ֑תוּ בָּאֶ֛בֶן יִרְגְּמ֥וּ אֹתָ֖ם דְּמֵיהֶ֥ם בָּֽם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If among the men or women there will be a medium or an oracle they shall surely be put to death. You shall stone them to death, their blood is on them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A man also or woman that is a medium or a wizard, shall surely be put to death: they shall stone them with stones: their blood shall be upon them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man or a woman if there be in them an ov or yidoni, they shall be put to death. With stones shall they stone them; their blood is in them. [Where there are witnesses and warning — stoning; wittingness, without warning — kareth; unwittingness — a sin-offering. The same applies to all transgressions punishable by death where "kareth" is stated.]



## Chapter 21

###### Leviticus 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:1)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֔ה אֱמֹ֥ר אֶל־הַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים בְּנֵ֣י אַהֲרֹ֑ן וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵהֶ֔ם לְנֶ֥פֶשׁ לֹֽא־יִטַּמָּ֖א בְּעַמָּֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy said to Moshe; speak, to the *kohanim*, the sons of Aharon, and you shall say to them, let him not defile himself with the dead among his people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to Moshe, Speak to the priests the sons of Aharon, and say to them, There shall none be defiled for the dead among his people:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to Moses: Speak to the Cohanim, [(excluding chalalim, those unfit to minister)], the sons of Aaron, [(even those with blemishes) (and not the daughters of Aaron)], and say to them [(adults being exhorted in respect to minors)]: For a dead body he (a Cohein) shall not become unclean among his people [i.e., when the dead one is "among his people," excluding a meth mitzvah (one who has no one else to bury him)].


###### Leviticus 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:2)


כִּ֚י אִם־לִשְׁאֵר֔וֹ הַקָּרֹ֖ב אֵלָ֑יו לְאִמּ֣וֹ וּלְאָבִ֔יו וְלִבְנ֥וֹ וּלְבִתּ֖וֹ וּלְאָחִֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Except to his kin to whom he is closely related, for his mother and his father, for his son, his daughter and for his brother.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but for his kin, that is near to him, for his mother, and for his father, and for his son and for his daughter, and for his brother,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only for his flesh [his wife] that is near to him, his mother and his father, his son and his daughter and his brother.


###### Leviticus 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:3)


וְלַאֲחֹת֤וֹ הַבְּתוּלָה֙ הַקְּרוֹבָ֣ה אֵלָ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־הָיְתָ֖ה לְאִ֑ישׁ לָ֖הּ יִטַּמָּֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For his virgin sister, to whom he is closely related, who has not yet been married, for her, he may be defiled.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and for his sister a virgin, that is near to him, and who has had no husband; for her may he be defiled.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for his sister, the virgin, who is near to him [(including one who is betrothed)], who was not wed to a man [for cohabitation] — for her shall he make himself unclean.


###### Leviticus 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:4)


לֹ֥א יִטַּמָּ֖א בַּ֣עַל בְּעַמָּ֑יו לְהֵ֖חַלּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A husband may not defile himself among his people [for a wife through whom] he becomes profaned.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he shall not defile himself, being a chief man among his people, to profane himself.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A husband (who is a Cohein) shall not defile himself [for his wife when she is] in [the midst of] his people [i.e., when there are others to bury her (in which instance she is not a meth mitzvah). (And the wife spoken of here is one)] through whom he is profaned [(made a chalal) by living with her].


###### Leviticus 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:5)


לֹֽא־[יִקְרְח֤וּ] (יקרחה) קׇרְחָה֙ בְּרֹאשָׁ֔ם וּפְאַ֥ת זְקָנָ֖ם לֹ֣א יְגַלֵּ֑חוּ וּבִ֨בְשָׂרָ֔ם לֹ֥א יִשְׂרְט֖וּ שָׂרָֽטֶת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They shall not make baldness on their heads and the corners of their beards they shall not shave, and they shall not make gashes in their flesh.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They shall not make baldness on their head, neither shall they shave off the corner of their beard, nor make any cuttings in their flesh.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They shall not make a baldness upon their head [for one who dies], and the corner of their beard they shall not shave off [with a razor], and in their flesh they shall not make any cutting [(There is liability for each cutting.)]


###### Leviticus 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:6)


קְדֹשִׁ֤ים יִהְיוּ֙ לֵאלֹ֣הֵיהֶ֔ם וְלֹ֣א יְחַלְּל֔וּ שֵׁ֖ם אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֑ם כִּי֩ אֶת־אִשֵּׁ֨י יְהֹוָ֜ה לֶ֧חֶם אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֛ם הֵ֥ם מַקְרִיבִ֖ם וְהָ֥יוּ קֹֽדֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They shall be holy to their God and they shall not profane the Name of their God, for [it is] the fire-offerings of Adonoy, the bread of their God [that] they bring [offer], and they must [therefore] be holy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They shall be holy to their God, and not profane the name of their God: for the offerings of the Lord made by fire, the bread of their God, they do offer: therefore they shall be holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Holy shall they be, [perforce. Beth-din compels them to remain holy (i.e., not to make themselves unclean)] to their G-d, and they shall not profane the name of their G-d; for the fire-offerings of the L-rd, the bread of their G-d, they offer up — and they shall be holy.


###### Leviticus 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:7)


אִשָּׁ֨ה זֹנָ֤ה וַחֲלָלָה֙ לֹ֣א יִקָּ֔חוּ וְאִשָּׁ֛ה גְּרוּשָׁ֥ה מֵאִישָׁ֖הּ לֹ֣א יִקָּ֑חוּ כִּֽי־קָדֹ֥שׁ ה֖וּא לֵאלֹהָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A harlot or a profaned woman they shall not marry. A woman divorced from her husband they shall not marry, for he is holy to his God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They shall not take a wife that is a harlot, or profaned; neither shall they take a woman put away from her husband: for he is holy to his God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A woman who is a harlot or profaned (chalalah) [a woman born of those unfit for the priesthood, e.g., the daughter of a widow by a high-priest, or of a divorced woman by an ordinary priest, or a woman who cohabited with one unfit for the priesthood] they shall not take; for he is holy to his G-d.


###### Leviticus 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:8)


וְקִ֨דַּשְׁתּ֔וֹ כִּֽי־אֶת־לֶ֥חֶם אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ ה֣וּא מַקְרִ֑יב קָדֹשׁ֙ יִֽהְיֶה־לָּ֔ךְ כִּ֣י קָד֔וֹשׁ אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה מְקַדִּשְׁכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall make him holy for he offers the bread of your God; he shall be holy unto you for I, Adonoy, am holy, Who makes you holy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt sanctify him therefore; for he offers the bread of thy God: he shall be holy to thee: for I the Lord, who sanctify you, am holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall make him holy, [perforce. (If he refuses to divorce her, lash him and afflict him until he does so)], for he offers up the bread of your G-d; holy shall he be unto you [(Relate to him with holiness, having him initiate all things and giving him precedence in blessing at the meal)], for holy am I the L-rd, who sanctifies you.


###### Leviticus 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:9)


וּבַת֙ אִ֣ישׁ כֹּהֵ֔ן כִּ֥י תֵחֵ֖ל לִזְנ֑וֹת אֶת־אָבִ֙יהָ֙ הִ֣יא מְחַלֶּ֔לֶת בָּאֵ֖שׁ תִּשָּׂרֵֽף׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The daughter of a *kohein*, who profanes herself by fornication—she profanes her father— she shall be burned with fire.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the daughter of any priest, if she profane herself by playing the harlot, she profanes her father: she shall be burnt with fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the [betrothed or married] daughter of a man who is a Cohein, if she profane herself through fornication, she profanes her father [(They say about him: "Cursed be he who begot such as she! Cursed be he who raised such as she!")]; in fire shall she be burned.


###### Leviticus 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:10)


וְהַכֹּהֵן֩ הַגָּד֨וֹל מֵאֶחָ֜יו אֲֽשֶׁר־יוּצַ֥ק עַל־רֹאשׁ֣וֹ**׀** שֶׁ֤מֶן הַמִּשְׁחָה֙ וּמִלֵּ֣א אֶת־יָד֔וֹ לִלְבֹּ֖שׁ אֶת־הַבְּגָדִ֑ים אֶת־רֹאשׁוֹ֙ לֹ֣א יִפְרָ֔ע וּבְגָדָ֖יו לֹ֥א יִפְרֹֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* who is greatest among his brethren upon whose head was poured the anointing oil; whose hands were consecrated to put on the special garments; shall not let his hair grow wild and he shall not tear his garments.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that is the high priest among his brethren, upon whose head the anointing oil was poured, and that is consecrated to put on the garments, shall not suffer the hair of his head to grow long, nor rend his clothes;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein who is greater than his brothers (i.e., the high-priest), upon whose head the oil of anointment has been poured, and who has been invested to wear the garments — his hair he shall not grow long [for more than thirty days (in mourning)] and his clothes he shall not rend.


###### Leviticus 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:11)


וְעַ֛ל כׇּל־נַפְשֹׁ֥ת מֵ֖ת לֹ֣א יָבֹ֑א לְאָבִ֥יו וּלְאִמּ֖וֹ לֹ֥א יִטַּמָּֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And adjacent to any dead body he shall not enter. For his father and mother he shall not become [ritually] impure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
neither shall he go in to any dead body, nor defile himself for his father, or for his mother;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to any (kol) dead body [(kol) including a revi'ith of blood of a dead person, which causes uncleanliness in a tent)] he shall not come [(into the tent of the dead)]. For his father and for his mother he shall not become unclean, [but he may become unclean for a meth mitzvah].


###### Leviticus 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:12)


וּמִן־הַמִּקְדָּשׁ֙ לֹ֣א יֵצֵ֔א וְלֹ֣א יְחַלֵּ֔ל אֵ֖ת מִקְדַּ֣שׁ אֱלֹהָ֑יו כִּ֡י נֵ֠זֶר שֶׁ֣מֶן מִשְׁחַ֧ת אֱלֹהָ֛יו עָלָ֖יו אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall not depart from the Sanctuary and he shall not profane his Gods Sanctuary, for the crown of his Gods anointing oil is upon him, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
neither shall he go out of the sanctuary, nor profane the sanctuary of his God; for the crown of the anointing oil of his God is upon him: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from the sanctuary he shall not go out [He does not follow the litter, and he sacrifices even when he is an onein (before the burial)], and he does not profane the sanctuary of his G-d [thereby (But an ordinary Cohein who serves when he is an onein does)], for the crown of the anointing oil of his G-d is upon him; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:13)


וְה֕וּא אִשָּׁ֥ה בִבְתוּלֶ֖יהָ יִקָּֽח׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall marry [only] a virgin.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall take a wife in her virginity.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he, a woman in her virginity he shall take.


###### Leviticus 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:14)


אַלְמָנָ֤ה וּגְרוּשָׁה֙ וַחֲלָלָ֣ה זֹנָ֔ה אֶת־אֵ֖לֶּה לֹ֣א יִקָּ֑ח כִּ֛י אִם־בְּתוּלָ֥ה מֵעַמָּ֖יו יִקַּ֥ח אִשָּֽׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A widow, divorcee, profaned woman or harlot; he may not marry [any] of these. But only a virgin from his own people may he marry.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A widow, or a divorced woman, or a profaned, or a harlot, these shall he not take: but he shall take a virgin of his own people to wife.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A widow, and a divorced woman, and a chalalah [a woman born of those unfit for the priesthood], (and) a harlot — these he shall not take; but only a virgin from his people shall he take as a wife.


###### Leviticus 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:15)


וְלֹֽא־יְחַלֵּ֥ל זַרְע֖וֹ בְּעַמָּ֑יו כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה מְקַדְּשֽׁוֹ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall not profane his offspring among his people, for I am Adonoy, who makes him holy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither shall he profane his seed among his people: for I the Lord do sanctify him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall not profane his seed among his people [(If he does take one of them, his child is a chalal)]; for I am the L-rd who makes him holy.


###### Leviticus 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:16)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:17)


דַּבֵּ֥ר אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֖ן לֵאמֹ֑ר אִ֣ישׁ מִֽזַּרְעֲךָ֞ לְדֹרֹתָ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִהְיֶ֥ה בוֹ֙ מ֔וּם לֹ֣א יִקְרַ֔ב לְהַקְרִ֖יב לֶ֥חֶם אֱלֹהָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Aharon saying; Anyone of your offspring, through [all] their generations who will have a blemish, shall not approach to offer the bread of his God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
speak to Aharon, saying, Whoever he be of thy seed in their generations that has any blemish, let him not approach to offer the bread of his God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to Aaron, saying: A man of your seed, through their generations, who will have in him a blemish, shall not come near to offer up the bread [i.e., "the food"] of his G-d.


###### Leviticus 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:18)


כִּ֥י כׇל־אִ֛ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־בּ֥וֹ מ֖וּם לֹ֣א יִקְרָ֑ב אִ֤ישׁ עִוֵּר֙ א֣וֹ פִסֵּ֔חַ א֥וֹ חָרֻ֖ם א֥וֹ שָׂרֽוּעַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For any man who has a blemish shall not approach; a blind man or a lame man, or a man with a sunken nose, or a man who has disproportionate limbs.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For whatever man he be that has a blemish, he shall not approach: a blind man, or a lame, or he that has a flat nose, or anything superfluous,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For any man who has in him a blemish, [by right] should not come near [to do so]: a man who is blind, or lame, or sunken-nosed, or asymmetrical [of limb or organ].


###### Leviticus 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:19)


א֣וֹ אִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־יִהְיֶ֥ה ב֖וֹ שֶׁ֣בֶר רָ֑גֶל א֖וֹ שֶׁ֥בֶר יָֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
or a man who has a broken (crippled) foot, or a broken (crippled) hand,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or a man that is brokenfooted, or brokenhanded,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or a man who is broken-footed or broken-handed.


###### Leviticus 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:20)


אֽוֹ־גִבֵּ֣ן אוֹ־דַ֔ק א֖וֹ תְּבַלֻּ֣ל בְּעֵינ֑וֹ א֤וֹ גָרָב֙ א֣וֹ יַלֶּ֔פֶת א֖וֹ מְר֥וֹחַ אָֽשֶׁךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
or one with overhanging eyebrows, or with a cataract, or a disarrangement [in the eye], or one with scurf or scabs, or one with crushed testicles.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or crookbacked, or a dwarf, or that has a blemish in his eye, or be scurvy, or scabbed, or whose stones are crushed.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or gibein [having long, flattened eye-brows] or dak [having a membrane over the eyes] or tevalul [having a disorientation of the black-white arrangement] of his eye, or [having] garav or yalefeth [types of skin conditions] or meroach ashech [having crushed testicles].


###### Leviticus 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:21)


כׇּל־אִ֞ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־בּ֣וֹ מ֗וּם מִזֶּ֙רַע֙ אַהֲרֹ֣ן הַכֹּהֵ֔ן לֹ֣א יִגַּ֔שׁ לְהַקְרִ֖יב אֶת־אִשֵּׁ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה מ֣וּם בּ֔וֹ אֵ֚ת לֶ֣חֶם אֱלֹהָ֔יו לֹ֥א יִגַּ֖שׁ לְהַקְרִֽיב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Any man who has a blemish, who is a descendant of Aharon the *kohein*, shall not approach to offer the fire-offerings of Adonoy. He has a blemish; the bread of his God he may not approach to offer [as an offering].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
No man that has a blemish of the seed of Aharon the priest shall come near to offer the offerings of the Lord made by fire: he has a blemish; he shall not come near to offer the bread of his God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every man who has a blemish [(Other blemishes are hereby included)] in him [(While it is in him he is pasul; if it leaves, he is kasher)] of the seed of Aaron the Cohein, shall not come near to offer up the fire-offerings of the L-rd. There is a blemish in him; the bread of his G-d he shall not come near to offer up.


###### Leviticus 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:22)


לֶ֣חֶם אֱלֹהָ֔יו מִקׇּדְשֵׁ֖י הַקֳּדָשִׁ֑ים וּמִן־הַקֳּדָשִׁ֖ים יֹאכֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The bread of his God which is of the most holy, and from the holy [offerings] he may eat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall eat the bread of his God, both of the most holy, and of the holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The bread of his G-d, of the holy of holies [(higher-order sanctities)], and of the holies [(lower-order sanctities)], he may eat.


###### Leviticus 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:23)


אַ֣ךְ אֶל־הַפָּרֹ֜כֶת לֹ֣א יָבֹ֗א וְאֶל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֛חַ לֹ֥א יִגַּ֖שׁ כִּֽי־מ֣וּם בּ֑וֹ וְלֹ֤א יְחַלֵּל֙ אֶת־מִקְדָּשַׁ֔י כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה מְקַדְּשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, he may not enter beyond the Curtain and he shall not approach the Altar for he has a blemish and he shall not profane My holy things, for I am Adonoy, who sanctifies them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only he shall not go in unto the veil, nor come near to the altar, because he has a blemish; that he profane not my holy places: for I the Lord do sanctify them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But to the parocheth [to sprinkle the seven sprinklings] and to the [outer] altar he shall not come near, for a blemish is in him; and he shall not profane My holy places [(If he does serve, the service is profaned and is pasul)]; for I am the L-rd who sanctifies them.


###### Leviticus 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/21:24)


וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר מֹשֶׁ֔ה אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֖ן וְאֶל־בָּנָ֑יו וְאֶֽל־כׇּל־בְּנֵ֖י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe spoke [these words] to Aharon, and to his sons and to all of Bnei Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe spoke to Aharon, and to his sons, and to all the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses spoke [(He related) this mitzvah] to Aaron and his sons and to all the children of Israel [(exhorting beth-din vis-à-vis the Cohanim)].



## Chapter 22

###### Leviticus 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:2)


דַּבֵּ֨ר אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֜ן וְאֶל־בָּנָ֗יו וְיִנָּֽזְרוּ֙ מִקׇּדְשֵׁ֣י בְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְלֹ֥א יְחַלְּל֖וּ אֶת־שֵׁ֣ם קׇדְשִׁ֑י אֲשֶׁ֨ר הֵ֧ם מַקְדִּשִׁ֛ים לִ֖י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Aharon and to his sons and let them keep away from the holy offerings of Bnei Yisroel and they shall not profane My holy name which they sanctify for Me, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to Aharon and to his sons, that they separate themselves from the holy things of the children of Yisra᾽el which they hallow to me, and they profane not my holy name, I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to Aaron and to his sons that they separate themselves [in the days of their uncleanliness] from the holy things of the children of Israel which they [(including the Cohanim themselves)] make holy unto Me, that they not profane My holy name; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 22:3

אֱמֹ֣ר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם לְדֹרֹ֨תֵיכֶ֜ם כׇּל־אִ֣ישׁ**׀** אֲשֶׁר־יִקְרַ֣ב מִכׇּל־זַרְעֲכֶ֗ם אֶל־הַקֳּדָשִׁים֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יַקְדִּ֤ישׁוּ בְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה וְטֻמְאָת֖וֹ עָלָ֑יו וְנִכְרְתָ֞ה הַנֶּ֧פֶשׁ הַהִ֛וא מִלְּפָנַ֖י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Say to them; throughout your generations; any person of your offspring who will draw near to the holy things which Bnei Yisroel will sanctify to Adonoy [while] his impurity is on him that soul shall be cut off from My Presence, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Say to them, Whoever he be of all your seed among your generations, that approaches the holy things, which the children of Yisra᾽el hallow to the Lord, having his uncleanness upon him, that soul shall be cut off from my presence: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Say to them: Throughout your generations, every man who draws near of all of your seed to [eat] the holy things that the children of Israel make holy unto the L-rd, with his uncleanliness upon him, that soul will be cut off from before Me; I am the L-rd.

אִ֣ישׁ אִ֞ישׁ מִזֶּ֣רַע אַהֲרֹ֗ן וְה֤וּא צָר֙וּעַ֙ א֣וֹ זָ֔ב בַּקֳּדָשִׁים֙ לֹ֣א יֹאכַ֔ל עַ֖ד אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִטְהָ֑ר וְהַנֹּגֵ֙עַ֙ בְּכׇל־טְמֵא־נֶ֔פֶשׁ א֣וֹ אִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־תֵּצֵ֥א מִמֶּ֖נּוּ שִׁכְבַת־זָֽרַע׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Any man of Aharons offspring; who has *tzoraas* or an impure flow, may not eat of the sacred things until he has purified himself. [The same is true] of anyone who touches anything defiled by the dead, or anyone who has had a seminal emission,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What man soever of the seed of Aharon that is stricken with żara῾at, or has a running issue; he shall not eat of the holy things, until he be clean. And whoever touches anything that is unclean by the dead, or a man whose semen goes from him;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A man of the seed of Aaron, if he is a leper or a zav, of the holy things he shall not eat until he is clean. And one who touches anyone who is unclean through the dead; or a man from whom semen issues,

or anyone who touches any creeping creature that causes impurity to him, or any person which causes impurity to him, [with] whatever impurity he has.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The soul who touches it [one of the above] shall be unclean until the evening; and he shall not eat of the holy things until he bathes his flesh in water.

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When the sun sets, he is pure, and afterwards, he may eat of the holy things, for it is his bread.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
An animal which has died on its own, or an animal that was fatally maimed, he must not eat to defile himself with it, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
That which dies of itself, or is torn with beasts, he shall not eat to defile himself with it: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Neveilah or treifah he shall not eat [see 17:15] to defile himself therewith [(If he does eat it, he is forbidden to eat kodshim)]; I am the L-rd.

וְשָׁמְר֣וּ אֶת־מִשְׁמַרְתִּ֗י וְלֹֽא־יִשְׂא֤וּ עָלָיו֙ חֵ֔טְא וּמֵ֥תוּ ב֖וֹ כִּ֣י יְחַלְּלֻ֑הוּ אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה מְקַדְּשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They shall keep my charge lest they bear [the burden] of sin and die because of it for having profaned [it]. I am Adonoy Who makes them holy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They shall therefore keep my charge, lest they bear sin for it, and die therefore, if they profane it: I the Lord do sanctify them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Leviticus 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:10)


וְכׇל־זָ֖ר לֹא־יֹ֣אכַל קֹ֑דֶשׁ תּוֹשַׁ֥ב כֹּהֵ֛ן וְשָׂכִ֖יר לֹא־יֹ֥אכַל קֹֽדֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
No stranger (non-*kohein*) may eat holy things. One indentured to a *kohein* and one hired [by a *kohein*] may not eat sacred-offerings.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
No stranger shall eat of the holy thing: a tenant of a priest, or a hired servant, shall not eat of the holy thing.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every non-priest (zar) shall not eat the holy thing [terumah]. A Cohein's toshav [a servant whose ear is bored, who serves until the Jubilee year] and (his) sachir [a hired servant, who leaves after six years] shall not eat the holy thing, [the Cohein not owning his body].


###### Leviticus 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:11)


וְכֹהֵ֗ן כִּֽי־יִקְנֶ֥ה נֶ֙פֶשׁ֙ קִנְיַ֣ן כַּסְפּ֔וֹ ה֖וּא יֹ֣אכַל בּ֑וֹ וִילִ֣יד בֵּית֔וֹ הֵ֖ם יֹאכְל֥וּ בְלַחְמֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a *kohein* shall purchase a person [as a slave, that person is] his monetary possession. He may eat of it, and the [slave] born in his house, they may eat of his bread.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if a priest buy any person with money, he may eat of it, and he that is born in his house: they shall eat of his bread.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Leviticus 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:12)

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When a koheins daughter marries a non-*kohein*, the sacred *terumah* portions may not be eaten by her.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the daughter of a Cohein, if she be wed to a man who is a zar [i.e., a Levite or an Israelite], she, of the terumah of the holy things shall not eat.

וּבַת־כֹּהֵן֩ כִּ֨י תִהְיֶ֜ה אַלְמָנָ֣ה וּגְרוּשָׁ֗ה וְזֶ֘רַע֮ אֵ֣ין לָהּ֒ וְשָׁבָ֞ה אֶל־בֵּ֤ית אָבִ֙יהָ֙ כִּנְעוּרֶ֔יהָ מִלֶּ֥חֶם אָבִ֖יהָ תֹּאכֵ֑ל וְכׇל־זָ֖ר לֹא־יֹ֥אכַל בּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a koheins daughter is either widowed or divorced, and she has no children, and she returns to her fathers home as in her youth, she may eat of her fathers bread. No non-*kohein* may eat of it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if a priest’s daughter be a widow, or divorced, and have no child, and has returned to her father’s house, as in her youth, she shall eat of her father’s bread: but no stranger shall eat of it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the daughter of a Cohein, if she be widowed or divorced [from the zar], and she have no seed [from him], then she may return to the house of her father as in her maidenhood (and) from the bread of her father may she eat. And no zar may eat of it, [but a Cohein who is an onein (a mourner) may].

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

They burden them[selves] with the sin of their guilt when they eat their holy things, for I am Adonoy, Who makes them holy.

and so cause them to bear the iniquity of trespass, when they eat their holy things: for I the Lord do sanctify them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃

Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:
