דַּבֵּ֨ר אֶֽל־אַהֲרֹ֜ן וְאֶל־בָּנָ֗יו וְאֶל֙ כׇּל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְאָמַרְתָּ֖ אֲלֵהֶ֑ם אִ֣ישׁ אִישׁ֩ מִבֵּ֨ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל וּמִן־הַגֵּ֣ר בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר יַקְרִ֤יב קׇרְבָּנוֹ֙ לְכׇל־נִדְרֵיהֶם֙ וּלְכׇל־נִדְבוֹתָ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־יַקְרִ֥יבוּ לַיהֹוָ֖ה לְעֹלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Aharon and to his sons and to all of Bnei Yisroel and say to them; any man of the House of Israel, and from the proselytes of Israel who will bring his offering for any of their vows or for any of their free-will-offerings that they will bring to Adonoy, for a burnt-offering.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to Aharon, and to his sons, and to all the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, Whatever he be of the house of Yisra᾽el, or of the strangers in Yisra᾽el, that will offer their sacrifice for all their vows, and for all their freewill offerings, which they will offer to the Lord for a burnt offering;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to Aaron and to his sons and to all the children of Israel, and say to them: A man of the house of Israel and of the stranger in Israel, who brings his offering, of all of their vows ["I take it upon myself to bring, etc."] and of all of their gift offerings ["This (animal) shall be, etc."], which they will present to the L-rd as a burnt-offering —


###### Leviticus 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:19)


לִֽרְצֹנְכֶ֑ם תָּמִ֣ים זָכָ֔ר בַּבָּקָ֕ר בַּכְּשָׂבִ֖ים וּבָֽעִזִּֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
To gain acceptance [it must be] an unblemished male of cattle, sheep or goats.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
you shall offer, that you may be accepted, a male without blemish, of the oxen, of the sheep, or of the goats.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that you find favor with it [(in My eyes) shall you bring it. And with what will you find favor?] A male without blemish in the cattle, in the sheep, and in the goats [(but a bird burnt-offering need not be male and unblemished. It becomes pasul only if it lacks a limb.)]


###### Leviticus 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:20)


כֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־בּ֥וֹ מ֖וּם לֹ֣א תַקְרִ֑יבוּ כִּי־לֹ֥א לְרָצ֖וֹן יִהְיֶ֥ה לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Anything that has a blemish on it you shall not bring, for it will not be accepted favorably for you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But whatever has a blemish, that shall you not offer: for it shall not be acceptable for you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Whatever has a blemish in it you shall not present, for it will not be acceptable for you.


###### Leviticus 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:21)


וְאִ֗ישׁ כִּֽי־יַקְרִ֤יב זֶֽבַח־שְׁלָמִים֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה לְפַלֵּא־נֶ֙דֶר֙ א֣וֹ לִנְדָבָ֔ה בַּבָּקָ֖ר א֣וֹ בַצֹּ֑אן תָּמִ֤ים יִֽהְיֶה֙ לְרָצ֔וֹן כׇּל־מ֖וּם לֹ֥א יִהְיֶה־בּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a man will bring a peace-offering to Adonoy, to fulfill an articulated vow or free-will-offering of cattle or of sheep, it shall be unblemished, that it be favorably accepted. No blemish shall be in it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whoever offers a sacrifice of peace offerings to the Lord to accomplish his vow, or a freewill offering in oxen or sheep, it shall be perfect to be accepted; no blemish shall be in it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man present a sacrifice of peace-offerings to the L-rd for an expressed vow or as a gift-offering, of the cattle or of the flock, perfect shall it be for acceptance; no blemish shall be in it.


###### Leviticus 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:22)


עַוֶּ֩רֶת֩ א֨וֹ שָׁב֜וּר אוֹ־חָר֣וּץ אֽוֹ־יַבֶּ֗לֶת א֤וֹ גָרָב֙ א֣וֹ יַלֶּ֔פֶת לֹא־תַקְרִ֥יבוּ אֵ֖לֶּה לַיהֹוָ֑ה וְאִשֶּׁ֗ה לֹא־תִתְּנ֥וּ מֵהֶ֛ם עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A blind, broken-limbed, cracked or wart[ed animal]; or [an animal] with scurf or scabs you shall not bring these to Adonoy; and as a fire-offering you shall not place of them on the altar of Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Blind, or broken, or maimed, or having a growth, or scurvy, or scabbed, you shall not offer these to the Lord, nor make an offering by fire of them upon the altar to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[It shall not be] blind or broken or charutz [having an eyelid or a lip that was split or injured], or having a wart or [having] garav or yalefeth [types of skin conditions]. You shall not present these to the L-rd. [("You shall not present") — three times (here, 24, 25): to exhort against their being dedicated, slaughtered, and their blood being sprinkled)] And a fire-offering you shall not make of them on the altar to the L-rd [(an exhortation against their being smoked)].


###### Leviticus 22:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:23)


וְשׁ֥וֹר וָשֶׂ֖ה שָׂר֣וּעַ וְקָל֑וּט נְדָבָה֙ תַּעֲשֶׂ֣ה אֹת֔וֹ וּלְנֵ֖דֶר לֹ֥א יֵרָצֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
An ox or lamb with one limb larger [or smaller] than the other or with uncleft hooves, you shall make (offer) it as a free-will-offering [to the temple treasury] [but] for [the fulfillment of] a vow it will not be favorably accepted.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Either a bullock or a lamb that has anything superfluous or too short, that mayst thou offer for a freewill offering; but for a vow it shall not be accepted.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And an ox or a lamb, asymmetrical [of limb] or "closed" [of hoof], a gift [for Temple maintenance] you may make it, but as a vow [for the altar] it shall not be acceptable.


###### Leviticus 22:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:24)


וּמָע֤וּךְ וְכָתוּת֙ וְנָת֣וּק וְכָר֔וּת לֹ֥א תַקְרִ֖יבוּ לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה וּֽבְאַרְצְכֶ֖ם לֹ֥א תַעֲשֽׂוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[An animal that has its testicles] crushed, mangled, torn or cut off you shall not bring to Adonoy and in your land you shall not inflict these injuries.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not offer to the Lord that which has its testicles bruised or crushed, or broken, or cut; neither shall you do thus in your land.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [an animal whose membrum or testicles are] bruised [by hand] or crushed, or torn [by hand from their cords but still resting in the scrotum], or cut [by instrument but still resting in the scrotum] you shall not present to the L-rd. And in your land [i.e., to all the animals in your land, including unclean ones] you shall not do this [i.e., you shall not castrate them].


###### Leviticus 22:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:25)


וּמִיַּ֣ד בֶּן־נֵכָ֗ר לֹ֥א תַקְרִ֛יבוּ אֶת־לֶ֥חֶם אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֖ם מִכׇּל־אֵ֑לֶּה כִּ֣י מׇשְׁחָתָ֤ם בָּהֶם֙ מ֣וּם בָּ֔ם לֹ֥א יֵרָצ֖וּ לָכֶֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And from a strangers [gentiles] hand you shall not bring (offer) the bread of your God from any of these for they have inflicted wounds in them; they will not be favorably accepted for you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither from a stranger’s hand shall you offer the bread of your God from any of these; because their corruption is in them, their blemish is in them: they shall not be accepted for you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from the hand of a gentile [who brings an offering through a Cohein] you shall not present the bread of your G-d of all these [blemished animals], for their defect is in them. There is a blemish in them; they will not be acceptable [to atone] for you.


###### Leviticus 22:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:26)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 22:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:27)


שׁ֣וֹר אוֹ־כֶ֤שֶׂב אוֹ־עֵז֙ כִּ֣י יִוָּלֵ֔ד וְהָיָ֛ה שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֖ים תַּ֣חַת אִמּ֑וֹ וּמִיּ֤וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִי֙ וָהָ֔לְאָה יֵרָצֶ֕ה לְקׇרְבַּ֥ן אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
An ox, lamb or goat, when it is born, shall be with its mother for seven days. From the eighth day and thereafter it may be favorably accepted as a sacrifice as a fire-offering to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When a bullock, or a sheep, or a goat, is brought forth, then it shall be seven days under its dam; and from the eighth day and thenceforth it shall be accepted for an offering made by fire to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
An ox, or a sheep, or a goat, when it is born [(to exclude one delivered by Caesarean section)], it shall be seven days under its mother; and from the eighth day and on, it shall be acceptable as an offering of fire to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 22:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:28)


וְשׁ֖וֹר אוֹ־שֶׂ֑ה אֹת֣וֹ וְאֶת־בְּנ֔וֹ לֹ֥א תִשְׁחֲט֖וּ בְּי֥וֹם אֶחָֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
An ox or a lamb, it and its offspring, you shall not slaughter in one day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whether it be cow or ewe, you shall not kill it and its young both in one day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And an ox or a lamb, it [the mother] and its son [or daughter] you shall not slaughter in one day.


###### Leviticus 22:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:29)


וְכִֽי־תִזְבְּח֥וּ זֶֽבַח־תּוֹדָ֖ה לַיהֹוָ֑ה לִֽרְצֹנְכֶ֖ם תִּזְבָּֽחוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you sacrifice a thanks-giving-offering to Adonoy, that it be favorably accepted for you you shall sacrifice it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when you will offer a sacrifice of thanksgiving to the Lord, offer it that it may be accepted.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when you slaughter a sacrifice of thanksgiving to the L-rd, that it be acceptable for you shall you slaughter it, [viz.,]


###### Leviticus 22:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:30)


בַּיּ֤וֹם הַהוּא֙ יֵאָכֵ֔ל לֹֽא־תוֹתִ֥ירוּ מִמֶּ֖נּוּ עַד־בֹּ֑קֶר אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On that day [that it is sacrificed] you should eat it; you shall leave none of it until the next morning, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
On the same day it shall be eaten up; you shall leave none of it until the morrow: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Slaughter it with the intention that] on that [same] day it will be eaten. You shall leave none of it over until the morning; I am the L-rd [Know who decreed this and let it not be light in your eyes.]


###### Leviticus 22:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:31)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם֙ מִצְוֺתַ֔י וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֖ם אֹתָ֑ם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall preserve My commandments and fulfill them, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall keep my commandments, and do them: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall heed [i.e., study] My mitzvoth and you shall do them; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 22:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:32)


וְלֹ֤א תְחַלְּלוּ֙ אֶת־שֵׁ֣ם קׇדְשִׁ֔י וְנִ֨קְדַּשְׁתִּ֔י בְּת֖וֹךְ בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה מְקַדִּשְׁכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not profane My holy Name; but I will be sanctified among Bnei Yisroel. I am Adonoy Who makes you holy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither shall you profane my holy name; but I will be hallowed among the children of Yisra᾽el: I am the Lord who make you holy,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall not profane My holy name [by wittingly transgressing My words], and I shall be sanctified [(Deliver yourself — even unto death — for sanctification of My name)] in the midst of the children of Israel [i.e., in public]; I am the L-rd who sanctifies you.


###### Leviticus 22:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/22:33)


הַמּוֹצִ֤יא אֶתְכֶם֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם לִהְי֥וֹת לָכֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִ֑ים אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Who brought you out of the land of Egypt to be your God, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who brought you out of the land of Miżrayim, to be your God: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who took you out of the land of Egypt [to this end], to be a G-d unto you; I am the L-rd.



## Chapter 23

###### Leviticus 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:2)


דַּבֵּ֞ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵהֶ֔ם מוֹעֲדֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־תִּקְרְא֥וּ אֹתָ֖ם מִקְרָאֵ֣י קֹ֑דֶשׁ אֵ֥לֶּה הֵ֖ם מוֹעֲדָֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them; the appointed times of Adonoy, which you shall proclaim them as holy assemblies; these are My appointed times.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, The feasts of the Lord, which you shall proclaim to be holy gatherings, these are my feasts.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel and say to them: The festivals of the L-rd, which you shall call (them) callings of holiness — these are My festivals. [Make Israel conversant in the times of the festivals, for the year is intercalated for (Jews of) the exile who leave their place for the festival pilgrimage and who have not yet arrived in Jerusalem (by the time of the festival.)]


###### Leviticus 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:3)


שֵׁ֣שֶׁת יָמִים֮ תֵּעָשֶׂ֣ה מְלָאכָה֒ וּבַיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִ֗י שַׁבַּ֤ת שַׁבָּתוֹן֙ מִקְרָא־קֹ֔דֶשׁ כׇּל־מְלָאכָ֖ה לֹ֣א תַעֲשׂ֑וּ שַׁבָּ֥ת הִוא֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה בְּכֹ֖ל מוֹשְׁבֹֽתֵיכֶֽם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Six days shall work be performed; and on the seventh day is a *Shabbos* of resting, a holy assembly. You shall not do any work, it is a *Shabbos* for Adonoy in all your dwelling places.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Six days shall work be done: but the seventh day is the sabbath of solemn rest, a holy gathering; you shall do no work: it is a sabbath to the Lord in all your dwellings.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Six days shall work be done, and on the seventh day is a Sabbath of resting, a calling of holiness. All work you shall not do; it is Sabbath to the L-rd in all your dwellings. [One who desecrates the festivals is considered to have desecrated the Sabbath, and one who keeps the festivals is considered to have kept the Sabbath.]


###### Leviticus 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:4)


אֵ֚לֶּה מוֹעֲדֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה מִקְרָאֵ֖י קֹ֑דֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר־תִּקְרְא֥וּ אֹתָ֖ם בְּמוֹעֲדָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are the appointed times of Adonoy, the holy assemblies that you shall proclaim them in their appointed times.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These are the feasts of the Lord, holy gatherings, which you shall proclaim in their seasons.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are the festivals of the L-rd, callings of holiness which you shall call (them) in their proper times. [The verse here speaks of the proclamation of the New Moon.]


###### Leviticus 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:5)


בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָרִאשׁ֗וֹן בְּאַרְבָּעָ֥ה עָשָׂ֛ר לַחֹ֖דֶשׁ בֵּ֣ין הָעַרְבָּ֑יִם פֶּ֖סַח לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In the first month (Nisan), on the fourteenth day of the month in the afternoon, is a Pesach (offering) for Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
On the fourteenth day of the first month towards evening is the Lord’s passover.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the first month, on the fourteenth day of the month, towards evening [i.e., from the sixth hour (of the day) onwards] is (the time for the sacrifice of) the Paschal offering to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:6)


וּבַחֲמִשָּׁ֨ה עָשָׂ֥ר יוֹם֙ לַחֹ֣דֶשׁ הַזֶּ֔ה חַ֥ג הַמַּצּ֖וֹת לַיהֹוָ֑ה שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֖ים מַצּ֥וֹת תֹּאכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the fifteenth day of this month is the festival of *matzos*, for Adonoy; for seven days you shall eat *matzos*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And on the fifteenth day of the same month is the feast of unleavened bread to the Lord: seven days you must eat unleavened bread.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And on the fifteenth day of this month is the festival of matzoth to the L-rd. Seven days shall you eat matzoth.


###### Leviticus 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:7)


בַּיּוֹם֙ הָֽרִאשׁ֔וֹן מִקְרָא־קֹ֖דֶשׁ יִהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֑ם כׇּל־מְלֶ֥אכֶת עֲבֹדָ֖ה לֹ֥א תַעֲשֽׂוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the first day [there] shall be a day of holy assembly for you; you shall not do any work of labor.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
On the first day you shall have a holy gathering: you shall do no servile work.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On the first day, a calling of holiness shall it be for you; all work of labor you shall not do.


###### Leviticus 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:8)


וְהִקְרַבְתֶּ֥ם אִשֶּׁ֛ה לַיהֹוָ֖ה שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֑ים בַּיּ֤וֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי֙ מִקְרָא־קֹ֔דֶשׁ כׇּל־מְלֶ֥אכֶת עֲבֹדָ֖ה לֹ֥א תַעֲשֽׂוּ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall bring a fire-offering to Adonoy [on each of] seven days. On the seventh day it is [a day] of holy assembly; you shall not do any work of labor.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But you shall offer an offering made by fire to the Lord for seven days: on the seventh day is a holy gathering: you shall do no servile work.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall present a fire-offering [the additional (mussaf) offerings (Numbers 28:19)] to the L-rd for a seven day period [(in any event. They are not mutually indispensable. If bullocks are not available, offer the rams, etc.)] On the seventh day is a calling of holiness; all work of labor [i.e., even work which you consider (crucial) labor] you shall not do.


###### Leviticus 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:9)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:10)


דַּבֵּ֞ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵהֶ֔ם כִּֽי־תָבֹ֣אוּ אֶל־הָאָ֗רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֤ר אֲנִי֙ נֹתֵ֣ן לָכֶ֔ם וּקְצַרְתֶּ֖ם אֶת־קְצִירָ֑הּ וַהֲבֵאתֶ֥ם אֶת־עֹ֛מֶר רֵאשִׁ֥ית קְצִירְכֶ֖ם אֶל־הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them; when you come into the land that I give to you and you reap its harvest, you shall bring an *omer* of the first fruits of your harvest to the *kohein*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, When you are come to the land which I give to you, and shall reap its harvest, then you shall bring an ῾omer of the firstfruits of your harvest to the priest:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel and say to them: When you come to the land which I give to you and you reap its harvest, you shall bring the omer [one-tenth of an ephah], the first (grain) of your harvest [i.e., the first grain to be harvested (in your land)] to the Cohein.

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall wave the *omer* before Adonoy that it be favorably accepted for you. On the day after the day of rest (Pesach), the *kohein* shall wave it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall wave the ῾omer before the Lord, to be accepted for you: on the morrow after the sabbath the priest shall wave it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall wave the omer [(back and forth to ward off harmful winds; up and down to ward off harmful dew)] before the L-rd for your favor [(if offered as prescribed)]. On the morrow of the Sabbath [i.e., on the morrow of the first day of Pesach] the Cohein shall wave it.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

And its [libation] meal-offering, two-tenths [(double the normal amount)] of fine flour mixed with oil, a fire-offering to the L-rd, a sweet savor; and its drink-offering, wine, a fourth of a hin [(the normal amount)].

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you reap the harvest of your land, you shall not cut completely the corner of your field when you reap. You shall not gather the gleaning of your harvest. You shall leave them for the poor and the proselyte, I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when you reap the harvest of your land, thou shalt not altogether remove the corners of thy field when thou reapest, nor shalt thou gather any gleaning of thy harvest: thou shalt leave them to the poor, and to the stranger: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when you harvest the harvest of your land, do not end off the corner (peah) of your field in your reaping. [This is mentioned a second time (see 19:9) to posit liability for transgression of two negative commandments]; and the gleaning (leket) of your harvest you shall not gather. To the poor man and to the stranger shall you leave them [(Leave it for them and let them gather it; do not assist any particular one of them)]; I am the L-rd your G-d. [This is inserted in the midst of the festivals to teach that if one gives leket, shikchah, and peah to the poor as prescribed, it is accounted to him as if he had built the Temple and offered up his sacrifices therein.]

And all work you shall not do on this self-same day; for it is a day of atonement to atone for you before the L-rd your G-d.


###### Leviticus 23:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:29)


כִּ֤י כׇל־הַנֶּ֙פֶשׁ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־תְעֻנֶּ֔ה בְּעֶ֖צֶם הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וְנִכְרְתָ֖ה מֵֽעַמֶּֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For any person who will not be afflicted on this very day shall be cut off from his people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For whatever person shall not be afflicted on that same day, he shall be cut off from his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For every soul which will not be afflicted on this self-same day will be cut off from its people.


###### Leviticus 23:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:30)


וְכׇל־הַנֶּ֗פֶשׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר תַּעֲשֶׂה֙ כׇּל־מְלָאכָ֔ה בְּעֶ֖צֶם הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וְהַֽאֲבַדְתִּ֛י אֶת־הַנֶּ֥פֶשׁ הַהִ֖וא מִקֶּ֥רֶב עַמָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Any person who does any work on this very day, I will cause that person to perish from among his people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whatever person does any work on that same day, the same person will I destroy from among his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every soul that shall do any work on this self-same day, I shall make that soul go lost from the midst of its people. [Thus, "cutting-off" (kareth), as in verse 29, is destruction.]


###### Leviticus 23:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:31)


כׇּל־מְלָאכָ֖ה לֹ֣א תַעֲשׂ֑וּ חֻקַּ֤ת עוֹלָם֙ לְדֹרֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם בְּכֹ֖ל מֹשְׁבֹֽתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not do any work; it is an everlasting statute for all your generations in all your dwelling places.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall do no manner of work: it shall be a statute for ever throughout your generations in all your dwellings.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All work you shall not do [(This posits transgression of numerous negative commandments, (or it is an exhortation against labor on the night as well as the day (of Yom Kippur)], an everlasting statute, throughout your generations, in all of your dwellings.


###### Leviticus 23:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:32)


שַׁבַּ֨ת שַׁבָּת֥וֹן הוּא֙ לָכֶ֔ם וְעִנִּיתֶ֖ם אֶת־נַפְשֹׁתֵיכֶ֑ם בְּתִשְׁעָ֤ה לַחֹ֙דֶשׁ֙ בָּעֶ֔רֶב מֵעֶ֣רֶב עַד־עֶ֔רֶב תִּשְׁבְּת֖וּ שַׁבַּתְּכֶֽם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It is a *Shabbos* of complete rest for you and you shall afflict yourselves on the ninth day of the month at evening; from evening to evening you shall rest, on your day of rest.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It shall be to you a sabbath of solemn rest, and you shall afflict your souls: on the ninth day of the month at evening, from evening to evening shall you celebrate your sabbath.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A Sabbath of resting shall it be for you, and you shall afflict your souls; on the ninth day of the month in the evening, from evening until evening, shall you rest your resting.

Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:

דַּבֵּ֛ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר בַּחֲמִשָּׁ֨ה עָשָׂ֜ר י֗וֹם לַחֹ֤דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִי֙ הַזֶּ֔ה חַ֧ג הַסֻּכּ֛וֹת שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֖ים לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel saying; on the fifteenth day of this seventh month is the festival of Sukkos for seven days, for Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, saying, The fifteenth day of this seventh month shall be the feast of booths for seven days to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel, saying: On the fifteenth day of this seventh month is the festival of Succoth, seven days to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 23:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:35)


בַּיּ֥וֹם הָרִאשׁ֖וֹן מִקְרָא־קֹ֑דֶשׁ כׇּל־מְלֶ֥אכֶת עֲבֹדָ֖ה לֹ֥א תַעֲשֽׂוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On the first day [there shall be] a holy assembly; you shall not do any work of labor.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
On the first day shall be a holy gathering: you shall do no servile work.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On the first day is a holy calling [Sanctify it (Yom Kippur) with clean garments and with prayer, (and the other festivals with food and drink, clean garments and prayer)]; all work of labor you shall not do.


###### Leviticus 23:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:36)


שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֔ים תַּקְרִ֥יבוּ אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַיהֹוָ֑ה בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֡י מִקְרָא־קֹ֩דֶשׁ֩ יִהְיֶ֨ה לָכֶ֜ם וְהִקְרַבְתֶּ֨ם אִשֶּׁ֤ה לַֽיהֹוָה֙ עֲצֶ֣רֶת הִ֔וא כׇּל־מְלֶ֥אכֶת עֲבֹדָ֖ה לֹ֥א תַעֲשֽׂוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For seven days, you shall bring a fire-offering to Adonoy; the eighth day shall be for you a holy assembly, and you shall bring a fire-offering to Adonoy. It is [a day] of convocation. you shall not do any work of labor.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Seven days you shall offer an offering made by fire to the Lord: on the eighth day shall be a holy gathering to you; and you shall offer an offering made by fire to the Lord; it is a solemn assembly; and you shall do no servile work.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Seven days shall you present a fire-offering to the L-rd. On the eighth day a holy calling shall there be for you, and you shall present a fire-offering to the L-rd. It is a "holding back" (atzereth) [the L-rd saying, as it were: "I have held you back with Me, it being difficult for Me to part with you")]; all work of labor [see 23:8] you shall not do. [("It is a holding back") — but on Chol Hamoed (the intermediate days of the festival) "work of labor" is permitted.]


###### Leviticus 23:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:37)


אֵ֚לֶּה מוֹעֲדֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־תִּקְרְא֥וּ אֹתָ֖ם מִקְרָאֵ֣י קֹ֑דֶשׁ לְהַקְרִ֨יב אִשֶּׁ֜ה לַיהֹוָ֗ה עֹלָ֧ה וּמִנְחָ֛ה זֶ֥בַח וּנְסָכִ֖ים דְּבַר־י֥וֹם בְּיוֹמֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are the appointed times of Adonoy which you shall proclaim them [as] holy assemblies, on which to bring a fire-offering to Adonoy; a burnt-offering and a meal-offering, a sacrifice and wine-offerings, each on its assigned day,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These are the feasts of the Lord, which you shall proclaim to be holy gatherings, to offer an offering made by fire to the Lord, a burnt offering, and a meal offering, a sacrifice, and drink offerings, every thing upon its day:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are the festivals of the L-rd, which you shall call holy callings to present a fire-offering to the L-rd, a burnt-offering and a meal-offering [(the libation meal-offering, which is offered up together with the burnt-offering)], sacrifice and drink-offerings, the thing of the day [(as prescribed in Numbers)] in its day [(but if the day passed, its sacrifice is forfeited)].


###### Leviticus 23:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:38)


מִלְּבַ֖ד שַׁבְּתֹ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה וּמִלְּבַ֣ד מַתְּנֽוֹתֵיכֶ֗ם וּמִלְּבַ֤ד כׇּל־נִדְרֵיכֶם֙ וּמִלְּבַד֙ כׇּל־נִדְבֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר תִּתְּנ֖וּ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Besides the [offerings of the] *Shabbasos* of Adonoy and besides your gifts and besides all your vow-offerings and besides all your free-will-offerings that you will give to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
beside the sabbaths of the Lord, and beside your gifts, and beside all your vows, and beside all your freewill offerings, which you give to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Aside from the Sabbaths of the L-rd, and aside from your gifts, and aside from all your vows and aside from all your gift-offerings that you give to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 23:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:39)


אַ֡ךְ בַּחֲמִשָּׁה֩ עָשָׂ֨ר י֜וֹם לַחֹ֣דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִ֗י בְּאׇסְפְּכֶם֙ אֶת־תְּבוּאַ֣ת הָאָ֔רֶץ תָּחֹ֥גּוּ אֶת־חַג־יְהֹוָ֖ה שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֑ים בַּיּ֤וֹם הָֽרִאשׁוֹן֙ שַׁבָּת֔וֹן וּבַיּ֥וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֖י שַׁבָּתֽוֹן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Indeed, on the fifteenth day of the seventh month, when you gather the produce of the land you shall celebrate the festival of Adonoy for seven days. The first day is a day of rest and the eighth day is a day of rest.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also on the fifteenth day of the seventh month, when you have gathered in the fruit of the land, you shall keep a feast to the Lord seven days: on the first day shall be a sabbath, and on the eighth day shall be a sabbath.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But [(since the festival peace-offering (chagigah) can be brought on any one of the seven days, it does not override the Sabbath)] on the fifteenth day of the seventh month, when you gather in the produce of the land [(Intercalate the years so that this harvest festival does not fall out sometimes in the middle of the summer, sometimes in the middle of the winter)], you shall celebrate [with the chagigah] the festival of the L-rd seven days [(If you did not bring the chagigah on one of the seven, you may bring it on another)]; on the first day, a resting, and on the eighth day, a resting.


###### Leviticus 23:40

וּלְקַחְתֶּ֨ם לָכֶ֜ם בַּיּ֣וֹם הָרִאשׁ֗וֹן פְּרִ֨י עֵ֤ץ הָדָר֙ כַּפֹּ֣ת תְּמָרִ֔ים וַעֲנַ֥ף עֵץ־עָבֹ֖ת וְעַרְבֵי־נָ֑חַל וּשְׂמַחְתֶּ֗ם לִפְנֵ֛י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֖ם שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall take for yourselves, on the first day, the fruit of the beautiful tree (*esrog*), a branch of palm trees (*lulav*), boughs of thick-leaved trees (*hadasim*), and willows of the brook (*aravos*), and you shall rejoice before Adonoy, your God, for seven days.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall take for yourselves on the first day the fruit of the tree hadar, branches of palm trees, and the boughs of thick leaved trees, and willows of the brook; and you shall rejoice before the Lord your God seven days.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall take for yourselves on the first day the fruit of a tree [(the fruit tasting like the tree)] which is hadar, [which lives (hadar, i.e., is produced) on its tree from year to year — an ethrog], a branch of date-palms, a branch of a plaited tree [(a tree the branches of which are plaited like ropes — a myrtle)], and willows of the brook, and you shall rejoice before the L-rd your G-d seven days.

וְחַגֹּתֶ֤ם אֹתוֹ֙ חַ֣ג לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֖ים בַּשָּׁנָ֑ה חֻקַּ֤ת עוֹלָם֙ לְדֹרֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם בַּחֹ֥דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִ֖י תָּחֹ֥גּוּ אֹתֽוֹ׃

You shall celebrate it as a festival to Adonoy seven days in the year; it is an everlasting statute throughout your generations in the seventh month, you shall celebrate it.

And you shall celebrate it [("it"): The chagigah is brought on only one of the days)] as a festival to the L-rd seven days in the year, a statute forever throughout your generations; on the seventh month shall you celebrate it.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:42)


בַּסֻּכֹּ֥ת תֵּשְׁב֖וּ שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֑ים כׇּל־הָֽאֶזְרָח֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל יֵשְׁב֖וּ בַּסֻּכֹּֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall dwell in the *sukkos* seven days, every native born Israelite shall dwell in the *sukkos*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall dwell in booths seven days; all that are home born in Yisra᾽el shall dwell in booths:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/23:43)


לְמַ֘עַן֮ יֵדְע֣וּ דֹרֹֽתֵיכֶם֒ כִּ֣י בַסֻּכּ֗וֹת הוֹשַׁ֙בְתִּי֙ אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בְּהוֹצִיאִ֥י אוֹתָ֖ם מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
So that your generations will know that in the *sukkos* I caused Bnei Yisroel to dwell when I took them out from the land of Egypt, I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that your generations may know that I made the children of Yisra᾽el to dwell in booths, when I brought them out of the land of Miżrayim: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that your generations know that in succoth [clouds of glory] did I cause the children of Israel to dwell when I took them out of the land of Egypt; I am the L-rd your G-d.

וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר מֹשֶׁ֔ה אֶֽת־מֹעֲדֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֑ה אֶל־בְּנֵ֖י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And thus Moshe declared the appointed times of Adonoy to Bnei Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

## Chapter 24

###### Leviticus 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:2)


צַ֞ו אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל וְיִקְח֨וּ אֵלֶ֜יךָ שֶׁ֣מֶן זַ֥יִת זָ֛ךְ כָּתִ֖ית לַמָּא֑וֹר לְהַעֲלֹ֥ת נֵ֖ר תָּמִֽיד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Command Bnei Yisroel to take (bring) to you pure, pressed, olive oil for illumination to keep the lamp burning constantly.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Command the children of Yisra᾽el, that they bring to thee pure oil olive beaten for the light, to cause the lamps to burn continually.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Command the children of Israel that they bring to you clear olive oil [(the first of the three oils issuing from the olive)], (the olives), crushed for illumination, to raise a light continually [(from night to night, like the "continual" — from day to day — burnt-offering)]. [This is the section of the mitzvah of the lamps. Exodus 27:20 (in this connection) is to be understood as: "And you are destined to command, etc." (the actual command taking place here.) It is mentioned there, in connection with the account of the construction of the tabernacle, only to explain the function of the menorah.]


###### Leviticus 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:3)


מִחוּץ֩ לְפָרֹ֨כֶת הָעֵדֻ֜ת בְּאֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֗ד יַעֲרֹךְ֩ אֹת֨וֹ אַהֲרֹ֜ן מֵעֶ֧רֶב עַד־בֹּ֛קֶר לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה תָּמִ֑יד חֻקַּ֥ת עוֹלָ֖ם לְדֹרֹֽתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Outside of the Curtain of the Testimony, which is inside the Tent of Meeting, Aharon shall arrange it [so that it burns] from evening until morning before Adonoy, continually; as an everlasting statute throughout your generations.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Outside the veil of the Testimony, in the Tent of Meeting, shall Aharon order it from evening unto morning before the Lord continually: it shall be a statute for ever in your generations.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Outside the parocheth, [which is before] the Testimony [i.e., the ark], in the tent of meeting, shall Aaron arrange it (the menorah) [so that it burn] from evening until morning before the L-rd continually, an eternal statute throughout your generations.


###### Leviticus 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:4)


עַ֚ל הַמְּנֹרָ֣ה הַטְּהֹרָ֔ה יַעֲרֹ֖ךְ אֶת־הַנֵּר֑וֹת לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה תָּמִֽיד׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Atop the pure menorah he shall arrange the lights before Adonoy, constantly.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall order the lamps upon the pure candlestick, before the Lord continually.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On the pure [gold] menorah shall he arrange the lamps before the L-rd always.


###### Leviticus 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:5)


וְלָקַחְתָּ֣ סֹ֔לֶת וְאָפִיתָ֣ אֹתָ֔הּ שְׁתֵּ֥ים עֶשְׂרֵ֖ה חַלּ֑וֹת שְׁנֵי֙ עֶשְׂרֹנִ֔ים יִהְיֶ֖ה הַֽחַלָּ֥ה הָאֶחָֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall take fine flour and bake twelve loaves, each individual loaf shall be two tenths [of an *ephah*].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt take fine flour, and bake of it twelve cakes: two tenth measures shall be in one cake.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall take fine flour and you shall bake it (into) twelve challoth; two-tenths (of an ephah) shall the one challah be.


###### Leviticus 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:6)


וְשַׂמְתָּ֥ אוֹתָ֛ם שְׁתַּ֥יִם מַֽעֲרָכ֖וֹת שֵׁ֣שׁ הַֽמַּעֲרָ֑כֶת עַ֛ל הַשֻּׁלְחָ֥ן הַטָּהֹ֖ר לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall place them in two stacks, six in each stack on the Pure Table, before Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt set them in two rows, six on a row, upon the pure table before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall place them in two rows, six in a row, on the pure [gold] table before the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:7)


וְנָתַתָּ֥ עַל־הַֽמַּעֲרֶ֖כֶת לְבֹנָ֣ה זַכָּ֑ה וְהָיְתָ֤ה לַלֶּ֙חֶם֙ לְאַזְכָּרָ֔ה אִשֶּׁ֖ה לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall put pure frankincense on [each] stack and it shall be as a memorial portion for the bread, as a fire-offering, to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt put pure frankincense upon each row, that it may be on the bread for a memorial, an offering made by fire to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall place upon the two [i.e., upon each of the two rows] pure frankincense [(On each of the two rows there were two cups of frankincense, a fistful in each)]. And it shall be a memorial for the bread [(For nothing of the bread was offered on high, but the incense was burned when they removed the bread each Sabbath. By the incense, then, the bread is "remembered" (in the same way that the fistful is a "memorial" for the meal-offering)], a fire-offering to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:8)


בְּי֨וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֜ת בְּי֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֗ת יַֽעַרְכֶ֛נּוּ לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה תָּמִ֑יד מֵאֵ֥ת בְּנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּרִ֥ית עוֹלָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On each and every *Shabbos* he shall arrange them before Adonoy, continually, from Bnei Yisroel as an eternal covenant.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every sabbath he shall set it in order before the Lord continually, an everlasting covenant from the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On the day of Sabbath, on the day of Sabbath, he shall arrange it before the L-rd always; from the children of Israel, an everlasting covenant.


###### Leviticus 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:9)


וְהָֽיְתָה֙ לְאַהֲרֹ֣ן וּלְבָנָ֔יו וַאֲכָלֻ֖הוּ בְּמָק֣וֹם קָדֹ֑שׁ כִּ֡י קֹ֩דֶשׁ֩ קׇֽדָשִׁ֨ים ה֥וּא ל֛וֹ מֵאִשֵּׁ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה חׇק־עוֹלָֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It shall belong to Aharon and to his sons and they shall eat it in a sacred place, for it is most holy for him from among the fire-offerings of Adonoy, as an everlasting statute.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be Aharon’s and his sons’; and they shall eat it in the holy place: for it is most holy to him of the offerings of the Lord made by fire by a perpetual due.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it [this meal-offering (everything coming from grain being in the category of "meal-offering")] shall be for Aaron and for his sons. And they shall eat it [the bread] in a holy place, for holy of holies is it to him, of the fire-offerings of the L-rd, an everlasting statute.


###### Leviticus 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:10)


וַיֵּצֵא֙ בֶּן־אִשָּׁ֣ה יִשְׂרְאֵלִ֔ית וְהוּא֙ בֶּן־אִ֣ישׁ מִצְרִ֔י בְּת֖וֹךְ בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיִּנָּצוּ֙ בַּֽמַּחֲנֶ֔ה בֶּ֚ן הַיִּשְׂרְאֵלִ֔ית וְאִ֖ישׁ הַיִּשְׂרְאֵלִֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The son of an Israelite woman went out, and he was the son of an Egyptian man, among Bnei Yisroel. They argued in the encampment—the son of the Israelite woman, and the Israelite man.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the son of a Yisra᾽elite woman, whose father was a Miżrian man, went out among the children of Yisra᾽el: and this son of the Yisra᾽elite woman and a man of Yisra᾽el strove together in the camp;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there went out the son of an Israelite woman, and he was the son of an Egyptian man, [the one slain by Moses (Exodus 2:11-12)], in the midst of the children of Israel [(He had become a proselyte)], and they strove in the camp [i.e., regarding matters of the camp], the son of the Israelite woman and the Israelite man [who opposed him].


###### Leviticus 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:11)


וַ֠יִּקֹּ֠ב בֶּן־הָֽאִשָּׁ֨ה הַיִּשְׂרְאֵלִ֤ית אֶת־הַשֵּׁם֙ וַיְקַלֵּ֔ל וַיָּבִ֥יאוּ אֹת֖וֹ אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֑ה וְשֵׁ֥ם אִמּ֛וֹ שְׁלֹמִ֥ית בַּת־דִּבְרִ֖י לְמַטֵּה־דָֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The Israelite womans son clearly pronounced the Name [of God] and blasphemed and they brought him to Moshe. The name of his mother was Shlomis, daughter of Divri of the tribe of Dan.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Yisra᾽elite woman’s son blasphemed the name of the Lord, and cursed. And they brought him to Moshe: (and his mother’s name was Shelomit, the daughter of Divri, of the tribe of Dan: )


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the son of the Israelite woman indited the [ineffable] Name [the Tetragrammaton, that he had heard at Sinai], and he blasphemed, and they brought him to Moses. And the name of his mother was Shlomith [(She said "Shalom," promiscuously, to all)] the daughter of Divri [(She was a "prattler" (dabranith), speaking indiscriminately to all men)], of the tribe of Dan [(an encomium to Israel — she alone went astray)]


###### Leviticus 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:12)


וַיַּנִּיחֻ֖הוּ בַּמִּשְׁמָ֑ר לִפְרֹ֥שׁ לָהֶ֖ם עַל־פִּ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They placed him in the guardhouse until [his penalty] would be explained to them through the word of Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and they put him in custody, that the mind of the Lord might be shown them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they placed him [alone] in confinement, that it be specified to them by the mouth of the L-rd [whether or not he was to be killed].


###### Leviticus 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:13)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:14)


הוֹצֵ֣א אֶת־הַֽמְקַלֵּ֗ל אֶל־מִחוּץ֙ לַֽמַּחֲנֶ֔ה וְסָמְכ֧וּ כׇֽל־הַשֹּׁמְעִ֛ים אֶת־יְדֵיהֶ֖ם עַל־רֹאשׁ֑וֹ וְרָגְמ֥וּ אֹת֖וֹ כׇּל־הָעֵדָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Take the blasphemer beyond the encampment, and all those who heard him shall lay their hands on his head and the entire congregation shall stone him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Bring forth him that has cursed outside the camp; and let all that heard him lay their hands upon his head, and let all the congregation stone him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take the curser outside the camp, and let all [(including the judges)], who heard [(These are the witnesses)] place their hands on his head [(They say to him: Your blood is on your own head, and not upon us; you brought it upon yourself)], and let all the congregation stone him [i.e., let him be stoned in the presence of the entire congregation (whence it is derived that one's emissary is like himself)].


###### Leviticus 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:15)


וְאֶל־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל תְּדַבֵּ֣ר לֵאמֹ֑ר אִ֥ישׁ אִ֛ישׁ כִּֽי־יְקַלֵּ֥ל אֱלֹהָ֖יו וְנָשָׂ֥א חֶטְאֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And to Bnei Yisroel you shall speak, saying; Any person who shall blaspheme his God shall bear [the burden of] his sin.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, saying, Whoever curses his God shall bear his sin.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to the children of Israel, you shall speak, saying: A man, if he curse his G-d, then he shall bear his sin [In the absence of warning, he incurs kareth.]


###### Leviticus 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:16)


וְנֹקֵ֤ב שֵׁם־יְהֹוָה֙ מ֣וֹת יוּמָ֔ת רָג֥וֹם יִרְגְּמוּ־ב֖וֹ כׇּל־הָעֵדָ֑ה כַּגֵּר֙ כָּֽאֶזְרָ֔ח בְּנׇקְבוֹ־שֵׁ֖ם יוּמָֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever pronounces clearly the Name of Adonoy [and blasphemes] he will surely be put to death. The entire congregation shall surely stone him. The proselyte and native born alike when he clearly articulates the Name, shall be put to death.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he who blasphemes the name of the Lord, shall surely be put to death, and all the congregation shall certainly stone him: both the stranger, and he that is born in the land, when he blasphemes the name of the Lord, shall be put to death.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And one who indites the Name of the L-rd [the Tetragrammaton (but not an epithet)] shall be put to death. All the congregation shall stone him. Proselyte, like born-Jew — if he indites the Name he shall die.


###### Leviticus 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:17)


וְאִ֕ישׁ כִּ֥י יַכֶּ֖ה כׇּל־נֶ֣פֶשׁ אָדָ֑ם מ֖וֹת יוּמָֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Any man who kills any human being must surely be put to death.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that kills any man shall surely be put to death.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man, if he smite [i.e., kill] any soul of man [(including women and minors)] shall be put to death.


###### Leviticus 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:18)


וּמַכֵּ֥ה נֶֽפֶשׁ־בְּהֵמָ֖ה יְשַׁלְּמֶ֑נָּה נֶ֖פֶשׁ תַּ֥חַת נָֽפֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever kills an animal shall pay for it, [the compensation of] an animal for an animal.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that kills a beast shall make it good; beast for beast.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And one that smites [i.e., kills] a beast shall make compensation for it, life for life.


###### Leviticus 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:19)


וְאִ֕ישׁ כִּֽי־יִתֵּ֥ן מ֖וּם בַּעֲמִית֑וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה כֵּ֖ן יֵעָ֥שֶׂה לּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A man who maims his neighbor just as he did, so shall be done to him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man maim his neighbour; as he has done, so shall it be done to him;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man, if he maims (lit., "gives an injury to") his neighbor — as he did, so shall it be done to him.


###### Leviticus 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:20)


שֶׁ֚בֶר תַּ֣חַת שֶׁ֔בֶר עַ֚יִן תַּ֣חַת עַ֔יִן שֵׁ֖ן תַּ֣חַת שֵׁ֑ן כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר יִתֵּ֥ן מוּם֙ בָּֽאָדָ֔ם כֵּ֖ן יִנָּ֥תֶן בּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[Compensation for] a broken bone [in exchange] for a broken bone, [compen-sation for] the loss of an eye [in exchange] for the loss of an eye, [compensation for] the loss to a tooth [in exchange] for the loss of a tooth; just as he maimed the man, so shall it be done to him [monetarily].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
breach for breach, eye for eye, tooth for tooth: as he has maimed a man, so shall it be done to him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A breaking for a breaking, an eye for an eye, a tooth for a tooth — as he gives an injury to a man, so shall it [money] be given by him [in compensation for the injury. He (the injured one) is assessed as a slave (and he is given the difference between the value of a slave with this injury and one without it)].


###### Leviticus 24:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:21)


וּמַכֵּ֥ה בְהֵמָ֖ה יְשַׁלְּמֶ֑נָּה וּמַכֵּ֥ה אָדָ֖ם יוּמָֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Whoever smites an animal shall pay for it. Whoever smites a person shall be put to death.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he that kills a beast, he shall restore it: and he that kills a man, he shall be put to death.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And one who strikes [i.e., wounds] a beast shall make compensation for it. And one who strikes [i.e., wounds] a man [i.e., his parent, (when he is alive, comparable to "beast")] shall be put to death.


###### Leviticus 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:22)


מִשְׁפַּ֤ט אֶחָד֙ יִהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֔ם כַּגֵּ֥ר כָּאֶזְרָ֖ח יִהְיֶ֑ה כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
One rule shall apply for all of you just as it is for the proselyte, so it shall be for the native born, for I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall have one manner of law, as well for the stranger, as for one of your own country: for I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One judgment shall there be for you; for proselyte as for born-Jew shall it be, for I am the L-rd your G-d [— the G-d of all of you. Just as I unite My name with you, so do I unite it with the proselytes].


###### Leviticus 24:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/24:23)


וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר מֹשֶׁה֮ אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵל֒ וַיּוֹצִ֣יאוּ אֶת־הַֽמְקַלֵּ֗ל אֶל־מִחוּץ֙ לַֽמַּחֲנֶ֔ה וַיִּרְגְּמ֥וּ אֹת֖וֹ אָ֑בֶן וּבְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל עָשׂ֔וּ כַּֽאֲשֶׁ֛ר צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־מֹשֶֽׁה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe spoke to Bnei Yisroel and they took the blasphemer, beyond the encampment and they stoned him with a stone. And Bnei Yisroel did just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe spoke to the children of Yisra᾽el, and they brought him that had cursed out of the camp, and stoned him with stones. And the children of Yisra᾽el did as the Lord commanded Moshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses spoke to the children of Israel, and they took the curser outside the camp, and they stoned him with stones, and the children of Israel did as the L-rd commanded Moses [in respect to stoning (see Exodus 19:13 and Deuteronomy 21:22)].



## Chapter 25

###### Leviticus 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֔ה בְּהַ֥ר סִינַ֖י לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe on Mount Sinai, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe in mount Sinay, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses on Mount Sinai [(Just as the general rules and specific ordinances of shemitah (the sabbatical year) were given at Sinai, so, with all the mitzvoth)], saying:


###### Leviticus 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:2)


דַּבֵּ֞ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵהֶ֔ם כִּ֤י תָבֹ֙אוּ֙ אֶל־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֲנִ֖י נֹתֵ֣ן לָכֶ֑ם וְשָׁבְתָ֣ה הָאָ֔רֶץ שַׁבָּ֖ת לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them, when you come to the land which I give to you the land shall be at rest—a *Shabbos* for Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, When you come to the land which I give you, then shall the land keep a sabbath to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel and say to them: When you come to the land that I give to you, the land shall rest a Sabbath to the L-rd [i.e., in the name of the L-rd.]


###### Leviticus 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:3)


שֵׁ֤שׁ שָׁנִים֙ תִּזְרַ֣ע שָׂדֶ֔ךָ וְשֵׁ֥שׁ שָׁנִ֖ים תִּזְמֹ֣ר כַּרְמֶ֑ךָ וְאָסַפְתָּ֖ אֶת־תְּבוּאָתָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For six years you shall plant your field and for six years you shall prune your vineyard and you shall harvest its produce.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Six years thou shalt sow thy field, and six years thou shalt prune thy vineyard, and gather in its fruit;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Six years shall you sow your field and six years shall you prune your vineyard, and you shall collect its produce.


###### Leviticus 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:4)


וּבַשָּׁנָ֣ה הַשְּׁבִיעִ֗ת שַׁבַּ֤ת שַׁבָּתוֹן֙ יִהְיֶ֣ה לָאָ֔רֶץ שַׁבָּ֖ת לַיהֹוָ֑ה שָֽׂדְךָ֙ לֹ֣א תִזְרָ֔ע וְכַרְמְךָ֖ לֹ֥א תִזְמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But the seventh year shall be a *Shabbos* of rest for the land, a *Shabbos* for Adonoy, you shall not plant your field and you shall not prune your vineyard.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but in the seventh year shall be a sabbath of solemn rest for the land, a sabbath for the Lord: thou shalt neither sow thy field, nor prune thy vineyard.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in the seventh year, a Sabbath of resting shall there be for the land [i.e., for the fields and for the vineyards], a Sabbath to the L-rd; your field you shall not sow, and your vineyard you shall not prune.


###### Leviticus 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:5)


אֵ֣ת סְפִ֤יחַ קְצִֽירְךָ֙ לֹ֣א תִקְצ֔וֹר וְאֶת־עִנְּבֵ֥י נְזִירֶ֖ךָ לֹ֣א תִבְצֹ֑ר שְׁנַ֥ת שַׁבָּת֖וֹן יִהְיֶ֥ה לָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[Even] crops that grew on their own [from the seeds of your previous] harvest you shall not reap, and the grapes of your untended vines you shall not gather; it shall be a year of complete rest for the land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
That which grows of its own accord of thy harvest thou shalt not reap, nor gather the grapes of thy undressed vine: for it shall be a year of rest for the land.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The after-growth of your harvest [i.e., what fell there at harvest time] you shall not reap [i.e., you shall not take possession of it], and the grapes of your guarded vine [i.e., your vine which you did not render hefker (ownerless)] you shall not gather; a year of rest shall there be for the land.


###### Leviticus 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:6)


וְ֠הָיְתָ֠ה שַׁבַּ֨ת הָאָ֤רֶץ לָכֶם֙ לְאׇכְלָ֔ה לְךָ֖ וּלְעַבְדְּךָ֣ וְלַאֲמָתֶ֑ךָ וְלִשְׂכִֽירְךָ֙ וּלְתוֹשָׁ֣בְךָ֔ הַגָּרִ֖ים עִמָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The [produce] of the lands *Shabbos*[-year] shall be for yourselves, for food, for you, your servant and your maidservant, for your hired hand and your resident sojourner who reside with you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the sabbath produce of the land shall be food for you; for thee, and for thy servant, and for thy maid, and for thy hired servant, and for thy stranger that sojourns with thee,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the resting of the land [i.e., your "non-guarded" (hefker) land] shall be for you to eat: for you, and your man-servant, and your maid-servant, and your hired man, and your sojourners who dwell with you.


###### Leviticus 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:7)


וְלִ֨בְהֶמְתְּךָ֔ וְלַֽחַיָּ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּאַרְצֶ֑ךָ תִּהְיֶ֥ה כׇל־תְּבוּאָתָ֖הּ לֶאֱכֹֽל׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[Also] for your domesticated animals and for the [wild] beasts that are in your land shall all of its produce be for food.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and for thy cattle, and for the beast in thy land, shall all its increase be food.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for your beast and for the animal which is in your land shall be all its produce to eat. [So long as the animal eats from the field (i.e., so long as produce is found in the field), you may feed it to your beast from (what you have stored) in the house. If it has ended for the animal in the field, "end it" (i.e., remove it) for your beast from the house.]


###### Leviticus 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:8)


וְסָפַרְתָּ֣ לְךָ֗ שֶׁ֚בַע שַׁבְּתֹ֣ת שָׁנִ֔ים שֶׁ֥בַע שָׁנִ֖ים שֶׁ֣בַע פְּעָמִ֑ים וְהָי֣וּ לְךָ֗ יְמֵי֙ שֶׁ֚בַע שַׁבְּתֹ֣ת הַשָּׁנִ֔ים תֵּ֥שַׁע וְאַרְבָּעִ֖ים שָׁנָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall count for yourself seven sabbatical years, seven years, seven times and it shall be for you, the days (period) of the seven sabbatical years, forty-nine years.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt number seven sabbaths of years to thee, seven times seven years; and the space of the seven sabbaths of years shall be to thee forty nine years.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall count for yourself seven Sabbaths [i.e., shemitoth] of years — seven [shemitah] years, seven [discrete] times [(and not seven consecutive shemitah years)] — and the seven Sabbaths of years will be for you forty-nine years.


###### Leviticus 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:9)


וְהַֽעֲבַרְתָּ֞ שׁוֹפַ֤ר תְּרוּעָה֙ בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִעִ֔י בֶּעָשׂ֖וֹר לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ בְּיוֹם֙ הַכִּפֻּרִ֔ים תַּעֲבִ֥ירוּ שׁוֹפָ֖ר בְּכׇל־אַרְצְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall make a proclamation with the *shofor* on the tenth day of the seventh month. On Yom Kippur shall this *shofor*-proclamation be made. throughout all your land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then shalt thou cause the shofar to sound on the tenth day of the seventh month, on the day of atonement shall you sound the shofar throughout all your land.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall sound the shofar [in proclamation of the Jubilee] in the seventh month on the tenth day of the month. On the day of atonement shall you sound the shofar throughout the land.


###### Leviticus 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:10)


וְקִדַּשְׁתֶּ֗ם אֵ֣ת שְׁנַ֤ת הַחֲמִשִּׁים֙ שָׁנָ֔ה וּקְרָאתֶ֥ם דְּר֛וֹר בָּאָ֖רֶץ לְכׇל־יֹשְׁבֶ֑יהָ יוֹבֵ֥ל הִוא֙ תִּהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֔ם וְשַׁבְתֶּ֗ם אִ֚ישׁ אֶל־אֲחֻזָּת֔וֹ וְאִ֥ישׁ אֶל־מִשְׁפַּחְתּ֖וֹ תָּשֻֽׁבוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall sanctify the year of the fiftieth year and proclaim freedom in the land for all of its inhabitants; it shall be for you a jubilee and each man shall return to his ancestral land and each man shall return to his family.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall hallow the fiftieth year, and proclaim liberty throughout all the land to all its inhabitants: it shall be a jubilee for you; and you shall return every man to his possession, and you shall return every man to his family.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall sanctify the fiftieth year [(At its advent it is sanctified in beth-din: "The year is sanctified!")], and you shall call out freedom [for all (Hebrew) bondsmen] in the land, to all its inhabitants. "Yovel" (Jubilee) [lit., "the shofar"] shall it [(the name of the year)] be for you. And you shall return a man to his holding [(Fields return to their original owners)]; and a man to his family [(including the bondsman whose ear has been bored)] shall you return.


###### Leviticus 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:11)


יוֹבֵ֣ל הִ֗וא שְׁנַ֛ת הַחֲמִשִּׁ֥ים שָׁנָ֖ה תִּהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֑ם לֹ֣א תִזְרָ֔עוּ וְלֹ֤א תִקְצְרוּ֙ אֶת־סְפִיחֶ֔יהָ וְלֹ֥א תִבְצְר֖וּ אֶת־נְזִרֶֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It is a jubilee, [that] the year of the fiftieth year shall be for you. You shall not plant and you shall not reap [even] crops that grew on their own; and you shall not gather the [grapes of] its untended vineyard,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A jubilee shall that fiftieth year be to you: you shall not sow, neither reap that which grows of itself in it, nor gather in it the grapes of thy undressed vine.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is a Jubilee year; the fiftieth year shall be for you. You shall not sow, and you shall not reap its aftergrowth, and you shall not glean (the grapes of) its guarded vine. [What applies to shemitah applies to Yovel, so that there are two consecutive sanctities, the forty-ninth year — shemitah, and the fiftieth year — Yovel.]


###### Leviticus 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:12)


כִּ֚י יוֹבֵ֣ל הִ֔וא קֹ֖דֶשׁ תִּהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֑ם מִ֨ן־הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה תֹּאכְל֖וּ אֶת־תְּבוּאָתָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For it is a jubilee, it shall be holy to you. Whatever is in the field, you may eat of its produce.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For it is the jubilee; it shall be holy to you: you shall eat its increase out of the field.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For it is a Jubilee year; holy shall it be for you [It (the "holiness" of the produce of that year) is transferred to its monies (those given in exchange for it) as is the case with (the redemption of) hekdesh (sacred property). I might think that it (the "redeemed" produce itself) becomes chullin (non-consecrated); it is, therefore, written "shall it be"; it remains in its state of holiness.] From the field [i.e., on the basis of what transpires in the field], you may eat its produce [that is stored in the house (see 25:7)].


###### Leviticus 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:13)


בִּשְׁנַ֥ת הַיּוֹבֵ֖ל הַזֹּ֑את תָּשֻׁ֕בוּ אִ֖ישׁ אֶל־אֲחֻזָּתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On this jubilee year, each man shall return to his ancestral land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the year of this jubilee you shall return every man to his possession.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the year of this Jubilee shall you return, each man, to his holding [(to include a field sold by a man and redeemed by his son as returning to the father on Yovel)].


###### Leviticus 25:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:14)


וְכִֽי־תִמְכְּר֤וּ מִמְכָּר֙ לַעֲמִיתֶ֔ךָ א֥וֹ קָנֹ֖ה מִיַּ֣ד עֲמִיתֶ֑ךָ אַל־תּוֹנ֖וּ אִ֥ישׁ אֶת־אָחִֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If you sell anything to your neighbor, or purchase [something] from your neighbor, do not cheat one another.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if thou sell aught to thy neighbour, or buy aught of thy neighbour’s hand, you shall not defraud one another:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if you sell a selling to your fellow, or acquire from the hand of your fellow, you shall not wrong [(money wronging)], one man, his brother.


###### Leviticus 25:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:15)


בְּמִסְפַּ֤ר שָׁנִים֙ אַחַ֣ר הַיּוֹבֵ֔ל תִּקְנֶ֖ה מֵאֵ֣ת עֲמִיתֶ֑ךָ בְּמִסְפַּ֥ר שְׁנֵֽי־תְבוּאֹ֖ת יִמְכׇּר־לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
According to the number of years after the jubilee you shall purchase [land] from your neighbor; according to the number of produce years he shall sell it to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
according to the number of years after the jubilee thou shalt buy of thy neighbour, and according to the number of years of the fruits he shall sell to thee:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
According to the number of years after the Jubilee year shall you buy from your fellow [i.e., Set the price according to the number of years remaining to the Yovel, when the field will return to the owner — a higher price for more years remaining; a proportionately lower price for less]; according to the number of years of crops shall he sell to you. [(It must be sold for at least two years of crops (and not blight)].


###### Leviticus 25:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:16)


לְפִ֣י**׀** רֹ֣ב הַשָּׁנִ֗ים תַּרְבֶּה֙ מִקְנָת֔וֹ וּלְפִי֙ מְעֹ֣ט הַשָּׁנִ֔ים תַּמְעִ֖יט מִקְנָת֑וֹ כִּ֚י מִסְפַּ֣ר תְּבוּאֹ֔ת ה֥וּא מֹכֵ֖ר לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Based on the abundance of years, you shall increase its purchase price and based on the lack of years you shall decrease its purchase price, since it is the number of produce-seasons that he is selling to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
according to the multitude of years thou shalt increase its price, and according to the fewness of years thou shalt diminish the price of it: for what he sells thee is a number of years of produce.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
According to the numerousness of years shall you increase its price, and according to the fewness of years shall you diminish its price; for a number of crops (until the Yovel) he sells to you (and not the field itself.)


###### Leviticus 25:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:17)


וְלֹ֤א תוֹנוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ אֶת־עֲמִית֔וֹ וְיָרֵ֖אתָ מֵֽאֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not cheat one another, and you shall fear your God for I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not therefore defraud one another; but thou shalt fear thy God: for I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall not wrong [(verbal wronging)] one man, his fellow [by taunting him or giving him improper advice.] And you shall fear your G-d, [who knows your intentions]. For I am the L-rd your G-d.


###### Leviticus 25:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:18)


וַעֲשִׂיתֶם֙ אֶת־חֻקֹּתַ֔י וְאֶת־מִשְׁפָּטַ֥י תִּשְׁמְר֖וּ וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֣ם אֹתָ֑ם וִֽישַׁבְתֶּ֥ם עַל־הָאָ֖רֶץ לָבֶֽטַח׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall fulfill My statutes and keep My laws and fulfill them, and you will then live in the land in security.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall do my statutes, and keep my judgments, and do them; and you shall dwell in the land in safety.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall observe My statutes, and My judgments shall you keep and do them, and you shall dwell on the land secure. [For non-observance of shemitah results in exile (see 26:34). (The seventy years of Babylonian exile were for seventy shemitoth that were not observed.)]


###### Leviticus 25:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:19)


וְנָתְנָ֤ה הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ פִּרְיָ֔הּ וַאֲכַלְתֶּ֖ם לָשֹׂ֑בַע וִֽישַׁבְתֶּ֥ם לָבֶ֖טַח עָלֶֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The land will give forth its fruit and you will eat your fill, and you will live securely on it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the land shall yield her fruit, and you shall eat your fill, and dwell therein in safety.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the land will give its fruit, and you will eat to satiety [(the food will be "blessed" in your innards)], and you will dwell securely thereon [without fear of drought].


###### Leviticus 25:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:20)


וְכִ֣י תֹאמְר֔וּ מַה־נֹּאכַ֖ל בַּשָּׁנָ֣ה הַשְּׁבִיעִ֑ת הֵ֚ן לֹ֣א נִזְרָ֔ע וְלֹ֥א נֶאֱסֹ֖ף אֶת־תְּבוּאָתֵֽנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If you shall say (ask) What will we eat in the seventh year, for lo! we have not planted nor gathered our produce?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if you shall say, What shall we eat in the seventh year? behold, we shall not sow, nor gather in our increase:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if you say: What shall we eat in the seventh year if we cannot sow or gather [into the house] our produce [like wine and fruits and after-growth, which falls of itself (and is not "sown," see 5)],


###### Leviticus 25:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:21)


וְצִוִּ֤יתִי אֶת־בִּרְכָתִי֙ לָכֶ֔ם בַּשָּׁנָ֖ה הַשִּׁשִּׁ֑ית וְעָשָׂת֙ אֶת־הַתְּבוּאָ֔ה לִשְׁלֹ֖שׁ הַשָּׁנִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I shall command (direct) My blessing to you in the sixth year and it will produce [enough] for three years.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then I will command my blessing upon you in the sixth year, and it shall bring forth fruit for three years.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I shall command My blessing for you in the sixth year, and it shall produce the crop for three years: [for Nissan (of the sixth year until Rosh HaShanah), for the seventh year, and for the eighth year until Nissan, (for in the eighth year, they will sow in Cheshvan and harvest in Nissan)].


###### Leviticus 25:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:22)


וּזְרַעְתֶּ֗ם אֵ֚ת הַשָּׁנָ֣ה הַשְּׁמִינִ֔ת וַאֲכַלְתֶּ֖ם מִן־הַתְּבוּאָ֣ה יָשָׁ֑ן עַ֣ד**׀** הַשָּׁנָ֣ה הַתְּשִׁיעִ֗ת עַד־בּוֹא֙ תְּב֣וּאָתָ֔הּ תֹּאכְל֖וּ יָשָֽׁן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall plant in the eighth year but you will still be eating from the old produce until the ninth year; until the [new] produce arrives (ripens) you shall eat [from] the old.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall sow the eighth year, and eat yet of old fruit until the ninth year; until her fruits come in, you shall eat of the old store.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall sow in the eighth year, and you shall eat of the old (sixth year) crop. Until the [Sukkoth of the] ninth year, until its [the eighth year's] produce comes [into the house, (the eighth year crop being stored in field granaries all summer and being brought into the house only in Tishrei)], you shall eat of the old crop.


###### Leviticus 25:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:23)


וְהָאָ֗רֶץ לֹ֤א תִמָּכֵר֙ לִצְמִתֻ֔ת כִּי־לִ֖י הָאָ֑רֶץ כִּֽי־גֵרִ֧ים וְתוֹשָׁבִ֛ים אַתֶּ֖ם עִמָּדִֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The land shall not be sold in perpetuity, for the land is Mine, for, you are [merely as] foreigners and resident aliens before Me.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The land shall not be sold for ever: for the land is mine; for you are strangers and sojourners with me.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the land shall not be sold in perpetuity [(a negative commandment directed to the buyer, against non-return of the field in Yovel)], for Mine is the land, [and not yours (so that you need not feel "put out" in returning it)]; for strangers and sojourners are you with Me.


###### Leviticus 25:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:24)


וּבְכֹ֖ל אֶ֣רֶץ אֲחֻזַּתְכֶ֑ם גְּאֻלָּ֖ה תִּתְּנ֥וּ לָאָֽרֶץ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In all of your ancestral lands you shall grant redemption to the land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in all the land of your possession you shall grant a redemption for the land.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in all the land of your holding, redemption shall you give to the land. [(This introduces what follows, that one who sells his holding may redeem it after two years and the buyer cannot refuse him)].


###### Leviticus 25:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:25)


כִּֽי־יָמ֣וּךְ אָחִ֔יךָ וּמָכַ֖ר מֵאֲחֻזָּת֑וֹ וּבָ֤א גֹֽאֲלוֹ֙ הַקָּרֹ֣ב אֵלָ֔יו וְגָאַ֕ל אֵ֖ת מִמְכַּ֥ר אָחִֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If your brother becomes impoverished and sells some of his ancestral land, and a close relative comes and redeems that which his brother sold.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thy brother become poor, and has sold away some of his possession, then shall his near kinsman come to redeem it, and shall redeem that which his brother sold.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
