If your brother grows poor and sells of his holding [(but not all of his holding — he should leave a field for himself) (It is derived from here that one should not sell his field unless he is sorely pressed by poverty)], then his redeemer who is near to him shall come and he shall redeem what his brother has sold [and the buyer cannot refuse him].


###### Leviticus 25:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:26)


וְאִ֕ישׁ כִּ֛י לֹ֥א יִֽהְיֶה־לּ֖וֹ גֹּאֵ֑ל וְהִשִּׂ֣יגָה יָד֔וֹ וּמָצָ֖א כְּדֵ֥י גְאֻלָּתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[Or] if the man has no redeemer (close relative) but has acquired sufficient means and finds [it] enough to redeem it [himself],


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the man have none to redeem it, and himself be able to redeem it;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man, if there be for him no redeemer [who is able to redeem what he has sold], and his hand attains, and he finds what suffices for its redemption,


###### Leviticus 25:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:27)


וְחִשַּׁב֙ אֶת־שְׁנֵ֣י מִמְכָּר֔וֹ וְהֵשִׁיב֙ אֶת־הָ֣עֹדֵ֔ף לָאִ֖ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֣ר מָֽכַר־ל֑וֹ וְשָׁ֖ב לַאֲחֻזָּתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall calculate the number of years for which he sold the land and return the remainder (excess) to the man to whom he had sold it, and he shall return to his ancestral land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then let him count the years of the sale of it, and restore the overplus to the man to whom he sold it; that he may return to his possession.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then he shall reckon the years of its sale [i.e., the value of the yearly crops from the time of its sale], and he shall return the balance [the difference between the yearly crop values and the sales price] to the man to whom he sold it, and he shall return to his holding.


###### Leviticus 25:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:28)


וְאִ֨ם לֹֽא־מָצְאָ֜ה יָד֗וֹ דֵּי֮ הָשִׁ֣יב לוֹ֒ וְהָיָ֣ה מִמְכָּר֗וֹ בְּיַד֙ הַקֹּנֶ֣ה אֹת֔וֹ עַ֖ד שְׁנַ֣ת הַיּוֹבֵ֑ל וְיָצָא֙ בַּיֹּבֵ֔ל וְשָׁ֖ב לַאֲחֻזָּתֽוֹ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or if he does not have within his means enough to retrieve the land to himself, then that which he sold remains in the possession of the buyer until the jubilee year. It is then released by the jubilee and he [the seller] returns to his ancestral land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if his means do not suffice to regain it, then that which is sold shall remain in the hand of him who has bought it until the year of jubilee: and in the jubilee it shall go out, and he shall return to his possession.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if his hand does not find what suffices to restore it to himself [(— whence it is derived that he may not redeem it by halves)], then his selling shall remain in the hand of its buyer until the Jubilee year, [the Jubilee year "releasing" it immediately upon its advent], and it shall go out in the Jubilee year, and he shall return to his holding.


###### Leviticus 25:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:29)


וְאִ֗ישׁ כִּֽי־יִמְכֹּ֤ר בֵּית־מוֹשַׁב֙ עִ֣יר חוֹמָ֔ה וְהָיְתָה֙ גְּאֻלָּת֔וֹ עַד־תֹּ֖ם שְׁנַ֣ת מִמְכָּר֑וֹ יָמִ֖ים תִּהְיֶ֥ה גְאֻלָּתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a man shall sell a residential home [that is] within a walled city, the time allotted for its redemption shall be until the end of one year after he sold it. He has the days [of a full year] in which to redeem it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man sell a dwelling house in a walled city, then he may redeem it within a whole year after it is sold; within a full year may he redeem it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man, if he sells a house of dwelling in a city with a wall [from the time of Joshua the son of Nun], then its redemption shall be [only] until the end of the year of its sale [with no possibility of redemption thereafter]; [a year of] days shall be its redemption.


###### Leviticus 25:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:30)


וְאִ֣ם לֹֽא־יִגָּאֵ֗ל עַד־מְלֹ֣את לוֹ֮ שָׁנָ֣ה תְמִימָה֒ וְ֠קָ֠ם הַבַּ֨יִת אֲשֶׁר־בָּעִ֜יר אֲשֶׁר־[ל֣וֹ] (לא) חֹמָ֗ה לַצְּמִיתֻ֛ת לַקֹּנֶ֥ה אֹת֖וֹ לְדֹרֹתָ֑יו לֹ֥א יֵצֵ֖א בַּיֹּבֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he does not redeem it by the end of a full year, the house within the city that is surrounded by a wall shall then remain in perpetuity to its purchaser for his generations (descendants.) It is not released by the jubilee.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be not redeemed within the space of a full year, then the house that is in the walled city shall become the permanent property of him that bought it throughout his generations: it shall not go out in the jubilee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if it is not redeemed until there is filled for it a complete year, then the house which is in the city which had a wall [(in the time of Joshua, though it may not have one now)] shall stand in perpetuity to the one who bought it throughout his generations; it shall not go out in the Jubilee year [if the Jubilee year arrived in the midst of the year of sale.]


###### Leviticus 25:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:31)


וּבָתֵּ֣י הַחֲצֵרִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֵין־לָהֶ֤ם חֹמָה֙ סָבִ֔יב עַל־שְׂדֵ֥ה הָאָ֖רֶץ יֵחָשֵׁ֑ב גְּאֻלָּה֙ תִּהְיֶה־לּ֔וֹ וּבַיֹּבֵ֖ל יֵצֵֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The houses of open cities which are not surrounded by a wall shall be considered as a field of the land. It shall have a redemption and it is released by the jubilee.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the houses of the villages which have no wall round about them shall be counted as the fields of the country: they may be redeemed, and they shall go out in the jubilee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the houses of open cities, which have no wall roundabout, as the field of the land shall they be reckoned [i.e., they may be redeemed until Yovel, and (if not redeemed) Yovel releases them to their original owners.] Redemption shall there be for it, [immediately, if the seller so wishes], and in the Jubilee year shall it go out [gratis].


###### Leviticus 25:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:32)


וְעָרֵי֙ הַלְוִיִּ֔ם בָּתֵּ֖י עָרֵ֣י אֲחֻזָּתָ֑ם גְּאֻלַּ֥ת עוֹלָ֖ם תִּהְיֶ֥ה לַלְוִיִּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The cities of the Levites the houses in the cities of their ancestral land, the Levites shall have an eternal right of redemption.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the cities of the Levites, and the houses of the cities of their possession, may the Levites redeem at any time.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the cities of the Levites [the forty-eight cities which were given to them (Numbers 35:6-7)], the houses of the cities of their holding, a perpetual redemption shall there be for the Levites. [In the instance of a field, they may redeem immediately (and need not wait two years), and, in the instance of a house in a walled city they may redeem forever (and the house does not become irredeemable after a year)].


###### Leviticus 25:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:33)


וַאֲשֶׁ֤ר יִגְאַל֙ מִן־הַלְוִיִּ֔ם וְיָצָ֧א מִמְכַּר־בַּ֛יִת וְעִ֥יר אֲחֻזָּת֖וֹ בַּיֹּבֵ֑ל כִּ֣י בָתֵּ֞י עָרֵ֣י הַלְוִיִּ֗ם הִ֚וא אֲחֻזָּתָ֔ם בְּת֖וֹךְ בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If anyone redeems [a house or field] from the Levites, then the house or his ancestral city shall be released by the jubilee, for the houses of the Levitic cities are [considered] their ancestral lands among Bnei Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man purchase of the Levites, then the house that was sold, and the city of his possession, shall go out in the year of jubilee: for the houses of the cities of the Levites are their possession among the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man purchase [a house or a city] of the Levites, then the selling of the house or of the city of his holding shall go out in the Jubilee year, [and not be irredeemable like the walled-city houses of Israelites]. For the houses of the cities of the Levites are their holding in the midst of the children of Israel. [The Levites did not have fields and vineyards as inheritances; therefore, their houses and open lots are their holdings in place of fields and are redeemable like fields, so that their inheritance not depart from them.]


###### Leviticus 25:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:34)


וּֽשְׂדֵ֛ה מִגְרַ֥שׁ עָרֵיהֶ֖ם לֹ֣א יִמָּכֵ֑ר כִּֽי־אֲחֻזַּ֥ת עוֹלָ֛ם ה֖וּא לָהֶֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The field on the outskirts of their cities may not be sold for it is their perpetual ancestral land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the field of the pasture lands of their cities may not be sold; for it is their perpetual possession.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the field of the open place around their cities may not be sold [by the Temple treasurer, i.e., If a Levite dedicated it to the Temple, and did not redeem it, and the treasurer sold it, it does not revert to the Cohanim in the Jubilee year (as it does in the case of an Israelite (see 27:16-20), but the Levite may always redeem it], for it is a perpetual holding for them.


###### Leviticus 25:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:35)


וְכִֽי־יָמ֣וּךְ אָחִ֔יךָ וּמָ֥טָה יָד֖וֹ עִמָּ֑ךְ וְהֶֽחֱזַ֣קְתָּ בּ֔וֹ גֵּ֧ר וְתוֹשָׁ֛ב וָחַ֖י עִמָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If your brother becomes impoverished and his hand (assets) become shaky among you, you must support him, [so too] the proselyte and the sojourner, [so] that he shall live with you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if thy brother grow poor, and his means fail with thee; then thou shalt relieve him: though he be a stranger, or a sojourner; that he may live with thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if your brother grows poor and his hand falls with you, then you shall uphold him [from the time of the falling of his hand], [even if he be] proselyte or sojourner (toshav) [one who took it upon himself not to serve idolatry, but who eats neveiloth]; and he shall live with you.


###### Leviticus 25:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:36)


אַל־תִּקַּ֤ח מֵֽאִתּוֹ֙ נֶ֣שֶׁךְ וְתַרְבִּ֔ית וְיָרֵ֖אתָ מֵֽאֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ וְחֵ֥י אָחִ֖יךָ עִמָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not take from him interest or usury and you shall fear your God, and your brother shall live with you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take thou no usury of him, or increase: but fear thy God; that thy brother may live with thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not take from him interest (neshech) or increase (tarbith) [(Though neshech and tarbith are one and the same, they are stated as two to posit liability for transgression of two negative commandments)], and you shall fear your G-d, [who knows your thoughts, and do not resort to rationalizations for the taking of interest]. And your brother shall live with you.


###### Leviticus 25:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:37)


אֶ֨ת־כַּסְפְּךָ֔ לֹֽא־תִתֵּ֥ן ל֖וֹ בְּנֶ֑שֶׁךְ וּבְמַרְבִּ֖ית לֹא־תִתֵּ֥ן אׇכְלֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You must not give (lend) him your money with interest, and usuriously you must not advance him your food.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not give him thy money upon usury, nor lend him thy foodstuffs for increase.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your money you shall not give him on interest, and on increase you shall not give your food.


###### Leviticus 25:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:38)


אֲנִ֗י יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹ֣הֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־הוֹצֵ֥אתִי אֶתְכֶ֖ם מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם לָתֵ֤ת לָכֶם֙ אֶת־אֶ֣רֶץ כְּנַ֔עַן לִהְי֥וֹת לָכֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִֽים׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I am Adonoy your God who brought you out from the land of Egypt to give you the land of Canaan, to be a God for you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I am the Lord your God, who brought you out of the land of Miżrayim, to give you the land of Kena῾an, and to be your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I am the L-rd your G-d who took you out of the land of Egypt [(I discriminated between a first-born and a non first-born, and I can also discern and exact punishment from one who lends his money to a Jew on interest on the pretence that it is a gentile's)] to give you the land of Canaan [as reward for accepting My mitzvoth], to be a G-d to you [(For all who live in Eretz Yisrael are considered to have a G-d, and all who depart from it are as worshippers of idolatry.)]


###### Leviticus 25:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:39)


וְכִֽי־יָמ֥וּךְ אָחִ֛יךָ עִמָּ֖ךְ וְנִמְכַּר־לָ֑ךְ לֹא־תַעֲבֹ֥ד בּ֖וֹ עֲבֹ֥דַת עָֽבֶד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If your brother becomes impoverished and is sold to you, you must not work him like a slave.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if thy brother who dwells by thee be grown poor, and be sold to thee; thou shalt not compel him to serve as a bondservant:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if your brother grows poor with you, and he is sold to you, do not work with him the [demeaning] services of a bondsman, [like carrying your things after you to the bathhouse or putting on your shoes for you.]


###### Leviticus 25:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:40)


כְּשָׂכִ֥יר כְּתוֹשָׁ֖ב יִהְיֶ֣ה עִמָּ֑ךְ עַד־שְׁנַ֥ת הַיֹּבֵ֖ל יַעֲבֹ֥ד עִמָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Like a hired hand or resident [worker] shall he be with you He shall serve you until the jubilee year.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but as a hired servant, and as a sojourner, he shall be with thee, and shall serve thee until the year of jubilee:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As a hired man and as a sojourner shall he be with you [Employ him for the tilling of the soil and for craft work, like other hired laborers.] Until the Jubilee year shall he work with you [(If the Jubilee year intervenes before the expiration of his six years, it releases him.)]


###### Leviticus 25:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:41)


וְיָצָא֙ מֵֽעִמָּ֔ךְ ה֖וּא וּבָנָ֣יו עִמּ֑וֹ וְשָׁב֙ אֶל־מִשְׁפַּחְתּ֔וֹ וְאֶל־אֲחֻזַּ֥ת אֲבֹתָ֖יו יָשֽׁוּב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Then he and his children shall depart from you and return to his own family. He shall return to his ancestral estate.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and then shall he depart from thee, both he and his children with him, and shall return to his own family, and to the possession of his fathers shall he return.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall go out from you, he and his children with him [ — whence it is derived (since his children had not actually been sold with him) that his master is responsible for the feeding of his children]. And he shall return to his family, and to the holding of his fathers shall he return. [He is to be restored to his former dignity and not demeaned for his prior servitude.]


###### Leviticus 25:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:42)


כִּֽי־עֲבָדַ֣י הֵ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־הוֹצֵ֥אתִי אֹתָ֖ם מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם לֹ֥א יִמָּכְר֖וּ מִמְכֶּ֥רֶת עָֽבֶד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For they are My servants, whom I brought out from the land of Egypt, they shall not be sold [in the market] as slaves.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For they are my servants, whom I brought out of the land of Miżrayim: they shall not be sold as bondmen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For they are My servants [(My "deeds" came first)], whom I took out of the land of Egypt; they shall not be sold as slaves are sold [on the auction block, by proclamation: "Slave for sale!"]


###### Leviticus 25:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:43)


לֹא־תִרְדֶּ֥ה ב֖וֹ בְּפָ֑רֶךְ וְיָרֵ֖אתָ מֵאֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You must not rule over him to crush him and you shall fear your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not rule over him with rigour; but shalt fear thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not rule over him oppressively [(Do not impose on him services that you have no need of.)] And, [lest you say: No one knows that I am working him gratuitously —] you shall fear your G-d, [who knows your heart].


###### Leviticus 25:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:44)


וְעַבְדְּךָ֥ וַאֲמָתְךָ֖ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִהְיוּ־לָ֑ךְ מֵאֵ֣ת הַגּוֹיִ֗ם אֲשֶׁר֙ סְבִיבֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם מֵהֶ֥ם תִּקְנ֖וּ עֶ֥בֶד וְאָמָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Your slaves and maidservants that you shall possess from the nations that surround you, from them you may purchase slaves and maidservants.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Both thy bondmen, and thy bondmaids, whom thou shalt have, shall be of the nations that are round about you; of them may you buy bondmen and bondmaids.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, [if you say: If so, who will serve me? I am forbidden to rule over my (Hebrew) bondsman, and I am commanded to destroy the seven nations!] — your bondsman and your bondswoman whom you may have — from the nations who are roundabout you, [and not those within your land], from them shall you buy a bondsman and a bondswoman.


###### Leviticus 25:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:45)


וְ֠גַ֠ם מִבְּנֵ֨י הַתּוֹשָׁבִ֜ים הַגָּרִ֤ים עִמָּכֶם֙ מֵהֶ֣ם תִּקְנ֔וּ וּמִמִּשְׁפַּחְתָּם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עִמָּכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר הוֹלִ֖ידוּ בְּאַרְצְכֶ֑ם וְהָי֥וּ לָכֶ֖ם לַֽאֲחֻזָּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Also, from the children of the sojourners who reside with you from them you may purchase [slaves], and from their families that live among you that were born in your land. [All these] shall be your permanent possession.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover of the children of the strangers that do sojourn among you, of them shall you buy, and of their families that are with you, whom they have begotten in your land: and they shall be your possession.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And also of the sons of the sojourners, who sojourn among you, [who came from roundabout you to take wives in your land, and who begot children (the children having the status of the father, so that they are not subsumed in (Deuteronomy 20:16): "You shall not allow any soul to live")] — from them (the sojourners) may you buy, and their families that are with you, that they begot in your land, and they shall be a holding for you.


###### Leviticus 25:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:46)


וְהִתְנַחַלְתֶּ֨ם אֹתָ֜ם לִבְנֵיכֶ֤ם אַחֲרֵיכֶם֙ לָרֶ֣שֶׁת אֲחֻזָּ֔ה לְעֹלָ֖ם בָּהֶ֣ם תַּעֲבֹ֑דוּ וּבְאַ֨חֵיכֶ֤ם בְּנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אִ֣ישׁ בְּאָחִ֔יו לֹא־תִרְדֶּ֥ה ב֖וֹ בְּפָֽרֶךְ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall will them as inheritance to your children after you as hereditary property; you shall keep them in servitude permanently. However regarding your bretheren, Bnei Yisroel, man over his brother, you must not rule over him to crush him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall take them as an inheritance for your children after you, to inherit them for a possession; they shall be your bondmen for ever: but over your brethren the children of Yisra᾽el, you shall not rule one over another with rigour.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall hold them as an inheritance for your sons after you to inherit as a holding. Forever shall you have them serve you. And in your brothers, the children of Israel, one man over his brother [including a leader over his people and a king over his ministers], you shall not rule over him oppressively.


###### Leviticus 25:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:47)


וְכִ֣י תַשִּׂ֗יג יַ֣ד גֵּ֤ר וְתוֹשָׁב֙ עִמָּ֔ךְ וּמָ֥ךְ אָחִ֖יךָ עִמּ֑וֹ וְנִמְכַּ֗ר לְגֵ֤ר תּוֹשָׁב֙ עִמָּ֔ךְ א֥וֹ לְעֵ֖קֶר מִשְׁפַּ֥חַת גֵּֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a foreigner or resident acquires means while your brother becomes impoverished by him, and is sold to a resident foreigner who resides with you or to the offspring of a foreign family.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a sojourner or stranger grow rich by thee, and thy brother that dwells by him grows poor, and sell himself to the stranger or sojourner by thee, or to the offspring of a stranger’s family:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the hand of a stranger-sojourner attain (wealth) with you [(What caused him to attain wealth? His clinging "with you")], and your brother grow poor with him [(What caused him to become poor? His clinging "with him")], and he be sold to the stranger-sojourner with you, or to the uprooted [i.e., idolatry] of the family of a stranger [i.e., if he is sold to be a servant of idolatry — to hew wood, draw water, and the like],


###### Leviticus 25:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:48)


אַחֲרֵ֣י נִמְכַּ֔ר גְּאֻלָּ֖ה תִּהְיֶה־לּ֑וֹ אֶחָ֥ד מֵאֶחָ֖יו יִגְאָלֶֽנּוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
After he is sold redemption shall be his, one of his bretheren shall redeem him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
after he is sold he may be redeemed again; one of his brethren may redeem him:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After he is sold, redemption shall there be for him [immediately. (Do not allow him to become assimilated with them)]; one of his brothers shall redeem him.


###### Leviticus 25:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:49)


אוֹ־דֹד֞וֹ א֤וֹ בֶן־דֹּדוֹ֙ יִגְאָלֶ֔נּוּ אֽוֹ־מִשְּׁאֵ֧ר בְּשָׂר֛וֹ מִמִּשְׁפַּחְתּ֖וֹ יִגְאָלֶ֑נּוּ אֽוֹ־הִשִּׂ֥יגָה יָד֖וֹ וְנִגְאָֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Either his uncle or his uncles son (cousin) shall redeem him or the closest (other) family relative shall redeem him. If he obtains sufficient means of his own he may be redeemed [on his own.]


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
either his uncle, or his uncle’s son, may redeem him, or any that is near of kin to him of his family may redeem him; or if his means suffice, he may redeem himself.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or his uncle, or his uncle's son shall redeem him; or those of his near of kin, of his family, shall redeem him; or, if his hand attain, he shall be redeemed (by himself).


###### Leviticus 25:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:50)


וְחִשַּׁב֙ עִם־קֹנֵ֔הוּ מִשְּׁנַת֙ הִמָּ֣כְרוֹ ל֔וֹ עַ֖ד שְׁנַ֣ת הַיֹּבֵ֑ל וְהָיָ֞ה כֶּ֤סֶף מִמְכָּרוֹ֙ בְּמִסְפַּ֣ר שָׁנִ֔ים כִּימֵ֥י שָׂכִ֖יר יִהְיֶ֥ה עִמּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall reckon with the one who bought him. [According to the number of years] from the year which he was sold to him until the jubilee year. His purchase price shall be [proportional] to the number of years; at the rate of the days of a hired hand it shall be [considered as he worked] with him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall reckon with him who bought him from the year that he was sold to him to the year of jubilee: and the price of his sale shall be according to the number of years, according to the time of a hired servant shall it be with him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall reckon with his buyer [(Even if the gentile who bought him be subservient to you, make a "straight reckoning" with him (to avoid desecration of the Name)], from the year that he was sold to him until the Jubilee year, [when he would, in any event, be released]. And the money of his selling will be according to the number of years [that he served him]. According to the days of a hired man shall he be with him [i.e., the buyer receives the difference between the purchase price and the wages of a hired man for the period that he serves him (that wage being determined proportionately, per year, by the purchase price divided by the number of years until the Jubilee year)].


###### Leviticus 25:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:51)


אִם־ע֥וֹד רַבּ֖וֹת בַּשָּׁנִ֑ים לְפִיהֶן֙ יָשִׁ֣יב גְּאֻלָּת֔וֹ מִכֶּ֖סֶף מִקְנָתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If there are yet many years [until the jubilee] accordingly shall he return his redemption money from his purchase price.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If there are yet many years, according to them he shall restore the price of his redemption out of the money that he was bought for.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there are yet many in the years [until the Jubilee year], according to them shall he return his redemption (money) from the money of his acquisition.


###### Leviticus 25:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:52)


וְאִם־מְעַ֞ט נִשְׁאַ֧ר בַּשָּׁנִ֛ים עַד־שְׁנַ֥ת הַיֹּבֵ֖ל וְחִשַּׁב־ל֑וֹ כְּפִ֣י שָׁנָ֔יו יָשִׁ֖יב אֶת־גְּאֻלָּתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If there remain but a few years until the jubilee year, he shall make a [similar] calculation, based on the number of years he shall return his redemption money [to the purchaser.]


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if there remain but few years until the year of jubilee, then he shall count with him, and according to his years shall he restore the price of his redemption.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if few are left in the years until the Jubilee year, then he shall reckon with him; according to his years shall he return his redemption.


###### Leviticus 25:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:53)


כִּשְׂכִ֥יר שָׁנָ֛ה בְּשָׁנָ֖ה יִהְיֶ֣ה עִמּ֑וֹ לֹֽא־יִרְדֶּ֥נּֽוּ בְּפֶ֖רֶךְ לְעֵינֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
As a hired hand [working] on a yearly basis shall he be considered with him; he shall not rule over him to crush him before your eyes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As a yearly hired servant shall he be with him: and the other shall not rule with rigour over him in thy sight.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As a hired man, year by year, shall he be with him; he shall not rule over him oppressively before your eyes.


###### Leviticus 25:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:54)


וְאִם־לֹ֥א יִגָּאֵ֖ל בְּאֵ֑לֶּה וְיָצָא֙ בִּשְׁנַ֣ת הַיֹּבֵ֔ל ה֖וּא וּבָנָ֥יו עִמּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he is not redeemed through these means he then goes out in the jubilee year, he, along with his children.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if he be not redeemed in these years, then he shall go out in the year of jubilee, both he, and his children with him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if he is not redeemed with these [(He is redeemed only with "these," and not after six years)], then he shall go out in the Jubilee year, he and his children with him [see 25:41].


###### Leviticus 25:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/25:55)


כִּֽי־לִ֤י בְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ עֲבָדִ֔ים עֲבָדַ֣י הֵ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־הוֹצֵ֥אתִי אוֹתָ֖ם מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For, Bnei Yisroel are My servants. They are My servants whom I have brought out of the land of Egypt, I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For to me the children of Yisra᾽el are servants; they are my servants whom I brought forth out of the land of Miżrayim: I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For unto Me are the children of Israel servants. They are My servants, whom I took out of the land of Egypt. I am the L-rd your G-d.



## Chapter 26

###### Leviticus 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:1)


לֹֽא־תַעֲשׂ֨וּ לָכֶ֜ם אֱלִילִ֗ם וּפֶ֤סֶל וּמַצֵּבָה֙ לֹֽא־תָקִ֣ימוּ לָכֶ֔ם וְאֶ֣בֶן מַשְׂכִּ֗ית לֹ֤א תִתְּנוּ֙ בְּאַרְצְכֶ֔ם לְהִֽשְׁתַּחֲוֺ֖ת עָלֶ֑יהָ כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not make idols for yourselves and graven images and memorial stones you shall not raise up for youselves, and a prostration stone you shall not set in your land upon which to prostrate oneself, for I am Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall make no idols, nor shall you erect a carved idol, or a pillar, nor shall you install a figured stone in your land, to bow down upon it: for I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[The following is addressed to a Jew who sold himself to a gentile, and who might come to say: Since my master serves idols, I, likewise, may do so, etc.] You shall not make for yourselves idols, and a graven image or a pillar you shall not upraise for yourselves, and a covering stone [(a stone pavement covering the ground)] shall you not make in your land to prostrate (yourselves) upon it [even unto Heaven (this being permitted only in the Temple)], for I am the L-rd your G-d.


###### Leviticus 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:2)


אֶת־שַׁבְּתֹתַ֣י תִּשְׁמֹ֔רוּ וּמִקְדָּשִׁ֖י תִּירָ֑אוּ אֲנִ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall keep My Shabbosos and you shall fear (revere) My Sanctuary, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall keep my sabbaths, and reverence my sanctuary, I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My Sabbaths shall you keep, and My sanctuary shall you fear; I am the L-rd [trusted to reward].


###### Leviticus 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:3)


אִם־בְּחֻקֹּתַ֖י תֵּלֵ֑כוּ וְאֶת־מִצְוֺתַ֣י תִּשְׁמְר֔וּ וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֖ם אֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If you follow My statutes and keep My commandments and you will fulfill them,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If you walk in my statutes, and keep my commandments, and do them;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If in My statutes you walk [i.e., If in My Torah you toil], and My mitzvoth you keep [that is, if you toil in My Torah in order to keep My mitzvoth and to fulfill them], and you do them,


###### Leviticus 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:4)


וְנָתַתִּ֥י גִשְׁמֵיכֶ֖ם בְּעִתָּ֑ם וְנָתְנָ֤ה הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ יְבוּלָ֔הּ וְעֵ֥ץ הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה יִתֵּ֥ן פִּרְיֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will provide your rains in their proper time. The earth shall give forth its produce and the tree of the field shall give forth its produce.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then I will give you rain in due season, and the land shall yield its increase, and the trees of the field shall yield their fruit.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then I shall give your rains in their [proper] times [i.e., when people are not generally found out of doors], and the earth will yield its produce and the tree of the field [i.e., the normally barren tree] will yield its fruit.


###### Leviticus 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:5)


וְהִשִּׂ֨יג לָכֶ֥ם דַּ֙יִשׁ֙ אֶת־בָּצִ֔יר וּבָצִ֖יר יַשִּׂ֣יג אֶת־זָ֑רַע וַאֲכַלְתֶּ֤ם לַחְמְכֶם֙ לָשֹׂ֔בַע וִֽישַׁבְתֶּ֥ם לָבֶ֖טַח בְּאַרְצְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[Because of the abundant produce] threshing will overtake the grape harvest and the grape harvest overtake the [time to] plant, and you will eat your bread to satiation and you will live securely in your land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And your threshing shall reach to the vintage, and the vintage shall reach to the sowing time: and you shall eat your bread to the full, and dwell in your land safely.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your threshing [will be so abundant that it] will reach for you [until] the [time of the] vintage [harvest] and the vintage will reach the sowing, and you will eat your bread to satiety [i.e., you will eat but little, and the food will be "blessed" in your innards], and you will dwell secure in your land.


###### Leviticus 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:6)


וְנָתַתִּ֤י שָׁלוֹם֙ בָּאָ֔רֶץ וּשְׁכַבְתֶּ֖ם וְאֵ֣ין מַחֲרִ֑יד וְהִשְׁבַּתִּ֞י חַיָּ֤ה רָעָה֙ מִן־הָאָ֔רֶץ וְחֶ֖רֶב לֹא־תַעֲבֹ֥ר בְּאַרְצְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will grant peace in the land; you will lie down (sleep) without disturbance (fear). I will banish evil beasts from the land, and no sword shall pass through your land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will give peace in the land, and you shall lie down, and none shall make you afraid: and I will remove evil beasts out of the land, neither shall the sword go through your land.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [(lest you say: Food and drink without peace is nothing)], I will put peace, [which is over and against all,] in the land, and you will lie down and not tremble; and I will eliminate evil beasts from the land, and a sword shall not pass through your land, [even a sword of "peace" (i.e., an army passing through Eretz Yisrael to attack a different land)].


###### Leviticus 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:7)


וּרְדַפְתֶּ֖ם אֶת־אֹיְבֵיכֶ֑ם וְנָפְל֥וּ לִפְנֵיכֶ֖ם לֶחָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will pursue your enemies and they will fall before you by the sword.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall chase your enemies, and they shall fall before you by the sword.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will pursue your enemies, and they will fall before you by the sword, [one by the sword of the other].


###### Leviticus 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:8)


וְרָדְפ֨וּ מִכֶּ֤ם חֲמִשָּׁה֙ מֵאָ֔ה וּמֵאָ֥ה מִכֶּ֖ם רְבָבָ֣ה יִרְדֹּ֑פוּ וְנָפְל֧וּ אֹיְבֵיכֶ֛ם לִפְנֵיכֶ֖ם לֶחָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Five of you will pursue one hundred and one hundred of you will pursue ten thousand, and your enemies will fall before you by the sword.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And five of you shall chase a hundred, and a hundred of you shall put ten thousand to flight: and your enemies shall fall before you by the sword.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there will pursue of you [(even of the weakest)] — five, one hundred; and one hundred of you will pursue ten thousand [(and not "five hundred, ten thousand," as expected proportionately, there being no comparing a small number of Torah observers to a large one)], and your enemies will fall before you [an unnatural falling] by the sword.


###### Leviticus 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:9)


וּפָנִ֣יתִי אֲלֵיכֶ֔ם וְהִפְרֵיתִ֣י אֶתְכֶ֔ם וְהִרְבֵּיתִ֖י אֶתְכֶ֑ם וַהֲקִימֹתִ֥י אֶת־בְּרִיתִ֖י אִתְּכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will turn to you and make you fruitful and multiply you, and establish My covenant with you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I will turn myself to you, and make you fruitful, and multiply you, and establish my covenant with you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will turn [My regard exclusively] to you [to reward you], and I will make you fruitful [in childbearing], and I will give you an upright stature, and I will establish My covenant with you, [not like the first covenant which you broke, but a new covenant, which will not be broken.]


###### Leviticus 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:10)


וַאֲכַלְתֶּ֥ם יָשָׁ֖ן נוֹשָׁ֑ן וְיָשָׁ֕ן מִפְּנֵ֥י חָדָ֖שׁ תּוֹצִֽיאוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will eat the old [store] long kept and the old [store] you will have to clear out because of the new.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall eat old store, and remove the old because of the new.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will eat the old [crop] grown old [The produce will be so well preserved that a three-year-old crop will be better to eat than last year's], and you will remove the old [from the overflowing granaries] to make place for the new.


###### Leviticus 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:11)


וְנָתַתִּ֥י מִשְׁכָּנִ֖י בְּתוֹכְכֶ֑ם וְלֹֽא־תִגְעַ֥ל נַפְשִׁ֖י אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will set My Sanctuary among you and My spirit will not loathe (reject) you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will set my tabernacle among you: and my soul shall not abhor you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will place My mishkan [the Temple] in your midst, and My soul will not repel you.


###### Leviticus 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:12)


וְהִתְהַלַּכְתִּי֙ בְּת֣וֹכְכֶ֔ם וְהָיִ֥יתִי לָכֶ֖ם לֵֽאלֹהִ֑ים וְאַתֶּ֖ם תִּהְיוּ־לִ֥י לְעָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will walk among you and I will be a God for you, and you will be My people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will walk among you, and will be your God, and you shall be my people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will walk [in Gan Eden] in your midst [as one of you, and you will not be in terror of Me] and [lest you think you will not fear Me], I will be to you as a G-d, and you will be to Me as a people.


###### Leviticus 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:13)


אֲנִ֞י יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר הוֹצֵ֤אתִי אֶתְכֶם֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם מִֽהְיֹ֥ת לָהֶ֖ם עֲבָדִ֑ים וָאֶשְׁבֹּר֙ מֹטֹ֣ת עֻלְּכֶ֔ם וָאוֹלֵ֥ךְ אֶתְכֶ֖ם קֽוֹמְמִיּֽוּת׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I am Adonoy your God who brought you out of the land of Egypt from where you were slaves, and I broke the bars of your yoke and led you forth standing upright.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I am the Lord your God, who brought you out of the land of Miżrayim, that you should not be their bondmen; and I have broken the bars of your yoke, and made you walk upright.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I am the L-rd your G-d, who took you out of the land of Egypt [You may well believe Me capable of doing all these things, for I took you out of the land of Egypt with great wonders] that you not be slaves to them, and I broke the bars of your yoke, and I made you walk upright.


###### Leviticus 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:14)


וְאִם־לֹ֥א תִשְׁמְע֖וּ לִ֑י וְלֹ֣א תַעֲשׂ֔וּ אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַמִּצְוֺ֖ת הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And if you will not listen to Me and you will not fulfill all of these commandments.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if you will not hearken to me, and will not do all these commands:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Leviticus 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:15)


וְאִם־בְּחֻקֹּתַ֣י תִּמְאָ֔סוּ וְאִ֥ם אֶת־מִשְׁפָּטַ֖י תִּגְעַ֣ל נַפְשְׁכֶ֑ם לְבִלְתִּ֤י עֲשׂוֹת֙ אֶת־כׇּל־מִצְוֺתַ֔י לְהַפְרְכֶ֖ם אֶת־בְּרִיתִֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If you despise My statutes and your souls loathe My laws so as not to fulfill all My commandments, thereby breaking My convenant.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and if you shall despise my statutes, or if your soul abhor my judgments, so that you will not do all my commandments, but that you break my covenant:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if [as a result of not doing], you despise [the doers of] My statutes, and if [as a result] your souls repel [(the exemplifiers of)] My ordinances, [the sages], so that [as a result] there not be done [i.e., you prevent others from doing] all of My mitzvoth [i.e., As a result, you deny that they are My mitzvoth], to break My covenant [i.e., as a result, you deny Me! — in all, seven transgressions],


###### Leviticus 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:16)


אַף־אֲנִ֞י אֶֽעֱשֶׂה־זֹּ֣את לָכֶ֗ם וְהִפְקַדְתִּ֨י עֲלֵיכֶ֤ם בֶּֽהָלָה֙ אֶת־הַשַּׁחֶ֣פֶת וְאֶת־הַקַּדַּ֔חַת מְכַלּ֥וֹת עֵינַ֖יִם וּמְדִיבֹ֣ת נָ֑פֶשׁ וּזְרַעְתֶּ֤ם לָרִיק֙ זַרְעֲכֶ֔ם וַאֲכָלֻ֖הוּ אֹיְבֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Then I will do the same with you. I will impose terror upon you, [which causes] swelling and fever that consume the eyes and fill the soul with grief. You will plant [your seeds] in vain because your enemies will consume [your crops.]


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I also will do this to you; I will even appoint over you terror, consumption, and fever, that shall consume the eyes, and cause sorrow of heart: and you shall sow your seed in vain, for your enemies shall eat it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I, too, will do this to you, and I will command panic upon you — consumption, and burning fever, making the eyes languish [in vain hope of recovery] and grieving the soul [of your family in your death], and you will sow your seed in vain, [for it will not sprout], and, [if it does sprout], your foes will eat it.


###### Leviticus 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:17)


וְנָתַתִּ֤י פָנַי֙ בָּכֶ֔ם וְנִגַּפְתֶּ֖ם לִפְנֵ֣י אֹיְבֵיכֶ֑ם וְרָד֤וּ בָכֶם֙ שֹֽׂנְאֵיכֶ֔ם וְנַסְתֶּ֖ם וְאֵין־רֹדֵ֥ף אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will set my face against you and you will be defeated before your enemies; your foes will rule over you and you will flee [even] when no one is pursuing you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will set my face against you, and you shall be slain before your enemies: they that hate you shall reign over you; and you shall flee when none pursues you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will turn My regard against you, and you will be smitten before your foes, and your haters shall rule over you, and you will flee [in fear] when none pursue you, [being powerless (to threaten them)].


###### Leviticus 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:18)


וְאִ֨ם־עַד־אֵ֔לֶּה לֹ֥א תִשְׁמְע֖וּ לִ֑י וְיָסַפְתִּי֙ לְיַסְּרָ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֔ם שֶׁ֖בַע עַל־חַטֹּאתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If after these [catastrophes] you still do not listen to Me, then I will increase your punishment sevenfold, as your sins.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if you will not yet for all this hearken to me, then I will punish you seven times more for your sins.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if while these [are yet upon you] you do not hearken to Me, then I will chastise you even more — seven-fold, for your [seven] sins [see 15].


###### Leviticus 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:19)


וְשָׁבַרְתִּ֖י אֶת־גְּא֣וֹן עֻזְּכֶ֑ם וְנָתַתִּ֤י אֶת־שְׁמֵיכֶם֙ כַּבַּרְזֶ֔ל וְאֶֽת־אַרְצְכֶ֖ם כַּנְּחֻשָֽׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will break the pride of your power. I will make your heavens like iron and your land like copper.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will break the pride of your power; and I will make your skies like iron, and your earth like brass:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I shall break the pride of your strength [the Temple], and I shall make your skies as iron and your earth as brass.


###### Leviticus 26:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:20)


וְתַ֥ם לָרִ֖יק כֹּחֲכֶ֑ם וְלֹֽא־תִתֵּ֤ן אַרְצְכֶם֙ אֶת־יְבוּלָ֔הּ וְעֵ֣ץ הָאָ֔רֶץ לֹ֥א יִתֵּ֖ן פִּרְיֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Your strength will be exhausted in vain. Your land will not yield its produce and the tree[s] of the land will not give forth its produce.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and your strength shall be spent in vain: for your land shall not yield her increase, neither shall the trees of the land yield their fruit.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your strength [in plowing, sowing, etc.] shall be spent in vain, and your land will not yield [even] what is brought to it [at sowing time], and the tree of the land will not yield [(It will be smitten in point of the land, the land not fructifying at blooming time)] its fruit [(It will also be smitten in point of its fruit, any fruit that is produced, falling — two curses; all together, seven punishments)].


###### Leviticus 26:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:21)


וְאִם־תֵּֽלְכ֤וּ עִמִּי֙ קֶ֔רִי וְלֹ֥א תֹאב֖וּ לִשְׁמֹ֣עַֽ לִ֑י וְיָסַפְתִּ֤י עֲלֵיכֶם֙ מַכָּ֔ה שֶׁ֖בַע כְּחַטֹּאתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If you walk contrary to Me and have no desire to listen to Me, I will increase the blows upon you sevenfold, as your sins.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if you walk contrary to me, and will not hearken to me; I will bring seven times more plagues upon you according to your sins.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if you walk with Me in obduracy, and you will not wish to hearken to Me, then I shall smite you even more — seven, according to your [seven] sins [i.e., seven other punishments for your seven sins].


###### Leviticus 26:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:22)


וְהִשְׁלַחְתִּ֨י בָכֶ֜ם אֶת־חַיַּ֤ת הַשָּׂדֶה֙ וְשִׁכְּלָ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֔ם וְהִכְרִ֙יתָה֙ אֶת־בְּהֶמְתְּכֶ֔ם וְהִמְעִ֖יטָה אֶתְכֶ֑ם וְנָשַׁ֖מּוּ דַּרְכֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will send among you wild animals of the field and they will make you bereft of children, destroy your cattle, reduce your [population] and make your roads into wasteland.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will also send wild beasts among you, which shall rob you of your children, and destroy your cattle, and make you few in number; and your highways shall be desolate.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will incite against you the animal of the field [(two more punishments, viz. (Deuteronomy 32:24): "and the teeth of (domestic) beasts will I send against them," and (Ibid.): "with the venom of crawling things of the dust" (i.e., death by biting)], and it will bereave you (of your children), and it will cut off your cattle [outside (the city)] and it will reduce you [inside], and your roads [both large and small] will be desolate [ — all together, seven punishments].


###### Leviticus 26:23
