וְאִ֨ם־בְּאֵ֔לֶּה לֹ֥א תִוָּסְר֖וּ לִ֑י וַהֲלַכְתֶּ֥ם עִמִּ֖י קֶֽרִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If after these [catastrophes] you have not been disciplined [to listen] to Me, and walk contrary to Me,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if you will not be admonished by me as a result of these things, but will walk contrary to me;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if with these you will not be chastised [to return] to Me, and you will walk with Me in obduracy,


###### Leviticus 26:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:24)


וְהָלַכְתִּ֧י אַף־אֲנִ֛י עִמָּכֶ֖ם בְּקֶ֑רִי וְהִכֵּיתִ֤י אֶתְכֶם֙ גַּם־אָ֔נִי שֶׁ֖בַע עַל־חַטֹּאתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[Then] I, too, will walk contrary to you; and I shall also smite you seven fold, for your sins.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then will I also walk contrary to you, and will punish you yet seven times for your sins.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then I, too, will walk with you in obduracy, and I, too, will smite you seven, for your [seven] sins.


###### Leviticus 26:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:25)


וְהֵבֵאתִ֨י עֲלֵיכֶ֜ם חֶ֗רֶב נֹקֶ֙מֶת֙ נְקַם־בְּרִ֔ית וְנֶאֱסַפְתֶּ֖ם אֶל־עָרֵיכֶ֑ם וְשִׁלַּ֤חְתִּי דֶ֙בֶר֙ בְּת֣וֹכְכֶ֔ם וְנִתַּתֶּ֖ם בְּיַד־אוֹיֵֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will bring an avenging sword upon you avenging the covenant You will be huddled [in your cities]. I will send pestilence among you and you will be given into the hand of the enemy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will bring a sword upon you, that shall avenge my covenant: and when you are gathered together within your cities, I will send the pestilence among you; and you shall be delivered into the hand of the enemy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will bring upon you a sword [i.e., an army], avenging the vengeance of [i.e., foretold in] the Covenant [the Torah], and you will be gathered together [because of the siege] into your cities, and I will send pestilence in your midst, and, [as a result because they were not permitted to leave the dead in Jerusalem overnight and were constrained to take them outside for burial)], you will be delivered into the hand of the foe.


###### Leviticus 26:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:26)


בְּשִׁבְרִ֣י לָכֶם֮ מַטֵּה־לֶ֒חֶם֒ וְ֠אָפ֠וּ עֶ֣שֶׂר נָשִׁ֤ים לַחְמְכֶם֙ בְּתַנּ֣וּר אֶחָ֔ד וְהֵשִׁ֥יבוּ לַחְמְכֶ֖ם בַּמִּשְׁקָ֑ל וַאֲכַלְתֶּ֖ם וְלֹ֥א תִשְׂבָּֽעוּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When I break the staff of your bread, ten women will bake your bread in one oven and they will return your bread by weight. You will eat but you will not be satiated.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when I have broken the staff of your bread, ten women shall bake your bread in one oven, and they shall return you your bread by weight: and you shall eat, and not be satisfied.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When I break your staff of bread [i.e., When I bring famine upon you], ten women shall bake your bread in one oven [for lack of wood], and they shall [sit and] dole out your bread by weight [(for the wheat will be rotten and the loaf will crumble)]. And you will eat and not be sated, [the bread being "cursed" in your intestines — all together, seven punishments].


###### Leviticus 26:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:27)


וְאִ֨ם־בְּזֹ֔את לֹ֥א תִשְׁמְע֖וּ לִ֑י וַהֲלַכְתֶּ֥ם עִמִּ֖י בְּקֶֽרִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If [in spite] of this you [still] will not listen to Me, and you will walk contrary to Me,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if you will not for all this hearken to me, but walk contrary to me;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if with this you will not hearken to Me, but walk with Me in obduracy,


###### Leviticus 26:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:28)


וְהָלַכְתִּ֥י עִמָּכֶ֖ם בַּחֲמַת־קֶ֑רִי וְיִסַּרְתִּ֤י אֶתְכֶם֙ אַף־אָ֔נִי שֶׁ֖בַע עַל־חַטֹּאתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will walk contrary to you in fury, and indeed I too shall punish you sevenfold, for your sins.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then I will walk contrary to you also in fury; and will chastise you seven times for your sins.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then I will walk with you in the fury of obduracy, and I, too, will chastise you, seven, for your sins.


###### Leviticus 26:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:29)


וַאֲכַלְתֶּ֖ם בְּשַׂ֣ר בְּנֵיכֶ֑ם וּבְשַׂ֥ר בְּנֹתֵיכֶ֖ם תֹּאכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will eat the flesh of your sons and you will eat the flesh of your daughters.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall eat the flesh of your sons, and the flesh of your daughters shall you eat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will eat the flesh of your sons, and the flesh of your daughters will you eat.


###### Leviticus 26:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:30)


וְהִשְׁמַדְתִּ֞י אֶת־בָּמֹֽתֵיכֶ֗ם וְהִכְרַתִּי֙ אֶת־חַמָּ֣נֵיכֶ֔ם וְנָֽתַתִּי֙ אֶת־פִּגְרֵיכֶ֔ם עַל־פִּגְרֵ֖י גִּלּוּלֵיכֶ֑ם וְגָעֲלָ֥ה נַפְשִׁ֖י אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will destroy your high places and I will smash your sun images. I will set your corpses upon the carcasses (remains) of your idols, and My soul will loathe you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will destroy your high places, and cut down your images, and cast your carcasses upon the carcasses of your idols, and my soul shall abhor you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will destroy your high places [your towers and castles], and I will cut down your [idolatrous] sun pillars [(which they set up on roofs)], and I will cast your carcasses upon the carcasses of your idols [(They would take their idols from their bosoms and kiss them, their hunger-swollen bellies would burst, and they would fall upon them)], and My soul will repel you.


###### Leviticus 26:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:31)


וְנָתַתִּ֤י אֶת־עָֽרֵיכֶם֙ חׇרְבָּ֔ה וַהֲשִׁמּוֹתִ֖י אֶת־מִקְדְּשֵׁיכֶ֑ם וְלֹ֣א אָרִ֔יחַ בְּרֵ֖יחַ נִיחֹֽחֲכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will turn your cities into ruins and bring your Sanctuaries into desolation. I will no longer scent (accept favorably) the fragrance of your offerings.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will make your cities waste, and bring your sanctuaries to desolation, and I will not smell the savour of your sweet odours.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will make your cities waste [of passersby], and I will make desolate your sanctified ones [i.e., the (festival) pilgrims, who used to sanctify themselves to ascend there], and I will not smell your sweet savors [ — all together, seven punishments].


###### Leviticus 26:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:32)


וַהֲשִׁמֹּתִ֥י אֲנִ֖י אֶת־הָאָ֑רֶץ וְשָֽׁמְמ֤וּ עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ אֹֽיְבֵיכֶ֔ם הַיֹּשְׁבִ֖ים בָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will make the land [so] desolate that your enemies who live on it will be astonished.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will bring the land into desolation: and your enemies who dwell in it shall be astonished at it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will make desolate the land, and your foes who dwell therein will be awe-struck thereby [(a "goodly provision" for Israel — its foes will find no satisfaction in the land, it being desolate of its inhabitants)].


###### Leviticus 26:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:33)


וְאֶתְכֶם֙ אֱזָרֶ֣ה בַגּוֹיִ֔ם וַהֲרִיקֹתִ֥י אַחֲרֵיכֶ֖ם חָ֑רֶב וְהָיְתָ֤ה אַרְצְכֶם֙ שְׁמָמָ֔ה וְעָרֵיכֶ֖ם יִהְי֥וּ חׇרְבָּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will scatter you among the nations and unsheath the sword after you. Your land will be desolate and your cities will be in ruins.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will scatter you among the heathen, and will draw out a sword after you: and your land shall be desolate, and your cities waste.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you, will I scatter among the nations [so that you lack even the consolation of togetherness], and I will draw out the sword after you, and your land will be desolate [(You will not quickly return there)], and [(as a result)] your cities will be waste [(You will perceive them as "ruined," not expecting to return there)].


###### Leviticus 26:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:34)


אָז֩ תִּרְצֶ֨ה הָאָ֜רֶץ אֶת־שַׁבְּתֹתֶ֗יהָ כֹּ֚ל יְמֵ֣י הׇשַּׁמָּ֔הֿ וְאַתֶּ֖ם בְּאֶ֣רֶץ אֹיְבֵיכֶ֑ם אָ֚ז תִּשְׁבַּ֣ת הָאָ֔רֶץ וְהִרְצָ֖ת אֶת־שַׁבְּתֹתֶֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The land will then be appeased for its Shabbosos [during] all the days of its desolation while you are in the land of your enemies. Then the land will rest and be appeased for its Shabbosos.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then shall the land enjoy her sabbaths, as long as it lies desolate, and you are in your enemies’ land; then shall the land rest, and enjoy her sabbaths.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then shall the land requite its Sabbaths [It will appease the wrath of the King over its unobserved shemitoth (sabbatical and Jubilee years)], all the days of its desolation — and you in the land of your foes! [see 25:18]. Then shall the land rest and it shall requite its Sabbaths.


###### Leviticus 26:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:35)


כׇּל־יְמֵ֥י הׇשַּׁמָּ֖הֿ תִּשְׁבֹּ֑ת אֵ֣ת אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹֽא־שָׁבְתָ֛ה בְּשַׁבְּתֹתֵיכֶ֖ם בְּשִׁבְתְּכֶ֥ם עָלֶֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All the days that it lies desolate it shall have its rest—the rest it did not have on your Shabbosos when you were living upon it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As long as it lies desolate it shall rest; because it did not rest in your sabbaths, when you dwelt upon it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the days of its desolation it shall rest — what it did not rest in your [seventy] sabbatical years when you dwelt upon it [(in sin) four hundred and thirty years].


###### Leviticus 26:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:36)


וְהַנִּשְׁאָרִ֣ים בָּכֶ֔ם וְהֵבֵ֤אתִי מֹ֙רֶךְ֙ בִּלְבָבָ֔ם בְּאַרְצֹ֖ת אֹיְבֵיהֶ֑ם וְרָדַ֣ף אֹתָ֗ם ק֚וֹל עָלֶ֣ה נִדָּ֔ף וְנָס֧וּ מְנֻֽסַת־חֶ֛רֶב וְנָפְל֖וּ וְאֵ֥ין רֹדֵֽף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
To those who survive among you, I will send timidity into their hearts [as they live] in the lands of their enemies. The sound of a blown leaf will put them to flight and they will flee as though fleeing from a sword; and they will fall with no one chasing them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And upon those who are left alive of you I will send a faintness into their hearts in the lands of their enemies; and the sound of a shaken leaf shall chase them; and they shall flee, as fleeing from a sword; and they shall fall when none pursues.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And those who are left of you — I will bring terror into their hearts in the lands of their foes, and they will be chased by the sound of a driven leaf, and they will flee as one flees the sword, and they will fall though none pursue.


###### Leviticus 26:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:37)


וְכָשְׁל֧וּ אִישׁ־בְּאָחִ֛יו כְּמִפְּנֵי־חֶ֖רֶב וְרֹדֵ֣ף אָ֑יִן וְלֹא־תִֽהְיֶ֤ה לָכֶם֙ תְּקוּמָ֔ה לִפְנֵ֖י אֹֽיְבֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They will stumble over one other as though before a sword when there is no pursuer, and you will have no power to stand upright before your enemies.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they shall fall one upon another, as it were before a sword, when none pursues: and you shall have no power to stand before your enemies.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they will stumble [in their panic] one man against the other, as if [they were fleeing] before the sword, though none pursue; and there will be no rising for you before your foes.


###### Leviticus 26:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:38)


וַאֲבַדְתֶּ֖ם בַּגּוֹיִ֑ם וְאָכְלָ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֔ם אֶ֖רֶץ אֹיְבֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will be lost among the nations and the land of your enemies will consume you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall perish among the heathen, and the land of your enemies shall eat you up.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will go lost [from each other by being scattered] among the nations, and the land of your foes will devour you [(The allusion is to those who die in exile)].


###### Leviticus 26:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:39)


וְהַנִּשְׁאָרִ֣ים בָּכֶ֗ם יִמַּ֙קּוּ֙ בַּֽעֲוֺנָ֔ם בְּאַרְצֹ֖ת אֹיְבֵיכֶ֑ם וְאַ֛ף בַּעֲוֺנֹ֥ת אֲבֹתָ֖ם אִתָּ֥ם יִמָּֽקּוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Those of you who survive will rot away in their iniquity in the land of your enemies, and also due to the iniquities of their ancestors which are [still] with them, [they] will they rot away.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they that are left of you shall pine away in their iniquity in your enemies’ lands; and also in the iniquities of their fathers shall they pine away with them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And those that are left of you will melt away in their sins in the land of their enemies; and also with the sins of their fathers with them [i.e., which they continue to hold onto] will they melt away.


###### Leviticus 26:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:40)


וְהִתְוַדּ֤וּ אֶת־עֲוֺנָם֙ וְאֶת־עֲוֺ֣ן אֲבֹתָ֔ם בְּמַעֲלָ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר מָֽעֲלוּ־בִ֑י וְאַ֕ף אֲשֶׁר־הָֽלְכ֥וּ עִמִּ֖י בְּקֶֽרִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They will [then] confess their iniquities and the iniquities of their ancestors in the unfaithfulness with which they were unfaithful to Me, and also for walking contrary to Me.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they shall confess their iniquity, and the iniquity of their fathers, with their trespass which they trespassed against me, and that also they have walked contrary to me;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they will confess their sin and the sin of their fathers, their faithlessness wherein they betrayed Me, and also their having walked with Me in obduracy [see 21]


###### Leviticus 26:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:41)


אַף־אֲנִ֗י אֵלֵ֤ךְ עִמָּם֙ בְּקֶ֔רִי וְהֵבֵאתִ֣י אֹתָ֔ם בְּאֶ֖רֶץ אֹיְבֵיהֶ֑ם אוֹ־אָ֣ז יִכָּנַ֗ע לְבָבָם֙ הֶֽעָרֵ֔ל וְאָ֖ז יִרְצ֥וּ אֶת־עֲוֺנָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I, too, will go contrary to them and bring them into the land of their enemies. Or perhaps then will be humbled their uncircumcised (unyielding) heart and then their iniquities will be appeased (forgiven).


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that I also walked contrary to them, and brought them into the land of their enemies; then only will their uncircumcised hearts be humbled, and then they will make amends for their sin.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I, too, will walk with them in obduracy, and I [Myself] will bring them into the land of their foes. [This is providential for Israel, viz.: I shall not allow them to become assimilated, though they see no other recourse, but I will raise up My prophets and bring them back under the wings of the Shechinah.] If then their uncircumcised heart be humbled, and then they requite their sin [i.e., if they atone for their sin through their afflictions],


###### Leviticus 26:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:42)


וְזָכַרְתִּ֖י אֶת־בְּרִיתִ֣י יַעֲק֑וֹב וְאַף֩ אֶת־בְּרִיתִ֨י יִצְחָ֜ק וְאַ֨ף אֶת־בְּרִיתִ֧י אַבְרָהָ֛ם אֶזְכֹּ֖ר וְהָאָ֥רֶץ אֶזְכֹּֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will [then] remember My covenant with Yaakov and also My covenant with Yitzchok and also My covenant with Avrohom, I will remember and I will remember the land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then will I remember my covenant with Ya῾aqov, and also my covenant with Yiżĥaq, and also my covenant with Avraham will I remember; and I will remember the land.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then I will remember My covenant with Jacob, and also My covenant with Isaac, and also My covenant with Abraham will I remember [(If Jacob, the "smallest" of the patriarchs is not worthy enough (to atone for them) then Isaac is with him, etc. ("remember" is not mentioned in respect to Isaac, for his ashes on the altar (of the akeidah) are always present to His sight)], and the land will I remember.


###### Leviticus 26:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:43)


וְהָאָ֩רֶץ֩ תֵּעָזֵ֨ב מֵהֶ֜ם וְתִ֣רֶץ אֶת־שַׁבְּתֹתֶ֗יהָ בׇּהְשַׁמָּהֿ֙ מֵהֶ֔ם וְהֵ֖ם יִרְצ֣וּ אֶת־עֲוֺנָ֑ם יַ֣עַן וּבְיַ֔עַן בְּמִשְׁפָּטַ֣י מָאָ֔סוּ וְאֶת־חֻקֹּתַ֖י גָּעֲלָ֥ה נַפְשָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The land, being bereft of them, will be appeased for its Shabbosos, during the time of its desolation from them and their iniquities will then be appeased (forgiven) since what certainly caused [this] is that they despised My laws and their soul loathed (rejected) My statutes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The land also shall be forsaken by them, and shall enjoy her sabbaths, while she lies desolate without them: and they shall make amends for their iniquity: because, even because they despised my judgments, and because their soul abhorred my statutes.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the land will be abandoned of them, and it will requite its Sabbaths in being desolate of them, and they will requite their sins — in retribution and in retribution — [because] they rejected My ordinances and because their souls repelled My statutes.


###### Leviticus 26:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:44)


וְאַף־גַּם־זֹ֠את בִּֽהְיוֹתָ֞ם בְּאֶ֣רֶץ אֹֽיְבֵיהֶ֗ם לֹֽא־מְאַסְתִּ֤ים וְלֹֽא־גְעַלְתִּים֙ לְכַלֹּתָ֔ם לְהָפֵ֥ר בְּרִיתִ֖י אִתָּ֑ם כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All this not withstanding, even when they are in the land of their enemies I will not despise them and I will not loathe them to annihilate them, to break My covenant with them, for I am Adonoy, their God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And yet for all that, when they are in the land of their enemies, I will not cast them away, nor will I abhor them, to destroy them utterly, and to break my covenant with them: for I am the Lord their God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And notwithstanding all this [i.e., that I shall bring this retribution upon them], while they are in the land of their foes, I will not reject them and I will not repel them to destroy them, to break My covenant with them, for I am the L-rd their G-d.


###### Leviticus 26:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:45)


וְזָכַרְתִּ֥י לָהֶ֖ם בְּרִ֣ית רִאשֹׁנִ֑ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר הוֹצֵֽאתִי־אֹתָם֩ מֵאֶ֨רֶץ מִצְרַ֜יִם לְעֵינֵ֣י הַגּוֹיִ֗ם לִהְי֥וֹת לָהֶ֛ם לֵאלֹהִ֖ים אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I shall remember for them the covenant of their ancestors whom I brought out of the land of Egypt in sight of the nations to be a God for them, I am Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But I will for their sakes remember the covenant of their ancestors, whom I brought out of the land of Miżrayim in the sight of the nations, that I might be their God: I am the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will remember for them the covenant of the first ones [the tribes], whom I took out of the land of Egypt before the eyes of the nations to be a G-d unto them; I am the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 26:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/26:46)


אֵ֠לֶּה הַֽחֻקִּ֣ים וְהַמִּשְׁפָּטִים֮ וְהַתּוֹרֹת֒ אֲשֶׁר֙ נָתַ֣ן יְהֹוָ֔ה בֵּינ֕וֹ וּבֵ֖ין בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל בְּהַ֥ר סִינַ֖י בְּיַד־מֹשֶֽׁה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are the statutes, the laws and the teachings that Adonoy set between Himself and between Bnei Yisroel, on Mount Sinai, through the hand of Moshe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These are the statutes and the judgments and the Torot, which the Lord made between him and the children of Yisra᾽el in Mount Sinay by the hand of Moshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are the statutes and the ordinances and the Toroth [the written and the oral laws (both having been given to Moses at Sinai)], which the L-rd gave between Himself and the children of Israel on Mount Sinai by the hand of Moses.



## Chapter 27

###### Leviticus 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:1)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־מֹשֶׁ֥ה לֵּאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Moshe, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Moses, saying:


###### Leviticus 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:2)


דַּבֵּ֞ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵהֶ֔ם אִ֕ישׁ כִּ֥י יַפְלִ֖א נֶ֑דֶר בְּעֶרְכְּךָ֥ נְפָשֹׁ֖ת לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them; when a person [orally] expresses a vow pledging valuation of souls, to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak to the children of Yisra᾽el, and say to them, If a man make a singular vow, to give to the Lord the estimated value of persons,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Speak to the children of Israel and say to them: A man, if he utter a vow in valuation of lives to the L-rd [i.e., If he says: "I vow to give (to the Temple) the valuation of this and this organ" (on which life depends)],


###### Leviticus 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:3)


וְהָיָ֤ה עֶרְכְּךָ֙ הַזָּכָ֔ר מִבֶּן֙ עֶשְׂרִ֣ים שָׁנָ֔ה וְעַ֖ד בֶּן־שִׁשִּׁ֣ים שָׁנָ֑ה וְהָיָ֣ה עֶרְכְּךָ֗ חֲמִשִּׁ֛ים שֶׁ֥קֶל כֶּ֖סֶף בְּשֶׁ֥קֶל הַקֹּֽדֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The valuation for a male shall be, from twenty years of age to sixty years of age your valuation shall be fifty holy silver *shekalim*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then the estimation shall be of the male from twenty years old even to sixty years old, the estimation shall be fifty shekels of silver, according to the shekel of the sanctuary.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the (fixed) valuation shall be: For the male, from twenty to sixty years, the valuation shall be fifty shekels of silver in the shekel of the sanctuary.


###### Leviticus 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:4)


וְאִם־נְקֵבָ֖ה הִ֑וא וְהָיָ֥ה עֶרְכְּךָ֖ שְׁלֹשִׁ֥ים שָֽׁקֶל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If it be a female, the valuation shall be thirty *shekalim*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be a female, then the estimation shall be thirty shekels.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if she is female, the valuation shall be thirty shekels.


###### Leviticus 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:5)


וְאִ֨ם מִבֶּן־חָמֵ֜שׁ שָׁנִ֗ים וְעַד֙ בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֣ים שָׁנָ֔ה וְהָיָ֧ה עֶרְכְּךָ֛ הַזָּכָ֖ר עֶשְׂרִ֣ים שְׁקָלִ֑ים וְלַנְּקֵבָ֖ה עֲשֶׂ֥רֶת שְׁקָלִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If from five years of age to twenty years of age the valuation for a male shall be twenty *shekalim*, and for a female, ten *shekalim*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be from five years old to twenty years old, then the estimation shall be of the male twenty shekels, and for the female ten shekels.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if from five years [(i.e., If an adult said: I vow to give the valuation of this five-year-old)] until twenty years, then the valuation shall be, for a male, twenty shekels, and for a female, ten shekels.


###### Leviticus 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:6)


וְאִ֣ם מִבֶּן־חֹ֗דֶשׁ וְעַד֙ בֶּן־חָמֵ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֔ים וְהָיָ֤ה עֶרְכְּךָ֙ הַזָּכָ֔ר חֲמִשָּׁ֥ה שְׁקָלִ֖ים כָּ֑סֶף וְלַנְּקֵבָ֣ה עֶרְכְּךָ֔ שְׁלֹ֥שֶׁת שְׁקָלִ֖ים כָּֽסֶף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If from one month of age to five years of age the valuation of a male shall be five silver *shekalim* and the valuation for a female is three silver *shekalim*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be from a month old to five years old, then the estimation shall be of the male five shekels of silver, and for the female the estimation shall be three shekels of silver.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if from one month until five years, then the valuation shall be: for a male, five shekels of silver; and for a female, the valuation, three shekels of silver.


###### Leviticus 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:7)


וְ֠אִ֠ם מִבֶּן־שִׁשִּׁ֨ים שָׁנָ֤ה וָמַ֙עְלָה֙ אִם־זָכָ֔ר וְהָיָ֣ה עֶרְכְּךָ֔ חֲמִשָּׁ֥ה עָשָׂ֖ר שָׁ֑קֶל וְלַנְּקֵבָ֖ה עֲשָׂרָ֥ה שְׁקָלִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If from sixty and above, if he is a male the valuation shall be fifteen *shekalim* and for a female, ten *shekalim*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be from sixty years old and above; if it be a male, then the estimation shall be fifteen shekels, and for the female ten shekels.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if from sixty years and above: If a male, then the valuation shall be fifteen shekels; and if a female, ten shekels.


###### Leviticus 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:8)


וְאִם־מָ֥ךְ הוּא֙ מֵֽעֶרְכֶּ֔ךָ וְהֶֽעֱמִידוֹ֙ לִפְנֵ֣י הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְהֶעֱרִ֥יךְ אֹת֖וֹ הַכֹּהֵ֑ן עַל־פִּ֗י אֲשֶׁ֤ר תַּשִּׂיג֙ יַ֣ד הַנֹּדֵ֔ר יַעֲרִיכֶ֖נּוּ הַכֹּהֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he is [too] poor to pay the valuation he shall set him[self] [=he shall be presented] before the *kohein,* and the *kohein* will determine his valuation. According to the means of the one who vowed the *kohein* will determine his valuation.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if he be too poor for the estimated value, then he shall present himself before the priest, and the priest shall value him; according to the ability of him that vowed shall the priest value him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if he (the vower) be too poor for the [fixed] valuation, then he [the one to be valuated] shall be stood before the Cohein, and the Cohein shall valuate him. According to the means of the vower shall the Cohein valuate him [and leave enough for subsistence: bed, mattress, pillows, tools, and the like].


###### Leviticus 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:9)


וְאִ֨ם־בְּהֵמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר יַקְרִ֧יבוּ מִמֶּ֛נָּה קׇרְבָּ֖ן לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה כֹּל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִתֵּ֥ן מִמֶּ֛נּוּ לַיהֹוָ֖ה יִֽהְיֶה־קֹּֽדֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If [it is] an animal which can be brought as an offering to Adonoy, any of it that he will give to Adonoy shall become sacred.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be a beast, of which men bring an offering to the Lord, all that any man gives of such to the Lord shall be holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if it be a beast, of which men present an offering to the L-rd, all that he gives of it to the L-rd shall be holy. [If he said: "The foot of this animal for a burnt-offering," he sells it to be offered as a burnt-offering, and the money he receives, excluding the value of that limb, is chullin (non-consecrated)].


###### Leviticus 27:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:10)


לֹ֣א יַחֲלִיפֶ֗נּוּ וְלֹֽא־יָמִ֥יר אֹת֛וֹ ט֥וֹב בְּרָ֖ע אוֹ־רַ֣ע בְּט֑וֹב וְאִם־הָמֵ֨ר יָמִ֤יר בְּהֵמָה֙ בִּבְהֵמָ֔ה וְהָֽיָה־ה֥וּא וּתְמוּרָת֖וֹ יִֽהְיֶה־קֹּֽדֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall not exchange it [with anothers animal] and he shall not change it [with his own animal] whether it be a good one for a worse one [or] a bad one for a better one. If he did exchange one animal for another animal, then both it and its replacement shall be sacred.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall not alter it, nor change it, a good for a bad, or a bad for a good: and if he shall at all change beast for beast, then it and its substitute shall be holy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He shall not exchange it and he shall not substitute for it, good [an unblemished animal] for bad [a blemished animal] or bad for good [(and certainly not good for good or bad for bad)]. And if he substitutes beast for beast, then it and its substitute shall be holy.


###### Leviticus 27:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:11)


וְאִם֙ כׇּל־בְּהֵמָ֣ה טְמֵאָ֔ה אֲ֠שֶׁ֠ר לֹא־יַקְרִ֧יבוּ מִמֶּ֛נָּה קׇרְבָּ֖ן לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה וְהֶֽעֱמִ֥יד אֶת־הַבְּהֵמָ֖ה לִפְנֵ֥י הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If it is any unclean (blemished) animal which cannot be offered to Adonoy, he shall present the animal to the *kohein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be any unclean beast, of which they do not offer a sacrifice to the Lord, then he shall present the beast before the priest:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if any unclean beast [a blemished beast, which is "unclean" for offering (whence it is seen that unblemished consecrated animals are not redeemable unless they sustain a blemish)], which they may not present of it as an offering to the L-rd, then he shall stand the beast before the Cohein.


###### Leviticus 27:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:12)


וְהֶעֱרִ֤יךְ הַכֹּהֵן֙ אֹתָ֔הּ בֵּ֥ין ט֖וֹב וּבֵ֣ין רָ֑ע כְּעֶרְכְּךָ֥ הַכֹּהֵ֖ן כֵּ֥ן יִהְיֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall determine its value whether it is good or bad as the *kohein* determines its value for you, so shall it be.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priest shall value it, whether it be good or bad: as the priest values it, so shall it be.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall valuate it, whether good or bad; as the Cohein valuates it, so shall it be [(for others who come to buy it from the Temple)].


###### Leviticus 27:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:13)


וְאִם־גָּאֹ֖ל יִגְאָלֶ֑נָּה וְיָסַ֥ף חֲמִישִׁת֖וֹ עַל־עֶרְכֶּֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he will indeed redeem it he shall add on one fifth to its valuation.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if he will at all redeem it, then he shall add a fifth part thereof to the estimation.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if he [the owner] would redeem it, then he shall add its fifth to the valuation.


###### Leviticus 27:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:14)


וְאִ֗ישׁ כִּֽי־יַקְדִּ֨שׁ אֶת־בֵּית֥וֹ קֹ֙דֶשׁ֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה וְהֶעֱרִיכוֹ֙ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן בֵּ֥ין ט֖וֹב וּבֵ֣ין רָ֑ע כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר יַעֲרִ֥יךְ אֹת֛וֹ הַכֹּהֵ֖ן כֵּ֥ן יָקֽוּם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a man will consecrate his house as being sacred to Adonoy, the *kohein* shall determine its value, whether it is good or bad; as the *kohein* determines its value, so shall it be established.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when a man shall sanctify his house to be dedicated to the Lord, then the priest shall estimate it, whether it be good or bad: as the priest shall estimate it, so shall it stand.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man consecrate his house, holy to the L-rd, then the Cohein shall valuate it, whether good or bad; as the Cohein shall valuate it, so shall it stand [for others].


###### Leviticus 27:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:15)


וְאִ֨ם־הַמַּקְדִּ֔ישׁ יִגְאַ֖ל אֶת־בֵּית֑וֹ וְ֠יָסַ֠ף חֲמִישִׁ֧ית כֶּֽסֶף־עֶרְכְּךָ֛ עָלָ֖יו וְהָ֥יָה לֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he who consecrated [his house] wishes to redeem his house, he shall add one fifth to its valuation money in addition to [the valuation], and [then] it belongs to him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if he that sanctified it will redeem his house, then he shall add the fifth part of the money of the estimation to it, and it shall be his.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the consecrator [himself] would redeem his house, then he shall add a fifth of the valuation upon it, and it shall be his.


###### Leviticus 27:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:16)


וְאִ֣ם**׀** מִשְּׂדֵ֣ה אֲחֻזָּת֗וֹ יַקְדִּ֥ישׁ אִישׁ֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה וְהָיָ֥ה עֶרְכְּךָ֖ לְפִ֣י זַרְע֑וֹ זֶ֚רַע חֹ֣מֶר שְׂעֹרִ֔ים בַּחֲמִשִּׁ֖ים שֶׁ֥קֶל כָּֽסֶף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If from his ancestral field a man will consecrate to Adonoy its valuation shall be according to the measure of its seed; [at the rate of] one chomer of barley seed per fifty silver *shekalim*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man shall dedicate to the Lord some part of a field of his possession, then the estimation shall be according to the seed required for it, a ĥomer of barley seed shall be valued at fifty shekels of silver.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if from the field of his holding a man shall consecrate to the L-rd, then the valuation shall be according to its sowing; the sowing of a chomer of barley, for fifty silver shekels.


###### Leviticus 27:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:17)


אִם־מִשְּׁנַ֥ת הַיֹּבֵ֖ל יַקְדִּ֣ישׁ שָׂדֵ֑הוּ כְּעֶרְכְּךָ֖ יָקֽוּם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If immediately after the jubilee year he consecrates his field it shall be established according to its vaulation.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If he dedicate his field from the year of jubilee, according to the estimation it shall stand.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If from [i.e., immediately after] the Jubilee year he shall consecrate [and immediately come to redeem] his field, according to the [above] valuation [a chomer of barley for fifty silver shekels] shall it stand.


###### Leviticus 27:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:18)


וְאִם־אַחַ֣ר הַיֹּבֵל֮ יַקְדִּ֣ישׁ שָׂדֵ֒הוּ֒ וְחִשַּׁב־ל֨וֹ הַכֹּהֵ֜ן אֶת־הַכֶּ֗סֶף עַל־פִּ֤י הַשָּׁנִים֙ הַנּ֣וֹתָרֹ֔ת עַ֖ד שְׁנַ֣ת הַיֹּבֵ֑ל וְנִגְרַ֖ע מֵֽעֶרְכֶּֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If after the jubilee he consecrates his field, then the *kohein* shall calculate the money [due] according to the remaining years until the jubilee year, and its valuation should be reduced accordingly.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if he dedicate his field after the jubilee, then the priest shall reckon to him the money according to the years that remain, even to the year of the jubilee, and it shall be abated from the estimation.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if after the Jubilee year he shall consecrate his field, [and it remain with the Temple treasurer, and he comes to redeem it (some time) after the Jubilee year], the Cohein shall reckon for him the money (to be returned to the Temple) according to the years remaining until the Jubilee year, and it [the (value of the) number of years from the (preceding) Jubilee year until the year of redemption] shall be deducted from the (original total) valuation.


###### Leviticus 27:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:19)


וְאִם־גָּאֹ֤ל יִגְאַל֙ אֶת־הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה הַמַּקְדִּ֖ישׁ אֹת֑וֹ וְ֠יָסַ֠ף חֲמִשִׁ֧ית כֶּֽסֶף־עֶרְכְּךָ֛ עָלָ֖יו וְקָ֥ם לֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he will [indeed] redeem the field, he who consecrated it, shall add one fifth to its monetary valuation, and it is then established as his.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if he that sanctified the field will redeem it, then he shall add the fifth part of the money of the estimation to it, and it shall become his property.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the consecrator (himself) would redeem the field, then he shall add a fifth of the money of the valuation upon it, and it shall be his.


###### Leviticus 27:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:20)


וְאִם־לֹ֤א יִגְאַל֙ אֶת־הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה וְאִם־מָכַ֥ר אֶת־הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה לְאִ֣ישׁ אַחֵ֑ר לֹ֥א יִגָּאֵ֖ל עֽוֹד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If he does not redeem the field and if he sells the field to another person, it shall not longer be redeemable.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if he will not redeem the field, or if he have sold the field to another man, it shall not be redeemed any more.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if he [the consecrator] does not redeem the field, and he [the Temple treasurer] sells the field to another man, it shall not be redeemed again [i.e., It does not return to the consecrator (as a field of holding)].


###### Leviticus 27:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:21)


וְהָיָ֨ה הַשָּׂדֶ֜ה בְּצֵאת֣וֹ בַיֹּבֵ֗ל קֹ֛דֶשׁ לַֽיהֹוָ֖ה כִּשְׂדֵ֣ה הַחֵ֑רֶם לַכֹּהֵ֖ן תִּהְיֶ֥ה אֲחֻזָּתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When the field is then released by the jubilee it becomes sacred to Adonoy, just as a field that was consecrated, it shall belong to the *kohein,* [as] his possession.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the field, when it goes out in the jubilee, shall be holy to the Lord, as a field devoted; the possession of it shall be the priest’s.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the field shall be, when it goes out on the Jubilee year [from the one who bought it from the treasurer], holy to the L-rd, as a devoted field. To the Cohein shall be his holding [i.e., to the Cohanim of that watch on which the Yom Kippur of the Jubilee falls out.]


###### Leviticus 27:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:22)


וְאִם֙ אֶת־שְׂדֵ֣ה מִקְנָת֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֕ר לֹ֖א מִשְּׂדֵ֣ה אֲחֻזָּת֑וֹ יַקְדִּ֖ישׁ לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If it is a field that he purchased, that is not his ancestral field, [that] he consecrates to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man dedicate to the Lord a field which he has bought, which is not of the fields of his possession;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the field of his acquisition (from another), which is not of the field of his (family) holding, he shall consecrate to the L-rd, [and he comes to redeem it],


###### Leviticus 27:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:23)


וְחִשַּׁב־ל֣וֹ הַכֹּהֵ֗ן אֵ֚ת מִכְסַ֣ת הָֽעֶרְכְּךָ֔ עַ֖ד שְׁנַ֣ת הַיֹּבֵ֑ל וְנָתַ֤ן אֶת־הָעֶרְכְּךָ֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא קֹ֖דֶשׁ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* will calculate for him the amount of its valuation until the jubilee year, and on that day he shall give its valuation as something sacred to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then the priest shall reckon to him the worth of the estimation, to the year of the jubilee, and he shall give the estimation in that day, as a holy thing to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the Cohein shall reckon for him the worth of the valuation until the Jubilee year, and he shall give the valuation on that day, holy to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 27:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:24)


בִּשְׁנַ֤ת הַיּוֹבֵל֙ יָשׁ֣וּב הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה לַאֲשֶׁ֥ר קָנָ֖הוּ מֵאִתּ֑וֹ לַאֲשֶׁר־ל֖וֹ אֲחֻזַּ֥ת הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In the jubilee year the field shall revert to him from whom it was purchased; to him whose ancestral land it is.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the year of the jubilee the field shall return to him of whom it was bought, to him to whom the possession of the land did belong.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And if he does not redeem it and the treasurer sells it to another, or if he himself redeemed it, then] in the year of the Jubilee the field shall return to the one from whom he [the consecrator] bought it, to the one who has the holding of the land.


###### Leviticus 27:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:25)


וְכׇ֨ל־עֶרְכְּךָ֔ יִהְיֶ֖ה בְּשֶׁ֣קֶל הַקֹּ֑דֶשׁ עֶשְׂרִ֥ים גֵּרָ֖ה יִהְיֶ֥ה הַשָּֽׁקֶל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All of your valuations shall be in holy *shekalim*; the *shekel* shall be twenty *geirah*.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all estimations shall be according to the shekel of the sanctuary: twenty gera shall be the shekel.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all valuations [concerning which it is written "shekalim"] shall be according to the shekel of the sanctuary; twenty gerah shall the shekel be. [(Thus they were in the beginning; afterwards they added a sixth.)]


###### Leviticus 27:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:26)


אַךְ־בְּכ֞וֹר אֲשֶׁר־יְבֻכַּ֤ר לַֽיהֹוָה֙ בִּבְהֵמָ֔ה לֹֽא־יַקְדִּ֥ישׁ אִ֖ישׁ אֹת֑וֹ אִם־שׁ֣וֹר אִם־שֶׂ֔ה לַֽיהֹוָ֖ה הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, a firstling [animal] that is born first shall belong to Adonoy [as a sacrifice] from among the domestic animals; a man shall not consecrate it [for any other purpose] whether it is an ox or a lamb, it belongs to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only the firstling of the beasts, which should be the Lord’s firstling, no man shall dedicate that; whether it be ox, or sheep: it is the Lord’s.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But a first-born, which is a firstling to the L-rd among the beasts, a man shall not consecrate it [as a different offering, for it is not his (see Numbers 18:17)]; whether ox or lamb, it is the L-rd's.


###### Leviticus 27:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:27)


וְאִ֨ם בַּבְּהֵמָ֤ה הַטְּמֵאָה֙ וּפָדָ֣ה בְעֶרְכֶּ֔ךָ וְיָסַ֥ף חֲמִשִׁת֖וֹ עָלָ֑יו וְאִם־לֹ֥א יִגָּאֵ֖ל וְנִמְכַּ֥ר בְּעֶרְכֶּֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If it is of an unclean animal, he shall redeem it based on the valuation, and add a fifth to [that amount]. If it is not redeemed, it shall be sold according to the valuation.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be of an unclean beast, then he shall redeem it according to the estimation, and shall add to it a fifth part of it: or if it be not redeemed, then it shall be sold according to the estimation.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if (he wishes to redeem) an unclean beast [that he dedicated to bedek habayith (Temple maintenance)], then he shall redeem it according to the valuation [i.e., according to the Cohein's assessment], and he shall add its fifth upon it. And if it is not redeemed [by the owner], then it shall be sold by the valuation [to others].


###### Leviticus 27:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:28)


אַךְ־כׇּל־חֵ֡רֶם אֲשֶׁ֣ר יַחֲרִם֩ אִ֨ישׁ לַֽיהֹוָ֜ה מִכׇּל־אֲשֶׁר־ל֗וֹ מֵאָדָ֤ם וּבְהֵמָה֙ וּמִשְּׂדֵ֣ה אֲחֻזָּת֔וֹ לֹ֥א יִמָּכֵ֖ר וְלֹ֣א יִגָּאֵ֑ל כׇּל־חֵ֕רֶם קֹֽדֶשׁ־קׇדָשִׁ֥ים ה֖וּא לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, any dedication that a man will dedicate to Adonoy from any of his belongings be it people (slave), or animal, or of the field of his ancestral land, [none of these] can be sold or redeemed. Everything that is dedicated is most holy, unto Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Notwithstanding no devoted thing, that a man shall devote to the Lord of all that he has, both of man and beast, and of the field of his possession, shall be sold or redeemed: every devoted thing is most holy to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[There are two possible readings for this verse, one, (28a) according to those who hold that unspecified devotions (stam charamim) go to the Cohanim; the other, (28b) according to those who hold that they go to hekdesh (Temple maintenance)]


###### Leviticus 27:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:29)


כׇּל־חֵ֗רֶם אֲשֶׁ֧ר יׇחֳרַ֛ם מִן־הָאָדָ֖ם לֹ֣א יִפָּדֶ֑ה מ֖וֹת יוּמָֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Any dedication that a man will dedcate, regarding a man [due to be put to death] cannot be redeemed, he shall surely be put to death.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
None devoted of men, which shall be doomed to death, shall be redeemed; but shall surely be put to death.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Any cherem that is devoted from a man [i.e., from a man going out to be executed (an observer saying: "I vow to give the valuation of that man")] shall not be redeemed, [for] he is going to be put to death [(and has no valuation)].


###### Leviticus 27:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:30)


וְכׇל־מַעְשַׂ֨ר הָאָ֜רֶץ מִזֶּ֤רַע הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ מִפְּרִ֣י הָעֵ֔ץ לַיהֹוָ֖ה ה֑וּא קֹ֖דֶשׁ לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All tithings of the land; whether of the seeds [crops] of the land or of the fruit of the tree, belongs to Adonoy, it is sacred to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the tithe of the land, whether of the seed of the land, or of the fruit of the tree, is the Lord’s: it is holy to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the tithe of the land [second-tithe (ma'aser sheni)], of the seed of the land [i.e., grain], of the fruit of the tree [wine and oil], belong to the L-rd [and He has commanded you (Deuteronomy 14:23) to go up and eat it "from His table" in Jerusalem]; it is holy to the L-rd.


###### Leviticus 27:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:31)


וְאִם־גָּאֹ֥ל יִגְאַ֛ל אִ֖ישׁ מִמַּֽעַשְׂר֑וֹ חֲמִשִׁית֖וֹ יֹסֵ֥ף עָלָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a man wishes to redeem of his tithe, he shall add a fifth to [the amount].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a man will at all redeem of his tithes, he shall add to it the fifth part of it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man would redeem of his tithe [to permit him to eat it anywhere, (and with the redemption money he buys food in Jerusalem, which he eats there)], then he shall add its fifth upon it [(but if he redeems his neighbor's tithe, he need not add a fifth)].


###### Leviticus 27:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:32)


וְכׇל־מַעְשַׂ֤ר בָּקָר֙ וָצֹ֔אן כֹּ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־יַעֲבֹ֖ר תַּ֣חַת הַשָּׁ֑בֶט הָֽעֲשִׂירִ֕י יִֽהְיֶה־קֹּ֖דֶשׁ לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All tithes of cattle and sheep, anything that passes under the rod, the tenth one shall be sacred to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And concerning the tithe of the herd, or of the flock, of whatever passes under the rod, the tenth shall be holy to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the tithe of herd or flock, whatever passes under the staff [(He would strike every tenth animal (passing in a row) with a staff painted red)], the tenth shall be holy to the L-rd [(The blood and eimurin are offered on the altar, and the flesh is eaten by the owner)].


###### Leviticus 27:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:33)


לֹ֧א יְבַקֵּ֛ר בֵּֽין־ט֥וֹב לָרַ֖ע וְלֹ֣א יְמִירֶ֑נּוּ וְאִם־הָמֵ֣ר יְמִירֶ֔נּוּ וְהָֽיָה־ה֧וּא וּתְמוּרָת֛וֹ יִֽהְיֶה־קֹּ֖דֶשׁ לֹ֥א יִגָּאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall not make a distinction between better or worse [animals] and he shall not exchange it. If he exchanges one for another, then [both] it and its replacement shall be sacred and it cannot be redeemed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall not search whether it be good or bad, neither shall he change it: and if he change it at all, then both it and its substitute shall be holy; it shall not be redeemed.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He shall not discriminate (in tithing) between good [unblemished] or bad [blemished. (Not that he sacrifices a blemished animal, but that he eats it in the status of ma'aser, and it is forbidden to shear it or work it)]; nor shall he substitute (another animal) for it — and if he did substitute for it, then it and its substitute shall be holy; it shall not be redeemed.


###### Leviticus 27:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Leviticus/r/27:34)


אֵ֣לֶּה הַמִּצְוֺ֗ת אֲשֶׁ֨ר צִוָּ֧ה יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶת־מֹשֶׁ֖ה אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל בְּהַ֖ר סִינָֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are the commandments which Adonoy commanded Moshe for Bnei Yisroel on Mount Sinai. **Chazak**


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These are the commandments, which the Lord commanded Moshe for the children of Yisra᾽el in Mount Sinay.
			
			
				Vayyiqra


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are the mitzvoth which the L-rd charged Moses to (command to) the children of Israel on Mount Sinai.
